# File: packages/contracts/src/ygaraty/backend_contracts/_validation.py
"""Small strict validators for immutable in-process contract values."""

from enum import Enum


def text(value: str, name: str, maximum: int = 256) -> None:
    if not isinstance(value, str) or not value or len(value) > maximum:
        raise ValueError(
            f"{name} must be nonempty text, at most {maximum} chars"
        )


def integer(value: int, name: str, minimum: int = 0) -> None:
    if type(value) is not int or value < minimum:
        raise ValueError(f"{name} must be an integer >= {minimum}")


def member(value: Enum, kind: type[Enum]) -> None:
    if not isinstance(value, kind):
        raise ValueError(f"Expected {kind.__name__}")


def immutable_items(value: tuple, kind: type, name: str) -> None:
    if not isinstance(value, tuple) or any(
        not isinstance(item, kind) for item in value
    ):
        raise ValueError(f"{name} must be a tuple of {kind.__name__}")


# File: packages/contracts/src/ygaraty/backend_contracts/access.py
"""Host-issued access ceilings; never trust grants supplied by HTTP clients."""

from dataclasses import dataclass
from enum import StrEnum

from ._validation import integer, text


class Action(StrEnum):
    GENERATE = "generate"
    CANCEL = "cancel"
    CONTRIBUTE = "contribute"
    MANAGE = "manage"


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class AccessGrant:
    session_id: str
    epoch: str
    principal_id: str
    grant_id: str
    version: int
    expires_at: int
    actions: frozenset[Action]
    max_active: int
    max_outstanding: int

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        for name in ("session_id", "epoch", "principal_id", "grant_id"):
            text(getattr(self, name), name)
        for name in ("version", "expires_at", "max_active", "max_outstanding"):
            integer(getattr(self, name), name, 1)
        if self.max_active > self.max_outstanding:
            raise ValueError("Active ceiling exceeds outstanding ceiling")
        if (
            not isinstance(self.actions, frozenset)
            or not self.actions
            or any(not isinstance(action, Action) for action in self.actions)
        ):
            raise ValueError("actions must be a nonempty frozenset of Action")

    def require(
        self, *, session_id: str, epoch: str, action: Action, now: int
    ) -> None:
        """Check local scope/expiry, NOT authenticity or live revocation.

        The host AccessPolicy must also validate current grant/key/membership
        versions. Time is explicit so checks can be tested deterministically.
        """
        integer(now, "now")
        if not isinstance(action, Action):
            raise ValueError("Expected Action")
        if (
            self.session_id != session_id
            or self.epoch != epoch
            or action not in self.actions
            or now >= self.expires_at
        ):
            raise PermissionError("Access scope or expiry rejected")


# File: packages/contracts/src/ygaraty/backend_contracts/chat.py
"""Backend-neutral text requests and ordered stream events."""

import re
from dataclasses import dataclass
from enum import StrEnum

from ._validation import immutable_items, integer, member, text
from .lifecycle import CancelReceipt
from .models import HistoryMode


class Role(StrEnum):
    SYSTEM = "system"
    USER = "user"
    ASSISTANT = "assistant"


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Message:
    role: Role
    content: str

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        member(self.role, Role)
        text(self.content, "content", 100000)


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class ChatRequest:
    request_id: str
    conversation_id: str
    model_id: str
    history_mode: HistoryMode
    messages: tuple[Message, ...]
    max_output_tokens: int | None = None

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        text(self.request_id, "request_id")
        text(self.model_id, "model_id")
        text(self.conversation_id, "conversation_id", 64)
        if not re.fullmatch(r"[A-Za-z0-9_-]+", self.conversation_id):
            raise ValueError("Invalid conversation ID")
        member(self.history_mode, HistoryMode)
        immutable_items(self.messages, Message, "messages")
        if len(self.messages) > 128:
            raise ValueError("At most 128 messages")
        if not self.messages and self.history_mode != HistoryMode.RESET:
            raise ValueError("Messages are required outside reset")
        if self.messages and self.messages[-1].role != Role.USER:
            raise ValueError("The final message must be from the user")
        if self.max_output_tokens is not None:
            integer(self.max_output_tokens, "max_output_tokens", 1)


class ErrorCode(StrEnum):
    INVALID_REQUEST = "invalid_request"
    UNSUPPORTED_CAPABILITY = "unsupported_capability"
    CONTEXT_EXCEEDED = "context_exceeded"
    NOT_READY = "not_ready"
    CONVERSATION_BUSY = "conversation_busy"
    OVERLOADED = "overloaded"
    ACCESS_REVOKED = "access_revoked"
    STALE_EPOCH = "stale_epoch"
    CONTRIBUTOR_LOST = "contributor_lost"
    DEADLINE_EXCEEDED = "deadline_exceeded"
    EXECUTION_FAILED = "execution_failed"
    STATE_RESET_REQUIRED = "state_reset_required"


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Failure:
    code: ErrorCode
    message: str
    retry_after_seconds: int | None = None

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        member(self.code, ErrorCode)
        text(self.message, "safe error message", 1024)
        if self.retry_after_seconds is not None:
            integer(self.retry_after_seconds, "retry_after_seconds")


class BackendError(Exception):
    def __init__(self, failure: Failure):
        if not isinstance(failure, Failure):
            raise ValueError("Expected Failure")
        self.failure = failure
        super().__init__(failure.message)


class EventKind(StrEnum):
    ACCEPTED = "accepted"
    TEXT = "text"
    COMPLETED = "completed"
    CANCELLED = "cancelled"
    FAILED = "failed"


class FinishReason(StrEnum):
    STOP = "stop"
    LENGTH = "length"


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Usage:
    prompt_tokens: int
    output_tokens: int

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        integer(self.prompt_tokens, "prompt_tokens")
        integer(self.output_tokens, "output_tokens")


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class GenerationEvent:
    request_id: str
    sequence: int
    kind: EventKind
    text: str = ""
    finish_reason: FinishReason | None = None
    usage: Usage | None = None
    failure: Failure | None = None
    cancellation: CancelReceipt | None = None

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        text(self.request_id, "request_id")
        integer(self.sequence, "sequence")
        member(self.kind, EventKind)
        if self.kind == EventKind.TEXT:
            text(self.text, "text delta", 100000)
        elif self.text != "":
            raise ValueError("Only text events carry text")
        if self.kind == EventKind.COMPLETED:
            member(self.finish_reason, FinishReason)
            if not isinstance(self.usage, Usage):
                raise ValueError("Completed events require usage")
        elif self.finish_reason is not None or self.usage is not None:
            raise ValueError("Only completed events carry final usage/reason")
        if self.kind == EventKind.FAILED:
            if not isinstance(self.failure, Failure):
                raise ValueError("Failed events require Failure")
        elif self.failure is not None:
            raise ValueError("Only failed events carry Failure")
        if self.kind == EventKind.CANCELLED:
            if (
                not isinstance(self.cancellation, CancelReceipt)
                or self.cancellation.request_id != self.request_id
            ):
                raise ValueError("Cancelled events require matching receipt")
        elif self.cancellation is not None:
            raise ValueError("Only cancelled events carry a cancel receipt")


class EventSequence:
    """Per-handle stream validator; not a durable delivery guarantee."""

    def __init__(self, request_id: str):
        text(request_id, "request_id")
        self.request_id = request_id
        self._next = 0
        self._terminal = False

    def accept(self, event: GenerationEvent) -> None:
        if (
            not isinstance(event, GenerationEvent)
            or self._terminal
            or event.request_id != self.request_id
            or event.sequence != self._next
            or (event.kind == EventKind.ACCEPTED) != (self._next == 0)
        ):
            raise ValueError("Invalid event identity, ordering or lifecycle")
        self._next += 1
        self._terminal = event.kind in {
            EventKind.COMPLETED,
            EventKind.CANCELLED,
            EventKind.FAILED,
        }

    def finish(self) -> None:
        if not self._terminal:
            raise ValueError("Stream ended without a terminal event")


# File: packages/contracts/src/ygaraty/backend_contracts/lifecycle.py
"""Control outcomes distinguish accepted cancellation from stopped compute."""

from dataclasses import dataclass
from enum import StrEnum

from ._validation import member, text


class CancelReason(StrEnum):
    CLIENT_REQUEST = "client_request"
    DISCONNECTED = "disconnected"
    REVOKED = "revoked"
    DEADLINE = "deadline"
    SESSION_CLOSED = "session_closed"


class CancelStatus(StrEnum):
    REQUESTED = "requested"
    ACKNOWLEDGED = "acknowledged"
    TIMED_OUT = "timed_out"


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class CancelReceipt:
    request_id: str
    reason: CancelReason
    status: CancelStatus

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        text(self.request_id, "request_id")
        member(self.reason, CancelReason)
        member(self.status, CancelStatus)


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Revocation:
    """Trusted host control: supplied filters intersect; none targets epoch."""

    session_id: str
    epoch: str
    principal_id: str | None = None
    grant_id: str | None = None
    conversation_id: str | None = None

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        text(self.session_id, "session_id")
        text(self.epoch, "epoch")
        for name in ("principal_id", "grant_id", "conversation_id"):
            value = getattr(self, name)
            if value is not None:
                text(value, name)


# File: packages/contracts/src/ygaraty/backend_contracts/models.py
"""Validated metadata without model loading, services or mutable payloads."""

from dataclasses import dataclass
from enum import StrEnum

from ._validation import immutable_items, integer, member, text

CONTRACT_MAJOR = 1


class Route(StrEnum):
    RELAY = "relay"
    DIRECT = "direct"
    PRIVATE_TURN = "private_turn"


class HistoryMode(StrEnum):
    FULL = "full"
    APPEND = "append"
    RESET = "reset"


class SessionState(StrEnum):
    PREPARING = "preparing"
    READY = "ready"
    UNAVAILABLE = "unavailable"
    DRAINING = "draining"
    CLOSED = "closed"
    FAILED = "failed"


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class ModelIdentity:
    model_id: str
    checkpoint_digest: str
    tokenizer_digest: str
    template_digest: str
    precision: str

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        for name in self.__dataclass_fields__:
            text(getattr(self, name), name)


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class BackendDescriptor:
    backend_id: str
    implementation_version: str
    contract_majors: tuple[int, ...] = (CONTRACT_MAJOR,)

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        text(self.backend_id, "backend_id")
        text(self.implementation_version, "implementation_version")
        immutable_items(self.contract_majors, int, "contract_majors")
        if not self.contract_majors:
            raise ValueError("At least one contract major is required")
        for version in self.contract_majors:
            integer(version, "contract major", 1)
        if len(set(self.contract_majors)) != len(self.contract_majors):
            raise ValueError("Duplicate contract majors")


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class BackendConfiguration:
    """Opaque immutable bytes. The selected factory MUST validate its schema.

    These are not credentials or permission to load arbitrary Python code.
    No generic decoder may treat this envelope as validated backend options.
    """

    backend_id: str
    schema_version: int
    payload: bytes

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        text(self.backend_id, "backend_id")
        integer(self.schema_version, "schema_version", 1)
        if (
            type(self.payload) is not bytes
            or not 0 < len(self.payload) <= 1 << 20
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                "Configuration must be 1..1048576 immutable bytes"
            )


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class SessionSpec:
    session_id: str
    epoch: str
    model: ModelIdentity
    configuration: BackendConfiguration

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        text(self.session_id, "session_id")
        text(self.epoch, "epoch")
        if not isinstance(self.model, ModelIdentity):
            raise ValueError("Expected ModelIdentity")
        if not isinstance(self.configuration, BackendConfiguration):
            raise ValueError("Expected BackendConfiguration")


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class EffectiveCapabilities:
    context_tokens: int
    output_tokens: int
    active_requests: int
    queued_requests: int
    history_modes: tuple[HistoryMode, ...]
    routes: tuple[Route, ...]
    browser_contribution: bool

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        for name in ("context_tokens", "output_tokens", "active_requests"):
            integer(getattr(self, name), name, 1)
        integer(self.queued_requests, "queued_requests")
        if self.output_tokens > self.context_tokens:
            raise ValueError("Output limit exceeds context capacity")
        for name, kind in (("history_modes", HistoryMode), ("routes", Route)):
            value = getattr(self, name)
            immutable_items(value, kind, name)
            if not value or len(set(value)) != len(value):
                raise ValueError(f"{name} must be nonempty and unique")
        if type(self.browser_contribution) is not bool:
            raise ValueError("browser_contribution must be boolean")


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class SessionSnapshot:
    session_id: str
    epoch: str
    revision: int
    state: SessionState
    capabilities: EffectiveCapabilities | None = None

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        text(self.session_id, "session_id")
        text(self.epoch, "epoch")
        integer(self.revision, "revision")
        member(self.state, SessionState)
        if self.capabilities is not None and not isinstance(
            self.capabilities, EffectiveCapabilities
        ):
            raise ValueError("Expected EffectiveCapabilities")
        if self.state == SessionState.READY and self.capabilities is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "Ready sessions must report effective capabilities"
            )


# File: packages/contracts/src/ygaraty/backend_contracts/persistence.py
"""Host-owned durable metadata; never persist live execution objects here."""

from dataclasses import dataclass, replace
from enum import StrEnum
from typing import Protocol

from ._validation import integer, member, text
from .models import SessionSpec


class StoreConflict(Exception):
    """Duplicate ID, stale epoch/revision or conflicting terminal write."""


class RequestOutcome(StrEnum):
    COMPLETED = "completed"
    CANCELLED = "cancelled"
    FAILED = "failed"
    INTERRUPTED = "interrupted"


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class RequestKey:
    session_id: str
    epoch: str
    request_id: str

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        for name in self.__dataclass_fields__:
            text(getattr(self, name), name)


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class RequestRecord:
    key: RequestKey
    principal_id: str
    conversation_id: str
    outcome: RequestOutcome | None = None

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if not isinstance(self.key, RequestKey):
            raise ValueError("Expected RequestKey")
        text(self.principal_id, "principal_id")
        text(self.conversation_id, "conversation_id", 64)
        if self.outcome is not None:
            member(self.outcome, RequestOutcome)

    def finish(self, outcome: RequestOutcome) -> "RequestRecord":
        """Deterministic terminal transition; replaying the outcome is safe."""
        member(outcome, RequestOutcome)
        if self.outcome is not None and self.outcome != outcome:
            raise StoreConflict("Request already has a different outcome")
        return replace(self, outcome=outcome)


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class SessionRecord:
    """Desired configuration, not a claim that runtime resources are ready."""

    spec: SessionSpec
    revision: int = 0
    closed: bool = False

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if not isinstance(self.spec, SessionSpec):
            raise ValueError("Expected SessionSpec")
        integer(self.revision, "revision")
        if type(self.closed) is not bool:
            raise ValueError("closed must be boolean")


class RequestLedger(Protocol):
    async def admit(self, record: RequestRecord) -> None:
        """Atomically insert unfinished record for an open, current epoch.

        Duplicate key, stale/closed/missing session or terminal input raises
        StoreConflict. A successful return acknowledges configured durability.
        No dispatch is permitted before this succeeds.
        """
        ...

    async def finish(
        self, key: RequestKey, outcome: RequestOutcome
    ) -> RequestRecord:
        """Atomically finish an existing record; same outcome is idempotent.

        Missing keys or conflicting outcomes raise StoreConflict. This must
        never create a record or resurrect an old-epoch interrupted request.
        """
        ...


class SessionStore(RequestLedger, Protocol):
    """Optional host adapter implemented by Coopy, SQL or another local store.

    Implementations serialize each mutation atomically, including across the
    session and request records during restart. Storage failures propagate;
    they must not silently degrade into successful in-memory-only writes.
    """

    async def create(self, record: SessionRecord) -> None:
        """Insert revision-zero open session, rejecting existing IDs."""
        ...

    async def get(self, session_id: str) -> SessionRecord | None: ...

    async def close(
        self, session_id: str, *, expected_revision: int
    ) -> SessionRecord:
        """CAS close and increment revision; reject stale/missing records.

        Stop new admissions; existing work is drained by the host separately.
        Already closed with matching revision returns the unchanged record.
        """
        ...

    async def restart(
        self, session_id: str, *, expected_revision: int, new_epoch: str
    ) -> SessionRecord:
        """CAS rotate an open session epoch and increment its revision.

        Atomically mark unfinished old-epoch requests INTERRUPTED. Reject a
        missing/closed session, stale revision or previously used epoch. Never
        replay generation. The host opens fresh runtime resources afterwards.
        """
        ...


# File: packages/contracts/src/ygaraty/backend_contracts/protocols.py
"""Asynchronous execution and host ports, independent of web frameworks."""

from collections.abc import AsyncIterator
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Protocol

from .access import AccessGrant, Action
from .chat import ChatRequest, GenerationEvent
from .lifecycle import CancelReason, CancelReceipt, Revocation
from .models import BackendDescriptor, SessionSnapshot, SessionSpec
from .persistence import RequestLedger


class Clock(Protocol):
    def unix_seconds(self) -> int: ...

    def monotonic_seconds(self) -> float: ...


class AccessPolicy(Protocol):
    async def validate(
        self,
        grant: AccessGrant,
        *,
        session_id: str,
        epoch: str,
        action: Action,
    ) -> None:
        """Check scope, expiry AND live membership/key/grant versions.

        Fail closed on rejection or unavailability. Check at admission, before
        queued dispatch and periodically during execution. Grants originate
        in the host's authenticated context, never directly from HTTP bodies.
        """
        ...


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class RuntimeServices:
    clock: Clock
    access: AccessPolicy
    requests: RequestLedger


class RequestHandle(Protocol):
    @property
    def request_id(self) -> str: ...

    def events(self) -> AsyncIterator[GenerationEvent]:
        """Single consumer, bounded buffering, one logical terminal outcome."""
        ...

    async def cancel(self, reason: CancelReason) -> CancelReceipt:
        """Idempotent signal, with a separate termination acknowledgement.

        Host authenticates the handle owner before exposing this operation.
        Keep cleanup usable when the authorization store is unavailable.
        """
        ...

    async def aclose(self) -> None:
        """Idempotently drain and release, or quarantine uncertain state."""
        ...


class BackendSession(Protocol):
    def snapshot(self) -> SessionSnapshot: ...

    async def admit(
        self, request: ChatRequest, grant: AccessGrant
    ) -> RequestHandle:
        """Validate, authorize, reserve bounded capacity and persist admission.

        Return before streaming headers; unwind reservations on failure.
        Tokenization/context/state ownership remain backend responsibilities.
        """
        ...

    async def revoke(self, scope: Revocation) -> None:
        """Trusted host signal, affecting matching queued and running work."""
        ...

    async def close(self) -> None:
        """Idempotently stop admission, drain and release with a deadline."""
        ...


class BackendFactory(Protocol):
    def describe(self) -> BackendDescriptor:
        """Discover metadata without loading models or starting resources."""
        ...

    def validate(self, spec: SessionSpec) -> None:
        """Reject unsupported schemas, unknown options and invalid bounds.

        No resource acquisition. Own the decoder for configuration.payload;
        the contracts package validates only its immutable envelope.
        """
        ...

    async def open(
        self, spec: SessionSpec, services: RuntimeServices
    ) -> BackendSession:
        """Prepare resources, never rent them; unwind partial failure.

        Open does not imply readiness. Factories must validate even when called
        directly, outside BackendRegistry.
        """
        ...


# File: packages/contracts/src/ygaraty/backend_contracts/registry.py
"""Explicit composition without plugin scanning or implicit private imports."""

from .models import CONTRACT_MAJOR, BackendDescriptor, SessionSpec
from .protocols import BackendFactory, BackendSession, RuntimeServices


class BackendRegistry:
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self._factories: dict[str, BackendFactory] = {}
        self._descriptors: dict[str, BackendDescriptor] = {}

    def register(self, factory: BackendFactory) -> None:
        descriptor = factory.describe()
        if not isinstance(descriptor, BackendDescriptor):
            raise ValueError("Expected BackendDescriptor")
        if CONTRACT_MAJOR not in descriptor.contract_majors:
            raise ValueError("Incompatible backend contract major")
        if descriptor.backend_id in self._factories:
            raise ValueError("Duplicate backend ID")
        self._factories[descriptor.backend_id] = factory
        self._descriptors[descriptor.backend_id] = descriptor

    def describe(self) -> tuple[BackendDescriptor, ...]:
        return tuple(self._descriptors.values())

    async def open(
        self, spec: SessionSpec, services: RuntimeServices
    ) -> BackendSession:
        try:
            factory = self._factories[spec.configuration.backend_id]
        except KeyError:
            raise ValueError("Backend is not registered") from None
        factory.validate(spec)
        return await factory.open(spec, services)


# File: packages/contracts/tests/test_events.py
"""Stream lifecycle checks, independent of network delivery or inference."""

import unittest

from ygaraty.backend_contracts.chat import (
    ErrorCode,
    EventKind,
    EventSequence,
    Failure,
    FinishReason,
    GenerationEvent,
    Usage,
)
from ygaraty.backend_contracts.lifecycle import (
    CancelReason,
    CancelReceipt,
    CancelStatus,
)


class StreamTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_all_terminal_outcomes(self):
        endings = (
            GenerationEvent(
                "r",
                2,
                EventKind.COMPLETED,
                finish_reason=FinishReason.STOP,
                usage=Usage(2, 1),
            ),
            GenerationEvent(
                "r",
                2,
                EventKind.FAILED,
                failure=Failure(ErrorCode.CONTRIBUTOR_LOST, "Worker lost"),
            ),
            GenerationEvent(
                "r",
                2,
                EventKind.CANCELLED,
                cancellation=CancelReceipt(
                    "r", CancelReason.DISCONNECTED, CancelStatus.TIMED_OUT
                ),
            ),
        )
        for ending in endings:
            with self.subTest(kind=ending.kind):
                stream = EventSequence("r")
                stream.accept(GenerationEvent("r", 0, EventKind.ACCEPTED))
                stream.accept(
                    GenerationEvent("r", 1, EventKind.TEXT, text="é")
                )
                stream.accept(ending)
                stream.finish()
                with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
                    stream.accept(ending)

    def test_bad_order_does_not_advance_validator(self):
        stream = EventSequence("r")
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            stream.accept(GenerationEvent("r", 0, EventKind.TEXT, text="x"))
        stream.accept(GenerationEvent("r", 0, EventKind.ACCEPTED))
        for event in (
            GenerationEvent("r", 1, EventKind.ACCEPTED),
            GenerationEvent("other", 1, EventKind.TEXT, text="x"),
            GenerationEvent("r", 2, EventKind.TEXT, text="x"),
        ):
            with self.subTest(event=event):
                with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
                    stream.accept(event)
        stream.accept(GenerationEvent("r", 1, EventKind.TEXT, text="valid"))

    def test_truncated_stream_is_not_success(self):
        stream = EventSequence("r")
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            stream.finish()
        stream.accept(GenerationEvent("r", 0, EventKind.ACCEPTED))
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            stream.finish()

    def test_payloads_are_discriminated(self):
        for args in (
            {"kind": EventKind.TEXT},
            {"kind": EventKind.ACCEPTED, "text": "unexpected"},
            {"kind": EventKind.COMPLETED},
            {"kind": EventKind.FAILED},
            {"kind": EventKind.CANCELLED},
            {"kind": EventKind.ACCEPTED, "usage": Usage(1, 1)},
            {
                "kind": EventKind.CANCELLED,
                "cancellation": CancelReceipt(
                    "other", CancelReason.REVOKED, CancelStatus.REQUESTED
                ),
            },
        ):
            with self.subTest(args=args):
                with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
                    GenerationEvent("r", 0, **args)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()


# File: packages/contracts/tests/test_isolation.py
"""Import the complete public package without site-packages or the monorepo."""

import subprocess
import sys
import tempfile
import unittest
from pathlib import Path


class IsolationTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_all_modules_import_without_private_or_optional_dependencies(self):
        source = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "src"
        program = """
import importlib
import importlib.abc
import pkgutil
import sys

class RejectPrivateImports(importlib.abc.MetaPathFinder):
    def find_spec(self, fullname, path=None, target=None):
        if fullname in {'ygaraty.core', 'ygaraty.webgpu'} or fullname.split('.')[0] in {
            'ygaraty_cloud', 'ygaraty.webgpu', 'ygaraty.core', 'ygaraty_native', 'django', 'fastapi', 'vllm',
            'numpy', 'coopy',
        }:
            raise AssertionError('Forbidden dependency: ' + fullname)

sys.meta_path.insert(0, RejectPrivateImports())
sys.path.insert(0, sys.argv[1])
import ygaraty.backend_contracts
for module in pkgutil.walk_packages(
    ygaraty.backend_contracts.__path__, 'ygaraty.backend_contracts.'
):
    importlib.import_module(module.name)
assert ygaraty.backend_contracts.CONTRACT_MAJOR == 1
assert ygaraty.__file__ is None
"""
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as directory:
            result = subprocess.run(
                [sys.executable, "-I", "-S", "-c", program, str(source)],
                cwd=directory,
                capture_output=True,
                text=True,
                check=False,
            )
        self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 0, result.stdout + result.stderr)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()


# File: packages/contracts/tests/test_registry.py
"""Composition tests use fakes, never a real engine or service."""

import json
import unittest
from dataclasses import replace
from unittest.mock import AsyncMock, Mock

from test_values import session_spec

from ygaraty.backend_contracts.models import BackendDescriptor
from ygaraty.backend_contracts.persistence import RequestLedger
from ygaraty.backend_contracts.protocols import (
    AccessPolicy,
    BackendSession,
    Clock,
    RuntimeServices,
)
from ygaraty.backend_contracts.registry import BackendRegistry


class FixtureFactory:
    """A strict metadata decoder, not an inference implementation."""

    def __init__(self, backend_id="fixture", versions=(1,)):
        self.descriptor = BackendDescriptor(backend_id, "test", versions)
        self.open = AsyncMock(return_value=Mock(spec=BackendSession))
        self.validations = 0

    def describe(self):
        return self.descriptor

    def validate(self, spec):
        self.validations += 1
        if spec.configuration.schema_version != 1:
            raise ValueError("Unsupported fixture schema")
        options = json.loads(spec.configuration.payload)
        if options != {"context_tokens": 128}:
            raise ValueError("Invalid fixture configuration")


class RegistryTests(unittest.IsolatedAsyncioTestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.registry = BackendRegistry()
        self.factory = FixtureFactory()
        self.registry.register(self.factory)
        self.services = RuntimeServices(
            Mock(spec=Clock), Mock(spec=AccessPolicy), Mock(spec=RequestLedger)
        )

    def test_discovery_does_not_open_or_validate(self):
        self.assertEqual(self.registry.describe(), (self.factory.descriptor,))
        self.assertEqual(self.factory.validations, 0)
        self.factory.open.assert_not_called()

    def test_duplicates_and_unsupported_versions_are_rejected(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            self.registry.register(FixtureFactory())
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            self.registry.register(FixtureFactory("other", (2,)))
        self.assertEqual(len(self.registry.describe()), 1)

    async def test_only_selected_factory_opens(self):
        other = FixtureFactory("other")
        self.registry.register(other)
        spec = session_spec()
        session = await self.registry.open(spec, self.services)
        self.assertIs(session, self.factory.open.return_value)
        self.factory.open.assert_awaited_once_with(spec, self.services)
        self.assertEqual(self.factory.validations, 1)
        self.assertEqual(other.validations, 0)
        other.open.assert_not_called()

    async def test_missing_backend_does_not_fall_back(self):
        spec = session_spec()
        spec = replace(
            spec,
            configuration=replace(spec.configuration, backend_id="missing"),
        )
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            await self.registry.open(spec, self.services)
        self.factory.open.assert_not_called()

    async def test_invalid_configuration_cannot_acquire_resources(self):
        spec = session_spec()
        for changes in (
            {"schema_version": 2},
            {"payload": b'{"unknown":true}'},
            {"payload": b"not-json"},
        ):
            with self.subTest(changes=changes):
                invalid = replace(
                    spec, configuration=replace(spec.configuration, **changes)
                )
                with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
                    await self.registry.open(invalid, self.services)
                self.factory.open.assert_not_called()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()


# File: packages/contracts/tests/test_values.py
"""Metadata-only validation, with no backend or workload imports."""

import unittest
from dataclasses import FrozenInstanceError, replace

from ygaraty.backend_contracts.access import AccessGrant, Action
from ygaraty.backend_contracts.chat import ChatRequest, Message, Role
from ygaraty.backend_contracts.models import (
    BackendConfiguration,
    BackendDescriptor,
    EffectiveCapabilities,
    HistoryMode,
    ModelIdentity,
    Route,
    SessionSnapshot,
    SessionSpec,
    SessionState,
)
from ygaraty.backend_contracts.persistence import (
    RequestKey,
    RequestOutcome,
    RequestRecord,
    SessionRecord,
    StoreConflict,
)


def session_spec() -> SessionSpec:
    return SessionSpec(
        "session-1",
        "epoch-1",
        ModelIdentity("fixture", "sha256:a", "sha256:b", "sha256:c", "f32"),
        BackendConfiguration("fixture", 1, b'{"context_tokens":128}'),
    )


def grant() -> AccessGrant:
    return AccessGrant(
        "session-1",
        "epoch-1",
        "alice",
        "grant-1",
        1,
        100,
        frozenset({Action.GENERATE}),
        1,
        2,
    )


class ValueTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_nested_values_are_immutable(self):
        spec = session_spec()
        with self.assertRaises(FrozenInstanceError):
            spec.model.precision = "f16"
        with self.assertRaises(FrozenInstanceError):
            spec.configuration.payload = b"changed"
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            replace(spec.configuration, payload=bytearray(b"mutable"))

    def test_configuration_requires_bounded_versioned_payload(self):
        for changes in (
            {"schema_version": True},
            {"schema_version": 0},
            {"payload": b""},
            {"payload": b"x" * ((1 << 20) + 1)},
        ):
            with self.subTest(changes=list(changes)):
                with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
                    replace(session_spec().configuration, **changes)

    def test_descriptor_rejects_invalid_versions(self):
        for versions in ((), (True,), (0,), (1, 1), [1]):
            with self.subTest(versions=versions):
                with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
                    BackendDescriptor("fixture", "dev", versions)

    def test_effective_capacity_is_strict_and_bounded(self):
        caps = EffectiveCapabilities(
            128, 32, 1, 0, (HistoryMode.FULL,), (Route.RELAY,), True
        )
        for changes in (
            {"context_tokens": True},
            {"output_tokens": 129},
            {"active_requests": 0},
            {"queued_requests": -1},
            {"routes": ()},
            {"history_modes": [HistoryMode.FULL]},
            {"routes": (Route.RELAY, Route.RELAY)},
            {"browser_contribution": 1},
        ):
            with self.subTest(changes=changes):
                with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
                    replace(caps, **changes)

    def test_ready_requires_effective_capabilities(self):
        snapshot = SessionSnapshot("s", "e", 0, SessionState.PREPARING)
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            replace(snapshot, state=SessionState.READY)
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            replace(snapshot, state="ready")

    def test_history_mode_and_conversation_are_explicit(self):
        request = ChatRequest(
            "r",
            "chat-1",
            "fixture",
            HistoryMode.FULL,
            (Message(Role.USER, "Hello"),),
        )
        for changes in (
            {"messages": []},
            {"messages": ()},
            {"messages": (Message(Role.ASSISTANT, "Hi"),)},
            {"conversation_id": "../other"},
            {"max_output_tokens": True},
            {"history_mode": "append"},
            {"messages": request.messages * 129},
        ):
            with self.subTest(changes=list(changes)):
                with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
                    replace(request, **changes)
        reset = replace(request, history_mode=HistoryMode.RESET, messages=())
        self.assertEqual(reset.messages, ())


class AccessTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_scope_and_expiry_boundaries(self):
        args = dict(
            session_id="session-1",
            epoch="epoch-1",
            action=Action.GENERATE,
            now=99,
        )
        grant().require(**args)
        for changes in (
            {"session_id": "other"},
            {"epoch": "old"},
            {"action": Action.MANAGE},
            {"now": 100},
        ):
            with self.subTest(changes=changes):
                with self.assertRaises(PermissionError):
                    grant().require(**(args | changes))

    def test_access_ceilings_and_scopes_are_validated(self):
        for changes in (
            {"max_active": 3},
            {"version": 0},
            {"expires_at": True},
            {"actions": {Action.GENERATE}},
            {"actions": frozenset()},
            {"actions": frozenset({"generate"})},
        ):
            with self.subTest(changes=changes):
                with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
                    replace(grant(), **changes)


class PersistenceValueTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.record = RequestRecord(
            RequestKey("s", "e", "r"), "alice", "chat-1"
        )

    def test_terminal_transition_is_immutable_and_idempotent(self):
        completed = self.record.finish(RequestOutcome.COMPLETED)
        self.assertIsNone(self.record.outcome)
        self.assertEqual(completed.finish(RequestOutcome.COMPLETED), completed)
        with self.assertRaises(StoreConflict):
            completed.finish(RequestOutcome.FAILED)

    def test_interrupted_work_cannot_later_be_marked_successful(self):
        interrupted = self.record.finish(RequestOutcome.INTERRUPTED)
        with self.assertRaises(StoreConflict):
            interrupted.finish(RequestOutcome.COMPLETED)

    def test_records_reject_invalid_state(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            replace(self.record, outcome="completed")
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            SessionRecord(session_spec(), revision=True)
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            SessionRecord(session_spec(), closed="yes")

    unittest.main()

# File: packages/core/src/ygaraty/core/analysis/convnet_scaling.py
"""Offline comparison of experiment-45 reports; no model or execution
imports."""


def comparison_lines(cases):
    """Validate completed, matching workloads and format the existing CLI
    table.

    Reports retain their original timing boundaries. This does not run a
    benchmark
    or infer hardware equivalence; comparison policy is unchanged from
    experiment 45.
    """
    if any(c["state"] != "completed" for c in cases):
        raise ValueError("Only completed cases can be compared")
    keys = (
        "local_steps",
        "measured_rounds",
        "warmup_rounds",
        "batch",
        "seed",
        "tiny",
        "model",
        "data",
        "allow_shared_host",
    )

    def identity(c):
        return (
            {k: c["config"][k] for k in keys},
            c["metadata"]["data_sha256"],
            c["metadata"].get("source_sha256"),
        )

    if any(identity(c) != identity(cases[0]) for c in cases):
        raise ValueError(
            "Cases have different workloads; compare each "
            "H/batch/dataset separately"
        )
    lines = [
        "backend/device/dtype | replicas | images/sec | measured "
        "seconds | scaling vs same-backend 1 host"
    ]
    for c in cases:
        m = next(iter(c["members"].values()))
        label = "/".join(
            (
                c["config"]["backend"],
                m.get("device", "GPU"),
                m.get("dtype", "mixed"),
            )
        )
        baseline = next(
            (
                b
                for b in cases
                if b["config"]["replicas"] == 1
                and b["config"]["backend"] == c["config"]["backend"]
                and all(
                    next(iter(b["members"].values())).get(k) == m.get(k)
                    for k in ("device", "dtype")
                )
            ),
            None,
        )
        scaling = (
            f"{c['images_per_second'] / baseline['images_per_second']:.2f}x"
            if baseline
            else "n/a"
        )
        lines.append(
            f"{label} | {c['config']['replicas']} | "
            f"{c['images_per_second']:.2f} | "
            f"{c['measured_seconds']:.3f} | {scaling}"
        )
    return lines


# File: packages/core/src/ygaraty/core/analysis/metrics.py
"""Pure workload metrics; all inputs are supplied by callers, with no dataset
access.

The nanochat yardstick is an illustrative extrapolation, not measured training
time.
"""

NANOCHAT_FLOPS = 4e19
YEAR = 365.25 * 86400


def layer_parameters(width, hidden, decoder=False):
    attention = 4 * (width * width + width)
    feed_forward = width * hidden + hidden + hidden * width + width
    return (
        (2 if decoder else 1) * attention
        + feed_forward
        + (6 if decoder else 4) * width
    )


def useful_flops(model, tokens):
    """Training FLOPs one update spent on real tokens. `model`: the job's
    model_config (vocab,
    embedding, hidden, layers); `tokens`: the batch's real tokens, as
    DatabaseTranslationJob
    records them in dataset_source['tokens'] (source and target, each with its
    EOS or BOS)."""
    width, hidden, layers = (
        model["embedding"],
        model["hidden"],
        model["layers"],
    )
    encoder = layers * layer_parameters(width, hidden)
    decoder = (
        layers * layer_parameters(width, hidden, decoder=True)
        + model["vocab"] * width
    )
    return 6 * (encoder * tokens["source"] + decoder * tokens["target"])


def nanochat_seconds(flops_per_second):
    return NANOCHAT_FLOPS / flops_per_second


def duration(seconds):
    days = seconds / 86400
    return (
        f"{seconds / YEAR:,.0f} years"
        if days >= 3 * 365
        else f"{days:,.0f} days"
        if days >= 3
        else f"{seconds / 3600:,.1f} hours"
    )


def steady(updates, checkpoint_every, last_update=None):
    """The updates the KPI counts: not the first (owners load their state), not
    a checkpoint's."""
    return [
        u
        for u in updates[1:]
        if u["update"] != last_update
        and not (checkpoint_every and u["update"] % checkpoint_every == 0)
    ]


def summarize(updates, model):
    """Steady updates' seconds, real tokens per second, useful FLOP/s and the
    nanochat time."""
    seconds = sum(u["seconds"] for u in updates)
    flops = sum(useful_flops(model, u["tokens"]) for u in updates)
    real = sum(u["tokens"]["source"] + u["tokens"]["target"] for u in updates)
    padded = sum(u["tokens"]["padded"] for u in updates)
    return dict(
        updates=len(updates),
        seconds_per_update=seconds / len(updates),
        real_tokens_per_second=real / seconds,
        real_share=real / padded,
        useful_gflops=flops / seconds / 1e9,
        nanochat_seconds=nanochat_seconds(flops / seconds),
    )


# File: packages/core/src/ygaraty/core/analysis/task_families.py
"""Stable operation labels shared by measured and simulated reports."""

FAMILIES = {
    "lstm_cell": "LSTM forward steps",
    "lstm_cell_grad": "LSTM backward steps",
    "lstm_state_grad": "LSTM backward steps",
    "tf_layer": "Transformer layers (forward)",
    "tf_layer_grad": "Transformer layers (backward)",
    "tf_owned_layer": "Transformer layers (forward)",
    "tf_owned_grad": "Transformer layers (backward)",
    "tf_owned_step": "optimizer",
    "tf_owned_load": "owned layer load and publish",
    "tf_owned_publish": "owned layer load and publish",
    "tf_vocab_load": "owned layer load and publish",
    "tf_vocab_publish": "owned layer load and publish",
    "tf_vocab_step": "optimizer",
    "tf_vocab_gather": "owned vocabulary (embedding rows)",
    "tf_vocab_embed_grad": "owned vocabulary (embedding rows)",
    "tf_vocab_stats": "output softmax and loss",
    "vocab_loss": "output softmax and loss",
    "tf_vocab_grad": "output gradient (owned vocabulary)",
    "ce_fused": "output softmax and loss",
    "layer_norm": "layer norms",
    "layer_norm_backward": "layer norms",
    "lstm_chain": "LSTM forward steps",
    "lstm_chain_grad": "LSTM backward steps",
    "matmul": "matmuls (projections, weight gradients)",
    "add_many": "sums of partial products",
    "reduce_rows": "bias gradients (reduce_rows)",
    "scatter_rows": "embedding gradient (scatter_rows)",
    "place_block": "placements and concatenations",
    "add": "gradient accumulation (add)",
    "bqk": "attention",
    "bpv": "attention",
    "bptq": "attention",
    "softmax": "attention",
    "softmax_backward": "attention",
    "cross_entropy": "output softmax and loss",
    "ce_backward": "output softmax and loss",
    "assemble": "metadata (coordinator)",
    "transpose": "metadata (coordinator)",
}


def family(op, task=""):
    if op.startswith("adam") or op == "sgd":
        return "optimizer"
    if op == "matmul" and (
        "/hw/" in task or "/dh/" in task or "/dh0/" in task
    ):
        return "recurrent products (chain)"
    return FAMILIES.get(op, "elementwise and other")


# File: packages/core/src/ygaraty/core/compiler/__init__.py
"""Transport-independent tensor graph construction and lowering."""

from importlib import import_module

_MODULES = {
    "Tape": "tape",
    "ShapeTape": "shapes",
    "Parameters": "parameters",
    "Input": "tasks",
    "Task": "tasks",
    "with_optimizer": "optimizer",
    **dict.fromkeys(
        ("split", "group", "bounded_reductions", "admit_plan", "prune_unused"),
        "plan",
    ),
}
__all__ = list(_MODULES)


def __getattr__(name):
    if name not in _MODULES:
        raise AttributeError(name)
    value = getattr(import_module(f".{_MODULES[name]}", __name__), name)
    globals()[name] = value
    return value
