    main()


# File: packages/core/src/ygaraty/core/compiler/convolution.py
"""Portable NCHW operation contracts, represented as rank-two tensors."""

from collections.abc import Mapping
from dataclasses import dataclass

Shape = tuple[int, int]
MAX_TENSOR_BYTES = 64 << 20
OPS = (
    "conv2d",
    "conv2d_input_grad",
    "conv2d_weight_grad",
    "conv2d_bias_grad",
    "maxpool2d",
    "maxpool2d_grad",
    "avgpool2d",
    "avgpool2d_grad",
)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ConvolutionSpec:
    """Validated dimensions shared by shape inference and numerical
    execution.
    """

    batch: int
    channels: int
    height: int
    width: int
    outputs: int
    kernel: int

    @classmethod
    def from_attributes(cls, attrs: Mapping[str, object]) -> "ConvolutionSpec":
        values = []
        for name, maximum in (
            ("batch", 64),
            ("in_channels", 256),
            ("height", 128),
            ("width", 128),
        ):
            value = attrs.get(name)
            if type(value) is not int or not 1 <= value <= maximum:
                raise ValueError("Invalid " + name)
            values.append(value)
        outputs = attrs.get("out_channels", values[1])
        kernel = attrs.get("kernel", 3)
        if (
            type(outputs) is not int
            or not 1 <= outputs <= 256
            or type(kernel) is not int
            or kernel not in (1, 3)
        ):
            raise ValueError("Invalid output channels or kernel")
        return cls(*values, outputs, kernel)

    def dimensions(self) -> tuple[int, int, int, int, int, int]:
        return (
            self.batch,
            self.channels,
            self.height,
            self.width,
            self.outputs,
            self.kernel,
        )

    def shapes(self, operation: str) -> tuple[list[Shape], Shape]:
        n, c, h, w, o, k = self.dimensions()
        image, output, weight = (n, c * h * w), (n, o * h * w), (o, c * k * k)
        convolution = {
            "conv2d": ([image, weight, (1, o)], output),
            "conv2d_input_grad": ([output, weight], image),
            "conv2d_weight_grad": ([image, output], weight),
            "conv2d_bias_grad": ([output], (1, o)),
        }
        if operation in convolution:
            return convolution[operation]
        if operation in ("maxpool2d", "maxpool2d_grad"):
            if h % 2 or w % 2:
                raise ValueError(
                    "Max pooling requires even spatial dimensions"
                )
            pooled = (n, c * (h // 2) * (w // 2))
            return (
                ([image], pooled)
                if operation == "maxpool2d"
                else ([image, pooled], image)
            )
        if operation == "avgpool2d":
            return [image], (n, c)
        if operation == "avgpool2d_grad":
            return [(n, c)], image
        raise ValueError("Unknown convolution operation")


def contract(op: str, attrs: Mapping[str, object]):
    """Return legacy input/output shapes and dimensions with the same
    limits.
    """
    if op not in OPS:
        raise ValueError("Unknown convolution operation")
    spec = ConvolutionSpec.from_attributes(attrs)
    inputs, output = spec.shapes(op)
    if any(
        rows * columns * 8 > MAX_TENSOR_BYTES
        for rows, columns in [*inputs, output]
    ):
        raise ValueError("Convolution tensor exceeds 64 MiB")
    return inputs, output, spec.dimensions()


# File: packages/core/src/ygaraty/core/compiler/cost.py
"""Operation work estimates for fitted timing models: seconds = a + b *
work.

These are operation-specific work units, not measured durations or FLOP
counts. The caller separately accounts for any constituent kernels in
``extra_work``.
"""

import math
from collections.abc import Mapping, Sequence
from typing import Any

Shape = Sequence[int]
_FACTORIZATION = frozenset(
    {
        "factorization_block",
        "factorization_tile",
        "factorization_tiles",
    }
)
_TRANSFORMER_FORWARD = frozenset({"tf_layer", "tf_owned_layer"})
_TRANSFORMER_BACKWARD = frozenset({"tf_layer_grad", "tf_owned_grad"})
_OWNER_TRANSFER = frozenset(
    {
        "tf_owned_step",
        "tf_vocab_step",
        "tf_owned_load",
        "tf_vocab_load",
    }
)


def _transformer_work(rows: int, width: int, attrs: Mapping[str, Any]):
    """Projection/feed-forward work plus attention over sequence positions."""
    projections = 8 if attrs.get("decoder") else 4
    return rows * width * width * projections + rows * width * attrs.get(
        "steps", 1
    )


def task_work(
    op: str,
    attrs: Mapping[str, Any],
    shape: Shape,
    inputs: Sequence[Shape],
) -> int | float:
    """Estimate work for an output shape and its input-region shapes.

    Shapes and attributes are supplied by validated compiler plans. Unknown
    operations retain the generic input-plus-output element estimate.
    """
    output_elements = math.prod(shape)
    input_elements = sum(math.prod(dims) for dims in inputs)
    rows, width = shape[0], shape[1]
    if op == "matmul":
        (rows, inner), (_, columns) = inputs[0], inputs[1]
        return rows * inner * columns
    if op in _FACTORIZATION:
        # extra_work already describes the constituent kernels. Retain only
        # the fitted dispatch intercept here; do not double-count their work.
        return 0
    if op in _TRANSFORMER_FORWARD:
        return _transformer_work(rows, width, attrs)
    if op in _TRANSFORMER_BACKWARD:
        return 3 * _transformer_work(inputs[0][0], width, attrs)
    if op == "ce_fused":
        return inputs[0][0] * inputs[0][1] * inputs[1][1]
    if op in ("tf_vocab_stats", "tf_vocab_grad"):
        start, stop = attrs["rows"]
        passes = 1 if op == "tf_vocab_stats" else 3
        return inputs[0][0] * inputs[0][1] * (stop - start) * passes
    if op in _OWNER_TRANSFER:
        return input_elements + attrs.get("elements", 0)
    return input_elements + output_elements


# File: packages/core/src/ygaraty/core/compiler/export.py
"""Export a saved Ygaraty task plan as one readable computation per line."""

import argparse
import json
from collections.abc import Mapping, Sequence
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

TaskDescription = Mapping[str, Any]


def _format_input(reference: Mapping[str, Any]) -> str:
    value = json.dumps(reference["value"])
    if reference["region"] is not None:
        row_start, row_stop, column_start, column_stop = reference["region"]
        value += f"[{row_start}:{row_stop}, {column_start}:{column_stop}]"
    return value


def _format_task(number: int, task: TaskDescription) -> str:
    inputs = ", ".join(_format_input(ref) for ref in task["inputs"])
    shape = " x ".join(map(str, task["shape"]))
    attrs = json.dumps(task["attrs"], sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"))
    return (
        f"{number:05d} | {json.dumps(task['id'])} = {task['op']}({inputs}) "
        f"-> [{shape}] | attrs={attrs}\n"
    )


class PlanExport:
    """Validated, detached text/JSONL renderings of an ordered task list.

    This validates task identity and dependency order, not numerical semantics
    or admission budgets. Construction finishes before any output is opened.
    """

    def __init__(self, tasks: Sequence[TaskDescription]):
        ids = [task["id"] for task in tasks]
        if len(set(ids)) != len(ids):
            raise ValueError("Duplicate task IDs")
        all_ids, seen, external = set(ids), set(), set()
        text_lines, json_lines = [], []
        for number, task in enumerate(tasks, 1):
            for reference in task["inputs"]:
                ident = reference["value"]
                if ident in all_ids and ident not in seen:
                    raise ValueError("Plan is not in dependency order")
                if ident not in all_ids:
                    external.add(ident)
            text_lines.append(_format_task(number, task))
            json_lines.append(json.dumps(task, separators=(",", ":")) + "\n")
            seen.add(task["id"])
        self._text = tuple(text_lines)
        self._jsonl = tuple(json_lines)
        self._external_inputs = tuple(sorted(external))

    @property
    def summary(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "tasks": len(self._text),
            "external_inputs": list(self._external_inputs),
        }

    def write(self, text_path: str | Path, jsonl_path: str | Path) -> None:
        """Create outputs exclusively; existing files are never
        overwritten.

        The two creations are sequential, not a transaction. A failure
        creating the second file can leave the first output in place.
        """
        with Path(text_path).open("x") as stream:
            stream.writelines(self._text)
        with Path(jsonl_path).open("x") as stream:
            stream.writelines(self._jsonl)


def export_plan(
    source: str | Path, text_path: str | Path, jsonl_path: str | Path
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Compatibility entry point for exporting a saved JSON task list."""
    plan = PlanExport(json.loads(Path(source).read_text()))
    plan.write(text_path, jsonl_path)
    return plan.summary


def main(argv: Sequence[str] | None = None) -> None:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("plan", type=Path)
    parser.add_argument("--text", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--jsonl", type=Path, required=True)
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
    print(json.dumps(export_plan(args.plan, args.text, args.jsonl), indent=2))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()


# File: packages/core/src/ygaraty/core/compiler/layouts.py
"""Shared operation layouts used by graph builders, lowering, and numerical
kernels.

These definitions describe tensor representation only; importing them
never loads execution code. Parameter order and packed offsets are part
of the task contract.
"""

# Serialized block element sizes, shared by storage and offline traffic models.
BLOCK_DTYPES = {"<f8": 8, "<f4": 4, "bf16": 2}

H_STATE, C_STATE, H_OUT, GATES = 0, 1, 2, 3  # column blocks of an LSTM value

ENCODER_PARAMS = [
    "norm1.weight",
    "norm1.bias",
    "self_attn.in_proj_weight",
    "self_attn.in_proj_bias",
    "self_attn.out_proj.weight",
    "self_attn.out_proj.bias",
    "norm2.weight",
    "norm2.bias",
    "linear1.weight",
    "linear1.bias",
    "linear2.weight",
    "linear2.bias",
]
DECODER_PARAMS = [
    "norm1.weight",
    "norm1.bias",
    "self_attn.in_proj_weight",
    "self_attn.in_proj_bias",
    "self_attn.out_proj.weight",
    "self_attn.out_proj.bias",
    "norm2.weight",
    "norm2.bias",
    "multihead_attn.in_proj_weight",
    "multihead_attn.in_proj_bias",
    "multihead_attn.out_proj.weight",
    "multihead_attn.out_proj.bias",
    "norm3.weight",
    "norm3.bias",
    "linear1.weight",
    "linear1.bias",
    "linear2.weight",
    "linear2.bias",
]


def packed_layout(shapes, d):
    """Where each parameter's gradient goes in the packed backward output
    (rows of width d): [(row offset, rows, mode)], total rows. Mode
    'rows': shape (a, d), stored as is. 'transposed': shape (d, b),
    stored as its transpose. 'vector': shape (1, k), k values over
    ceil(k/d) rows, zero padded.
    """
    layout, offset = [], 0
    for a, b in shapes:
        if b == d:
            mode, rows = "rows", a
        elif a == d:
            mode, rows = "transposed", b
        elif a == 1:
            mode, rows = "vector", -(-b // d)
        else:
            raise ValueError(
                f"Cannot pack a gradient of shape {(a, b)} "
                f"into rows of width {d}"
            )
        layout.append((offset, rows, mode))
        offset += rows
    return layout, offset


# File: packages/core/src/ygaraty/core/compiler/optimizer.py
"""Append worker-side optimizer tasks without executing or storing tensors."""

import math
from collections.abc import Mapping, Sequence
from typing import Any

from .plan import MAX_OUTPUT_BYTES
from .shapes import regions
from .tasks import Input, Task


def adam_options(config: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, float]:
    """Validate optimizer settings, preserving defaults and ranges."""
    options = dict(
        beta1=config.get("beta1", 0.9),
        beta2=config.get("beta2", 0.999),
        epsilon=config.get("epsilon", 1e-8),
        clip_norm=config.get("clip_norm", 0.0),
    )
    if not all(
        isinstance(v, (float, int)) and math.isfinite(v)
        for v in options.values()
    ):
        raise ValueError("Optimizer settings must be finite numbers")
    if (
        not 0 <= options["beta1"] < 1
        or not 0 <= options["beta2"] < 1
        or options["epsilon"] <= 0
        or options["clip_norm"] < 0
    ):
        raise ValueError("Invalid optimizer settings")
    return options


class OptimizerPlanBuilder:
    """One append operation: clipping, update kernels and output assembly."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        tasks: Sequence[Task],
        shapes: Mapping[str, tuple[int, int]],
        tile_size: int,
        learning_rate: float,
        optimizer: str = "sgd",
        step: int = 1,
        options: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
        fused_bytes: int | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        if optimizer not in ("sgd", "adam") or step < 1:
            raise ValueError("Invalid optimizer or update index")
        self.options = adam_options(options or {})
        self.tasks = list(tasks)
        self.shapes, self.tile_size = shapes, tile_size
        self.learning_rate, self.optimizer = learning_rate, optimizer
        self.step, self.fused_bytes = step, fused_bytes
        self.updated: dict[str, str] = {}

    def _norm(self, parameters, gradients):
        if not self.options["clip_norm"]:
            return
        squares = []
        for parameter in parameters:
            for region in regions(self.shapes[parameter], self.tile_size):
                ident = f"norm/{parameter}/{region[0]}/{region[2]}"
                self.tasks.append(
                    Task(
                        ident,
                        "sum_squares",
                        [Input(gradients[parameter], region)],
                        (1, 1),
                    )
                )
                squares.append(Input(ident))
        self.tasks.append(
            Task("gradient_squares", "add_many", squares, (1, 1))
        )

    def _gradient(self, ident, gradient, region, shape):
        reference = Input(gradient, region)
        if self.options["clip_norm"]:
            self.tasks.append(
                Task(
                    ident + "/clip",
                    "clip_gradient",
                    [reference, Input("gradient_squares")],
                    shape,
                    self.options,
                )
            )
            reference = Input(ident + "/clip")
        return reference

    def _fused(self, parameter, gradient, parts, positions):
        rows, columns = self.shapes[parameter]
        # Admission counts four times the bytes of four inputs and three
        # outputs.
        band = max(
            1,
            min(
                rows,
                self.fused_bytes // (4 * 7 * 8 * columns),
                MAX_OUTPUT_BYTES // (3 * 8 * columns),
            ),
        )
        for start in range(0, rows, band):
            stop = min(rows, start + band)
            region, count = [start, stop, 0, columns], stop - start
            ident = f"update/{parameter}/{start}/0"
            grad = self._gradient(ident, gradient, region, (count, columns))
            inputs = [
                Input(name, region)
                for name in (
                    parameter,
                    f"adam_m_{parameter}",
                    f"adam_v_{parameter}",
                )
            ]
            self.tasks.append(
                Task(
                    ident,
                    "adam_step",
                    [*inputs, grad],
                    (3 * count, columns),
                    dict(
                        self.options,
                        step=self.step,
                        learning_rate=self.learning_rate,
                        upload_rows=[count, count, count],
                    ),
                )
            )
            for index, name in enumerate(
                (f"adam_m_{parameter}", f"adam_v_{parameter}", parameter)
            ):
                parts[name].append(
                    Input(
                        ident, [index * count, (index + 1) * count, 0, columns]
                    )
                )
            positions.append(region)

    def _tiled(self, parameter, gradient, parts, positions):
        for region in regions(self.shapes[parameter], self.tile_size):
            start, stop, left, right = region
            shape = (stop - start, right - left)
            ident = f"update/{parameter}/{start}/{left}"
            grad = self._gradient(ident, gradient, region, shape)
            if self.optimizer == "adam":
                moments = []
                for moment in ("m", "v"):
                    name, mid = (
                        f"adam_{moment}_{parameter}",
                        ident + "/" + moment,
                    )
                    self.tasks.append(
                        Task(
                            mid,
                            "adam_" + moment,
                            [Input(name, region), grad],
                            shape,
                            self.options,
                        )
                    )
                    parts[name].append(Input(mid))
                    moments.append(Input(mid))
                self.tasks.append(
                    Task(
                        ident,
                        "adam_update",
                        [Input(parameter, region), *moments],
                        shape,
                        dict(
                            self.options,
                            step=self.step,
                            learning_rate=self.learning_rate,
                        ),
                    )
                )
            else:
                self.tasks.append(
                    Task(
                        ident,
                        "sgd",
                        [Input(parameter, region), grad],
                        shape,
                        {"learning_rate": self.learning_rate},
                    )
                )
            parts[parameter].append(Input(ident))
            positions.append(region)

    def _assemble(self, parameter, parts, positions):
        for name, references in parts.items():
            ident = "updated/" + name
            shape = self.shapes[parameter]
            self.tasks.append(
                Task(
                    ident,
                    "assemble",
                    references,
                    tuple(shape),
                    {"shape": list(shape), "blocks": positions},
                )
            )
            self.updated[name] = ident

    def build(
        self,
        parameters: Sequence[str],
        gradients: Mapping[str, str],
    ) -> tuple[list[Task], dict[str, str]]:
        """Append once to this builder; use a new builder for each update
        plan.
        """
        self._norm(parameters, gradients)
        for parameter in parameters:
            names = [parameter]
            if self.optimizer == "adam":
                names += [f"adam_m_{parameter}", f"adam_v_{parameter}"]
            parts, positions = {name: [] for name in names}, []
            method = (
                self._fused
                if self.optimizer == "adam" and self.fused_bytes
                else self._tiled
            )
            method(parameter, gradients[parameter], parts, positions)
            self._assemble(parameter, parts, positions)
        return self.tasks, self.updated


def with_optimizer(
    tasks,
    shapes,
    parameters,
    gradients,
    tile_size,
    learning_rate,
    optimizer="sgd",
    step=1,
    options=None,
    fused_bytes=None,
):
    """Compatibility wrapper for clipping, tiled or fused updates and
    assembly.
    """
    builder = OptimizerPlanBuilder(
        tasks,
        shapes,
        tile_size,
        learning_rate,
        optimizer,
        step,
        options,
        fused_bytes,
    )
    return builder.build(parameters, gradients)


def decode_task(description: Mapping[str, Any]) -> Task:
    """Reconstruct the existing task description format."""
    return Task(
        description["id"],
        description["op"],
        [Input(**reference) for reference in description["inputs"]],
        tuple(description["shape"]),
        description["attrs"],
    )


# File: packages/core/src/ygaraty/core/compiler/parameters.py
"""Bind model parameter names to one tape; no execution or initialization
I/O.
"""

from collections.abc import Mapping
from types import MappingProxyType

from .shapes import ShapeTape


class Parameters(Mapping):
    """Read-only parameter bindings with tape-local, lazy transpose reuse.

    Numeric tapes receive arrays; ShapeTape receives initializer
    specifications. Model names retain their dots here and use
    underscores only in tensor IDs. Bindings and cached graph nodes
    belong to this tape, never to a global model.
    """

    def __init__(self, tape, state):
        self._tape = tape
        self._ids = MappingProxyType(
            {name: self._bind(name, value) for name, value in state.items()}
        )
        self._transposes = {}

    def _bind(self, name, value):
        tensor_id = name.replace(".", "_")
        if isinstance(self._tape, ShapeTape):
            initializer = {
                k: v
                for k, v in value.items()
                if k not in ("shape", "original_shape")
            }
            return self._tape.declare(
                tensor_id, value["shape"], initializer, True
            )
        array = value.reshape(1, -1) if value.ndim == 1 else value
        return self._tape.parameter(tensor_id, array)

    def __getitem__(self, name):
        return self._ids[name]

    def __iter__(self):
        return iter(self._ids)

    def __len__(self):
        return len(self._ids)

    def transpose(self, name):
        """Create a transpose at first use, preserving graph construction
        order.
        """
        if name not in self._transposes:
            self._transposes[name] = self._tape.transpose(self[name])
        return self._transposes[name]

    def linear(self, value, prefix):
        """Apply a named weight (output × input) and its row bias."""
        return self._tape.add_bias(
            self._tape.matmul(value, self.transpose(prefix + ".weight")),
            self[prefix + ".bias"],
        )

    def layer_norm(self, value, prefix):
        return self._tape.layer_norm(
            value, self[prefix + ".weight"], self[prefix + ".bias"]
        )


# File: packages/core/src/ygaraty/core/compiler/plan.py
"""Turn a tensor graph into a dependency DAG of worker-sized operations."""

import math
from dataclasses import dataclass

from .tasks import Input, Task

# A matmul runs as one task per output block, over its whole inner dimension,
# when that task's
# two input blocks fit in this many bytes; otherwise one partial product per
# inner tile plus
# an add_many that reads them all back. A job with a larger task budget passes
# a larger value.
FUSED_K_BYTES = 12 << 20
# The largest result one task may produce (admit_plan enforces the same bound).
MAX_OUTPUT_BYTES = 8 << 20


CHAIN_OPS = ("lstm_chain", "lstm_chain_grad")
# Tasks admitted under the large-task budget rather than the per-tile one
# (results may exceed
# MAX_OUTPUT_BYTES, so they need the relay): LSTM chains and fused Transformer
# layers.
LARGE_OPS = CHAIN_OPS + (
    "tf_layer",
    "tf_layer_grad",
    "tf_owned_load",
    "tf_vocab_load",
    "tf_vocab_publish",
)
FUSED_OUTPUT_OPS = (
    "ce_fused",
    "factorization_tiles",
)  # results above MAX_OUTPUT_BYTES, bounded by the caller's output_budget


def split(
    tape,
    tile_size=64,
    fuse_bytes=None,
    chain_steps=1,
    chain_bytes=None,
    output_bytes=None,
    owned=None,
):
    """Lower a tape into tasks of about `tile_size` square tiles.

    `fuse_bytes` is the byte budget for the two inputs of a matmul fused
    over its inner dimension (default FUSED_K_BYTES). A caller that
    passes one (the production job, from its task budget) also allows
    output blocks wider than a tile, up to MAX_OUTPUT_BYTES; by default
    every task's output stays within one tile. `chain_steps` is the most
    consecutive LSTM steps one task may run (experiment 27); 1 keeps one
    task per step. `chain_bytes`, when set, is the admission budget for
    those chain tasks instead of the output cap (a whole layer per task
    needs results larger than MAX_OUTPUT_BYTES, which only relay uploads
    carry). `output_bytes`, when set, fuses the output layer (see
    fuse_output) under that task budget. `owned`, for a tape with owned
    layers: dict(load, publish, step, learning_rate, beta1, beta2,
    epsilon); the owned layers' extra tasks' results land in
    tape.owned_updates.
    """
    if tile_size < 1:
        raise ValueError("tile_size must be positive")
    from ygaraty.core.compiler.sequence import Lowering, prune, resolve_views

    tasks = []
    output_cap = (
        MAX_OUTPUT_BYTES
        if fuse_bytes is not None
        else tile_size * tile_size * 8
    )
    if fuse_bytes is None:
        fuse_bytes = FUSED_K_BYTES
    # Bytes one task may move: its inputs and its output (admit_plan budgets
    # four times that).
    budget = fuse_bytes + tile_size * tile_size * 8
    if (
        getattr(tape, "owned_layers", None)
        or getattr(tape, "owned_vocab", None)
    ) and owned is None:
        raise ValueError(
            "A tape with owned layers needs owned=dict(load, publish, step, "
            "learning_rate, beta1, beta2, epsilon)"
        )
    lowering = Lowering(
        tape,
        tile_size,
        tasks,
        budget,
        output_cap,
        chain_steps=chain_steps,
        chain_bytes=chain_bytes,
        owned=owned,
    )
    fused = find_output_layer(tape) if output_bytes else None
    aliases = {}
    for node in tape.nodes:
        if fused and node.id in fused["skip"]:
            continue
        if fused and node.id == fused["loss"]:
            aliases[fused["gradient"]] = fuse_output(
                tape, fused, tasks, output_cap, output_bytes
            )
            continue
        if fused and node.id == fused["gradient"]:
            continue  # an alias of the fused bands' first V columns
        if lowering.lower(node):
            continue
        if node.op in ("embedding", "embedding_backward", "embed"):
            blocks, positions = [], []
            m, n = node.shape
            for i in range(0, m, tile_size):
                for j in range(0, n, tile_size):
                    ei, ej = min(m, i + tile_size), min(n, j + tile_size)
                    shape, region = (ei - i, ej - j), [i, ei, j, ej]
                    ident = f"{node.id}/block/{i}/{j}"
                    if node.op == "embedding":
                        refs = [
                            Input(node.inputs[0], [row, row + 1, j, ej])
                            for row in node.attrs["ids"][i:ei]
                        ]
                        tasks.append(Task(ident, "gather_rows", refs, shape))
                    elif node.op == "embed":
                        refs = [
                            Input(node.inputs[0], [row, row + 1, j, ej])
                            for row in node.attrs["ids"][i:ei]
                        ] + [
                            Input(node.inputs[1], [row, row + 1, j, ej])
                            for row in node.attrs["positions"][i:ei]
                        ]
                        tasks.append(
                            Task(
                                ident,
                                "embed_rows",
                                refs,
                                shape,
                                {
                                    "rows": ei - i,
                                    "factor": node.attrs["factor"],
                                },
                            )
                        )
                    elif len(node.inputs) > 1:
                        # The gradient as row parts (the bands its producers
                        # made, read where they
                        # are): one scatter per output tile over all of them,
                        # nothing to sum after.
                        tasks.append(
                            Task(
                                ident,
                                "scatter_rows",
                                [
                                    Input(v, [0, tape.shapes[v][0], j, ej])
                                    for v in node.inputs
                                ],
                                shape,
                                {
                                    "shape": list(shape),
                                    "ids": [
                                        x - i
                                        if x != node.attrs.get("padding_idx")
                                        else -1
                                        for x in node.attrs["ids"]
                                    ],
                                    **(
                                        {"factor": node.attrs["factor"]}
                                        if node.attrs.get("factor", 1.0) != 1.0
                                        else {}
                                    ),
                                },
                            )
                        )
                    else:
                        parts = []
                        # Rows of the gradient per scatter task: as many as the
                        # budget allows.
                        chunk = max(
                            tile_size,
                            min(
                                len(node.attrs["ids"]),
                                budget // (16 * (ej - j)),
                            ),
                        )
                        for row in range(0, len(node.attrs["ids"]), chunk):
                            end = min(row + chunk, len(node.attrs["ids"]))
                            part = ident + f"/rows/{row}"
                            tasks.append(
                                Task(
                                    part,
                                    "scatter_rows",
                                    [Input(node.inputs[0], [row, end, j, ej])],
                                    shape,
                                    {
                                        "shape": list(shape),
                                        "ids": [
                                            x - i
                                            if x
                                            != node.attrs.get("padding_idx")
                                            else -1
                                            for x in node.attrs["ids"][row:end]
                                        ],
                                        **(
                                            {"factor": node.attrs["factor"]}
                                            if node.attrs.get("factor", 1.0)
                                            != 1.0
                                            else {}
                                        ),
                                    },
                                )
                            )
                            parts.append(Input(part))
                        tasks.append(Task(ident, "add_many", parts, shape))
                    blocks.append(Input(ident))
                    positions.append(region)
            tasks.append(
                Task(
                    node.id,
                    "assemble",
                    blocks,
                    node.shape,
                    {"shape": list(node.shape), "blocks": positions},
                )
            )
            continue
        # Bound elementwise operations, shared-weight gradient accumulation,
        # gate slicing/scattering, and bias reductions by the tile size too.
        tiled = {
            "add",
            "add_many",
            "mul",
            "add_bias",
            "relu",
            "relu_backward",
            "sigmoid",
            "tanh",
            "sigmoid_backward",
            "tanh_backward",
            "scale",
            "slice_columns",
            "slice_backward",
            "reduce_rows",
        }
        if node.op in tiled:
            blocks, positions = [], []
            m, n = node.shape
            for i in range(0, m, tile_size):
                for j in range(0, n, tile_size):
                    ei, ej = min(m, i + tile_size), min(n, j + tile_size)
                    region = [i, ei, j, ej]
                    shape = (ei - i, ej - j)
                    ident = f"{node.id}/block/{i}/{j}"
                    op, attrs = node.op, dict(node.attrs)
                    refs = [Input(x, region) for x in node.inputs]
                    if op == "add_bias":
                        refs[1] = Input(node.inputs[1], [0, 1, j, ej])
                    elif op == "slice_columns":
                        start = attrs["start"]
                        refs = [
                            Input(
                                node.inputs[0], [i, ei, j + start, ej + start]
                            )
                        ]
                        op, attrs = "scale", {"factor": 1.0}
                    elif op == "slice_backward":
                        start, end = (
                            attrs["start"],
                            attrs["start"] + attrs["width"],
                        )
                        lo, hi = max(j, start), min(ej, end)
                        refs = (
                            [
                                Input(
                                    node.inputs[0],
                                    [i, ei, lo - start, hi - start],
                                )
                            ]
                            if hi > lo
                            else []
                        )
                        op, attrs = (
                            "slice_block",
                            {"shape": list(shape), "offset": max(0, lo - j)},
                        )
                    elif op == "reduce_rows":
                        parts = []
                        total = tape.shapes[node.inputs[0]][0]
                        chunk = max(
                            tile_size, min(total, budget // (16 * (ej - j)))
                        )
                        for row in range(0, total, chunk):
                            part = ident + f"/rows/{row}"
                            tasks.append(
                                Task(
                                    part,
                                    op,
                                    [
                                        Input(
                                            node.inputs[0],
                                            [
                                                row,
                                                min(row + chunk, total),
                                                j,
                                                ej,
                                            ],
                                        )
                                    ],
                                    shape,
                                )
                            )
                            parts.append(Input(part))
                        op, refs, attrs = "add_many", parts, {}
                    tasks.append(Task(ident, op, refs, shape, attrs))
                    blocks.append(Input(ident))
                    positions.append(region)
            tasks.append(
                Task(
                    node.id,
                    "assemble",
                    blocks,
                    node.shape,
                    {"shape": list(node.shape), "blocks": positions},
                )
            )
            continue
        if node.op in ("layer_norm", "layer_norm_backward"):
            # Rows are independent: bands of rows, each within the output cap
            # and the budget. The
            # affine gradients (index 1 and 2) are row sums: one partial per
            # band, then a sum.
            rows, cols = tape.shapes[node.inputs[0]]
            reduces = (
                node.op == "layer_norm_backward"
                and node.attrs.get("index", 0) > 0
            )
            band = max(
                1,
                min(rows, output_cap // (cols * 8), budget // (8 * 3 * cols)),
            )
            blocks, positions, parts = [], [], []
            for i in range(0, rows, band):
                ei = min(rows, i + band)
                refs = [Input(node.inputs[0], [i, ei, 0, cols])] + [
                    Input(v)
                    if tape.shapes[v][0] == 1
                    else Input(v, [i, ei, 0, cols])
                    for v in node.inputs[1:]
                ]
                ident = f"{node.id}/rows/{i}"
                if reduces:
                    tasks.append(
                        Task(ident, node.op, refs, (1, cols), node.attrs)
                    )
                    parts.append(Input(ident))
                else:
                    tasks.append(
                        Task(ident, node.op, refs, (ei - i, cols), node.attrs)
                    )
                    blocks.append(Input(ident))
                    positions.append([i, ei, 0, cols])
            if reduces:
                tasks.append(Task(node.id, "add_many", parts, (1, cols)))
            else:
                tasks.append(
                    Task(
                        node.id,
                        "assemble",
                        blocks,
                        node.shape,
                        {"shape": list(node.shape), "blocks": positions},
                    )
                )
            continue
        if node.op != "matmul":
            tasks.append(
                Task(
                    node.id,
                    node.op,
                    [Input(x) for x in node.inputs],
                    node.shape,
                    node.attrs,
                )
            )
            continue
        a, b = node.inputs
        m, n = node.shape
        k = tape.shapes[a][1]
        blocks, positions = [], []
        for i in range(0, m, tile_size):
            ei, j = min(m, i + tile_size), 0
            while j < n:
                # Widest output block (in tiles, doubling) whose whole-K task
                # fits the budget: the
                # left row block is then read once, and a consumer of these
                # rows (an LSTM step
                # reading all gate columns) depends on this block alone, not on
                # the whole product.
                span = tile_size
                while (
                    span < n - j
                    and (
                        (ei - i) * k
                        + k * min(n - j, 2 * span)
                        + (ei - i) * min(n - j, 2 * span)
                    )
                    * 8
                    <= budget
                    and (ei - i) * min(n - j, 2 * span) * 8 <= output_cap
                ):
                    span *= 2
                ej = min(n, j + span)
                block = f"{node.id}/block/{i}/{j}"
                if (
                    (ei - i) * k + k * (ej - j) + (ei - i) * (ej - j)
                ) * 8 <= budget:
                    # The whole inner dimension in one task: no partial
                    # products written and read back.
                    tasks.append(
                        Task(
                            block,
                            "matmul",
                            [Input(a, [i, ei, 0, k]), Input(b, [0, k, j, ej])],
                            (ei - i, ej - j),
                        )
                    )
                    blocks.append(Input(block))
                    positions.append([i, ei, j, ej])
                    j = ej
                    continue
                partials = []
                for q in range(0, k, tile_size):
                    eq = min(k, q + tile_size)
                    ident = f"{block}/partial/{q}"
                    tasks.append(
                        Task(
                            ident,
                            "matmul",
                            [
                                Input(a, [i, ei, q, eq]),
                                Input(b, [q, eq, j, ej]),
                            ],
                            (ei - i, ej - j),
                        )
                    )
                    partials.append(Input(ident))
                tasks.append(
                    Task(block, "add_many", partials, (ei - i, ej - j))
                )
                blocks.append(Input(block))
                positions.append([i, ei, j, ej])
                j = ej
        tasks.append(
            Task(
                node.id,
                "assemble",
                blocks,
                node.shape,
                {"shape": list(node.shape), "blocks": positions},
            )
        )
    owned_keep = set()
    if getattr(tape, "owned_layers", None) or getattr(
        tape, "owned_vocab", None
    ):
        tape.owned_updates, owned_keep = lowering.owned_tasks()
    tasks = resolve_views(tape, tasks)
    if aliases:
        tasks = resolve_aliases(tasks, aliases, tape)
    # Every tape value stays available (tests and diagnostics read
    # intermediates by name), except
    # row concatenations nobody reads whole: their readers resolved to the
    # parts, and the copies
    # behind them (planner-made ids, with a '/') go with them.
    keep = {n.id for n in tape.nodes if n.op != "concat_rows"} | {tape.loss}
    keep.update(getattr(tape, "gradients", {}).values())
    keep.update(getattr(tape, "carry", {}).values())
    keep.update(owned_keep)
    return prune(resolve_assemblies(tasks), keep)


# Results that keep the wire's dtype on a narrow activation wire: losses,
# statistics and
# scalars, optimizer state and updates, owners' state, and metadata.
FULL_WIDTH_OPS = {
    "assemble",
    "transpose",
    "ce_fused",
    "cross_entropy",
    "sum",
    "tf_vocab_stats",
    "vocab_loss",
    "sum_squares",
    "scatter_rows",
    "embedding_backward",
    "clip_gradient",
    "adam_step",
    "adam_m",
    "adam_v",
    "adam_update",
    "sgd",
    "tf_owned_step",
    "tf_vocab_step",
    "tf_owned_load",
    "tf_vocab_load",
    "tf_owned_publish",
    "tf_vocab_publish",
    "tf_vocab_embed_grad",
    "tf_owned_share",
}


# Reviewed functional operations: a task of one of these that nothing reads can
# go. Anything
# else (owned loads and publishes, statistics a job reads from the store,
# kernels with side
# effects) is kept, with everything it depends on.
PURE_OPS = frozenset(
    {
        # runtime/kernels.py
        "matmul",
        "add",
        "add_bias",
        "mul",
        "scale",
        "relu",
        "relu_backward",
        "sum",
        "sum_squares",
        "transpose",
        "reduce_rows",
        "broadcast",
        "add_many",
        "assemble",
        "sigmoid",
        "tanh",
        "sigmoid_backward",
        "tanh_backward",
        "concat_columns",
        "slice_columns",
        "slice_backward",
        "slice_block",
        "softmax",
        "softmax_backward",
        "layer_norm",
        "layer_norm_backward",
        "cross_entropy",
        "ce_backward",
        "ce_fused",
        "embedding",
        "embedding_backward",
        "embed",
        "embed_rows",
        "gather_rows",
        "scatter_rows",
        "clip_gradient",
        "sgd",
        "adam_m",
        "adam_v",
        "adam_step",
        "adam_update",
        "group",
        # sequence_kernels.py
        "view",
        "concat_rows",
        "place",
        "place_block",
        "lstm",
        "lstm_backward",
        "bqk",
        "bpv",
        "bptq",
        "lstm_cell",
        "lstm_cell_backward",
        "lstm_cell_carry",
        "lstm_cell_grad",
        "lstm_state_grad",
        "lstm_chain",
        "lstm_chain_grad",
        # transformer_kernels.py: the unowned layer kernels (owned and
        # vocabulary ops read and write the store)
        "tf_layer",
        "tf_layer_grad",
        "vocab_loss",
    }
)


def prune_unused(tasks, keep):
    """The tasks that reach a kept value, in the given order: dead work
    leaves the plan.

    `keep` names the values the job reads after the update (its
    parameter updates and loss; leaves are not tasks). A task is kept
    when something kept depends on it, when its operation is not in
    PURE_OPS, or when it is owned (attrs 'owner'): those are roots too.
    Typical removals are the whole-matrix `assemble` the planner emits
    for every named value and the gradient of an untrained input. Values
    are unchanged: the kept tasks read exactly what they read before.
    """
    by_id = {t.id: t for t in tasks}
    roots = set(keep) | {
        t.id
        for t in tasks
        if t.op not in PURE_OPS or t.attrs.get("owner") is not None
    }
    live, todo = set(), [r for r in roots if r in by_id]
    while todo:
        ident = todo.pop()
        if ident in live:
            continue
        live.add(ident)
        todo.extend(
            d
            for d in by_id[ident].dependencies
            if d in by_id and d not in live
        )
    return [t for t in tasks if t.id in live]


def narrow_activations(tasks, tape, dtype="bf16"):
    """Mark the tasks whose results may travel as `dtype` (attrs['wire']):
    activations and their gradients. Parameters, their gradients
    (tape.parameter_side), optimizer state, losses, statistics and
    owners' state keep the wire's dtype; a fused layer's backward result
    is narrowed band by band (the input gradients, not the packed
    parameter gradients).
    """
    protected = set(tape.parameter_side) | set(tape.parameter_like)
    for task in tasks:
        node = task.id.split("/")[0]
        if (
            task.op in FULL_WIDTH_OPS
            or node in protected
            or math.prod(task.shape) <= 4
        ):
            continue
        if task.op == "tf_layer_grad":
            activations = 2 if task.attrs.get("decoder") else 1
            task.attrs["wire"] = [dtype] * activations + [None] * (
                len(task.attrs["upload_rows"]) - activations
            )
        else:
            task.attrs["wire"] = dtype
    return tasks


def critical_path(tasks):
    """Worker hops on the longest dependency path of a plan: the tasks a
    worker must run one after another, whatever the number of workers.
    Assemblies and transposes are coordinator metadata and cost no hop.
    Each hop costs at least one network round trip.
    """
    by_id = {t.id: t for t in tasks}
    indegree = {t.id: 0 for t in tasks}
    dependents = {}
    for t in tasks:
        for d in t.dependencies:
            if d in by_id:
                indegree[t.id] += 1
                dependents.setdefault(d, []).append(t.id)
    depth = {}
    ready = [i for i, n in indegree.items() if n == 0]
    for i in ready:
        depth[i] = 0 if by_id[i].op in ("assemble", "transpose") else 1
    while ready:
        i = ready.pop()
        for j in dependents.get(i, ()):
            cost = 0 if by_id[j].op in ("assemble", "transpose") else 1
            depth[j] = max(depth.get(j, 0), depth[i] + cost)
            indegree[j] -= 1
            if indegree[j] == 0:
                ready.append(j)
    return max(depth.values(), default=0)


def find_output_layer(tape):
    """The pattern loss = cross_entropy(add_bias(matmul(x, W), b)) with its
    ce_backward, where the matmul and the bias add feed nothing else:
    the output layer of a classifier or language model.
    """
    nodes = {n.id: n for n in tape.nodes}
    consumers = {}
    for n in tape.nodes:
        for v in n.inputs:
            consumers.setdefault(v, set()).add(n.id)
    for c in tape.nodes:
        if c.op != "cross_entropy":
            continue
        biased = nodes.get(c.inputs[0])
        if not biased or biased.op != "add_bias":
            continue
        product = nodes.get(biased.inputs[0])
        if not product or product.op != "matmul":
            continue
        grads = [
            nodes[i]
            for i in consumers.get(biased.id, ())
            if nodes[i].op == "ce_backward"
        ]
        if len(grads) != 1 or consumers.get(biased.id, set()) != {
            c.id,
            grads[0].id,
        }:
            continue
        if consumers.get(product.id, set()) != {biased.id}:
            continue
        x, w = product.inputs
        return dict(
            skip={product.id, biased.id},
            loss=c.id,
            gradient=grads[0].id,
            x=x,
            w=w,
            bias=biased.inputs[1],
            seed=grads[0].inputs[1],
            attrs=c.attrs,
            rows=tape.shapes[x][0],
            width=tape.shapes[x][1],
            classes=tape.shapes[w][1],
        )


def fuse_output(tape, f, tasks, output_cap, budget):
    """Emit the fused output layer: per band of rows, one ce_fused task
    computes the logits, the loss and its gradient, returning [logit
    gradient | row loss]; the logits never leave the worker. The loss is
    the sum of the bands' row-loss columns. Returns (assembled value,
    columns) for the ce_backward alias.
    """
    rows, d, V = f["rows"], f["width"], f["classes"]
    per_row = d + 2 * (V + 1)  # input row, output row, and logits scratch
    fixed = d * V + V + 1
    band = min(output_cap // ((V + 1) * 8), (budget // 32 - fixed) // per_row)
    if band < 1:
        raise MemoryError(
            "The output-layer task budget is too small for one row; "
            "raise YGARATY_LSTM_OUTPUT_TASK_MIB"
        )
    attrs, targets = f["attrs"], f["attrs"]["targets"]
    count = sum(1 for t in targets if t != attrs.get("ignore_index"))
    parts, sums = [], []
    for r0 in range(0, rows, band):
        r1 = min(rows, r0 + band)
        ident = f"{f['loss']}/fused/{r0}"
        tasks.append(
            Task(
                ident,
                "ce_fused",
                [
                    Input(f["x"], [r0, r1, 0, d]),
                    Input(f["w"]),
                    Input(f["bias"]),
                    Input(f["seed"]),
                ],
                (r1 - r0, V + 1),
                dict(
                    targets=targets[r0:r1],
                    ignore_index=attrs.get("ignore_index"),
                    label_smoothing=attrs.get("label_smoothing", 0.0),
                    count=count,
                ),
            )
        )
        parts.append((ident, [r0, r1, 0, V + 1]))
        tasks.append(
            Task(
                ident + "/loss",
                "sum",
                [Input(ident, [0, r1 - r0, V, V + 1])],
                (1, 1),
            )
        )
        sums.append(Input(ident + "/loss"))
    tasks.append(
        Task(f["loss"], "add_many", sums, (1, 1))
        if len(sums) > 1
        else Task(f["loss"], "scale", sums, (1, 1), {"factor": 1.0})
    )
    whole = f"{f['loss']}/fused"
    tasks.append(
        Task(
            whole,
            "assemble",
            [Input(p) for p, _ in parts],
            (rows, V + 1),
            {"shape": [rows, V + 1], "blocks": [r for _, r in parts]},
        )
    )
    return whole, V


def resolve_aliases(tasks, aliases, tape):
    """Inputs naming an aliased value read the first columns of the value
    it aliases instead.
    """
    for task in tasks:
        inputs = []
        for x in task.inputs:
            if x.value in aliases:
                if task.op in ("assemble", "transpose"):
                    raise ValueError(
                        f"{task.id} needs {x.value} as a whole value; "
                        "it is fused into the output layer"
                    )
                base, width = aliases[x.value]
                rows = tape.shapes[x.value][0]
                x = Input(base, x.region or [0, rows, 0, width])
            inputs.append(x)
        task.inputs = inputs
    return tasks


def resolve_assemblies(tasks):
    """A task that reads a region of an assembled value reads it from the
    part that holds it.

    Assemblies are metadata, so this costs nothing, and it removes the
    barrier an assembly puts between producers and consumers: a step of
    one LSTM layer can start as soon as the rows it needs from the
    previous layer exist, instead of after that layer's last step.
    Regions that span several parts, and whole-value inputs, are left
    alone.
    """
    parts = {
        t.id: [
            (x.value, x.region, block)
            for x, block in zip(t.inputs, t.attrs["blocks"])
        ]
        for t in tasks
        if t.op == "assemble"
    }
    if not parts:
        return tasks

    def resolve(value, region):
        while value in parts and region is not None:
            i, ei, j, ej = region
            for part, source, (bi, bei, bj, bej) in parts[value]:
                if bi <= i and ei <= bei and bj <= j and ej <= bej:
                    r0, c0 = (source[0], source[2]) if source else (0, 0)
                    value, region = (
                        part,
                        [i - bi + r0, ei - bi + r0, j - bj + c0, ej - bj + c0],
                    )
                    break
            else:
                return value, region
        return value, region

    for task in tasks:
        if task.op in ("assemble", "transpose"):
            continue
        inputs = []
        for x in task.inputs:
            value, region = resolve(x.value, x.region)
            inputs.append(x if value == x.value else Input(value, region))
        task.inputs = inputs
    return tasks


def group(tasks, max_ops=4):
    """Fuse single-consumer chains, never crossing a fan-out or a join.

    Original operations and outputs remain inspectable inside each task.
    Restrict successors to one task dependency to avoid introducing cycles.
    """
    if max_ops < 1:
        raise ValueError("max_ops must be positive")
    by_id = {t.id: t for t in tasks}
    consumers = {t.id: set() for t in tasks}
    for t in tasks:
        for dep in t.dependencies & by_id.keys():
            consumers[dep].add(t.id)
    used, result = set(), []
    for first in tasks:
        if first.id in used:
            continue
        chain = [first]
        used.add(first.id)
        while len(chain) < max_ops and len(consumers[chain[-1].id]) == 1:
            following = by_id[next(iter(consumers[chain[-1].id]))]
            if (
                following.id in used
                or following.dependencies & by_id.keys() != {chain[-1].id}
            ):
                break
            chain.append(following)
            used.add(following.id)
        if len(chain) == 1:
            result.append(first)
            continue
        internal = {t.id for t in chain}
        external, indices, nodes = [], {}, []
        for t in chain:
            refs = []
            for ref in t.inputs:
                if ref.value in internal:
                    refs.append({"value": ref.value, "region": ref.region})
                else:
                    key = (ref.value, tuple(ref.region or []))
                    if key not in indices:
                        indices[key] = len(external)
                        external.append(ref)
                    refs.append({"external": indices[key]})
            nodes.append(
                dict(
                    id=t.id,
                    op=t.op,
                    inputs=refs,
                    shape=list(t.shape),
                    attrs=t.attrs,
                )
            )
        result.append(
            Task(
                chain[-1].id,
                "group",
                external,
                chain[-1].shape,
                {"nodes": nodes},
            )
        )
    return result


def bounded_reductions(tasks, fan_in=8, budget=None):
    """Keep joins from gathering an entire matrix's partial products at
    once.
    """
    if fan_in < 2:
        raise ValueError("fan_in must be at least 2")
    result = []
    for task in tasks:
        inputs = task.inputs
        if task.op == "add_many":
            limit = (
                min(
                    fan_in,
                    max(2, budget // (4 * math.prod(task.shape) * 8) - 1),
                )
                if budget
                else fan_in
            )
            level = 0
            while len(inputs) > limit:
                next_inputs = []
                for start in range(0, len(inputs), limit):
                    ident = f"{task.id}/join/{level}/{start}"
                    result.append(
                        Task(
                            ident,
                            "add_many",
                            inputs[start : start + limit],
                            task.shape,
                        )
                    )
                    next_inputs.append(Input(ident))
                inputs = next_inputs
                level += 1
            task = Task(task.id, task.op, inputs, task.shape, task.attrs)
        result.append(task)
    return result


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class AdmissionPolicy:
    """Per-task array/transport estimates, not a process RSS guarantee."""

    budget: int
    chain_budget: int | None = None
    output_budget: int | None = None

    def _special_budget(self, task: Task) -> int | None:
        if task.op in LARGE_OPS:
            return self.chain_budget
        if task.op in FUSED_OUTPUT_OPS:
            return self.output_budget
        return None

    def check(self, task: Task, shapes: dict) -> int:
        if task.op in ("assemble", "transpose"):
            return 0
        input_bytes = 0
        for ref in task.inputs:
            if ref.region:
                r0, r1, c0, c1 = ref.region
                dimensions = (r1 - r0, c1 - c0)
            else:
                dimensions = shapes[ref.value]
            input_bytes += math.prod(dimensions) * 8
        output_bytes = math.prod(task.shape) * 8
        estimate = 4 * (input_bytes + output_bytes)
        special = self._special_budget(task)
        if special:
            if estimate > special:
                raise MemoryError(
                    f"Task {task.id} ({task.op}) needs an estimated "
                    f"{estimate} bytes; "
                    f"its task budget is {special}."
                )
        elif estimate > self.budget or output_bytes > MAX_OUTPUT_BYTES:
            raise MemoryError(
                f"Task {task.id} ({task.op}) needs an estimated "
                f"{estimate} bytes; "
                f"budget is {self.budget}. "
                "Reduce the tile, batch or sentence size."
            )
        return estimate

    def admit(self, tasks, leaf_shapes) -> int:
        shapes = dict(leaf_shapes)
        shapes.update((task.id, task.shape) for task in tasks)
        return max((self.check(task, shapes) for task in tasks), default=0)


def admit_plan(
    tasks, leaf_shapes, budget, chain_budget=None, output_budget=None
):
    """Compatibility entry point for task-array and transport admission."""
    return AdmissionPolicy(budget, chain_budget, output_budget).admit(
        tasks, leaf_shapes
    )

"""Sequence layout, dispatch and metadata passes."""

from __future__ import annotations

from .sequence_transformer import TransformerLowering
from .sequence_views import prune, resolve_views
from .tasks import Input, Task

if TYPE_CHECKING:
    from .tape import Node, Tape

__all__ = [
    "Lowering",
    "PUBLISH_WIRE",
    "ROW_OPS",
    "SEQUENCE_OPS",
    "order",
    "prune",
    "resolve_views",
    "spans",
]

SEQUENCE_OPS = {
    "view",
    "concat_rows",
    "concat_columns",
    "place",
    "lstm",
    "lstm_backward",
    "bqk",
    "bpv",
    "bptq",
    "tf_layer",
    "tf_layer_grad",
    "vocab_gather",
    "vocab_output",
    "vocab_grad",
    "vocab_embed_grad",
}
ROW_OPS = {"cross_entropy", "ce_backward", "softmax", "softmax_backward"}


class Lowering(TransformerLowering, RecurrentLowering, AttentionLowering):
    """Shared per-plan state and emission for stateless operation-family
    mixins.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        tape: Tape,
        tile: int,
        tasks: list[Task],
        budget: int | None = None,
        output_cap: int | None = None,
        chain_steps: int = 1,
        chain_bytes: int | None = None,
        owned: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        self.tape, self.tile, self.tasks = tape, tile, tasks
        self.budget = budget if budget is not None else 3 * tile * tile * 8
        self.output_cap = (
            output_cap if output_cap is not None else tile * tile * 8
        )
        self.chain_steps, self.chain_bytes = chain_steps, chain_bytes
        self.owned = owned
        self.views = {n.id for n in tape.nodes if n.op == "view"}

    def emit(
        self,
        ident: str,
        op: str,
        inputs: list[Input],
        shape: tuple[int, int],
        attrs: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
    ) -> str:
        self.tasks.append(Task(ident, op, inputs, tuple(shape), attrs or {}))
        return ident

    def assemble(self, ident, shape, parts):
        """parts: (whole value, [r0, r1, c0, c1]) blocks covering `shape`. One
        part is renamed, not assembled.

        Many full-width row parts (the steps of an LSTM chain) are first
        assembled per band of `tile` rows, so that a consumer of one band
        depends on that band's steps only.
        """
        if len(parts) == 1 and parts[0][1] == [0, shape[0], 0, shape[1]]:
            task = next(t for t in reversed(self.tasks) if t.id == parts[0][0])
            task.id = ident
            return ident
        rows, cols = shape
        if len(parts) > 2 and all(
            c0 == 0 and c1 == cols and r1 - r0 < self.tile
            for _, (r0, r1, c0, c1) in parts
        ):
            bands = []
            for b0, b1 in spans(rows, self.tile):
                inside = [
                    (v, r) for v, r in parts if b0 <= r[0] and r[1] <= b1
                ]
                if (
                    len(inside)
                    == len([1 for _, r in parts if r[0] < b1 and b0 < r[1]])
                    and inside
                ):
                    if len(inside) == 1:
                        bands.append((inside[0][0], inside[0][1]))
                        continue
                    band = self.emit(
                        f"{ident}/band/{b0}",
                        "assemble",
                        [Input(v) for v, _ in inside],
                        (b1 - b0, cols),
                        {
                            "shape": [b1 - b0, cols],
                            "blocks": [
                                [r0 - b0, r1 - b0, 0, cols]
                                for _, (r0, r1, _, _) in inside
                            ],
                        },
                    )
                    bands.append((band, [b0, b1, 0, cols]))
                else:
                    bands = None
                    break
            if bands:
                parts = bands
        return self.emit(
            ident,
            "assemble",
            [Input(v) for v, _ in parts],
            shape,
            {"shape": list(shape), "blocks": [r for _, r in parts]},
        )

    def place(self, ident, shape, pieces):
        """Zeros of `shape` plus each (value, [row, column]) piece, tiled into
        place_block tasks.
        """
        parts = []
        # Column span of a placement block: as wide as the budget allows (a
        # block reads at most its
        # own size in pieces and writes itself), so row consumers depend on one
        # block.
        width = self.tile
        while (
            width < shape[1]
            and 2 * self.tile * min(shape[1], 2 * width) * 8 <= self.budget
            and self.tile * min(shape[1], 2 * width) * 8 <= self.output_cap
        ):
            width *= 2
        for r0, r1 in spans(shape[0], self.tile):
            for c0, c1 in spans(shape[1], width):
                inputs, offsets = [], []
                for value, (r, c) in pieces:
                    rows, cols = self.tape.shapes[value]
                    i0, i1, j0, j1 = (
                        max(r0, r),
                        min(r1, r + rows),
                        max(c0, c),
                        min(c1, c + cols),
                    )
                    if i0 < i1 and j0 < j1:
                        inputs.append(
                            Input(value, [i0 - r, i1 - r, j0 - c, j1 - c])
                        )
                        offsets.append([i0 - r0, j0 - c0])
                parts.append(
                    (
                        self.emit(
                            f"{ident}/block/{r0}/{c0}",
                            "place_block",
                            inputs,
                            (r1 - r0, c1 - c0),
                            {"shape": [r1 - r0, c1 - c0], "offsets": offsets},
                        ),
                        [r0, r1, c0, c1],
                    )
                )
        return self.assemble(ident, shape, parts)

    def whole(self, value: str) -> str:
        """A stored copy of a view, for operations that read whole values
        (transpose, assemble).
        """
        if value not in self.views:
            return value
        # One copy per view, however many values read it whole (experiment 78:
        # a parallel layer group's input gradient feeds every part's).
        copies = self.__dict__.setdefault("_copies", {})
        if value not in copies:
            copies[value] = self.place(
                value + "/copy", self.tape.shapes[value], [(value, [0, 0])]
            )
        return copies[value]

    def lower(self, node: Node) -> bool:
        handler = getattr(self, "lower_" + node.op, None)
        if node.op == "transpose" and node.inputs[0] in self.views:
            self.emit(
                node.id,
                "transpose",
                [Input(self.whole(node.inputs[0]))],
                node.shape,
                node.attrs,
            )
            return True
        if node.op in ROW_OPS:
            return self.lower_rows(node)
        if node.op not in SEQUENCE_OPS:
            return False
        handler(node)
        return True

    def lower_view(self, node: Node) -> None:
        """Views are metadata; resolve_views rewrites their consumers
        later.
        """

    def lower_concat_rows(self, node):
        cols, start, parts = node.shape[1], 0, []
        for value in node.inputs:
            rows = self.tape.shapes[value][0]
            parts.append((self.whole(value), [start, start + rows, 0, cols]))
            start += rows
        self.assemble(node.id, node.shape, parts)

    def lower_concat_columns(self, node):
        offsets, start = [], 0
        for value in node.inputs:
            offsets.append((value, [0, start]))
            start += self.tape.shapes[value][1]
        self.place(node.id, node.shape, offsets)

    def lower_place(self, node):
        self.place(
            node.id, node.shape, list(zip(node.inputs, node.attrs["offsets"]))
        )


# File: packages/core/src/ygaraty/core/compiler/sequence_attention.py
"""Attention task planning using the shared sequence lowering context."""

from .sequence_common import spans
from .tasks import Input


class AttentionLowering:
    """Internal lowering mixin; state and emission belong to
    sequence.Lowering.
    """

    def chunk_steps(self, batch):
        return max(1, self.tile // batch) * batch

    def narrow(self, rows):
        """Column block width for an operand read at its full height: about
        one tile's elements.
        """
        return max(1, min(self.tile, self.tile * self.tile // rows))

    def lower_bqk(self, node):
        q, k = node.inputs
        batch, (rows, width), keys = (
            node.attrs["batch"],
            self.tape.shapes[q],
            self.tape.shapes[k][0],
        )
        parts = []
        for r0, r1 in spans(rows, self.chunk_steps(batch)):
            partials = [
                Input(
                    self.emit(
                        f"{node.id}/rows/{r0}/{d0}",
                        "bqk",
                        [
                            Input(q, [r0, r1, d0, d1]),
                            Input(k, [0, keys, d0, d1]),
                        ],
                        (r1 - r0, node.shape[1]),
                        node.attrs,
                    )
                )
                for d0, d1 in spans(width, self.narrow(keys))
            ]
            chunk = (
                partials[0].value
                if len(partials) == 1
                else self.emit(
                    f"{node.id}/rows/{r0}",
                    "add_many",
                    partials,
                    (r1 - r0, node.shape[1]),
                )
            )
            parts.append((chunk, [r0, r1, 0, node.shape[1]]))
        self.assemble(node.id, node.shape, parts)

    def lower_bpv(self, node):
        p, v = node.inputs
        batch, rows, keys = (
            node.attrs["batch"],
            node.shape[0],
            self.tape.shapes[p][1],
        )
        parts = []
        for r0, r1 in spans(rows, self.chunk_steps(batch)):
            for j0, j1 in spans(
                node.shape[1], self.narrow(self.tape.shapes[v][0])
            ):
                parts.append(
                    (
                        self.emit(
                            f"{node.id}/block/{r0}/{j0}",
                            "bpv",
                            [
                                Input(p, [r0, r1, 0, keys]),
                                Input(v, [0, self.tape.shapes[v][0], j0, j1]),
                            ],
                            (r1 - r0, j1 - j0),
                            node.attrs,
                        ),
                        [r0, r1, j0, j1],
                    )
                )
        self.assemble(node.id, node.shape, parts)

    def lower_bptq(self, node):
        p, q = node.inputs
        batch, rows = node.attrs["batch"], self.tape.shapes[p][0]
        parts = []
        for s0, s1 in spans(
            self.tape.shapes[p][1], self.chunk_steps(batch) // batch
        ):
            for j0, j1 in spans(node.shape[1], self.narrow(rows)):
                parts.append(
                    (
                        self.emit(
                            f"{node.id}/block/{s0}/{j0}",
                            "bptq",
                            [
                                Input(p, [0, rows, s0, s1]),
                                Input(q, [0, rows, j0, j1]),
                            ],
                            ((s1 - s0) * batch, j1 - j0),
                            node.attrs,
                        ),
                        [s0 * batch, s1 * batch, j0, j1],
                    )
                )
        self.assemble(node.id, node.shape, parts)

    def lower_rows(self, node):
        """Row-wise operations over long rows (the vocabulary): chunks of
        whole rows.
        """
        rows, cols = self.tape.shapes[node.inputs[0]]
        chunk = max(1, self.tile * self.tile // cols)
        if rows <= chunk:
            return False
        attrs, parts = dict(node.attrs), []
        if node.op in ("cross_entropy", "ce_backward"):
            targets = attrs["targets"]
            attrs["count"] = sum(
                1 for x in targets if x != attrs.get("ignore_index")
            )
        for r0, r1 in spans(rows, chunk):
            piece = dict(attrs)
            if "targets" in piece:
                piece["targets"] = attrs["targets"][r0:r1]
            if "mask" in piece:
                piece["mask"] = attrs["mask"][r0:r1]
            inputs = [Input(node.inputs[0], [r0, r1, 0, cols])]
            if node.op == "ce_backward":
                inputs.append(Input(node.inputs[1]))
            elif node.op == "softmax_backward":
                inputs.append(Input(node.inputs[1], [r0, r1, 0, cols]))
            shape = (1, 1) if node.op == "cross_entropy" else (r1 - r0, cols)
            parts.append(
                (
                    self.emit(
                        f"{node.id}/rows/{r0}", node.op, inputs, shape, piece
                    ),
                    [r0, r1, 0, cols],
                )
            )
        if node.op == "cross_entropy":
            self.emit(
                node.id, "add_many", [Input(v) for v, _ in parts], (1, 1)
            )
        else:
            self.assemble(node.id, node.shape, parts)
        return True


# File: packages/core/src/ygaraty/core/compiler/sequence_common.py
"""Shared sequence iteration contracts; no graph or execution state."""


def spans(size: int, tile: int) -> list[tuple[int, int]]:
    return [(start, min(start + tile, size)) for start in range(0, size, tile)]


def order(steps: int, reverse: bool) -> range:
    return range(steps - 1, -1, -1) if reverse else range(steps)


# Preserve owner checkpoints exactly across reloads and re-placement.
# Preserve owner checkpoints exactly across reloads and re-placement.
PUBLISH_WIRE = "<f8"


# File: packages/core/src/ygaraty/core/compiler/sequence_recurrent.py
"""Recurrent task planning using the shared sequence lowering context."""

from .sequence_common import order, spans
from .tasks import Input


class RecurrentLowering:
    """Internal lowering mixin; state and emission belong to
    sequence.Lowering.
    """

    def recurrent(self, prefix, rows, left, left_columns, right, out_width):
        """Partial products left[:, left_columns] @ right, tiled; returns
        (inputs, blocks).
        """
        start, width = left_columns
        inputs, blocks = [], []
        for j0, j1 in spans(out_width, self.tile):
            ks = spans(width, self.tile)
            for q0, q1 in ks:
                inputs.append(
                    Input(
                        self.emit(
                            f"{prefix}/{j0}/{q0}",
                            "matmul",
                            [
                                Input(left, [0, rows, start + q0, start + q1]),
                                Input(right, [q0, q1, j0, j1]),
                            ],
                            (rows, j1 - j0),
                        )
                    )
                )
            blocks.append([j0, j1, len(ks)])
        return inputs, blocks

    def inline_recurrent(self, weight):
        """Whether a step task computes its recurrent product itself, reading
        the whole recurrent weight (cached at the worker, since it is a
        view of a leaf). One hop per step instead of two. Beyond four
        tiles' worth of weight the product stays split into separate tasks.
        """
        rows, cols = self.tape.shapes[weight]
        return rows * cols <= 4 * self.tile * self.tile

    def steps_per_task(self, per_step, fixed, width, batch):
        """Consecutive LSTM steps one chain task may run: at most chain_steps,
        with its output (`width` columns per step) under the output cap and
        its arrays (`fixed` elements plus `per_step` per step) within the
        task budget. 1 means one task per step, as before.
        """
        if self.chain_bytes:
            # admit_plan's estimate is four times the bytes a task moves
            # (plan.admit_plan).
            return max(
                1,
                min(
                    self.chain_steps,
                    (self.chain_bytes // 32 - fixed) // per_step,
                ),
            )
        k = min(
            self.chain_steps,
            max(1, self.output_cap // (batch * width * 8)),
            max(1, (self.budget // 8 - fixed) // per_step),
        )
        return max(1, k)

    def lower_lstm(self, node):
        xp, whh_t, bias_ih, bias_hh, mask, h0, c0 = node.inputs
        batch, hidden, reverse = (
            node.attrs["batch"],
            node.attrs["hidden"],
            node.attrs["reverse"],
        )
        rows = self.tape.shapes[xp][0]
        inline = self.inline_recurrent(whh_t)
        k = (
            self.steps_per_task(
                batch * (4 * hidden + 1 + 7 * hidden),
                hidden * 4 * hidden + 8 * hidden + 2 * batch * hidden,
                7 * hidden,
                batch,
            )
            if inline
            else 1
        )
        if k > 1:
            # Chunks of consecutive steps, in processing order, each one task
            # (the whole-layer
            # kernel over the chunk's rows). The next chunk reads its state
            # from the row of the
            # last step the previous chunk processed.
            walk, previous, parts = (
                list(order(rows // batch, reverse)),
                None,
                [],
            )
            for i in range(0, len(walk), k):
                chunk = walk[i : i + k]
                lo, hi = min(chunk), max(chunk)
                r = [lo * batch, (hi + 1) * batch]
                if previous is None:
                    state_h, state_c = Input(h0), Input(c0)
                else:
                    task, j = previous
                    state_h = Input(
                        task, [j * batch, (j + 1) * batch, 0, hidden]
                    )
                    state_c = Input(
                        task, [j * batch, (j + 1) * batch, hidden, 2 * hidden]
                    )
                ident = self.emit(
                    f"{node.id}/chain/{lo}",
                    "lstm_chain",
                    [
                        Input(xp, r + [0, 4 * hidden]),
                        Input(bias_ih),
                        Input(bias_hh),
                        state_h,
                        state_c,
                        Input(mask, r + [0, 1]),
                        Input(whh_t),
                    ],
                    (len(chunk) * batch, 7 * hidden),
                    {
                        "hidden": hidden,
                        "batch": batch,
                        "reverse": reverse,
                        "inline": True,
                    },
                )
                previous = (ident, chunk[-1] - lo)
                parts.append((ident, r + [0, 7 * hidden]))
            self.assemble(
                node.id, node.shape, sorted(parts, key=lambda p: p[1][0])
            )
            return
        previous, parts = None, []
        for t in order(rows // batch, reverse):
            r = [t * batch, (t + 1) * batch]
            h_source, h_columns = (
                (h0, 0) if previous is None else (previous, 0)
            )
            if inline:
                partials, attrs = (
                    [Input(whh_t)],
