                    {"hidden": hidden, "inline": True},
                )
            else:
                partials, blocks = self.recurrent(
                    f"{node.id}/hw/{t}",
                    batch,
                    h_source,
                    (h_columns, hidden),
                    whh_t,
                    4 * hidden,
                )
                attrs = {"hidden": hidden, "blocks": blocks}
            state_h = (
                Input(h0)
                if previous is None
                else Input(previous, [0, batch, 0, hidden])
            )
            state_c = (
                Input(c0)
                if previous is None
                else Input(previous, [0, batch, hidden, 2 * hidden])
            )
            previous = self.emit(
                f"{node.id}/s/{t}",
                "lstm_cell",
                [
                    Input(xp, r + [0, 4 * hidden]),
                    Input(bias_ih),
                    Input(bias_hh),
                    state_h,
                    state_c,
                    Input(mask, r + [0, 1]),
                ]
                + partials,
                (batch, 7 * hidden),
                attrs,
            )
            parts.append((previous, r + [0, 7 * hidden]))
        self.assemble(
            node.id, node.shape, sorted(parts, key=lambda p: p[1][0])
        )

    def lower_lstm_backward(self, node):
        attrs = node.attrs
        if attrs.get("full"):
            raise NotImplementedError(
                "Tiled LSTM gradients support h_out and "
                "final-state gradients only"
            )
        values = list(node.inputs)
        out_value = values.pop(0)
        g_out = values.pop(0) if attrs.get("has_out") else None
        g_state = values.pop(0) if attrs.get("has_state") else None
        whh, mask, h0, c0 = values
        batch, hidden, reverse = (
            attrs["batch"],
            attrs["hidden"],
            attrs["reverse"],
        )
        rows = self.tape.shapes[out_value][0]
        walk = list(order(rows // batch, reverse))
        # One task per step produces [gate gradients (4h) | carry (2h)]; the
        # next step reads
        # the gates as the left operand of its recurrent products and the carry
        # as a region.
        inline = self.inline_recurrent(whh)
        k = (
            self.steps_per_task(
                batch * (7 * hidden + 1 + hidden + 6 * hidden),
                hidden * 4 * hidden + 8 * batch * hidden,
                6 * hidden,
                batch,
            )
            if inline
            else 1
        )
        if k > 1:
            # Chunks of consecutive steps in processing order (the reverse of
            # the forward walk).
            # A chunk reads the forward rows of its steps plus the row before
            # its last processed
            # step (for that step's c_prev), or c0 when there is none.
            proc, previous, parts = list(reversed(walk)), None, []
            for i in range(0, len(proc), k):
                chunk = proc[i : i + k]
                lo, hi = min(chunk), max(chunk)
                before = proc[i + k] if i + k < len(proc) else None
                out_lo, out_hi = (
                    (lo, hi)
                    if before is None
                    else (min(lo, before), max(hi, before))
                )
                inputs = [
                    Input(
                        out_value,
                        [out_lo * batch, (out_hi + 1) * batch, 0, 7 * hidden],
                    )
                ]
                flags = {
                    "hidden": hidden,
                    "batch": batch,
                    "reverse": reverse,
                    "steps": len(chunk),
                    "offset": lo - out_lo,
                    "inline": True,
                }
                if before is None:
                    inputs.append(Input(c0))
                    flags["has_c0"] = True
                inputs.append(
                    Input(mask, [lo * batch, (hi + 1) * batch, 0, 1])
                )
                if g_out is not None:
                    inputs.append(
                        Input(g_out, [lo * batch, (hi + 1) * batch, 0, hidden])
                    )
                    flags["has_out"] = True
                if previous is None:
                    if g_state is not None:
                        inputs.append(Input(g_state))
                        flags["has_carry"] = True
                else:
                    task, j = previous
                    inputs.append(
                        Input(
                            task,
                            [
                                j * batch,
                                (j + 1) * batch,
                                4 * hidden,
                                6 * hidden,
                            ],
                        )
                    )
                    flags["has_carry"] = True
                    inputs.append(
                        Input(
                            task, [j * batch, (j + 1) * batch, 0, 4 * hidden]
                        )
                    )
                    flags["has_gates"] = True
                inputs.append(Input(whh))
                ident = self.emit(
                    f"{node.id}/chain/{lo}",
                    "lstm_chain_grad",
                    inputs,
                    (len(chunk) * batch, 6 * hidden),
                    flags,
                )
                previous = (ident, chunk[-1] - lo)
                parts.append(
                    (ident, [lo * batch, (hi + 1) * batch, 0, 6 * hidden])
                )
            task, j = previous
            state = self.emit(
                f"{node.id}/dstate",
                "lstm_state_grad",
                [
                    Input(
                        task,
                        [j * batch, (j + 1) * batch, 4 * hidden, 6 * hidden],
                    ),
                    Input(task, [j * batch, (j + 1) * batch, 0, 4 * hidden]),
                    Input(whh),
                ],
                (batch, 6 * hidden),
                {
                    "hidden": hidden,
                    "has_carry": True,
                    "width": 6 * hidden,
                    "inline": True,
                },
            )
            parts = sorted(parts, key=lambda p: p[1][0]) + [
                (state, [rows, rows + batch, 0, 6 * hidden])
            ]
            self.assemble(node.id, node.shape, parts)
            return
        previous = None
        parts = []
        for k, t in enumerate(reversed(walk)):
            r = [t * batch, (t + 1) * batch]
            before = walk[len(walk) - 2 - k] if k < len(walk) - 1 else None
            c_prev = (
                Input(c0)
                if before is None
                else Input(
                    out_value,
                    [before * batch, (before + 1) * batch, hidden, 2 * hidden],
                )
            )
            inputs, flags = (
                [
                    Input(out_value, r + [0, 7 * hidden]),
                    c_prev,
                    Input(mask, r + [0, 1]),
                ],
                {"hidden": hidden},
            )
            if g_out is not None:
                inputs.append(Input(g_out, r + [0, hidden]))
                flags["has_out"] = True
            if previous is None and g_state is not None:
                inputs.append(Input(g_state))
                flags["has_carry"] = True
            elif previous is not None:
                inputs.append(
                    Input(previous, [0, batch, 4 * hidden, 6 * hidden])
                )
                flags["has_carry"] = True
            if previous is None:
                partials, blocks = [], []
            elif inline:
                partials, blocks = (
                    [Input(previous, [0, batch, 0, 4 * hidden]), Input(whh)],
                    None,
                )
                flags["inline"] = True
            else:
                partials, blocks = self.recurrent(
                    f"{node.id}/dh/{t}",
                    batch,
                    previous,
                    (0, 4 * hidden),
                    whh,
                    hidden,
                )
            if blocks is not None:
                flags["blocks"] = blocks
            previous = self.emit(
                f"{node.id}/dg/{t}",
                "lstm_cell_grad",
                inputs + partials,
                (batch, 6 * hidden),
                flags,
            )
            parts.append((previous, r + [0, 6 * hidden]))
        final = {"hidden": hidden, "has_carry": True, "width": 6 * hidden}
        if inline:
            partials, final["inline"] = (
                [Input(previous, [0, batch, 0, 4 * hidden]), Input(whh)],
                True,
            )
        else:
            partials, final["blocks"] = self.recurrent(
                f"{node.id}/dh0", batch, previous, (0, 4 * hidden), whh, hidden
            )
        state = self.emit(
            f"{node.id}/dstate",
            "lstm_state_grad",
            [Input(previous, [0, batch, 4 * hidden, 6 * hidden])] + partials,
            (batch, 6 * hidden),
            final,
        )
        parts = sorted(parts, key=lambda p: p[1][0]) + [
            (state, [rows, rows + batch, 0, 6 * hidden])
        ]
        self.assemble(node.id, node.shape, parts)


# File: packages/core/src/ygaraty/core/compiler/sequence_transformer.py
"""Transformer task planning using the shared sequence lowering context."""

from .sequence_common import PUBLISH_WIRE
from .tasks import Input, Task


class TransformerLowering:
    """Internal lowering mixin; state and emission belong to
    sequence.Lowering.
    """

    def lower_tf_layer(self, node):
        """A fused Transformer layer over a band of examples is one task
        (experiment 30). An owned layer's task takes no parameters: its
        owner holds them (experiment 31).
        """
        if not node.attrs.get("owned"):
            self.emit(
                node.id,
                node.op,
                [Input(v) for v in node.inputs],
                node.shape,
                node.attrs,
            )
            return
        decoder, tag = node.attrs["decoder"], node.attrs["layer"]
        # The input, the memory and experiment 78's parallel parts.
        data = node.inputs[
            : (2 if decoder else 1) + node.attrs.get("parts", 0)
        ]
        if node.op == "tf_layer":
            deps = (
                [Input(f"owner/{tag}/load")]
                if self.owned and self.owned.get("load")
                else []
            )
            self.emit(
                node.id,
                "tf_owned_layer",
                [Input(v) for v in data] + deps,
                node.shape,
                dict(node.attrs, owner=tag),
            )
        else:
            g = node.inputs[len(data)]
            rows, d = self.tape.shapes[node.inputs[0]]
            mrows = self.tape.shapes[node.inputs[1]][0] if decoder else 0
            self.emit(
                node.id,
                "tf_owned_grad",
                [Input(v) for v in data] + [Input(g)],
                (rows + mrows + 1, d),
                dict(
                    node.attrs,
                    owner=tag,
                    upload_rows=[rows] + ([mrows] if decoder else []) + [1],
                ),
            )

    def vocab_load(self, attrs):
        return (
            [Input(f"owner/{attrs['owner']}/load")]
            if self.owned and self.owned.get("load")
            else []
        )

    def lower_vocab_gather(self, node):
        self.emit(
            node.id,
            "tf_vocab_gather",
            self.vocab_load(node.attrs),
            node.shape,
            node.attrs,
        )

    def lower_vocab_output(self, node):
        """Per band and shard, the shard's logit statistics; per band, its
        rows of the loss from every shard's statistics; the loss is their
        sum.
        """
        bands, shards = node.inputs[:-2], self.tape.owned_vocab["shards"]
        losses, start = [], 0
        for k, band in enumerate(bands):
            rows = self.tape.shapes[band][0]
            targets = node.attrs["targets"][start : start + rows]
            stats = []
            for s, (v0, v1) in enumerate(shards):
                owner = dict(
                    owner=f"vocab.{s}",
                    layer=f"vocab.{s}",
                    shard=s,
                    rows=[v0, v1],
                )
                stats.append(
                    self.emit(
                        f"{node.id}/stats/{k}/{s}",
                        "tf_vocab_stats",
                        [Input(band)] + self.vocab_load(owner),
                        (rows, 4),
                        dict(owner, band=k, targets=targets),
                    )
                )
            losses.append(
                self.emit(
                    f"{node.id}/loss/{k}",
                    "vocab_loss",
                    [Input(v) for v in stats],
                    (1, 1),
                    dict(
                        targets=targets,
                        ignore_index=node.attrs.get("ignore_index"),
                        vocab=node.attrs["vocab"],
                        count=node.attrs["count"],
                        label_smoothing=node.attrs.get("label_smoothing", 0.0),
                    ),
                )
            )
            start += rows
        if len(losses) > 1:
            self.emit(node.id, "add_many", [Input(v) for v in losses], (1, 1))
        else:
            self.emit(
                node.id, "scale", [Input(losses[0])], (1, 1), {"factor": 1.0}
            )

    def lower_vocab_grad(self, node):
        band, seed, attrs = node.inputs[0], node.inputs[3], node.attrs
        stats = [
            Input(f"{attrs['loss']}/stats/{attrs['band']}/{s}")
            for s in range(attrs["shards"])
        ]
        self.emit(
            node.id,
            "tf_vocab_grad",
            [Input(band)] + stats + [Input(seed)],
            node.shape,
            attrs,
        )

    def lower_vocab_embed_grad(self, node):
        # Either the shard's gathered rows' gradient, or (attrs['scatter']) the
        # embeddings'
        # gradient bands, read as regions of the layers' packed results.
        self.emit(
            node.id,
            "tf_vocab_embed_grad",
            [Input(v) for v in node.inputs],
            node.shape,
            node.attrs,
        )

    def vocab_tasks(self, updates, keep):
        """Per vocabulary shard: the load of its rows (first update), its Adam
        step after every band's gradient and the embedding gradient, and on
        publish updates its rows of the table, the bias and their moments,
        assembled into whole values.
        """
        vocab = self.tape.owned_vocab
        table, bias = vocab["params"]
        v, d = self.tape.shapes[table]
        published = {}
        for s, (v0, v1) in enumerate(vocab["shards"]):
            tag = f"vocab.{s}"
            owner = dict(owner=tag, layer=tag, shard=s, rows=[v0, v1])
            if self.owned.get("load"):
                regions = [
                    Input(name, region)
                    for prefix in ("", "adam_m_", "adam_v_")
                    for name, region in (
                        (prefix + table, [v0, v1, 0, d]),
                        (prefix + bias, [0, 1, v0, v1]),
                    )
                ]
                self.tasks.insert(
                    0,
                    Task(
                        f"owner/{tag}/load",
                        "tf_vocab_load",
                        regions,
                        (1, 1),
                        owner,
                    ),
                )
            markers = []
            for ident in vocab["grads"][s]:
                rows = self.tape.shapes[ident][0]
                markers.append(Input(ident, [rows - 1, rows, 0, 1]))
            embed = vocab["embeds"].get(s)
            if embed is not None:
                markers.append(Input(embed))
            step = f"owner/{tag}/step"
            self.emit(
                step,
                "tf_vocab_step",
                markers,
                (1, 1),
                dict(
                    owner,
                    bands=len(vocab["grads"][s]),
                    embed=embed is not None,
                    step=self.owned["step"],
                    learning_rate=self.owned["learning_rate"],
                    beta1=self.owned["beta1"],
                    beta2=self.owned["beta2"],
                    epsilon=self.owned["epsilon"],
                ),
            )
            keep.add(step)
            if self.owned.get("publish"):
                for index, (name, region) in enumerate(
                    ((table, [v0, v1, 0, d]), (bias, [0, 1, v0, v1]))
                ):
                    for kind, prefix in (
                        ("w", ""),
                        ("m", "adam_m_"),
                        ("v", "adam_v_"),
                    ):
                        ident = f"owner/{tag}/publish/{kind}/{index}"
                        self.emit(
                            ident,
                            "tf_vocab_publish",
                            [Input(step)],
                            (region[1] - region[0], region[3] - region[2]),
                            dict(
                                owner,
                                kind=kind,
                                index=index,
                                wire=PUBLISH_WIRE,
                            ),
                        )
                        published.setdefault((prefix + name, name), []).append(
                            (ident, region)
                        )
        for (name, base), parts in published.items():
            # A moment has its parameter's shape (the job's moments are leaves,
            # not tape values).
            ident, shape = (
                f"owner/vocab/publish/{name}",
                self.tape.shapes[base],
            )
            self.emit(
                ident,
                "assemble",
                [Input(i) for i, _ in parts],
                shape,
                {"shape": list(shape), "blocks": [r for _, r in parts]},
            )
            updates[name] = ident
            keep.add(ident)

    def owned_tasks(self):
        """Per owned layer: the load (first update), the Adam step after every
        band's gradient, and the publish tasks (checkpoint updates) whose
        results replace the stored parameters.
        """
        updates, keep = {}, set()
        if self.tape.owned_vocab:
            self.vocab_tasks(updates, keep)
        for tag, layer in self.tape.owned_layers.items():
            params, owner = (
                layer["params"],
                dict(owner=tag, layer=tag, decoder=layer["decoder"]),
            )
            if self.owned.get("load"):
                # First in the plan: the layer's band tasks read it.
                self.tasks.insert(
                    0,
                    Task(
                        f"owner/{tag}/load",
                        "tf_owned_load",
                        [Input(p) for p in params]
                        + [Input("adam_m_" + p) for p in params]
                        + [Input("adam_v_" + p) for p in params],
                        (1, 1),
                        owner,
                    ),
                )
            markers = []
            for ident in layer["backward"]:
                _rows = self.tape.shapes[ident][0]
                task = next(t for t in reversed(self.tasks) if t.id == ident)
                markers.append(
                    Input(ident, [task.shape[0] - 1, task.shape[0], 0, 1])
                )
            step = f"owner/{tag}/step"
            self.emit(
                step,
                "tf_owned_step",
                markers,
                (1, 1),
                dict(
                    owner,
                    bands=len(markers),
                    step=self.owned["step"],
                    learning_rate=self.owned["learning_rate"],
                    beta1=self.owned["beta1"],
                    beta2=self.owned["beta2"],
                    epsilon=self.owned["epsilon"],
                    # M-2: Muon on the layer's matrices when the job asks.
                    **{
                        k: self.owned[k]
                        for k in (
                            "optimizer",
                            "muon_learning_rate",
                            "momentum",
                            "ns_steps",
                        )
                        if k in self.owned
                    },
                ),
            )
            keep.add(step)
            if self.owned.get("publish"):
                for index, p in enumerate(params):
                    for kind, name in (
                        ("w", p),
                        ("m", "adam_m_" + p),
                        ("v", "adam_v_" + p),
                    ):
                        ident = f"owner/{tag}/publish/{kind}/{index}"
                        self.emit(
                            ident,
                            "tf_owned_publish",
                            [Input(step)],
                            self.tape.shapes[p],
                            dict(
                                owner,
                                kind=kind,
                                index=index,
                                wire=PUBLISH_WIRE,
                            ),
                        )
                        updates[name] = ident
                        keep.add(ident)
        return updates, keep

    lower_tf_layer_grad = lower_tf_layer


# File: packages/core/src/ygaraty/core/compiler/sequence_views.py
"""Metadata-only view resolution and dead-task removal."""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import TYPE_CHECKING

from .tasks import Input, Task

if TYPE_CHECKING:
    from .tape import Tape


class ViewResolver:
    """Index aliases and concatenations once, then rewrite consumer
    regions.
    """

    def __init__(self, tape: Tape) -> None:
        self.tape = tape
        self.alias, self.concats = {}, {}
        alias, concats = self.alias, self.concats
        for node in tape.nodes:
            if node.op == "view":
                base = node.inputs[0]
                r0, _, c0, _ = node.attrs["region"]
                if base in alias:
                    base, (br, bc) = alias[base][0], alias[base][1]
                    r0, c0 = r0 + br, c0 + bc
                alias[node.id] = (base, (r0, c0))
            elif node.op == "concat_rows":
                start, parts = 0, []
                for part in node.inputs:
                    parts.append((part, start))
                    start += tape.shapes[part][0]
                concats[node.id] = parts

    def resolve(self, value: str, region: list[int] | None):
        alias, concats, tape = self.alias, self.concats, self.tape
        while True:
            if value in alias:
                base, (r0, c0) = alias[value]
                rows, cols = tape.shapes[value]
                i, ei, j, ej = region or [0, rows, 0, cols]
                value, region = base, [i + r0, ei + r0, j + c0, ej + c0]
                continue
            if value in concats and region is not None:
                for part, start in concats[value]:
                    if (
                        start <= region[0]
                        and region[1] <= start + tape.shapes[part][0]
                    ):
                        value, region = (
                            part,
                            [
                                region[0] - start,
                                region[1] - start,
                                region[2],
                                region[3],
                            ],
                        )
                        break
                else:
                    return value, region
                continue
            return value, region

    def rewrite(self, tasks: list[Task]) -> list[Task]:
        """Mutate inputs in place, retaining whole-value assembly reads."""
        if not self.alias and not self.concats:
            return tasks
        for task in tasks:
            if task.op in ("assemble", "transpose"):
                continue
            inputs = []
            for x in task.inputs:
                value, region = self.resolve(x.value, x.region)
                inputs.append(
                    x
                    if value == x.value and region == x.region
                    else Input(value, region)
                )
            task.inputs = inputs
        return tasks


def resolve_views(tape: Tape, tasks: list[Task]) -> list[Task]:
    """Rewrite views and contained concatenation regions in task inputs."""
    return ViewResolver(tape).rewrite(tasks)


def prune(tasks: list[Task], keep: set[str]) -> list[Task]:
    """Drop tasks whose outputs nobody reads and that are not kept (gradients,
    the loss): the copies behind a concatenation every reader resolved
    past, for instance.
    """
    while True:
        used = {d for t in tasks for d in t.dependencies}
        alive = [
            t for t in tasks if t.id in used or t.id in keep or "/" not in t.id
        ]
        if len(alive) == len(tasks):
            return tasks
        tasks = alive


# File: packages/core/src/ygaraty/core/compiler/shapes.py
"""Shape-only tape declarations and tensor regions, without execution or
storage.
"""

import numpy as np

from ygaraty.core.compiler.tape import Tape


class ShapeTape(Tape):
    def declare(self, name, shape, initializer, parameter=False):
        name = self._name(name)
        self.shapes[name] = tuple(shape)
        self.values[name] = dict(shape=list(shape), **initializer)
        if parameter:
            self.parameters.append(name)
        return name

    def input(self, name, data, *, parameter=False):
        # Reverse-mode construction only needs a scalar seed for this model.
        a = np.asarray(data, dtype=np.float64)
        if a.size > 1024:
            raise ValueError("Use declare() for large shape-only inputs")
        return self.declare(
            name, a.shape, dict(kind="constant", data=a.tolist()), parameter
        )


def regions(shape, tile):
    for i in range(0, shape[0], tile):
        for j in range(0, shape[1], tile):
            yield [i, min(i + tile, shape[0]), j, min(j + tile, shape[1])]


# File: packages/core/src/ygaraty/core/compiler/spec.py
"""Bounded, data-only forward tapes. Rebuild through public shape rules."""

import math
from copy import deepcopy
from dataclasses import asdict
from typing import Any

from .tape import Tape

MAX_NODES = 128
MAX_ELEMENTS = 65536
OPERATIONS = {
    "matmul": (2, set()),
    "add": (2, set()),
    "mul": (2, set()),
    "add_bias": (2, set()),
    "relu": (1, set()),
    "sigmoid": (1, set()),
    "tanh": (1, set()),
    "transpose": (1, set()),
    "sum": (1, set()),
    "scale": (1, {"factor"}),
    "slice_columns": (1, {"start", "width"}),
    "cross_entropy": (1, {"targets", "ignore_index", "label_smoothing"}),
}


def describe(tape: Tape, loss: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Snapshot a forward tape; differentiation is the compiler's
    responsibility.
    """
    if not isinstance(tape, Tape) or any(
        not hasattr(v, "tolist") for v in tape.values.values()
    ):
        raise ValueError(
            "Model expects a numeric Tape with explicit input values"
        )
    if tape.loss is not None or tape.owned_layers or tape.owned_vocab:
        raise ValueError(
            "Model expects a forward tape, before backward(), "
            "without ownership"
        )
    spec = dict(
        inputs={name: value.tolist() for name, value in tape.values.items()},
        parameters=list(tape.parameters),
        nodes=[asdict(n) for n in tape.nodes],
        loss=loss,
    )
    rebuild(spec)
    return deepcopy(spec)


class TapeSpecBuilder:
    """Rebuild bounded forward graphs through validated public Tape
    operations.

    Each build starts a fresh tape; no numerical kernels are evaluated.
    """

    def build(self, spec: dict[str, Any]) -> Tape:
        """Validate dependencies and dimensions without evaluating a
        numerical kernel.
        """
        if not isinstance(spec, dict) or set(spec) != {
            "inputs",
            "parameters",
            "nodes",
            "loss",
        }:
            raise ValueError("Invalid tape specification")
        nodes = spec["nodes"]
        if not isinstance(nodes, list) or not 1 <= len(nodes) <= MAX_NODES:
            raise ValueError("A tape needs 1..128 forward operations")
        tape = self._tape = Tape()
        self._bind_inputs(spec)
        for node in nodes:
            self._append_node(node)
        loss = spec["loss"]
        if not isinstance(loss, str) or tape.shapes.get(loss) != (1, 1):
            raise ValueError("The model loss must name a scalar tensor")
        return tape

    def build_forward(self, spec: dict[str, Any]) -> Tape:
        """Validate named forward outputs and optional parameters."""
        if not isinstance(spec, dict) or set(spec) != {
            "inputs",
            "parameters",
            "nodes",
            "outputs",
        }:
            raise ValueError("Invalid forward specification")
        if (
            not isinstance(spec["nodes"], list)
            or len(spec["nodes"]) > MAX_NODES
        ):
            raise ValueError("A forward tape supports at most 128 operations")
        tape = self._tape = Tape()
        self._bind_inputs(spec, require_parameters=False)
        for node in spec["nodes"]:
            self._append_node(node)
        outputs = spec["outputs"]
        if (
            not isinstance(outputs, dict)
            or not outputs
            or any(
                not isinstance(name, str)
                or not name
                or not isinstance(value, str)
                or value not in tape.shapes
                for name, value in outputs.items()
            )
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                "Outputs must map public names to forward tensors"
            )
        return tape

    def _bind_inputs(
        self, spec: dict[str, Any], *, require_parameters: bool = True
    ) -> None:
        tape = self._tape
        values, parameters = spec["inputs"], spec["parameters"]
        if not isinstance(values, dict) or not 1 <= len(values) <= MAX_NODES:
            raise ValueError("A tape needs 1..128 input tensors")
        if (
            not isinstance(parameters, list)
            or (require_parameters and not parameters)
            or any(not isinstance(n, str) for n in parameters)
            or len(set(parameters)) != len(parameters)
            or not set(parameters) <= values.keys()
        ):
            raise ValueError("Parameters must be unique named input tensors")
        elements = 0
        for name, value in values.items():
            if not isinstance(name, str):
                raise ValueError("Input names must be strings")
            if (
                not isinstance(value, list)
                or not 1 <= len(value) <= 128
                or any(
                    not isinstance(row, list) or not 1 <= len(row) <= 128
                    for row in value
                )
            ):
                raise ValueError("Input dimensions must be 1..128")
            elements += sum(map(len, value))
            if elements > MAX_ELEMENTS:
                raise ValueError("Tape inputs exceed 65,536 elements")
            if name in parameters:
                tape.parameter(name, value)
            else:
                tape.input(name, value)
        tape.parameters = list(parameters)

    def _append_node(self, node: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        tape = self._tape
        if not isinstance(node, dict) or set(node) != {
            "id",
            "op",
            "inputs",
            "shape",
            "attrs",
        }:
            raise ValueError("Invalid forward operation")
        op, inputs, attrs = node["op"], node["inputs"], node["attrs"]
        if (
            not isinstance(node["id"], str)
            or not isinstance(node["shape"], (list, tuple))
            or len(node["shape"]) != 2
            or any(type(n) is not int or n < 1 for n in node["shape"])
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                "Operations need a string ID and "
                "two positive integer dimensions"
            )
        if not isinstance(op, str) or op not in OPERATIONS:
            raise ValueError(f"Unsupported public tape operation: {op!r}")
        count, fields = OPERATIONS[op]
        if (
            not isinstance(inputs, list)
            or len(inputs) != count
            or any(
                not isinstance(n, str) or n not in tape.shapes for n in inputs
            )
            or not isinstance(attrs, dict)
            or set(attrs) != fields
        ):
            raise ValueError("Invalid operation inputs or attributes")
        if op == "scale" and (
            type(attrs["factor"]) not in (int, float)
            or not math.isfinite(attrs["factor"])
        ):
            raise ValueError("Scale factor must be finite")
        if op == "slice_columns" and any(
            type(attrs[k]) is not int for k in fields
        ):
            raise ValueError("Slice bounds must be integers")
        if op == "cross_entropy":
            if (
                attrs["ignore_index"] is not None
                and type(attrs["ignore_index"]) is not int
                or type(attrs["label_smoothing"]) not in (int, float)
            ):
                raise ValueError("Invalid cross-entropy attributes")
        ident = getattr(tape, op)(*inputs, **attrs, name=node["id"])
        if list(tape.shapes[ident]) != list(node["shape"]):
            raise ValueError("Declared shape differs from the operation shape")
        if math.prod(tape.shapes[ident]) > MAX_ELEMENTS:
            raise ValueError("Operation exceeds the public tape element limit")


def rebuild(spec: dict[str, Any]) -> Tape:
    """Compatibility entry point for bounded, data-only forward tapes."""
    return TapeSpecBuilder().build(spec)


# File: packages/core/src/ygaraty/core/compiler/tape.py
"""Small rank-two tensor tape, with an explicit reverse-mode graph builder."""

import re
from dataclasses import dataclass, field

import numpy as np


@dataclass
class Node:
    id: str
    op: str
    inputs: list[str]
    shape: tuple[int, int]
    attrs: dict = field(default_factory=dict)


class Tape:
    def __init__(self):
        self.values = {}
        self.shapes = {}
        self.nodes = []
        self.parameters = []
        self.gradients = {}
        self.loss = None
        self._counter = 0
        # Owned Transformer layers: tag -> dict(params, decoder, forward ids,
        # backward ids, ...).
        # Their parameters get no gradient on the tape; the owning worker
        # updates them.
        self.owned_layers = {}
        # Owned vocabulary (experiment 32): dict(params=[table, bias],
        # shards=[[v0, v1], ...],
        # grads={shard: [vocab_grad ids]}, embeds={shard: vocab_embed_grad id},
        # table=the
        # gathered rows' concatenation that `embed` reads), or None.
        self.owned_vocab = None
        # Values whose gradients are parameter gradients (parameters, and
        # vocabulary gathers
        # that stand for an owner's rows), and the nodes that compute those
        # gradients: the
        # planner keeps them at full width on a narrow wire
        # (plan.narrow_activations).
        self.parameter_like, self.parameter_side = set(), set()

    def forward(self, *, outputs):
        """Compile named outputs without a loss or differentiation.

        Returns an immutable inference plan. Compilation validates graph
        metadata only; call the plan's run(inputs=...) to evaluate it.
        """
        from ..inference.forward import ForwardPlan

        return ForwardPlan.from_tape(self, outputs=outputs)

    def _name(self, name=None):
        if name is None:
            while f"v{self._counter}" in self.shapes:
                self._counter += 1
            name = f"v{self._counter}"
            self._counter += 1
        if (
            not re.fullmatch(r"[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*", name)
            or name in self.shapes
        ):
            raise ValueError(f"Invalid or duplicate tensor ID: {name!r}")
        return name

    def input(self, name, data, *, parameter=False):
        data = np.asarray(data, dtype=np.float64)
        if (
            data.ndim != 2
            or min(data.shape) < 1
            or not np.isfinite(data).all()
        ):
            raise ValueError("Inputs must be nonempty, finite rank-two arrays")
        name = self._name(name)
        self.values[name] = data.copy()
        self.shapes[name] = data.shape
        if parameter:
            self.parameters.append(name)
        return name

    def parameter(self, name, data):
        name = self.input(name, data, parameter=True)
        self.parameter_like.add(name)
        return name

    def _op(self, op, inputs, shape, attrs=None, name=None):
        name = self._name(name)
        self.shapes[name] = shape
        self.nodes.append(Node(name, op, list(inputs), shape, attrs or {}))
        return name

    def matmul(self, a, b, name=None):
        m, k = self.shapes[a]
        kb, n = self.shapes[b]
        if k != kb:
            raise ValueError("matmul inner dimensions differ")
        return self._op("matmul", [a, b], (m, n), name=name)

    def _elementwise(self, op, a, b, name):
        if self.shapes[a] != self.shapes[b]:
            raise ValueError(f"{op} requires equal shapes")
        return self._op(op, [a, b], self.shapes[a], name=name)

    def add(self, a, b, name=None):
        return self._elementwise("add", a, b, name)

    def mul(self, a, b, name=None):
        return self._elementwise("mul", a, b, name)

    def add_bias(self, a, b, name=None):
        if self.shapes[b] != (1, self.shapes[a][1]):
            raise ValueError("bias must be 1 x columns")
        return self._op("add_bias", [a, b], self.shapes[a], name=name)

    def relu(self, a, name=None):
        return self._op("relu", [a], self.shapes[a], name=name)

    def embedding(self, table, ids, name=None, padding_idx=None):
        ids = np.asarray(ids)
        if (
            ids.ndim != 1
            or len(ids) < 1
            or ids.dtype.kind not in "iu"
            or np.any(ids < 0)
            or np.any(ids >= self.shapes[table][0])
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                "Embedding IDs must be a nonempty vector of valid integer rows"
            )
        return self._op(
            "embedding",
            [table],
            (len(ids), self.shapes[table][1]),
            {"ids": ids.tolist(), "padding_idx": padding_idx},
            name,
        )

    def embed(
        self,
        table,
        ids,
        positions,
        position_ids,
        factor,
        padding_idx=None,
        name=None,
    ):
        """table[ids] * factor + positions[position_ids]: a token
        embedding, its scaling and its position embedding as one
        operation (one task per tile; its backward scales inside the
        scatter). The same arithmetic as scale(embedding(...)) +
        embedding(...).
        """
        ids, position_ids = np.asarray(ids), np.asarray(position_ids)
        for rows, table_ in ((ids, table), (position_ids, positions)):
            if (
                rows.ndim != 1
                or len(rows) < 1
                or rows.dtype.kind not in "iu"
                or np.any(rows < 0)
                or np.any(rows >= self.shapes[table_][0])
            ):
                raise ValueError(
                    "Embedding IDs must be a nonempty vector "
                    "of valid integer rows"
                )
        if (
            len(ids) != len(position_ids)
            or self.shapes[table][1] != self.shapes[positions][1]
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                "Token and position embeddings must agree on rows and width"
            )
        return self._op(
            "embed",
            [table, positions],
            (len(ids), self.shapes[table][1]),
            {
                "ids": ids.tolist(),
                "positions": position_ids.tolist(),
                "factor": float(factor),
                "padding_idx": padding_idx,
            },
            name,
        )

    def softmax(self, a, mask=None):
        attrs = (
            {}
            if mask is None
            else {"mask": np.asarray(mask, dtype=bool).tolist()}
        )
        if mask is not None and (
            np.shape(mask) != self.shapes[a] or np.all(mask, axis=1).any()
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                "Attention mask must match scores and leave a key per row"
            )
        return self._op("softmax", [a], self.shapes[a], attrs)

    def layer_norm(self, a, weight, bias, epsilon=1e-5):
        if (
            self.shapes[weight] != (1, self.shapes[a][1])
            or self.shapes[bias] != self.shapes[weight]
        ):
            raise ValueError("LayerNorm affine shape mismatch")
        return self._op(
            "layer_norm",
            [a, weight, bias],
            self.shapes[a],
            {"epsilon": epsilon},
        )

    def concat_columns(self, values):
        if not values or len({self.shapes[v][0] for v in values}) != 1:
            raise ValueError("Concatenation requires equal row counts")
        return self._op(
            "concat_columns",
            values,
            (
                self.shapes[values[0]][0],
                sum(self.shapes[v][1] for v in values),
            ),
        )

    def sigmoid(self, a, name=None):
        return self._op("sigmoid", [a], self.shapes[a], name=name)

    def tanh(self, a, name=None):
        return self._op("tanh", [a], self.shapes[a], name=name)

    def scale(self, a, factor, name=None):
        return self._op(
            "scale", [a], self.shapes[a], {"factor": float(factor)}, name
        )

    def slice_columns(self, a, start, width, name=None):
        if start < 0 or width < 1 or start + width > self.shapes[a][1]:
            raise ValueError("Invalid column slice")
        return self._op(
            "slice_columns",
            [a],
            (self.shapes[a][0], width),
            {"start": start, "width": width},
            name,
        )

    def sum(self, a, name=None):
        return self._op("sum", [a], (1, 1), name=name)

    def add_many(self, values, name=None):
        """Elementwise sum of several equal-shape values (the planner tiles
        it).
        """
        if not values or len({self.shapes[v] for v in values}) != 1:
            raise ValueError("add_many requires equal shapes")
        return self._op(
            "add_many", list(values), self.shapes[values[0]], name=name
        )

    def transformer_layer(
        self,
        x,
        params,
        attrs,
        memory=None,
        norm=None,
        name=None,
        parts=(),
        residual=False,
    ):
        """One fused pre-norm Transformer layer over a band of examples
        (example-major rows; ygaraty.core.runtime.transformer_kernels).
        `params` in ENCODER_PARAMS or DECODER_PARAMS order. `norm`:
        (weight, bias) of a final layer norm the task applies to its
        output, for the stack's last layer, whose owner then holds those
        weights too.
        `parts` (experiment 78): values added to `x` inside the task,
        inputs after the memory. `residual`: the task returns only
        the layer's residual update, y - x.
        """
        from ygaraty.core.compiler.layouts import (
            DECODER_PARAMS,
            ENCODER_PARAMS,
        )

        parts = list(parts)
        if any(self.shapes[v] != self.shapes[x] for v in parts):
            raise ValueError("Layer input parts must be shaped as the input")
        if residual and norm is not None:
            raise ValueError(
                "A residual-only layer can't apply the final norm"
            )

        expected = DECODER_PARAMS if memory is not None else ENCODER_PARAMS
        if len(params) != len(expected):
            raise ValueError(
                f"A {'decoder' if memory is not None else 'encoder'} "
                f"layer takes {len(expected)} parameters"
            )
        if memory is not None and self.shapes[memory][1] != self.shapes[x][1]:
            raise ValueError("Memory width must match the layer width")
        if norm is not None and any(
            self.shapes[p] != (1, self.shapes[x][1]) for p in norm
        ):
            raise ValueError("Final norm weight and bias must be 1 x width")
        inputs = (
            [x]
            + ([memory] if memory is not None else [])
            + parts
            + list(params)
            + (list(norm) if norm is not None else [])
        )
        extra = dict(parts=len(parts)) if parts else {}
        if residual:
            extra["residual"] = True
        return self._op(
            "tf_layer",
            inputs,
            self.shapes[x],
            dict(
                attrs,
                decoder=memory is not None,
                final_norm=norm is not None,
                upload_rows=[self.shapes[x][0]],
                **extra,
            ),
            name,
        )

    # Owned vocabulary (experiment 32)
    # ----------------------------------------------
    # The tied embedding table and the output bias split by vocabulary rows
    # over owning workers.
    # The table and bias are inputs of these nodes so the tape keeps their
    # meaning; the planner
    # sends none of them to a worker, and they get no tape gradient.

    def vocab_gather(self, table, shard, rows, ids, name=None):
        """Rows `ids` (local to the shard's rows [v0, v1)) of the table,
        served by the shard's owner.
        """
        v0, v1 = rows
        if not ids or min(ids) < 0 or max(ids) >= v1 - v0:
            raise ValueError("Gathered rows must lie in the shard")
        node = self._op(
            "vocab_gather",
            [table],
            (len(ids), self.shapes[table][1]),
            dict(
                owner=f"vocab.{shard}",
                layer=f"vocab.{shard}",
                shard=shard,
                rows=[v0, v1],
                ids=list(ids),
            ),
            name,
        )
        self.parameter_like.add(node)
        return node

    def vocab_output(self, bands, table, bias, attrs, name=None):
        """The mean label-smoothed cross-entropy of logits = x @ table.T +
        bias, with x given as row bands (views of the decoder output, in
        order); attrs: shards, targets (per row), ignore_index,
        label_smoothing.
        """
        v, d = self.shapes[table]
        if self.shapes[bias] != (1, v) or any(
            self.shapes[b][1] != d for b in bands
        ):
            raise ValueError("Vocabulary output shapes disagree")
        targets = list(attrs["targets"])
        if len(targets) != sum(self.shapes[b][0] for b in bands):
            raise ValueError("One target per row is required")
        count = sum(1 for t in targets if t != attrs.get("ignore_index"))
        return self._op(
            "vocab_output",
            list(bands) + [table, bias],
            (1, 1),
            dict(
                attrs,
                vocab=v,
                count=count,
                rows=[self.shapes[b][0] for b in bands],
            ),
            name,
        )

    # Batched sequence operations
    # -------------------------------------------------
    # Sequences are time-major: row t*batch+b holds step t of example b.

    def view(self, a, region, name=None):
        """A rectangle [row_start, row_stop, column_start, column_stop] of
        `a`, without a copy. The planner turns it into a region of `a`
        for every task that reads it.
        """
        r0, r1, c0, c1 = region
        rows, cols = self.shapes[a]
        if not (0 <= r0 < r1 <= rows and 0 <= c0 < c1 <= cols):
            raise ValueError("Invalid view region")
        return self._op(
            "view", [a], (r1 - r0, c1 - c0), {"region": [r0, r1, c0, c1]}, name
        )

    def concat_rows(self, values, name=None):
        if not values or len({self.shapes[v][1] for v in values}) != 1:
            raise ValueError("Row concatenation requires equal column counts")
        if len(values) == 1:
            return values[0]
        return self._op(
            "concat_rows",
            values,
            (
                sum(self.shapes[v][0] for v in values),
                self.shapes[values[0]][1],
            ),
            name=name,
        )

    def place(self, shape, pieces, name=None):
        """Zeros of `shape` plus each (value, [row, column]) added at its
        offset.
        """
        for value, (r, c) in pieces:
            if (
                r < 0
                or c < 0
                or r + self.shapes[value][0] > shape[0]
                or c + self.shapes[value][1] > shape[1]
            ):
                raise ValueError("Piece outside the placed shape")
        return self._op(
            "place",
            [v for v, _ in pieces],
            tuple(shape),
            {"shape": list(shape), "offsets": [list(o) for _, o in pieces]},
            name,
        )

    def lstm(
        self,
        xp,
        whh_t,
        bias_ih,
        bias_hh,
        mask,
        h0,
        c0,
        batch,
        reverse=False,
        name=None,
    ):
        """One LSTM layer direction over a time-major batch, as one fused
        step per time step.

        xp: input projections X @ W_ih.T (steps*batch x 4h); whh_t:
        W_hh.T (h x 4h); mask (steps*batch x 1): 1 for real tokens, 0
        for padding, where the state is carried unchanged and the output
        is zero (packed-sequence semantics). Output per row: [h_state |
        c_state | h_out | i | f | g | o], each h wide (see
        LSTM_COLUMNS).
        """
        rows, four = self.shapes[xp]
        hidden = four // 4
        if (
            four != 4 * hidden
            or rows % batch
            or self.shapes[whh_t] != (hidden, four)
            or self.shapes[bias_ih] != (1, four)
            or self.shapes[bias_hh] != (1, four)
            or self.shapes[mask] != (rows, 1)
            or self.shapes[h0] != (batch, hidden)
            or self.shapes[c0] != (batch, hidden)
        ):
            raise ValueError("Inconsistent LSTM operand shapes")
        return self._op(
            "lstm",
            [xp, whh_t, bias_ih, bias_hh, mask, h0, c0],
            (rows, 7 * hidden),
            {"batch": batch, "hidden": hidden, "reverse": bool(reverse)},
            name,
        )

    def bqk(self, q, k, batch, name=None):
        """Per example b: Q_b @ K_b.T. Rows t*batch+b of q and s*batch+b of
        k; output (steps*batch x keys).
        """
        if (
            self.shapes[q][1] != self.shapes[k][1]
            or self.shapes[q][0] % batch
            or self.shapes[k][0] % batch
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                "bqk operands must share width and hold whole batches"
            )
        return self._op(
            "bqk",
            [q, k],
            (self.shapes[q][0], self.shapes[k][0] // batch),
            {"batch": batch},
            name,
        )

    def bpv(self, p, v, batch, name=None):
        """Per example b: P_b @ V_b, with P (steps*batch x keys) and V
        (keys*batch x width).
        """
        if (
            self.shapes[p][1] * batch != self.shapes[v][0]
            or self.shapes[p][0] % batch
        ):
            raise ValueError("bpv operands disagree on keys or batch")
        return self._op(
            "bpv",
            [p, v],
            (self.shapes[p][0], self.shapes[v][1]),
            {"batch": batch},
            name,
        )

    def bptq(self, p, q, batch, name=None):
        """Per example b: P_b.T @ Q_b, the gradient-side partner of bqk and
        bpv.
        """
        if self.shapes[p][0] != self.shapes[q][0] or self.shapes[p][0] % batch:
            raise ValueError(
                "bptq operands must share rows that hold whole batches"
            )
        return self._op(
            "bptq",
            [p, q],
            (self.shapes[p][1] * batch, self.shapes[q][1]),
            {"batch": batch},
            name,
        )

    def transpose(self, a, name=None):
        return self._op("transpose", [a], self.shapes[a][::-1], name=name)

    def _broadcast(self, a, shape):
        return self._op("broadcast", [a], shape, {"shape": list(shape)})

    def _rows(self, a):
        return self._op("reduce_rows", [a], (1, self.shapes[a][1]))

    def cross_entropy(
        self,
        scores,
        targets,
        name=None,
        ignore_index=None,
        label_smoothing=0.0,
    ):
        rows, cols = self.shapes[scores]
        labels = np.asarray(targets)
        if (
            labels.shape != (rows,)
            or labels.dtype.kind not in "iu"
            or np.any(labels < 0)
            or np.any(labels >= cols)
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                "One integer target per row, in [0, classes), is required"
            )
        if not 0 <= label_smoothing <= 1 or not np.any(labels != ignore_index):
            raise ValueError("Invalid smoothing or no unmasked targets")
        return self._op(
            "cross_entropy",
            [scores],
            (1, 1),
            {
                "targets": labels.tolist(),
                "ignore_index": ignore_index,
                "label_smoothing": label_smoothing,
            },
            name,
        )

    def backward(self, loss, input_gradients=True):
        """Append derivative operations once. Shared paths become explicit
        adds.

        input_gradients=False skips the gradient of every leaf input
        that is not a parameter (the data matrix of a factorization, an
        MLP's examples): nothing trains them, so their gradient tasks,
        often a full matrix product per tile, would be computed and
        dropped. The default keeps the complete graph, which pinned plan
        hashes depend on.
        """
        if self.loss is not None:
            raise ValueError("Call backward only once per tape")
        if self.shapes[loss] != (1, 1):
            raise ValueError("backward requires a scalar loss")
        forward = list(self.nodes)
        nodes = {n.id: n for n in forward}
        seed = self.input(None, [[1.0]])
        gradients = {loss: seed}
        # Gradients reaching a value through views, as (gradient, [row,
        # column]) pieces.
        # They are combined when the reverse pass reaches the value itself,
        # since every
        # view of a value comes after it.
        pieces = {}

        def acc(value, contribution):
            gradients[value] = (
                self.add(gradients[value], contribution)
                if value in gradients
                else contribution
            )
            if value in self.parameter_like:
                self.parameter_side.update((contribution, gradients[value]))

        # Owned vocabulary: the embeddings' gradients as their row bands (per
        # embed, in the
        # reverse pass's order), for the owners to scatter (vocab_embed_grad)
        # and for one
        # scatter of the position table, instead of a gradient of the gathered
        # table.
        embed_grads = []

        def node_of(ident):
            # Nodes made by this pass are recent: search from the end.
            return nodes.get(ident) or next(
                (n for n in reversed(self.nodes) if n.id == ident), None
            )

        def combine(value):
            parts = pieces.pop(value, [])
            if not parts:
                return
            shape = self.shapes[value]
            if (
                value not in gradients
                and len(parts) == 1
                and parts[0][1] == [0, 0]
                and self.shapes[parts[0][0]] == shape
            ):
                gradients[value] = parts[0][0]
                return
            if value not in gradients:
                # Full-width row bands covering the value in order: a
                # concatenation, which the
                # planner resolves without a copy, instead of placing them into
                # a fresh value.
                ordered, start, bands = (
                    sorted(parts, key=lambda p: p[1][0]),
                    0,
                    True,
                )
                for g, (r, c) in ordered:
                    bands = (
                        bands
                        and r == start
                        and c == 0
                        and self.shapes[g][1] == shape[1]
                    )
                    start += self.shapes[g][0]
                if bands and start == shape[0]:
                    gradients[value] = self.concat_rows(
                        [g for g, _ in ordered]
                    )
                    return
                # Several readers, each contributing the same full-width row
                # bands (the decoder
                # layers' gradients of the encoder output): one sum per band,
                # then their
                # concatenation, so a band's gradient waits only for its own
                # band's pieces. Tiles
                # of a placement would cross band boundaries. Each band's
                # pieces keep their order,
                # so the sums are place()'s, element by element.
                bands, start = {}, 0
                if all(
                    c == 0 and self.shapes[g][1] == shape[1]
                    for g, (r, c) in parts
                ):
                    for g, (r, c) in parts:
                        bands.setdefault((r, self.shapes[g][0]), []).append(g)
                    ranges = sorted(bands)
                    for r, rows in ranges:
                        if r != start:
                            break
                        start += rows
                if bands and start == shape[0] and len(bands) > 1:
                    sums = [
                        group[0] if len(group) == 1 else self.add_many(group)
                        for _, group in sorted(bands.items())
                    ]
                    gradients[value] = self.concat_rows(sums)
                    return
            whole = (
                [(gradients[value], [0, 0])] if value in gradients else []
            ) + parts
            gradients[value] = self.place(shape, whole)

        handled = set()
        for node in reversed(forward):
            if node.op == "vocab_output" and node.id in gradients:
                self._vocab_output_backward(node, gradients[node.id], acc)
                continue
            if node.op == "vocab_gather":
                combine(node.id)
                s = node.attrs["shard"]
                if embed_grads:
                    self.owned_vocab["embeds"][s] = self._vocab_embed_scatter(
                        node, embed_grads, nodes
                    )
                elif node.id in gradients:
                    self.owned_vocab["embeds"][s] = self._op(
                        "vocab_embed_grad",
                        [gradients[node.id]],
                        (1, 1),
                        dict(node.attrs),
                    )
                continue
            if node.op == "tf_layer":
                if node.id in handled:
                    continue
                # Every band of this layer at once, so each parameter's
                # gradient is one sum.
                group = [
                    n
                    for n in forward
                    if n.op == "tf_layer"
                    and n.attrs.get("layer") == node.attrs.get("layer")
                ]
                handled.update(n.id for n in group)
                self._tf_layer_backward(
                    [n for n in group if n.id in gradients], gradients, acc
                )
                continue
            if (
                node.op == "lstm"
                and node.id in pieces
                and node.id not in gradients
            ):
                self._lstm_backward(node, pieces.pop(node.id), acc)
                continue
            combine(node.id)
            if node.id not in gradients:
                continue
            g = gradients[node.id]
            a = node.inputs[0]
            b = node.inputs[1] if len(node.inputs) > 1 else None
            if node.op == "matmul":
                acc(a, self.matmul(g, self.transpose(b)))
                acc(b, self.matmul(self.transpose(a), g))
            elif node.op == "add":
                acc(a, g)
                acc(b, g)
            elif node.op == "mul":
                acc(a, self.mul(g, b))
                acc(b, self.mul(g, a))
            elif node.op == "add_bias":
                acc(a, g)
                acc(b, self._rows(g))
            elif node.op == "softmax":
                acc(
                    a,
                    self._op("softmax_backward", [node.id, g], self.shapes[a]),
                )
            elif node.op == "layer_norm":
                for index, value in enumerate(node.inputs):
                    acc(
                        value,
                        self._op(
                            "layer_norm_backward",
                            [a, node.inputs[1], g],
                            self.shapes[value],
                            dict(node.attrs, index=index),
                        ),
                    )
            elif node.op == "concat_columns":
                start = 0
                for value in node.inputs:
                    width = self.shapes[value][1]
                    acc(value, self.slice_columns(g, start, width))
                    start += width
            elif node.op == "relu":
                acc(a, self._op("relu_backward", [a, g], self.shapes[a]))
            elif node.op == "embedding":
                acc(
                    a,
                    self._op(
                        "embedding_backward",
                        [g],
                        self.shapes[a],
                        dict(node.attrs, shape=list(self.shapes[a])),
                    ),
                )
            elif (
                node.op == "embed"
                and self.owned_vocab is not None
                and a == self.owned_vocab.get("table")
            ):
                # The gradient's row bands as the first layer's tasks produced
                # them (a row
                # concatenation of views costs no copy): read where they are,
                # by the owners.
                gnode = node_of(g)
                parts = (
                    list(gnode.inputs)
                    if gnode is not None and gnode.op == "concat_rows"
                    else [g]
                )
                # A band read by several layers (experiment 78) has
                # its gradient as a sum: the owners scatter each term
                # themselves, so the sum costs no task of its own.
                ids, positions = node.attrs["ids"], node.attrs["positions"]
                terms, term_ids, term_positions, row = [], [], [], 0
                for part in parts:
                    rows = self.shapes[part][0]
                    pnode = node_of(part)
                    addends = (
                        list(pnode.inputs)
                        if pnode is not None
                        and pnode.op == "add_many"
                        and all(
                            self.shapes[v] == self.shapes[part]
                            for v in pnode.inputs
                        )
                        else [part]
                    )
                    for addend in addends:
                        terms.append(addend)
                        term_ids += list(ids[row : row + rows])
                        term_positions += list(positions[row : row + rows])
                    row += rows
                embed_grads.append(
                    dict(
                        parts=terms,
                        ids=term_ids if len(terms) > len(parts) else ids,
                        factor=node.attrs["factor"],
                        padding_idx=node.attrs["padding_idx"],
                        positions=(
                            term_positions
                            if len(terms) > len(parts)
                            else positions
                        ),
                        table=b,
                    )
                )
            elif node.op == "embed":
                acc(
                    a,
                    self._op(
                        "embedding_backward",
                        [g],
                        self.shapes[a],
                        dict(
                            ids=node.attrs["ids"],
                            padding_idx=node.attrs["padding_idx"],
                            factor=node.attrs["factor"],
                            shape=list(self.shapes[a]),
                        ),
                    ),
                )
                acc(
                    b,
                    self._op(
                        "embedding_backward",
                        [g],
                        self.shapes[b],
                        dict(
                            ids=node.attrs["positions"],
                            padding_idx=None,
                            shape=list(self.shapes[b]),
                        ),
                    ),
                )
            elif node.op in ("sigmoid", "tanh"):
                acc(
                    a,
                    self._op(
                        node.op + "_backward", [node.id, g], self.shapes[a]
                    ),
                )
            elif node.op == "scale":
                acc(a, self.scale(g, node.attrs["factor"]))
            elif node.op == "slice_columns":
                acc(
                    a,
                    self._op(
                        "slice_backward",
                        [g],
                        self.shapes[a],
                        dict(node.attrs, shape=list(self.shapes[a])),
                    ),
                )
            elif node.op == "sum":
                acc(a, self._broadcast(g, self.shapes[a]))
            elif node.op == "transpose":
                acc(a, self.transpose(g))
            elif node.op == "cross_entropy":
                acc(
                    a,
                    self._op(
                        "ce_backward", [a, g], self.shapes[a], node.attrs
                    ),
                )
            elif node.op == "view":
                r0, _, c0, _ = node.attrs["region"]
                pieces.setdefault(a, []).append((g, [r0, c0]))
            elif node.op == "concat_rows":
                start = 0
                for value in node.inputs:
                    rows, cols = self.shapes[value]
                    acc(value, self.view(g, [start, start + rows, 0, cols]))
                    start += rows
            elif node.op == "place":
                for value, (r, c) in zip(node.inputs, node.attrs["offsets"]):
                    rows, cols = self.shapes[value]
                    acc(value, self.view(g, [r, r + rows, c, c + cols]))
            elif node.op == "lstm":
                self._lstm_backward(node, [(g, [0, 0])], acc)
            elif node.op == "bqk":
                acc(a, self.bpv(g, b, node.attrs["batch"]))
                acc(b, self.bptq(g, a, node.attrs["batch"]))
            elif node.op == "bpv":
                acc(a, self.bqk(g, b, node.attrs["batch"]))
                acc(b, self.bptq(a, g, node.attrs["batch"]))
            else:
                raise ValueError(f"No derivative rule for {node.op}")
        # The position table's gradient: one scatter over every embedding's row
        # bands that read it
        # (hint rows, experiment 55, read a zero table of their own).
        for table in dict.fromkeys(e["table"] for e in embed_grads):
            group = [e for e in embed_grads if e["table"] == table]
            acc(
                table,
                self._op(
                    "embedding_backward",
                    [p for e in group for p in e["parts"]],
                    self.shapes[table],
                    dict(
                        ids=[i for e in group for i in e["positions"]],
                        padding_idx=None,
                        shape=list(self.shapes[table]),
                    ),
                ),
            )
        for value in list(pieces):
            combine(value)
        self.loss = loss
        self.gradients = {}
        owned = {
            p for layer in self.owned_layers.values() for p in layer["params"]
        }
        owned.update(self.owned_vocab["params"] if self.owned_vocab else ())
        for p in self.parameters:
            if p in owned:
                continue
            g = (
                gradients[p]
                if p in gradients
                else self.input(None, np.zeros(self.shapes[p]))
            )
            # The optimizer reads gradients as stored values, never as views.
            self.gradients[p] = (
                self.scale(g, 1.0)
                if nodes.get(g) and nodes[g].op == "view"
                else g
            )
            self.parameter_side.add(self.gradients[p])
        # A rearrangement (a sum, a view, a transpose: the unpacking of a
        # layer's packed
        # gradients) read only by parameter-side nodes is parameter-side too. A
        # kernel's output
        # is not: a fused backward result holds activation gradients beside
        # parameter ones.
        rearrangements = {
            "transpose",
            "view",
            "concat_columns",
            "concat_rows",
            "add",
            "add_many",
            "scale",
            "place",
        }
        consumers = {}
        for n in self.nodes:
            for v in n.inputs:
                consumers.setdefault(v, set()).add(n.id)
        changed = True
        while changed:
            changed = False
            for n in self.nodes:
                if (
                    n.op in rearrangements
                    and n.id not in self.parameter_side
                    and consumers.get(n.id)
                    and consumers[n.id] <= self.parameter_side
                ):
                    self.parameter_side.add(n.id)
                    changed = True
        if (
            not input_gradients
            and not self.owned_layers
            and not self.owned_vocab
        ):
            # Drop the reverse-pass nodes that reach neither the loss nor a
            # parameter gradient:
            # the gradients of untrained inputs and the sums that fed them.
            # Forward nodes stay.
            by_id = {n.id: n for n in self.nodes}
            live, todo = (
                {loss, *self.gradients.values()},
                [loss, *self.gradients.values()],
            )
            while todo:
                node = by_id.get(todo.pop())
                for value in node.inputs if node else ():
                    if value not in live:
                        live.add(value)
                        todo.append(value)
            self.nodes = [
                n for n in self.nodes if n.id in live or n.id in nodes
            ]
            self.parameter_side &= {n.id for n in self.nodes} | set(
                self.values
            )
        return dict(self.gradients)

    def _vocab_embed_scatter(self, gather, embed_grads, nodes):
        """The shard's vocab_embed_grad reading the embeddings' gradient
        bands directly: per part, each row's place among the shard's
        gathered rows (-1: another shard's, or padding), so the owner
        scatters its rows itself. Sides (one per embed) are scattered in
        turn, then summed in the order the tape would have added them.
        """
        concat = nodes[self.owned_vocab["table"]]
        offset = 0
        for part in concat.inputs:
            if part == gather.id:
                break
            offset += self.shapes[part][0]
        count = self.shapes[gather.id][0]
        inputs, scatter, sides, factors = [], [], [], []
        for e in embed_grads:
            row = 0
            for part in e["parts"]:
                rows = self.shapes[part][0]
                ids = e["ids"][row : row + rows]
                scatter.append(
                    [
                        x - offset
                        if offset <= x < offset + count
                        and x != e["padding_idx"]
                        else -1
                        for x in ids
                    ]
                )
                inputs.append(part)
                row += rows
            sides.append(len(e["parts"]))
            factors.append(e["factor"])
        return self._op(
            "vocab_embed_grad",
            inputs,
            (1, 1),
            dict(gather.attrs, scatter=scatter, sides=sides, factors=factors),
        )

    def _vocab_output_backward(self, node, seed, acc):
        """Per band and shard, one vocab_grad: the shard's part of the
        band's gradient (its rows first, then a marker row the shard's
        step depends on). The band's gradient is their sum.
        """
        bands, (table, bias) = node.inputs[:-2], node.inputs[-2:]
        shards = self.owned_vocab["shards"]
        start = 0
        for k, band in enumerate(bands):
            rows, d = self.shapes[band]
            parts = []
            for s, (v0, v1) in enumerate(shards):
                attrs = dict(
                    owner=f"vocab.{s}",
                    layer=f"vocab.{s}",
                    shard=s,
                    shards=len(shards),
                    rows=[v0, v1],
                    band=k,
                    loss=node.id,
                    targets=node.attrs["targets"][start : start + rows],
                    vocab=node.attrs["vocab"],
                    count=node.attrs["count"],
                    ignore_index=node.attrs.get("ignore_index"),
                    label_smoothing=node.attrs.get("label_smoothing", 0.0),
                    upload_rows=[rows, 1],
                )
                full = self._op(
                    "vocab_grad",
                    [band, table, bias, seed],
                    (rows + 1, d),
                    attrs,
                )
                self.owned_vocab["grads"].setdefault(s, []).append(full)
                parts.append(self.view(full, [0, rows, 0, d]))
            acc(band, parts[0] if len(parts) == 1 else self.add_many(parts))
            start += rows

    def _tf_layer_backward(self, nodes, gradients, acc):
        """Gradients of fused layer bands: one tf_layer_grad per band,
        whose packed output holds the input's gradient, the memory's
        (decoder) and every parameter's (packed_layout).
        """
        from ygaraty.core.compiler.layouts import packed_layout

        contributions = {}
        owned = bool(nodes) and nodes[0].attrs.get("owned", False)
        if owned:
            tag = nodes[0].attrs["layer"]
            first = nodes[0]
            self.owned_layers[tag] = dict(
                params=list(
                    first.inputs[
                        (2 if first.attrs["decoder"] else 1)
                        + first.attrs.get("parts", 0) :
                    ]
                ),
                decoder=first.attrs["decoder"],
                forward=[n.id for n in nodes],
                backward=[],
            )
        for node in nodes:
            decoder = node.attrs["decoder"]
            x = node.inputs[0]
            mem = node.inputs[1] if decoder else None
            start = 2 if decoder else 1
            extra = node.inputs[start : start + node.attrs.get("parts", 0)]
            params = node.inputs[start + len(extra) :]
            rows, d = self.shapes[x]
            mrows = self.shapes[mem][0] if decoder else 0
            layout, packed = packed_layout([self.shapes[p] for p in params], d)
            # upload_rows: the bands a relay upload is cut into, one per reader
            # (the input's
            # gradient, the memory's, each parameter's), so no reader fetches
            # more than its own.
            bounds = (
                [rows]
                + ([mrows] if decoder else [])
                + [count for _, count, _ in layout]
            )
            full = self._op(
                "tf_layer_grad",
                [x]
                + ([mem] if decoder else [])
                + list(extra)
                + [gradients[node.id]]
                + list(params),
                (rows + mrows + packed, d),
                dict(node.attrs, upload_rows=bounds),
            )
            dx = self.view(full, [0, rows, 0, d])
            acc(x, dx)
            for part in extra:  # x + parts: the same gradient for each
                acc(part, dx)
            if decoder:
                acc(mem, self.view(full, [rows, rows + mrows, 0, d]))
            if owned:
                self.owned_layers[tag]["backward"].append(full)
                continue  # the owner keeps the parameter gradients
            base = rows + mrows
            for p, (offset, count, mode) in zip(params, layout):
                contributions.setdefault(p, []).append(
                    (
                        self.view(
                            full, [base + offset, base + offset + count, 0, d]
                        ),
                        count,
                        mode,
                    )
                )
        # Sum the packed rows over the bands first, then unpack once per layer:
        # one transpose
        # or concatenation per parameter instead of one per band.
        for p, parts in contributions.items():
            (first, count, mode), d = parts[0], self.shapes[parts[0][0]][1]
            summed = (
                first
                if len(parts) == 1
                else self.add_many([v for v, _, _ in parts])
            )
            if mode == "rows":
                part = summed
            elif mode == "transposed":
                part = self.transpose(summed)
            else:
                b = self.shapes[p][1]
                slices = [
                    self.view(summed, [r, r + 1, 0, min(d, b - r * d)])
                    for r in range(count)
                ]
                part = (
                    slices[0]
                    if len(slices) == 1
                    else self.concat_columns(slices)
                )
            acc(p, part)

    def _lstm_backward(self, node, parts, acc):
        """Gradient of an `lstm` node from its gradient pieces.

        Only h_out (all rows) and the final state (h_state, c_state of
