        the last step processed) normally receive gradients; anything
        else is placed into a full-width gradient first. lstm_backward
        returns, per step row, the gate gradients (4h) and the carry it
        passed to the previous step (2h), then a last block of rows [dh0
        | dc0 | zeros].
        """
        xp, whh_t, bias_ih, bias_hh, mask, h0, c0 = node.inputs
        batch, hidden, reverse = (
            node.attrs["batch"],
            node.attrs["hidden"],
            node.attrs["reverse"],
        )
        rows = self.shapes[xp][0]
        final = 0 if reverse else rows - batch
        out, state, other = [], [], []
        for g, (r, c) in parts:
            gr, gc = self.shapes[g]
            if c >= 2 * hidden and c + gc <= 3 * hidden:
                out.append((g, [r, c - 2 * hidden]))
            elif (
                r >= final and r + gr <= final + batch and c + gc <= 2 * hidden
            ):
                state.append((g, [r - final, c]))
            else:
                other.append((g, [r, c]))
        inputs, flags = [node.id], {}
        if other:
            # Uncommon: gradient into gate columns or earlier states. Fold
            # everything into
            # one full-width gradient and let the kernel read every part of it.
            inputs.append(self.place(self.shapes[node.id], parts))
            flags["full"] = True
        else:
            if out:
                inputs.append(
                    out[0][0]
                    if len(out) == 1
                    and out[0][1] == [0, 0]
                    and self.shapes[out[0][0]] == (rows, hidden)
                    else self.place((rows, hidden), out)
                )
                flags["has_out"] = True
            if state:
                inputs.append(self.place((batch, 2 * hidden), state))
                flags["has_state"] = True
        whh = self.transpose(whh_t)
        inputs += [whh, mask, h0, c0]
        full = self._op(
            "lstm_backward",
            inputs,
            (rows + batch, 6 * hidden),
            dict(node.attrs, **flags),
        )
        gates = self.view(full, [0, rows, 0, 4 * hidden])
        acc(xp, gates)
        bias = self._rows(gates)
        acc(bias_ih, bias)
        acc(bias_hh, bias)
        # Each step's recurrent input is the previous step's h_state (h0 for
        # the first).
        steps = rows // batch
        if steps == 1:
            previous = h0
        elif reverse:
            previous = self.concat_rows(
                [self.view(node.id, [batch, rows, 0, hidden]), h0]
            )
        else:
            previous = self.concat_rows(
                [h0, self.view(node.id, [0, rows - batch, 0, hidden])]
            )
        acc(whh_t, self.matmul(self.transpose(previous), gates))
        acc(h0, self.view(full, [rows, rows + batch, 0, hidden]))
        acc(c0, self.view(full, [rows, rows + batch, hidden, 2 * hidden]))


# File: packages/core/src/ygaraty/core/compiler/tasks.py
"""Task and input-region data contracts shared by compiler passes."""

from dataclasses import asdict, dataclass, field


@dataclass
class Input:
    value: str
    # [row_start, row_stop, column_start, column_stop], or the full tensor.
    region: list[int] | None = None


@dataclass
class Task:
    id: str
    op: str
    inputs: list[Input]
    shape: tuple[int, int]
    attrs: dict = field(default_factory=dict)

    @property
    def dependencies(self):
        return set(x.value for x in self.inputs)

    def arrays(self, store):
        result = []
        for spec in self.inputs:
            a = store[spec.value]
            if spec.region:
                i, ei, j, ej = spec.region
                a = a[i:ei, j:ej]
            result.append(a)
        return result

    def describe(self):
        return asdict(self)


# File: packages/core/src/ygaraty/core/inference/__init__.py
"""Public inference support for Tape forward plans."""


# File: packages/core/src/ygaraty/core/inference/forward.py
"""Immutable admission boundary for public, forward-only Tape computations."""

import json
from dataclasses import asdict, dataclass
from typing import Any

from ..compiler.plan import Input, Task
from ..compiler.spec import TapeSpecBuilder
from ..compiler.tape import Tape


def select_tasks(tasks, outputs):
    """Select a forward dependency closure, preserving execution order."""
    needed = set(outputs)
    for task in reversed(tasks):
        if task.id in needed:
            needed.update(task.dependencies)
    return [task for task in tasks if task.id in needed], needed


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True, init=False)
class ForwardPlan:
    """Validated JSON snapshot; exported dictionaries are independent copies.

    The local target uses F64 rank-two tensors and the bounded public Tape
    operation set. It does not imply a generic WebGPU lowering.
    """

    _payload: str

    def __new__(cls):
        raise TypeError("Use Tape.forward() or ForwardPlan.from_dict()")

    @classmethod
    def from_tape(
        cls, tape: Tape, *, outputs: dict[str, str]
    ) -> "ForwardPlan":
        if not isinstance(tape, Tape) or tape.loss is not None:
            raise ValueError("Expected a forward Tape before backward()")
        if tape.owned_layers or tape.owned_vocab:
            raise ValueError("Owned training operations are not forward plans")
        if any(not hasattr(value, "tolist") for value in tape.values.values()):
            raise ValueError("Forward inputs need explicit numeric values")
        return cls.from_dict(
            {
                "version": 1,
                "target": "local",
                "graph": {
                    "inputs": {k: v.tolist() for k, v in tape.values.items()},
                    "parameters": list(tape.parameters),
                    "nodes": [asdict(node) for node in tape.nodes],
                    "outputs": outputs,
                },
            }
        )

    @classmethod
    def from_dict(cls, value: dict[str, Any]) -> "ForwardPlan":
        if (
            not isinstance(value, dict)
            or set(value) != {"version", "target", "graph"}
            or type(value["version"]) is not int
            or value["version"] != 1
            or value["target"] != "local"
        ):
            raise ValueError("Unsupported forward plan")
        # Serialization severs caller aliases, rejects nonfinite JSON values,
        # and normalizes shape tuples before validation.
        payload = json.dumps(value, allow_nan=False, sort_keys=True)
        snapshot = json.loads(payload)
        tape = TapeSpecBuilder().build_forward(snapshot["graph"])
        if sum(r * c for r, c in tape.shapes.values()) * 8 > 64 << 20:
            raise ValueError("Forward plan exceeds the 64 MiB tensor budget")
        plan = object.__new__(cls)
        object.__setattr__(plan, "_payload", payload)
        return plan

    def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """Return an independent, serializable copy of the admitted graph."""
        return json.loads(self._payload)

    def run(self, *, inputs: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """Evaluate outputs using the captured parameters."""
        import numpy as np

        from ..runtime.local import execute_plan

        spec = self.to_dict()["graph"]
        tasks = [
            Task(
                n["id"],
                n["op"],
                [Input(i) for i in n["inputs"]],
                tuple(n["shape"]),
                n["attrs"],
            )
            for n in spec["nodes"]
        ]
        tasks, needed = select_tasks(tasks, spec["outputs"].values())
        parameters = set(spec["parameters"])
        required = (needed & spec["inputs"].keys()) - parameters
        if not isinstance(inputs, dict) or set(inputs) != required:
            raise ValueError(f"Expected forward inputs: {sorted(required)}")
        values = {}
        for name in needed & spec["inputs"].keys():
            default = np.asarray(spec["inputs"][name], dtype=np.float64)
            value = np.asarray(
                default if name in parameters else inputs[name],
                dtype=np.float64,
            )
            if value.shape != default.shape or not np.isfinite(value).all():
                raise ValueError(f"Invalid forward input: {name}")
            values[name] = value.copy()
        result = execute_plan(tasks, values, set(spec["outputs"].values()))
        if any(not np.isfinite(value).all() for value in result.values()):
            raise ValueError("Forward execution produced nonfinite values")
        return {
            name: result[tensor].copy()
            for name, tensor in spec["outputs"].items()
        }


# File: packages/core/src/ygaraty/core/models/__init__.py
"""Public models support for Tape forward plans."""


# File: packages/core/src/ygaraty/core/models/factorization_sharded.py
"""Two-dimensional task-distributed SGD factorization: X_ij, U_i and V_j are
all a task ever reads.

X (rows x columns) is cut into tiles by `block_rows` and `block_columns`; U
(rows x rank) into row
shards that line up with X's tile rows and V (columns x rank) into shards that
line up with X's tile
columns. Every shard is a leaf of its own (`X/i/j`, `U/i`, `V/j`), so no task,
worker or tensor ever
names a complete factor:

* `tile/i/j` (`factorization_tile`) reads X/i/j, U/i and V/j, forms the
residual locally and returns
  its share of dU_i, dV_j and the loss, packed. The dense residual never leaves the worker.
* dU_i is the sum over j of the tiles' U bands and dV_j the sum over i of their
V bands: bounded
  `add_many` trees (fan-in `fan_in`; `bounded_reductions` also bounds by the task budget).
* L2 regularization is added once per shard, to the shard's own gradient.
* `update/U/i` and `update/V/j` are SGD steps on one shard with that shard's
gradient.
* The loss is a bounded sum of the tiles' scalars plus the shards' squared
norms.

An update reads U and V only through the leaves named in `plan.updates`' keys;
the update tasks write
new immutable versions, which a job installs as the leaves for the next update
after the last task
of this one. So every tile of an update sees the same factor snapshot, whatever
order the scheduler
runs the tasks in. The graph has no `assemble` task: a full U or V exists only
if a diagnostic joins
the shards (`join_shards`), never in the graph or on a worker.

Communication is what capacity costs. A tile reads b_r*rank + b_c*rank +
b_r*b_c elements and
returns (b_r + b_c + 1)*rank, so one update moves about (columns/b_c)*|U| + (rows/b_r)*|V| bytes of
partial gradients on top of the factor and X reads; smaller tiles fit smaller
workers and move more.

Graph construction is separate from execution and holds no arrays.
`initializers` and `shard_values`
generate the seeded problem (a low-rank X plus noise, small random factors)
shard by shard, so any
shard layout sees the same values and no process needs a whole matrix.
"""  # noqa: E501 -- preserve documentation layout

import hashlib
import math
from functools import lru_cache
from numbers import Real
from types import SimpleNamespace

import numpy as np

from ygaraty.core.compiler.plan import (
    Input,
    Task,
    admit_plan,
    bounded_reductions,
)

MASK64 = (1 << 64) - 1


def shard_bounds(total, block):
    """[(start, stop), ...]: consecutive shards of `block` entries, the last
    one possibly shorter."""
    return [
        (start, min(total, start + block)) for start in range(0, total, block)
    ]


def x_leaf(i, j):
    return f"X/{i}/{j}"


def xl_leaf(i):
    return f"XL/{i}"


def xr_leaf(j):
    return f"XR/{j}"


def u_leaf(i):
    return f"U/{i}"


def v_leaf(j):
    return f"V/{j}"


# Seeded problem, independent of the shard layout
# -------------------------------------------------


def _row_generator(seed, name, row):
    key = f"{seed}:{name}:{row}".encode()
    return np.random.default_rng(
        int.from_bytes(hashlib.sha256(key).digest()[:16], "little")
    )


def factor_rows(seed, name, start, stop, width, scale):
    """Rows start..stop of a normal(0, scale) matrix of `width` columns; row r
    depends only on (seed, name, r)."""
    return np.stack(
        [
            _row_generator(seed, name, row).normal(0.0, scale, width)
            for row in range(start, stop)
        ]
    )


@lru_cache(maxsize=16)
def _latent(seed, name, count, width):
    """The whole latent factor of X (count x width, small): shards slice it.
    Cached per process."""
    return factor_rows(seed, name, 0, count, width, 1.0)


def noise_words(seed, rows, cols, columns):
    """hashed_noise's 64-bit splitmix64 word for entries (rows, cols) of a
    matrix `columns` wide
    (uint64 arrays that broadcast). WebGPU workers compute the same words
    (experiment 48)."""
    salt = np.uint64((seed * 0x9E3779B97F4A7C15) & MASK64)
    with np.errstate(over="ignore"):
        z = (
            np.asarray(rows, dtype=np.uint64) * np.uint64(columns)
            + np.asarray(cols, dtype=np.uint64)
            + salt
        )
        z = (z ^ (z >> np.uint64(30))) * np.uint64(0xBF58476D1CE4E5B9)
        z = (z ^ (z >> np.uint64(27))) * np.uint64(0x94D049BB133111EB)
        return z ^ (z >> np.uint64(31))


def hashed_noise(
    seed, row_start, row_stop, column_start, column_stop, columns
):
    """Zero-mean, unit-variance noise whose (r, c) entry depends only on (seed,
    r, c, columns).

    A splitmix64 hash of the entry's index feeds two 26-bit uniforms, summed
    (triangular), so a tile
    is generated without generating the rows or columns around it."""
    z = noise_words(
        seed,
        np.arange(row_start, row_stop, dtype=np.uint64)[:, None],
        np.arange(column_start, column_stop, dtype=np.uint64)[None, :],
        columns,
    )
    first = (z >> np.uint64(38)).astype(np.float64) / (1 << 26)
    second = ((z >> np.uint64(12)) & np.uint64((1 << 26) - 1)).astype(
        np.float64
    ) / (1 << 26)
    return (first + second - 1.0) * math.sqrt(6.0)


def x_values(spec, region=None):
    """X (or a region of it, [i, ei, j, ej] relative to the spec's tile): a
    low-rank product plus noise."""
    shape = spec["shape"]
    i, ei, j, ej = region or [0, shape[0], 0, shape[1]]
    row0, col0 = spec["row_offset"] + i, spec["column_offset"] + j
    row1, col1 = spec["row_offset"] + ei, spec["column_offset"] + ej
    latent = spec["latent"]
    left = _latent(spec["seed"], "X-left", spec["rows"], latent)[row0:row1]
    right = _latent(spec["seed"], "X-right", spec["columns"], latent)[
        col0:col1
    ]
    return x_from_latent(spec, left, right, row0, col0)


def x_from_latent(spec, left, right, row0=None, col0=None):
    """X entries from their rows of the two latent factors (left: rows x
    latent, right: columns x
    latent), the tile's own by default. A worker given those rows
    (`latent_inputs`) builds the
    same values as x_values without generating the whole latent factor."""
    row0 = spec["row_offset"] if row0 is None else row0
    col0 = spec["column_offset"] if col0 is None else col0
    latent = spec["latent"]
    if left.shape[1] != latent or right.shape[1] != latent:
        raise ValueError("latent rows must have x_spec latent columns")
    # A fixed-order sum over the latent dimension (not a BLAS product, whose
    # rounding depends on the
    # slice's shape): each entry is the same floating-point expression in every
    # shard layout.
    values = left[:, :1] * right[:, 0][None, :]
    for k in range(1, latent):
        values += left[:, k : k + 1] * right[:, k][None, :]
    values /= math.sqrt(latent)
    if spec["noise"]:
        values = values + spec["noise"] * hashed_noise(
            spec["seed"],
            row0,
            row0 + left.shape[0],
            col0,
            col0 + right.shape[0],
            spec["columns"],
        )
    return values


def shard_values(spec, region=None):
    """The values of a leaf described by `initializers`' spec (or a region of
    it, relative to the leaf)."""
    shape = spec["shape"]
    i, ei, j, ej = region or [0, shape[0], 0, shape[1]]
    if spec["kind"] == "factor_rows":
        return factor_rows(
            spec["seed"],
            spec["factor"],
            spec["row_offset"] + i,
            spec["row_offset"] + ei,
            shape[1],
            spec["scale"],
        )[:, j:ej]
    if spec["kind"] == "lowrank_x":
        return x_values(spec, [i, ei, j, ej])
    raise ValueError(f"Unsupported factorization initializer: {spec['kind']}")


def initializers(
    rows,
    columns,
    rank,
    block_rows,
    block_columns,
    *,
    seed=2026,
    scale=0.1,
    latent=8,
    noise=0.05,
):
    """Leaf name -> initializer spec (data only, a few hundred bytes per leaf)
    for every shard and tile."""
    specs = {}
    row_shards, column_shards = (
        shard_bounds(rows, block_rows),
        shard_bounds(columns, block_columns),
    )
    for i, (start, stop) in enumerate(row_shards):
        specs[u_leaf(i)] = dict(
            kind="factor_rows",
            factor="U",
            shape=[stop - start, rank],
            row_offset=start,
            scale=scale,
            seed=seed,
        )
        for j, (cstart, cstop) in enumerate(column_shards):
            specs[x_leaf(i, j)] = dict(
                kind="lowrank_x",
                shape=[stop - start, cstop - cstart],
                row_offset=start,
                column_offset=cstart,
                rows=rows,
                columns=columns,
                latent=latent,
                noise=noise,
                seed=seed,
            )
    for j, (start, stop) in enumerate(column_shards):
        specs[v_leaf(j)] = dict(
            kind="factor_rows",
            factor="V",
            shape=[stop - start, rank],
            row_offset=start,
            scale=scale,
            seed=seed,
        )
    return specs


def join_shards(leaves, names):
    """A diagnostic's whole matrix from its shards, in order. Not part of any
    plan or worker."""
    return np.vstack([leaves[name] for name in names])


# The plan
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------  # noqa: E501 -- preserve literal/report layout


def _joins(count, fan_in):
    total = 0
    while count > fan_in:
        count = -(-count // fan_in)
        total += count
    return total


class ShardedFactorizationPlan:
    """One plan assembly: validation, declarations, tasks, reductions and
    admission."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        rows,
        columns,
        rank,
        *,
        block_rows=256,
        block_columns=256,
        learning_rate=0.01,
        regularization=0.001,
        max_task_bytes=512 << 20,
        max_plan_tasks=100000,
        fan_in=8,
        seed=2026,
        scale=0.1,
        latent=8,
        noise=0.05,
        generated_x=False,
        latent_inputs=False,
        group=None,
        max_result_bytes=12 << 20,
        wire_bytes=8,
    ):
        self.rows = rows
        self.columns = columns
        self.rank = rank
        self.block_rows = block_rows
        self.block_columns = block_columns
        self.learning_rate = learning_rate
        self.regularization = regularization
        self.max_task_bytes = max_task_bytes
        self.max_plan_tasks = max_plan_tasks
        self.fan_in = fan_in
        self.seed = seed
        self.scale = scale
        self.latent = latent
        self.noise = noise
        self.generated_x = generated_x
        self.latent_inputs = latent_inputs
        self.group = group
        self.max_result_bytes = max_result_bytes
        self.wire_bytes = wire_bytes

    def build(self):
        self._validate()
        self._declare()
        self._tiles()
        self._updates()
        self._loss()
        self._admit()
        return self._result()

    def _validate(self):
        """Validate options and count the worst-case graph before allocating
        tasks."""
        for name, value in (
            ("rows", self.rows),
            ("columns", self.columns),
            ("rank", self.rank),
            ("block_rows", self.block_rows),
            ("block_columns", self.block_columns),
            ("max_task_bytes", self.max_task_bytes),
            ("max_plan_tasks", self.max_plan_tasks),
            ("fan_in", self.fan_in),
            ("latent", self.latent),
        ):
            if type(value) is not int or value < 1:
                raise ValueError(f"{name} must be a positive integer")
        if self.wire_bytes not in (4, 8):
            raise ValueError("wire_bytes must be 4 or 8")
        if self.fan_in < 2:
            raise ValueError("fan_in must be at least 2")
        if self.latent_inputs and (not self.generated_x):
            raise ValueError("latent_inputs needs generated_x")
        if self.group is not None:
            self.group = (
                (self.group, self.group)
                if type(self.group) is int
                else tuple(self.group)
            )
            if (
                not self.generated_x
                or len(self.group) != 2
                or (not all(type(g) is int and g >= 1 for g in self.group))
            ):
                raise ValueError(
                    "group must be a positive integer or pair, with "
                    "generated_x"
                )
        if type(self.seed) is not int or self.seed < 0:
            raise ValueError("seed must be a nonnegative integer")
        for name, value, allow_zero in (
            ("learning_rate", self.learning_rate, False),
            ("regularization", self.regularization, True),
            ("scale", self.scale, False),
            ("noise", self.noise, True),
        ):
            if (
                isinstance(value, bool)
                or not isinstance(value, Real)
                or (not math.isfinite(value))
                or (value < 0)
                or (not allow_zero and value == 0)
            ):
                raise ValueError(
                    f"{name} must be finite and "
                    f"{('nonnegative' if allow_zero else 'positive')}"
                )
        self.learning_rate, self.regularization, self.scale, self.noise = (
            float(self.learning_rate),
            float(self.regularization),
            float(self.scale),
            float(self.noise),
        )
        self.nr, self.nc = (
            -(-self.rows // self.block_rows),
            -(-self.columns // self.block_columns),
        )
        gr, gc = (
            (-(-self.nr // self.group[0]), -(-self.nc // self.group[1]))
            if self.group
            else (self.nr, self.nc)
        )
        estimated = (
            gr * gc
            + (self.nr + self.nc) * (2 + (3 if self.regularization else 0))
            + self.nr * _joins(gc, self.fan_in)
            + self.nc * _joins(gr, self.fan_in)
            + 2
            + _joins(gr * gc, self.fan_in)
            + (self.nr + self.nc + 4) * bool(self.regularization)
            + _joins(self.nr + self.nc, self.fan_in)
        )
        if estimated > self.max_plan_tasks:
            raise ValueError(
                "estimated graph exceeds max_plan_tasks; increase "
                "block_rows/block_columns or reduce the matrix"
            )

    def _declare(self):
        """Describe input leaves and initialize fresh per-build assembly
        state."""
        self.row_shards, self.column_shards = (
            shard_bounds(self.rows, self.block_rows),
            shard_bounds(self.columns, self.block_columns),
        )
        self.shapes = {}
        self.specs = initializers(
            self.rows,
            self.columns,
            self.rank,
            self.block_rows,
            self.block_columns,
            seed=self.seed,
            scale=self.scale,
            latent=self.latent,
            noise=self.noise,
        )
        if self.latent_inputs:
            for i, (start, stop) in enumerate(self.row_shards):
                self.specs[xl_leaf(i)] = dict(
                    kind="factor_rows",
                    factor="X-left",
                    shape=[stop - start, self.latent],
                    row_offset=start,
                    scale=1.0,
                    seed=self.seed,
                )
            for j, (start, stop) in enumerate(self.column_shards):
                self.specs[xr_leaf(j)] = dict(
                    kind="factor_rows",
                    factor="X-right",
                    shape=[stop - start, self.latent],
                    row_offset=start,
                    scale=1.0,
                    seed=self.seed,
                )
        for name, spec in self.specs.items():
            if not (self.generated_x and name.startswith("X/")):
                self.shapes[name] = tuple(spec["shape"])
        self.tasks, self.losses, self.norms = ([], [], [])
        self.template = dict(
            kind="lowrank_x",
            rows=self.rows,
            columns=self.columns,
            latent=self.latent,
            noise=self.noise,
            seed=self.seed,
        )
        self.p, self.q = self.group or (1, 1)
        self.tile_parts = {}

    def _tiles(self):
        """Visit tile groups in deterministic row-major order."""
        for gi in range(0, self.nr, self.p):
            row_block = list(range(gi, min(gi + self.p, self.nr)))
            for gj in range(0, self.nc, self.q):
                column_block = list(range(gj, min(gj + self.q, self.nc)))
                self._tile_group(row_block, column_block, gi, gj)

    def _tile_group(self, row_block, column_block, gi, gj):
        sizes = [
            self.row_shards[i][1] - self.row_shards[i][0] for i in row_block
        ] + [
            self.column_shards[j][1] - self.column_shards[j][0]
            for j in column_block
        ]
        extra_work = self._tile_work(row_block, column_block)
        attrs = dict(
            total_elements=self.rows * self.columns,
            extra_work=extra_work,
            upload_rows=sizes + [1],
        )
        if self.group is None:
            i, j = (row_block[0], column_block[0])
            ident, op = (f"tile/{i}/{j}", "factorization_tile")
            inputs = [Input(u_leaf(i)), Input(v_leaf(j))]
            if self.latent_inputs:
                inputs = [Input(xl_leaf(i)), Input(xr_leaf(j)), *inputs]
            if self.generated_x:
                attrs["x_spec"] = self.specs[x_leaf(i, j)]
            else:
                inputs.insert(0, Input(x_leaf(i, j)))
        else:
            ident, op = (
                f"tiles/{gi // self.p}/{gj // self.q}",
                "factorization_tiles",
            )
            inputs = [Input(u_leaf(i)) for i in row_block] + [
                Input(v_leaf(j)) for j in column_block
            ]
            if self.latent_inputs:
                inputs = (
                    [Input(xl_leaf(i)) for i in row_block]
                    + [Input(xr_leaf(j)) for j in column_block]
                    + inputs
                )
            attrs.update(
                x_spec=self.template,
                row_shards=[list(self.row_shards[i]) for i in row_block],
                column_shards=[
                    list(self.column_shards[j]) for j in column_block
                ],
            )
        total_rows = sum(sizes)
        if (
            self.group is not None
            and (total_rows + 1) * self.rank * self.wire_bytes
            > self.max_result_bytes
        ):
            raise MemoryError(
                f"Task {ident} returns "
                f"{(total_rows + 1) * self.rank * self.wire_bytes} "
                f"bytes; at most {self.max_result_bytes}. Reduce the "
                f"group or the tile."
            )
        self.tasks.append(
            Task(ident, op, inputs, (total_rows + 1, self.rank), attrs)
        )
        self.losses.append(Input(ident, [total_rows, total_rows + 1, 0, 1]))
        self._record_bands(ident, row_block, column_block)

    def _tile_work(self, row_block, column_block):
        """Conservative decomposed costs and generated-X scratch-space
        guard."""
        extra_work = []
        for i in row_block:
            count = self.row_shards[i][1] - self.row_shards[i][0]
            for j in column_block:
                width = self.column_shards[j][1] - self.column_shards[j][0]
                extra_work += [("matmul", count * width * self.rank)] * 3
                extra_work += [
                    ("scale", 2 * count * width),
                    ("add", 3 * count * width),
                    ("scale", 2 * count * self.rank),
                    ("scale", 2 * width * self.rank),
                    ("mul", 3 * count * width),
                    ("sum", count * width + 1),
                    ("scale", 2),
                ]
                if self.generated_x:
                    extra_work.append(("scale", count * width))
                    generated = 4 * count * width * 8
                    if generated > self.max_task_bytes:
                        raise MemoryError(
                            f"Task tile/{i}/{j} generates an X tile of "
                            f"{count * width * 8} bytes; its estimate "
                            f"exceeds the budget {self.max_task_bytes}."
                        )
        return extra_work

    def _record_bands(self, ident, row_block, column_block):
        offset = 0
        for i in row_block:
            for j in column_block:
                self.tile_parts[i, j] = (ident, offset, None)
            offset += self.row_shards[i][1] - self.row_shards[i][0]
        for j in column_block:
            for i in row_block:
                self.tile_parts[i, j] = (*self.tile_parts[i, j][:2], offset)
            offset += self.column_shards[j][1] - self.column_shards[j][0]

    def _updates(self):
        """Assemble or reduce gradients and apply regularization once per
        factor element."""
        self.gradients, self.updates = ({}, {})
        for factor, shards, leaf, band, label in (
            (
                "U",
                self.row_shards,
                u_leaf,
                lambda i, j, a, b: [0, a, 0, self.rank],
                "row",
            ),
            ("V", self.column_shards, v_leaf, None, "column"),
        ):
            for index, (start, stop) in enumerate(shards):
                size = stop - start
                if factor == "U":
                    holders = dict.fromkeys(
                        (
                            self.tile_parts[index, j][0],
                            self.tile_parts[index, j][1],
                        )
                        for j in range(self.nc)
                    )
                else:
                    holders = dict.fromkeys(
                        (
                            self.tile_parts[i, index][0],
                            self.tile_parts[i, index][2],
                        )
                        for i in range(self.nr)
                    )
                parts = [
                    Input(ident, [row, row + size, 0, self.rank])
                    for ident, row in holders
                ]
                data = (
                    f"gradient/{factor}/data/{index}"
                    if self.regularization
                    else f"gradient/{factor}/{index}"
                )
                self.tasks.append(
                    Task(data, "add_many", parts, (size, self.rank))
                )
                gradient = Input(data)
                if self.regularization:
                    self.tasks.append(
                        Task(
                            f"gradient/{factor}/regularization/{index}",
                            "scale",
                            [Input(leaf(index))],
                            (size, self.rank),
                            {"factor": self.regularization},
                        )
                    )
                    self.tasks.append(
                        Task(
                            f"gradient/{factor}/{index}",
                            "add",
                            [
                                gradient,
                                Input(
                                    f"gradient/{factor}/regularization/{index}"
                                ),
                            ],
                            (size, self.rank),
                        )
                    )
                    gradient = Input(f"gradient/{factor}/{index}")
                    self.tasks.append(
                        Task(
                            f"factor_norm/{factor}/{index}",
                            "sum_squares",
                            [Input(leaf(index))],
                            (1, 1),
                        )
                    )
                    self.norms.append(Input(f"factor_norm/{factor}/{index}"))
                self.tasks.append(
                    Task(
                        f"update/{factor}/{index}",
                        "sgd",
                        [Input(leaf(index)), gradient],
                        (size, self.rank),
                        {"learning_rate": self.learning_rate},
                    )
                )
                self.gradients[leaf(index)] = gradient.value
                self.updates[leaf(index)] = f"update/{factor}/{index}"

    def _loss(self):
        """Combine data loss and factor norms without changing reduction
        order."""
        self.tasks.append(
            Task(
                "data_loss" if self.regularization else "loss",
                "add_many",
                self.losses,
                (1, 1),
            )
        )
        if self.regularization:
            self.tasks.append(
                Task("factor_norm", "add_many", self.norms, (1, 1))
            )
            self.tasks.append(
                Task(
                    "regularization_loss",
                    "scale",
                    [Input("factor_norm")],
                    (1, 1),
                    {"factor": self.regularization / 2},
                )
            )
            self.tasks.append(
                Task(
                    "loss",
                    "add",
                    [Input("data_loss"), Input("regularization_loss")],
                    (1, 1),
                )
            )

    def _admit(self):
        """Bound reductions and enforce task-count and memory limits."""
        self.tasks = bounded_reductions(
            self.tasks, fan_in=self.fan_in, budget=self.max_task_bytes
        )
        if len(self.tasks) > self.max_plan_tasks:
            raise ValueError("compiled graph exceeds max_plan_tasks")
        self.admitted = admit_plan(
            self.tasks,
            self.shapes,
            self.max_task_bytes,
            output_budget=self.max_task_bytes,
        )
        if self.generated_x:

            def largest_tile(t):
                if t.op == "factorization_tile":
                    return math.prod(t.attrs["x_spec"]["shape"])
                return max(
                    (r[1] - r[0]) * (c[1] - c[0])
                    for r in t.attrs["row_shards"]
                    for c in t.attrs["column_shards"]
                )

            self.admitted = max(
                self.admitted,
                max(
                    4
                    * (
                        sum(math.prod(self.shapes[x.value]) for x in t.inputs)
                        + math.prod(t.shape)
                        + largest_tile(t)
                    )
                    * 8
                    for t in self.tasks
                    if t.op in ("factorization_tile", "factorization_tiles")
                ),
            )
            if self.admitted > self.max_task_bytes:
                raise MemoryError(
                    f"a generated-X tile needs an estimated "
                    f"{self.admitted} bytes; budget is "
                    f"{self.max_task_bytes}. Reduce the tile."
                )

    def _result(self):
        """Publish the existing plan metadata contract."""
        version = f"synthetic-factorization-X-v1:{self.seed}:{self.rows}x{self.columns}:{self.latent}:{self.noise}"  # noqa: E501 -- preserve literal/report layout
        persistent = (
            {}
            if self.generated_x
            else {
                x_leaf(i, j): f"{version}:{r[0]}-{r[1]}:{c[0]}-{c[1]}"
                for i, r in enumerate(self.row_shards)
                for j, c in enumerate(self.column_shards)
            }
        )
        return SimpleNamespace(
            tasks=self.tasks,
            leaves={
                name: dict(shape=list(shape))
                for name, shape in self.shapes.items()
            },
            owner_keys=[],
            owned=False,
            reusable=set(self.shapes),
            persistent_inputs=persistent,
            updates=self.updates,
            gradients=self.gradients,
            loss="loss",
            admitted_task_bytes=self.admitted,
            shards=dict(rows=self.row_shards, columns=self.column_shards),
            initializers={
                n: v for n, v in self.specs.items() if self.shapes.get(n)
            },
            model=dict(
                kind="dense_sgd_factorization",
                implementation="tiles_2d",
                rows=self.rows,
                columns=self.columns,
                rank=self.rank,
                block_rows=self.block_rows,
                block_columns=self.block_columns,
                learning_rate=self.learning_rate,
                regularization=self.regularization,
                fan_in=self.fan_in,
                seed=self.seed,
                latent=self.latent,
                noise=self.noise,
                scale=self.scale,
                generated_x=self.generated_x,
                latent_inputs=self.latent_inputs,
                group=list(self.group) if self.group else None,
            ),
        )


def build_sharded_plan(
    rows,
    columns,
    rank,
    *,
    block_rows=256,
    block_columns=256,
    learning_rate=0.01,
    regularization=0.001,
    max_task_bytes=512 << 20,
    max_plan_tasks=100000,
    fan_in=8,
    seed=2026,
    scale=0.1,
    latent=8,
    noise=0.05,
    generated_x=False,
    latent_inputs=False,
    group=None,
    max_result_bytes=12 << 20,
    wire_bytes=8,
):
    """A shape-only full-batch SGD update over 2D tiles. See the module
    docstring for the graph.

    `max_task_bytes` is the admission budget of every task (admit_plan: four
    times the bytes it
    reads and writes, and no result above 8 MiB); a tile or reduction that does
    not fit raises
    MemoryError, so a plan that builds fits the budget it was built for.
    `seed`, `scale`, `latent`
    and `noise` describe the seeded problem (`initializers`), not the graph.

    With `generated_x` no X leaf exists: a tile task reads only U_i and V_j and
    its worker generates the
    tile from the seeded spec in the task's attributes (`x_spec`), so X is
    never stored or sent, and a
    matrix larger than any machine's memory is a plan like any other. The
    tile's admission counts the
    generated block as an input.

    With `latent_inputs` (generated_x only) a tile also reads its rows of X's
    two latent factors
    (leaves XL/i and XR/j, latent columns each, never updated): workers that
    cannot run NumPy's
    generator (WebGPU browsers, experiment 48) build X from them and the hashed
    noise.

    With `group` = (p, q) (generated_x only) one task, `tiles/I/J` with op
    `factorization_tiles`,
    computes the p x q tiles of row shards I and column shards J one at a time
    and returns the
    summed gradient of each of its p + q shards: bytes follow the group's
    perimeter while compute
    follows its area, so a 5 x 5 group moves one fifth of the result bytes of
    its 25 tiles, at the
    memory of one tile. An int is a square group; the last groups along each
    side may be smaller. A
    group's result ((p + q) bands) may exceed the usual 8 MiB result cap, up to
    `max_result_bytes`
    (12 MiB: one result must fit a 16 MiB request body with its header) at
    `wire_bytes` per value
    (8; 4 on a float32 wire, which fits a group twice as long on a side).
    """
    return ShardedFactorizationPlan(
        rows,
        columns,
        rank,
        block_rows=block_rows,
        block_columns=block_columns,
        learning_rate=learning_rate,
        regularization=regularization,
        max_task_bytes=max_task_bytes,
        max_plan_tasks=max_plan_tasks,
        fan_in=fan_in,
        seed=seed,
        scale=scale,
        latent=latent,
        noise=noise,
        generated_x=generated_x,
        latent_inputs=latent_inputs,
        group=group,
        max_result_bytes=max_result_bytes,
        wire_bytes=wire_bytes,
    ).build()


# Locality
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------  # noqa: E501 -- preserve literal/report layout


def audit_locality(plan):
    """What every task reads, against the 2D contract. Returns a report;
    `violations` is empty when
    no task reads a complete factor (or a leaf outside its own shard indices)
    and the graph has no
    assembly of one. A factor with one shard is its own whole by construction
    and not a violation."""
    shapes = {name: tuple(leaf["shape"]) for name, leaf in plan.leaves.items()}
    shapes.update((task.id, tuple(task.shape)) for task in plan.tasks)
    rows, columns = plan.shards["rows"], plan.shards["columns"]
    violations, widest = [], {}
    for task in plan.tasks:
        if task.op in ("assemble", "transpose"):
            violations.append(
                f"{task.id}: {task.op} builds a whole value inside the graph"
            )
            continue
        read = 0
        for ref in task.inputs:
            dims = (
                (ref.region[1] - ref.region[0], ref.region[3] - ref.region[2])
                if ref.region
                else shapes.get(ref.value, (0, 0))
            )
            read += math.prod(dims) * 8
            if ref.value in ("U", "V") or (
                ref.value.split("/")[0] in ("U", "V")
                and ref.value.count("/") == 0
            ):
                violations.append(f"{task.id}: reads the whole {ref.value}")
        family = task.op if task.op != "add_many" else "add_many"
        widest[family] = max(widest.get(family, 0), read)
        if task.op == "factorization_tile":
            i, j = (int(part) for part in task.id.split("/")[1:])
            expected = (
                [u_leaf(i), v_leaf(j)]
                if "x_spec" in task.attrs
                else [x_leaf(i, j), u_leaf(i), v_leaf(j)]
            )
            if [ref.value for ref in task.inputs] != expected or any(
                ref.region for ref in task.inputs
            ):
                violations.append(
                    f"{task.id}: must read exactly " + ", ".join(expected)
                )
            elif "x_spec" in task.attrs and (
                task.attrs["x_spec"].get("row_offset"),
                task.attrs["x_spec"].get("column_offset"),
            ) != (rows[i][0], columns[j][0]):
                violations.append(
                    f"{task.id}: generates a tile that is not its own"
                )
        elif task.op == "factorization_tiles":
            # A group reads exactly the shards it lists (contiguous, its own),
            # their latent rows first if any.
            r_idx = [
                rows.index(tuple(b)) if tuple(b) in rows else None
                for b in task.attrs.get("row_shards", [])
            ]
            c_idx = [
                columns.index(tuple(b)) if tuple(b) in columns else None
                for b in task.attrs.get("column_shards", [])
            ]

            def contiguous(idx):
                return (
                    idx
                    and None not in idx
                    and idx == list(range(idx[0], idx[0] + len(idx)))
                )

            if not contiguous(r_idx) or not contiguous(c_idx):
                violations.append(
                    f"{task.id}: its shards are not a contiguous block "
                    f"of the plan's"
                )
            else:
                expected = [u_leaf(i) for i in r_idx] + [
                    v_leaf(j) for j in c_idx
                ]
                if any(ref.value.startswith("XL/") for ref in task.inputs):
                    expected = (
                        [xl_leaf(i) for i in r_idx]
                        + [xr_leaf(j) for j in c_idx]
                        + expected
                    )
                if [ref.value for ref in task.inputs] != expected or any(
                    ref.region for ref in task.inputs
                ):
                    violations.append(
                        f"{task.id}: must read exactly " + ", ".join(expected)
                    )
        elif task.op in ("sgd", "scale", "sum_squares") and task.inputs[
            0
        ].value[:2] in ("U/", "V/"):
            leaf = task.inputs[0].value
            if any(
                ref.value not in (leaf,)
                and not ref.value.endswith("/" + leaf.split("/", 1)[1])
                for ref in task.inputs[1:]
            ):
                violations.append(
                    f"{task.id}: a shard-local step reads another shard"
                )
    for leaf, shape in shapes.items():
        if leaf[:2] in ("U/", "V/"):
            index = int(leaf[2:])
            bounds = (rows if leaf[0] == "U" else columns)[index]
            if shape[0] != bounds[1] - bounds[0]:
                violations.append(f"{leaf}: shape does not match its shard")
    return dict(
        violations=violations,
        widest_read_bytes=widest,
        tasks=len(plan.tasks),
        tiles=len(rows) * len(columns),
        tile_tasks=sum(
            t.op in ("factorization_tile", "factorization_tiles")
            for t in plan.tasks
        ),
        row_shards=len(rows),
        column_shards=len(columns),
    )


# File: packages/core/src/ygaraty/core/runtime/__init__.py
"""Public runtime support for Tape forward plans."""


# File: packages/core/src/ygaraty/core/runtime/convolution_kernels.py
"""Float64 portable convolution kernels; validation and operation dispatch are
separate."""

from collections.abc import Mapping, Sequence

import numpy as np
from numpy.typing import NDArray

from ygaraty.core.compiler.convolution import OPS as OPS
from ygaraty.core.compiler.convolution import contract


def _convolve(arrays, dimensions, op):
    n, c, h, w, o, k = dimensions
    p = k // 2
    x = np.pad(arrays[0].reshape(n, c, h, w), ((0, 0), (0, 0), (p, p), (p, p)))
    weight = arrays[1].reshape(o, c, k, k)
    result = np.broadcast_to(
        arrays[2].reshape(1, o, 1, 1), (n, o, h, w)
    ).copy()
    for ky in range(k):
        for kx in range(k):
            result += np.einsum(
                "nchw,oc->nohw",
                x[:, :, ky : ky + h, kx : kx + w],
                weight[:, :, ky, kx],
            )
    return result


def _input_gradient(arrays, dimensions, op):
    n, c, h, w, o, k = dimensions
    p = k // 2
    dy = arrays[0].reshape(n, o, h, w)
    weight = arrays[1].reshape(o, c, k, k)
    dx = np.zeros((n, c, h + 2 * p, w + 2 * p))
    for ky in range(k):
        for kx in range(k):
            dx[:, :, ky : ky + h, kx : kx + w] += np.einsum(
                "nohw,oc->nchw",
                dy,
                weight[:, :, ky, kx],
            )
    result = dx[:, :, p : p + h, p : p + w]
    return result


def _weight_gradient(arrays, dimensions, op):
    n, c, h, w, o, k = dimensions
    p = k // 2
    x = np.pad(arrays[0].reshape(n, c, h, w), ((0, 0), (0, 0), (p, p), (p, p)))
    dy = arrays[1].reshape(n, o, h, w)
    result = np.empty((o, c, k, k))
    for ky in range(k):
        for kx in range(k):
            result[:, :, ky, kx] = np.einsum(
                "nchw,nohw->oc",
                x[:, :, ky : ky + h, kx : kx + w],
                dy,
            )
    return result


def _bias_gradient(arrays, dimensions, op):
    n, c, h, w, o, k = dimensions
    result = arrays[0].reshape(n, o, h, w).sum(axis=(0, 2, 3))
    return result


def _max_pool(arrays, dimensions, op):
    n, c, h, w, o, k = dimensions
    x = arrays[0].reshape(n, c, h, w)
    blocks = (
        x.reshape(n, c, h // 2, 2, w // 2, 2)
        .transpose(0, 1, 2, 4, 3, 5)
        .reshape(n, c, h // 2, w // 2, 4)
    )
    if op == "maxpool2d":
        result = blocks.max(axis=-1)
    else:
        winners = blocks.argmax(axis=-1)
        dy = arrays[1].reshape(n, c, h // 2, w // 2)
        result = np.zeros_like(x)
        for index in range(4):
            result[:, :, index // 2 :: 2, index % 2 :: 2] = dy * (
                winners == index
            )
    return result


def _average_pool(arrays, dimensions, op):
    n, c, h, w, o, k = dimensions
    result = arrays[0].reshape(n, c, h, w).mean(axis=(2, 3))
    return result


def _average_pool_gradient(arrays, dimensions, op):
    n, c, h, w, o, k = dimensions
    result = np.broadcast_to(
        arrays[0].reshape(n, c, 1, 1) / (h * w), (n, c, h, w)
    )
    return result


_OPERATIONS = {
    "conv2d": _convolve,
    "conv2d_input_grad": _input_gradient,
    "conv2d_weight_grad": _weight_gradient,
    "conv2d_bias_grad": _bias_gradient,
    "maxpool2d": _max_pool,
    "avgpool2d": _average_pool,
    "avgpool2d_grad": _average_pool_gradient,
    "maxpool2d_grad": _max_pool,
}


def execute(
    op: str, inputs: Sequence[NDArray], attrs: Mapping[str, object]
) -> NDArray[np.float64]:
    """Validate tensors, dispatch one operation, and return an owned finite
    result."""
    shapes, out, dimensions = contract(op, attrs)
    if len(inputs) != len(shapes):
        raise ValueError("Wrong convolution input count")
    arrays = []
    for value, shape in zip(inputs, shapes):
        a = np.asarray(value)
        if (
            a.shape != shape
            or a.dtype.kind not in "fiu"
            or (not np.isfinite(a).all())
        ):
            raise ValueError("Invalid convolution input")
        arrays.append(a.astype(np.float64, copy=False))
    result = _OPERATIONS[op](arrays, dimensions, op)
    result = np.asarray(result).reshape(out).copy()
    if not np.isfinite(result).all():
        raise ValueError("Nonfinite convolution output")
    return result


# File: packages/core/src/ygaraty/core/runtime/factorization_kernels.py
"""Numerical kernels for fully observed dense matrix factorization.

Two operations share one computation: ``factorization_block`` (a row shard of X
with the whole of V,
the fused plan of experiments/factorization-scaling) and ``factorization_tile``
(one tile X_ij with
only U_i and V_j, the two-dimensional plan of models/factorization_sharded.py).
They differ only in
what the attributes must satisfy.
"""

from collections.abc import Mapping

import numpy as np

OPS = {"factorization_block", "factorization_tile", "factorization_tiles"}


MAX_GENERATED_BYTES = 1 << 30


def _generated_x(spec):
    """One X tile from its seeded spec (models/factorization_sharded.py),
    bounded before it is allocated."""
    _generated_x_check(spec)
    from ygaraty.core.models.factorization_sharded import shard_values

    return shard_values(dict(spec))


def _generated_x_check(spec):
    shape = spec.get("shape")
    if (
        not isinstance(shape, (list, tuple))
        or len(shape) != 2
        or not all(type(n) is int and n > 0 for n in shape)
        or shape[0] * shape[1] * 8 > MAX_GENERATED_BYTES
        or spec.get("kind") != "lowrank_x"
    ):
        raise ValueError("x_spec must describe one bounded low-rank X tile")
    for name, limit in (
        ("rows", 1 << 21),
        ("columns", 1 << 21),
        ("latent", 64),
        ("row_offset", 1 << 21),
        ("column_offset", 1 << 21),
        ("seed", 1 << 62),
    ):
        if type(spec.get(name)) is not int or not 0 <= spec[name] <= limit:
            raise ValueError(f"x_spec {name} is out of range")


def _latent_x(spec, left, right):
    """One X tile from its latent rows, bounded and shape-checked like
    _generated_x."""
    _generated_x_check(spec)
    from ygaraty.core.models.factorization_sharded import x_from_latent

    left, right = (np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64) for a in (left, right))
    rows, columns = spec["shape"]
    if (
        left.shape != (rows, spec["latent"])
        or right.shape != (columns, spec["latent"])
        or not (np.isfinite(left).all() and np.isfinite(right).all())
    ):
        raise ValueError(
            "latent rows must be finite and match the x_spec tile"
        )
    return x_from_latent(dict(spec), left, right)


def _tile_group(inputs, attrs):
    """`factorization_tiles` (a plan's `group`): the p x q tiles of row shards
    `row_shards` and column
    shards `column_shards`, one at a time, their gradients summed per shard in
    a fixed order. The
    result packs one band per row shard, one per column shard, then the loss
    (the CPU kernel's
    layout for one tile, with p + q bands). Inputs: the latent rows of the
    shards (p + q, with
    latent_inputs) then U_i (p) and V_j (q)."""
    rows, columns, spec, count = (
        attrs.get("row_shards"),
        attrs.get("column_shards"),
        attrs.get("x_spec"),
        attrs.get("total_elements"),
    )

    def bounds(value):
        return (
            isinstance(value, (list, tuple))
            and 1 <= len(value) <= 64
            and all(
                isinstance(b, (list, tuple))
                and len(b) == 2
                and all(type(n) is int for n in b)
                and 0 <= b[0] < b[1]
                for b in value
            )
        )

    if (
        not bounds(rows)
        or not bounds(columns)
        or not isinstance(spec, Mapping)
        or type(count) is not int
    ):
        raise ValueError(
            "factorization_tiles needs row_shards, column_shards, an "
            "x_spec and total_elements"
        )
    p, q = len(rows), len(columns)
    if len(inputs) == 2 * (p + q):
        left, right, inputs = inputs[:p], inputs[p : p + q], inputs[p + q :]
    elif len(inputs) == p + q:
        left = right = None
    else:
        raise ValueError(
            "factorization_tiles needs one input per shard (U then "
            "V), with their latent rows first"
        )
    u, v = inputs[:p], inputs[p:]
    rank = np.asarray(u[0]).shape[1] if np.asarray(u[0]).ndim == 2 else 0
    sizes = [b[1] - b[0] for b in rows] + [b[1] - b[0] for b in columns]
    out = np.zeros((sum(sizes) + 1, rank), dtype=np.float64)
    row_offsets = np.cumsum([0] + sizes[:p]).tolist()
    column_offsets = (sum(sizes[:p]) + np.cumsum([0] + sizes[p:])).tolist()
    for a, (r0, r1) in enumerate(rows):
        for b, (c0, c1) in enumerate(columns):
            tile_spec = dict(
                spec, shape=[r1 - r0, c1 - c0], row_offset=r0, column_offset=c0
            )
            tile_inputs = (
                [u[a], v[b]]
                if left is None
                else [left[a], right[b], u[a], v[b]]
            )
            tile = execute(
                "factorization_tile",
                tile_inputs,
                dict(x_spec=tile_spec, total_elements=count),
            )
            size, width = r1 - r0, c1 - c0
            out[row_offsets[a] : row_offsets[a] + size] += tile[:size]
            out[column_offsets[b] : column_offsets[b] + width] += tile[
                size : size + width
            ]
            out[-1, 0] += tile[-1, 0]
    return out


def execute(op, inputs, attrs):
    """Return packed data gradients and loss for one immutable shard or tile.

    The result has rows + columns + 1 rows of `rank` columns: the gradient of
    the loss with respect
    to this block's U rows, then the gradient with respect to its V rows, then
    the block's share of
    the loss in the first column of the last row. All blocks in an update read
    the same U/V
    snapshot; the gradients are normalized by the whole matrix's element count
    (`total_elements`),
    so blocks add up to the full gradient. Regularization and optimizer updates
    belong to the
    surrounding graph, not this kernel.

    With `x_spec` in the attributes of a tile, X is not an input: it is
    generated from the spec, from
    inputs (U, V), or from (left, right, U, V) given the tile's rows of X's
    latent factors (a plan's
    `latent_inputs`, which WebGPU workers need); both give the same values.

    A block's total_elements must be a multiple of its width (the row shards'
    whole-matrix count
    always is); a tile's only has to cover the tile, since its width need not
    divide the matrix's.
    """
    if op not in OPS:
        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported factorization operation: {op}")
    if not isinstance(attrs, Mapping):
        raise ValueError(f"{op} requires attributes")
    if op == "factorization_tiles":
        return _tile_group(inputs, attrs)
    if (
        len(inputs) == 2
        and op == "factorization_tile"
        and isinstance(attrs.get("x_spec"), Mapping)
    ):
        inputs = [
            _generated_x(attrs["x_spec"]),
            *inputs,
        ]  # the worker builds its own X tile from the seeded spec
    elif (
        len(inputs) == 4
        and op == "factorization_tile"
        and isinstance(attrs.get("x_spec"), Mapping)
    ):
        inputs = [
            _latent_x(attrs["x_spec"], inputs[0], inputs[1]),
            *inputs[2:],
        ]
    elif len(inputs) != 3:
        raise ValueError(f"{op} requires X, U, V and attributes")
    arrays = []
    for name, value in zip(("X", "U", "V"), inputs):
        raw = np.asarray(value)
        if raw.dtype.kind not in "fiu" or raw.ndim != 2 or 0 in raw.shape:
            raise ValueError(f"{name} must be a nonempty real matrix")
        array = np.asarray(raw, dtype=np.float64)
        if not np.isfinite(array).all():
            raise ValueError(f"{name} must contain finite values")
        arrays.append(array)
    x, u, v = arrays
    rows, columns = x.shape
    if u.shape[0] != rows or v.shape != (columns, u.shape[1]):
        raise ValueError("incompatible factorization block shapes")
    count = attrs.get("total_elements")
    if (
        type(count) is not int
        or count < x.size
        or (op == "factorization_block" and count % columns)
    ):
        raise ValueError(
            "total_elements must be the positive whole-matrix element count"
        )
    # Keep the dense residual local. Only the two gradient bands and scalar
    # loss are uploaded; input arrays are never changed in place.
    with np.errstate(over="ignore", invalid="ignore"):
        residual = u @ v.T - x
        rank = u.shape[1]
        result = np.zeros((rows + columns + 1, rank), dtype=np.float64)
        result[:rows] = (residual @ v) / count
        result[rows : rows + columns] = (residual.T @ u) / count
        result[-1, 0] = np.sum(residual * residual) / (2 * count)
    if not np.isfinite(result).all():
        raise ValueError("Kernel produced invalid/nonfinite output")
    return result


# File: packages/core/src/ygaraty/core/runtime/kernels.py
"""Allowlisted numerical operations; no executable code arrives over the
wire."""

import numpy as np

from ygaraty.core.runtime import convolution_kernels as convolution
from ygaraty.core.runtime import factorization_kernels as factorization
from ygaraty.core.runtime import sequence_kernels as sequence
from ygaraty.core.runtime import transformer_kernels as transformer

SEQUENCE_OPS = {
    "view",
    "concat_rows",
    "place",
    "place_block",
    "lstm",
    "lstm_backward",
    "bqk",
    "bpv",
    "bptq",
    "lstm_cell",
    "lstm_cell_backward",
    "lstm_cell_carry",
    "lstm_cell_grad",
    "lstm_state_grad",
    "lstm_chain",
    "lstm_chain_grad",
}


def execute(op, inputs, attrs):
    a = inputs[0] if inputs else None
    if op in convolution.OPS:
        result = convolution.execute(op, inputs, attrs)
    elif op in factorization.OPS:
        result = factorization.execute(op, inputs, attrs)
    elif op in transformer.OPS:
        result = transformer.execute(op, inputs, attrs)
    elif op in SEQUENCE_OPS:
        result = sequence.execute(op, inputs, attrs)
    elif op == "matmul":
        result = a @ inputs[1]
    elif op in ("add", "add_bias"):
        result = a + inputs[1]
    elif op == "mul":
        result = a * inputs[1]
    elif op == "embedding":
        result = a[attrs["ids"]]
    elif op == "embedding_backward":
        a = (
            np.concatenate(inputs, axis=0) if len(inputs) > 1 else a
        )  # row parts, in order
        result = np.zeros(attrs["shape"])
        ids = np.asarray(attrs["ids"])
        valid = ids != attrs.get("padding_idx")
        rows = (
            a[valid] * attrs["factor"]
            if attrs.get("factor", 1.0) != 1.0
            else a[valid]
        )
        np.add.at(result, ids[valid], rows)
    elif op == "embed":
        # A token embedding, scaled, plus its position embedding (tape.embed).
        result = (
            a[attrs["ids"]] * attrs["factor"] + inputs[1][attrs["positions"]]
        )
    elif op == "embed_rows":
        # One tile of `embed`: n token rows then n position rows, each (1,
        # columns).
        n = attrs["rows"]
        result = np.concatenate(inputs[:n], axis=0) * attrs[
            "factor"
        ] + np.concatenate(inputs[n:], axis=0)
    elif op == "gather_rows":
        result = np.concatenate(inputs, axis=0)
    elif op == "scatter_rows":
        a = (
            np.concatenate(inputs, axis=0) if len(inputs) > 1 else a
        )  # row parts, in order
        result = np.zeros(attrs["shape"])
        ids = np.asarray(attrs["ids"])
        selected = (ids >= 0) & (ids < result.shape[0])
        rows = (
            a[selected] * attrs["factor"]
            if attrs.get("factor", 1.0) != 1.0
            else a[selected]
        )
        np.add.at(result, ids[selected], rows)
    elif op == "sum_squares":
        result = np.array([[np.sum(a * a)]])
    elif op == "clip_gradient":
        norm = np.sqrt(inputs[1][0, 0])
        result = a * (min(1.0, attrs["clip_norm"] / norm) if norm else 1.0)
    elif op == "sgd":
        result = a - attrs["learning_rate"] * inputs[1]
    elif op == "adam_m":
        result = attrs["beta1"] * a + (1 - attrs["beta1"]) * inputs[1]
    elif op == "adam_v":
        result = attrs["beta2"] * a + (1 - attrs["beta2"]) * inputs[1] ** 2
    elif op == "adam_step":
        # Adam's moments and update in one task: [m'; v'; w'], the arithmetic
        # of adam_m, adam_v
        # and adam_update. Uploaded as three row bands (upload_rows), each
        # assembled by region.
        b1, b2, step = attrs["beta1"], attrs["beta2"], attrs["step"]
        m = b1 * inputs[1] + (1 - b1) * inputs[3]
        v = b2 * inputs[2] + (1 - b2) * inputs[3] ** 2
        w = a - attrs["learning_rate"] * (m / (1 - b1**step)) / (
            np.sqrt(v / (1 - b2**step)) + attrs["epsilon"]
        )
        result = np.concatenate([m, v, w], axis=0)
    elif op == "adam_update":
        m = inputs[1] / (1 - attrs["beta1"] ** attrs["step"])
        v = inputs[2] / (1 - attrs["beta2"] ** attrs["step"])
        result = a - attrs["learning_rate"] * m / (
            np.sqrt(v) + attrs["epsilon"]
        )
    elif op == "sigmoid":
        result = np.exp(-np.logaddexp(0, -a))
    elif op == "tanh":
        result = np.tanh(a)
    elif op == "sigmoid_backward":
        result = inputs[1] * a * (1 - a)
    elif op == "tanh_backward":
        result = inputs[1] * (1 - a * a)
    elif op == "scale":
        result = a * attrs["factor"]
    elif op == "slice_columns":
        result = a[:, attrs["start"] : attrs["start"] + attrs["width"]]
    elif op == "slice_backward":
        result = np.zeros(attrs["shape"])
        result[:, attrs["start"] : attrs["start"] + attrs["width"]] = a
    elif op == "slice_block":
        result = np.zeros(attrs["shape"])
        if inputs:
            result[:, attrs["offset"] : attrs["offset"] + a.shape[1]] = a
    elif op == "concat_columns":
        result = np.concatenate(inputs, axis=1)
    elif op == "softmax":
        scores = np.where(
            attrs.get("mask", np.zeros_like(a, dtype=bool)), -np.inf, a
        )
        ex = np.exp(scores - scores.max(axis=1, keepdims=True))
        result = ex / ex.sum(axis=1, keepdims=True)
    elif op == "softmax_backward":
        result = a * (inputs[1] - (a * inputs[1]).sum(axis=1, keepdims=True))
    elif op in ("layer_norm", "layer_norm_backward"):
        centered = a - a.mean(axis=1, keepdims=True)
        inv = 1 / np.sqrt(
            np.mean(centered**2, axis=1, keepdims=True) + attrs["epsilon"]
        )
        normalized = centered * inv
        if op == "layer_norm":
            result = normalized * inputs[1] + inputs[2]
        elif attrs["index"] == 0:
            g = inputs[2] * inputs[1]
            result = inv * (
                g
                - g.mean(axis=1, keepdims=True)
                - normalized * (g * normalized).mean(axis=1, keepdims=True)
            )
        elif attrs["index"] == 1:
            result = (inputs[2] * normalized).sum(axis=0, keepdims=True)
        else:
            result = inputs[2].sum(axis=0, keepdims=True)
    elif op == "relu":
        result = np.maximum(a, 0)
    elif op == "relu_backward":
        result = inputs[1] * (a > 0)
    elif op == "sum":
        result = np.array([[a.sum()]])
    elif op == "transpose":
        result = a.T
    elif op == "reduce_rows":
        result = a.sum(axis=0, keepdims=True)
    elif op == "broadcast":
        result = np.broadcast_to(a, attrs["shape"])
    elif op in ("cross_entropy", "ce_backward"):
        shifted = a - a.max(axis=1, keepdims=True)
        ex = np.exp(shifted)
        total = ex.sum(axis=1, keepdims=True)
        target = np.asarray(attrs["targets"], dtype=np.int64)
        valid = target != attrs.get("ignore_index")
        # A row chunk of a longer cross-entropy divides by the whole batch's
        # count.
        count = attrs.get("count", np.count_nonzero(valid))
        smoothing = attrs.get("label_smoothing", 0.0)
        if op == "cross_entropy":
            nll = np.log(total[:, 0]) - shifted[np.arange(len(a)), target]
            smooth = np.log(total[:, 0]) - shifted.mean(axis=1)
            result = np.array(
                [
                    [
                        (
                            (1 - smoothing) * nll[valid]
                            + smoothing * smooth[valid]
                        ).sum()
                        / count
                    ]
                ]
            )
        else:
            result = ex / total
            result -= smoothing / a.shape[1]
            result[np.arange(len(a)), target] -= 1 - smoothing
            result *= valid[:, None] * inputs[1][0, 0] / count
    elif op == "ce_fused":
        # The output layer for a band of rows: logits = x @ W + b, then the
        # loss and its gradient,
        # with the arithmetic of matmul, add_bias, cross_entropy and
        # ce_backward. Returns
        # [logit gradient | row loss]; the band's rows of the loss sum to its
        # share of the mean.
        logits = (a @ inputs[1]) + inputs[2]
        shifted = logits - logits.max(axis=1, keepdims=True)
        ex = np.exp(shifted)
        total = ex.sum(axis=1, keepdims=True)
        target = np.asarray(attrs["targets"], dtype=np.int64)
        valid = target != attrs.get("ignore_index")
        count, smoothing = attrs["count"], attrs.get("label_smoothing", 0.0)
        nll = np.log(total[:, 0]) - shifted[np.arange(len(logits)), target]
        smooth = np.log(total[:, 0]) - shifted.mean(axis=1)
        rows = (
            np.where(valid, (1 - smoothing) * nll + smoothing * smooth, 0.0)
            / count
        )
        grad = ex / total
        grad -= smoothing / logits.shape[1]
        grad[np.arange(len(logits)), target] -= 1 - smoothing
        grad *= valid[:, None] * inputs[3][0, 0] / count
        result = np.concatenate([grad, rows[:, None]], axis=1)
    elif op == "add_many":
        result = np.zeros_like(a)
        # Fixed input order, regardless of worker completion order.
        for value in inputs:
            result += value
    elif op == "assemble":
        result = np.zeros(attrs["shape"], dtype=np.float64)
        for value, (i, end_i, j, end_j) in zip(inputs, attrs["blocks"]):
            result[i:end_i, j:end_j] = value
    else:
        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported operation: {op}")
    result = np.asarray(result, dtype=np.float64)
    if result.ndim != 2 or not np.isfinite(result).all():
        raise ValueError("Kernel produced invalid/nonfinite output")
    return result


def evaluate(tape, values=None):
    """Local untiled reference execution, without networking."""
    store = {
        k: v.copy()
        for k, v in (tape.values if values is None else values).items()
    }
    for node in tape.nodes:
        store[node.id] = execute(
            node.op, [store[k] for k in node.inputs], node.attrs
        )
    return store


def execute_task(op, inputs, attrs):
    """Return all original results, including grouped intermediates for
    auditing."""
    if op != "group":
        return [execute(op, inputs, attrs)]
    store, outputs = {}, []
    for node in attrs["nodes"]:
        args = []
        for ref in node["inputs"]:
            if "external" in ref:
                a = inputs[ref["external"]]
            else:
                a = store[ref["value"]]
                if ref["region"]:
                    i, ei, j, ej = ref["region"]
                    a = a[i:ei, j:ej]
            args.append(a)
        value = execute(node["op"], args, node["attrs"])
        if list(value.shape) != node["shape"]:
            raise ValueError("Invalid grouped output shape")
        store[node["id"]] = value
        outputs.append(value)
    return outputs


# File: packages/core/src/ygaraty/core/runtime/local.py
"""Execute a topologically ordered plan locally, releasing dead intermediates.

No scheduler, network or tensor store is needed when a participant holds the
whole model. Task/Input and the numerical kernels remain the shared contracts.
"""

from collections import Counter

from ygaraty.core.runtime.kernels import execute


def execute_plan(tasks, inputs, keep):
    """Return only `keep`; inputs are borrowed and kernels must not mutate
    them.

    Unlike the parity executor, this does not retain the entire forward and
    backward graph. Plans must be ordered and contain ordinary (ungrouped)
    tasks.
    """
    store = dict(inputs)
    keep = set(keep)
    remaining = Counter(dep for task in tasks for dep in task.dependencies)
    known = set(store)
    for task in tasks:
        if task.id in known or not task.dependencies <= known:
            raise ValueError(f"Invalid or unordered local plan at {task.id}")
        known.add(task.id)
    if not keep <= known:
        raise ValueError("Local plan is missing requested outputs")
    for key in list(store):
        if not remaining[key] and key not in keep:
            del store[key]
    for task in tasks:
        value = execute(task.op, task.arrays(store), task.attrs)
        if value.shape != tuple(task.shape):
            raise ValueError(f"Invalid local output shape for {task.id}")
        store[task.id] = value
        for dep in task.dependencies:
            remaining[dep] -= 1
            if not remaining[dep] and dep not in keep:
                del store[dep]
        if not remaining[task.id] and task.id not in keep:
            del store[task.id]
    return {key: store[key] for key in keep}


# File: packages/core/src/ygaraty/core/runtime/sequence_kernels.py
"""Kernels for batched sequences: the fused LSTM step, its gradients, and
batched attention.

Sequences are time-major: row t*batch+b is step t of example b. An `lstm` value
holds, per row, [h_state | c_state | h_out | i | f | g | o], each `hidden` wide.
Whole-layer kernels (lstm, lstm_backward) run every step in turn and define the
result; the planner splits them into one task per step that calls the same step
functions, so both give identical numbers.
"""  # noqa: E501 -- preserve documentation layout

import numpy as np

from ygaraty.core.compiler.layouts import C_STATE, GATES, H_OUT, H_STATE


def sigmoid(x):
    return np.exp(-np.logaddexp(0, -x))


def block(a, index, hidden, count=1):
    return a[:, index * hidden : (index + count) * hidden]


def recurrent(partials, blocks, rows):
    """Sum matmul partial products laid out as [[col_start, col_stop, count],
    ...]."""
    width = blocks[-1][1] if blocks else 0
    out, k = np.zeros((rows, width)), 0
    for start, stop, count in blocks:
        for _ in range(count):
            out[:, start:stop] += partials[k]
            k += 1
    return out


def cell(xp, hw, bias_ih, bias_hh, h_prev, c_prev, mask):
    """One step: returns [h_state | c_state | h_out | i | f | g | o]."""
    hidden = h_prev.shape[1]
    # Same order as PyTorch: (x @ W_ih.T + b_ih) + (h @ W_hh.T + b_hh).
    a = (xp + bias_ih) + (hw + bias_hh)
    i, f, o = (sigmoid(a[:, k * hidden : (k + 1) * hidden]) for k in (0, 1, 3))
    g = np.tanh(a[:, 2 * hidden : 3 * hidden])
    c_new = f * c_prev + i * g
    h_new = o * np.tanh(c_new)
    live = mask > 0
    return np.concatenate(
        [
            np.where(live, h_new, h_prev),
            np.where(live, c_new, c_prev),
            np.where(live, h_new, 0.0),
            i,
            f,
            g,
            o,
        ],
        axis=1,
    )


def cell_backward(state, c_prev, mask, dh_state, dc_state, dh_out):
    """Gate gradients (rows x 4h) and the carry [dh_prev passed through | dc_prev]."""  # noqa: E501 -- preserve documentation layout
    hidden = c_prev.shape[1]
    i, f, g, o = (block(state, GATES + k, hidden) for k in range(4))
    live = mask > 0
    tc = np.tanh(block(state, C_STATE, hidden))  # c_new on live rows
    dh_new = np.where(live, dh_state + dh_out, 0.0)
    dc_new = np.where(live, dc_state + dh_new * o * (1 - tc * tc), 0.0)
    gates = np.concatenate(
        [
            dc_new * g * i * (1 - i),
            dc_new * c_prev * f * (1 - f),
            dc_new * i * (1 - g * g),
            dh_new * tc * o * (1 - o),
        ],
        axis=1,
    )
    carry = np.concatenate(
        [
            np.where(live, 0.0, dh_state),
            dc_new * f + np.where(live, 0.0, dc_state),
        ],
        axis=1,
    )
    return gates, carry


def order(steps, reverse):
    return range(steps - 1, -1, -1) if reverse else range(steps)


def lstm(xp, whh_t, bias_ih, bias_hh, mask, h0, c0, batch, hidden, reverse):
    steps = len(xp) // batch
    out = np.zeros((len(xp), 7 * hidden))
    h, c = h0, c0
    for t in order(steps, reverse):
        rows = slice(t * batch, (t + 1) * batch)
        state = cell(xp[rows], h @ whh_t, bias_ih, bias_hh, h, c, mask[rows])
        out[rows] = state
        h, c = block(state, H_STATE, hidden), block(state, C_STATE, hidden)
    return out


def lstm_backward(inputs, attrs):
    batch, hidden, reverse = attrs["batch"], attrs["hidden"], attrs["reverse"]
    values = list(inputs)
    out_value = values.pop(0)
    full = values.pop(0) if attrs.get("full") else None
    g_out = values.pop(0) if attrs.get("has_out") else None
    g_state = values.pop(0) if attrs.get("has_state") else None
    whh, mask, h0, c0 = values
    rows = len(out_value)
    steps = rows // batch
    if full is not None:
        g_out = block(full, H_OUT, hidden)
    result = np.zeros((rows + batch, 6 * hidden))
    walk = list(order(steps, reverse))
    _final = walk[-1]
    dh = np.zeros((batch, hidden))
    dc = np.zeros((batch, hidden))
    if g_state is not None:
        dh, dc = g_state[:, :hidden].copy(), g_state[:, hidden:].copy()
    for k, t in enumerate(reversed(walk)):
        rows_t = slice(t * batch, (t + 1) * batch)
        if full is not None:
            dh = dh + block(full[rows_t], H_STATE, hidden)
            dc = dc + block(full[rows_t], C_STATE, hidden)
        previous = walk[len(walk) - 2 - k] if k < len(walk) - 1 else None
        c_prev = (
            c0
            if previous is None
            else block(
                out_value[previous * batch : (previous + 1) * batch],
                C_STATE,
                hidden,
            )
        )
        gates, carry = cell_backward(
            out_value[rows_t],
            c_prev,
            mask[rows_t],
            dh,
            dc,
            np.zeros((batch, hidden)) if g_out is None else g_out[rows_t],
        )
        result[rows_t, : 4 * hidden], result[rows_t, 4 * hidden :] = (
            gates,
            carry,
        )
        dh, dc = carry[:, :hidden] + gates @ whh, carry[:, hidden:]
    result[rows:, :hidden], result[rows:, hidden : 2 * hidden] = dh, dc
    return result


def lstm_chain_grad(inputs, attrs):
    """Backward steps of one chunk of a chain (experiment 27): the per-step
    arithmetic of
    lstm_cell_grad, run for `steps` consecutive steps in one task. Returns rows
    in step order,
    each [gate gradients (4h) | carry (2h)], exactly as the per-step tasks would."""  # noqa: E501 -- preserve documentation layout
    hidden, batch, reverse, k, offset = (
        attrs["hidden"],
        attrs["batch"],
        attrs["reverse"],
        attrs["steps"],
        attrs["offset"],
    )
    values = list(inputs)
    out = values.pop(0)
    c0 = values.pop(0) if attrs.get("has_c0") else None
    mask = values.pop(0)
    g_out = values.pop(0) if attrs.get("has_out") else None
    carry = values.pop(0) if attrs.get("has_carry") else None
    gates_prev = values.pop(0) if attrs.get("has_gates") else None
    whh = values.pop(0)
    dh = np.zeros((batch, hidden)) if carry is None else carry[:, :hidden]
    if gates_prev is not None:
        dh = dh + gates_prev @ whh
    dc = (
        np.zeros((batch, hidden))
        if carry is None
        else carry[:, hidden : 2 * hidden]
    )
    result = np.zeros((k * batch, 6 * hidden))
    proc = list(
        reversed(list(order(k, reverse)))
    )  # local step indexes, in processing order
    for n, j in enumerate(proc):
        rows = slice(j * batch, (j + 1) * batch)
        state = out[(offset + j) * batch : (offset + j + 1) * batch]
        prev_j = (
            j + 1 if reverse else j - 1
        )  # the step before j in the forward walk
        if c0 is not None and n == k - 1:
            c_prev = c0
        else:
            c_prev = block(
                out[(offset + prev_j) * batch : (offset + prev_j + 1) * batch],
                C_STATE,
                hidden,
            )
        gates, carried = cell_backward(
            state,
            c_prev,
            mask[rows],
            dh,
            dc,
            np.zeros((batch, hidden)) if g_out is None else g_out[rows],
        )
        result[rows, : 4 * hidden], result[rows, 4 * hidden :] = gates, carried
        dh, dc = carried[:, :hidden] + gates @ whh, carried[:, hidden:]
    return result


def by_example(a, batch):
    """(steps*batch x n) time-major rows -> (batch, steps, n)."""
    return a.reshape(len(a) // batch, batch, -1).transpose(1, 0, 2)


def from_examples(a):
