    return a.transpose(1, 0, 2).reshape(-1, a.shape[2])


def bqk(q, k, batch):
    return from_examples(
        by_example(q, batch) @ by_example(k, batch).transpose(0, 2, 1)
    )


def bpv(p, v, batch):
    return from_examples(by_example(p, batch) @ by_example(v, batch))


def bptq(p, q, batch):
    return from_examples(
        by_example(p, batch).transpose(0, 2, 1) @ by_example(q, batch)
    )


def place(shape, values, offsets):
    out = np.zeros(shape)
    for value, (r, c) in zip(values, offsets):
        out[r : r + value.shape[0], c : c + value.shape[1]] += value
    return out


def execute(op, inputs, attrs):
    """Sequence kernels by name; None if `op` isn't one of them."""
    if op == "view":
        r0, r1, c0, c1 = attrs["region"]
        return inputs[0][r0:r1, c0:c1]
    if op == "concat_rows":
        return np.concatenate(inputs, axis=0)
    if op in ("place", "place_block"):
        return place(attrs["shape"], inputs, attrs["offsets"])
    if op == "lstm":
        return lstm(*inputs, attrs["batch"], attrs["hidden"], attrs["reverse"])
    if op == "lstm_backward":
        return lstm_backward(inputs, attrs)
    if op == "lstm_chain":
        # A chunk of consecutive forward steps in one task: the whole-layer
        # kernel over its rows.
        xp, bias_ih, bias_hh, h0, c0, mask, whh_t = inputs
        return lstm(
            xp,
            whh_t,
            bias_ih,
            bias_hh,
            mask,
            h0,
            c0,
            attrs["batch"],
            attrs["hidden"],
            attrs["reverse"],
        )
    if op == "lstm_chain_grad":
        return lstm_chain_grad(inputs, attrs)
    if op in ("bqk", "bpv", "bptq"):
        return dict(bqk=bqk, bpv=bpv, bptq=bptq)[op](*inputs, attrs["batch"])
    if op == "lstm_cell":
        xp, bias_ih, bias_hh, h_prev, c_prev, mask, *partials = inputs
        # The recurrent product inline (the whole W_hh.T as the last input), or
        # as summed partials.
        hw = (
            h_prev @ partials[0]
            if attrs.get("inline")
            else recurrent(partials, attrs["blocks"], len(xp))
        )
        return cell(xp, hw, bias_ih, bias_hh, h_prev, c_prev, mask)
    if op in (
        "lstm_cell_backward",
        "lstm_cell_carry",
        "lstm_cell_grad",
        "lstm_state_grad",
    ):
        # Inputs: [state, c_prev, mask] (not for lstm_state_grad), then g_out
        # if has_out,
        # the incoming [dh | dc] if has_carry, then the recurrent partials
        # gates_next @ W_hh.
        hidden, values = attrs["hidden"], list(inputs)
        if op != "lstm_state_grad":
            state, c_prev, mask = values[:3]
            values = values[3:]
        rows = len(values[0]) if values else len(state)
        g_out = (
            values.pop(0) if attrs.get("has_out") else np.zeros((rows, hidden))
        )
        carry = (
            values.pop(0)
            if attrs.get("has_carry")
            else np.zeros((rows, 2 * hidden))
        )
        if attrs.get("inline"):
            gates_prev, whh = values
            dh = carry[:, :hidden] + gates_prev @ whh
        else:
            dh = carry[:, :hidden] + (
                recurrent(values, attrs["blocks"], rows) if values else 0.0
            )
        dc = carry[:, hidden : 2 * hidden]
        if op == "lstm_state_grad":
            return np.concatenate(
                [
                    dh,
                    dc,
                    np.zeros(
                        (rows, attrs.get("width", 4 * hidden) - 2 * hidden)
                    ),
                ],
                axis=1,
            )
        gates, carried = cell_backward(state, c_prev, mask, dh, dc, g_out)
        if (
            op == "lstm_cell_grad"
        ):  # one task per step: [gate gradients (4h) | carry (2h)]
            return np.concatenate([gates, carried], axis=1)
        return gates if op == "lstm_cell_backward" else carried
    return None


# File: packages/core/src/ygaraty/core/runtime/transformer_kernels.py
"""Fused Transformer layers: one task per band of examples (experiment 30).

Rows are example-major: row b*steps + t is position t of example b. A
`tf_layer` task runs one
pre-norm layer (self-attention, cross-attention for the decoder, the
feed-forward block, and
their residuals) over a band of examples. `tf_layer_grad` recomputes that
forward from the
layer's input and returns, packed into one matrix of width d: the input's
gradient, the
memory's gradient (decoder), then every parameter's gradient (packed_layout).
The attention
probabilities and the feed-forward activations never leave the worker.
"""

import os

import numpy as np

from ygaraty.core.compiler.layouts import (
    DECODER_PARAMS,
    ENCODER_PARAMS,
    packed_layout,
)

OPS = ("tf_layer", "tf_layer_grad", "vocab_loss")


def pack(grads, layout, d):
    out = []
    for g, (offset, rows, mode) in zip(grads, layout):
        if mode == "rows":
            out.append(g)
        elif mode == "transposed":
            out.append(g.T)
        else:
            flat = np.zeros(rows * d)
            flat[: g.size] = g.ravel()
            out.append(flat.reshape(rows, d))
    return np.concatenate(out, axis=0) if out else np.zeros((0, d))


def masks(key_mask, tq, tk, causal):
    """(examples, tq, tk) boolean: True where a query may not attend to a
    key."""
    m = np.broadcast_to(
        np.asarray(key_mask, dtype=bool)[:, None, :], (len(key_mask), tq, tk)
    )
    if causal:
        m = m | np.triu(np.ones((tq, tk), dtype=bool), 1)[None]
    return m


def layer_norm(x, w, b, eps):
    xc = x - x.mean(axis=1, keepdims=True)
    inv = 1 / np.sqrt((xc * xc).mean(axis=1, keepdims=True) + eps)
    n = xc * inv
    return n * w + b, (inv, n)


def layer_norm_backward(dy, w, cache):
    inv, n = cache
    g = dy * w
    dx = inv * (
        g
        - g.mean(axis=1, keepdims=True)
        - n * (g * n).mean(axis=1, keepdims=True)
    )
    return (
        dx,
        (dy * n).sum(axis=0, keepdims=True),
        dy.sum(axis=0, keepdims=True),
    )


def attention(q_in, kv_in, in_w, in_b, out_w, out_b, nb, tq, tk, heads, mask):
    """Multi-head attention as nn.MultiheadAttention computes it (q, k, v rows
    of in_proj)."""
    d = q_in.shape[1]
    dh = d // heads
    q = q_in @ in_w[:d].T + in_b[:, :d]
    k = kv_in @ in_w[d : 2 * d].T + in_b[:, d : 2 * d]
    v = kv_in @ in_w[2 * d :].T + in_b[:, 2 * d :]
    Q = q.reshape(nb, tq, heads, dh).transpose(0, 2, 1, 3)
    K = k.reshape(nb, tk, heads, dh).transpose(0, 2, 1, 3)
    V = v.reshape(nb, tk, heads, dh).transpose(0, 2, 1, 3)
    s = (Q @ K.transpose(0, 1, 3, 2)) / np.sqrt(dh)
    s = np.where(mask[:, None], -np.inf, s)
    s = s - s.max(axis=-1, keepdims=True)
    p = np.exp(s)
    p /= p.sum(axis=-1, keepdims=True)
    o = (p @ V).transpose(0, 2, 1, 3).reshape(nb * tq, d)
    return o @ out_w.T + out_b, (q_in, kv_in, Q, K, V, p, o)


def weight_products(pairs):
    """A deferred weight gradient (DO-9): the products a.T @ b of its pairs,
    stacked by rows, with the arithmetic the combined backward pass uses."""
    if len(pairs) == 1:
        return pairs[0][0].T @ pairs[0][1]
    return np.concatenate([a.T @ b for a, b in pairs], axis=0)


def resolve_grads(grads):
    """Gradients with deferred weight products (tuples of pairs) computed."""
    return [weight_products(g) if isinstance(g, tuple) else g for g in grads]


def attention_backward(dy, in_w, out_w, cache, nb, tq, tk, heads, defer=False):
    q_in, kv_in, Q, K, V, p, o = cache
    d = q_in.shape[1]
    dh = d // heads
    d_out_w, d_out_b = ((dy, o),), dy.sum(axis=0, keepdims=True)
    dO = (dy @ out_w).reshape(nb, tq, heads, dh).transpose(0, 2, 1, 3)
    dp = dO @ V.transpose(0, 1, 3, 2)
    dV = p.transpose(0, 1, 3, 2) @ dO
    ds = p * (dp - (dp * p).sum(axis=-1, keepdims=True)) / np.sqrt(dh)
    dQ, dK = ds @ K, ds.transpose(0, 1, 3, 2) @ Q
    dq = dQ.transpose(0, 2, 1, 3).reshape(nb * tq, d)
    dk = dK.transpose(0, 2, 1, 3).reshape(nb * tk, d)
    dv = dV.transpose(0, 2, 1, 3).reshape(nb * tk, d)
    d_in_w = ((dq, q_in), (dk, kv_in), (dv, kv_in))
    d_in_b = np.concatenate(
        [
            dq.sum(axis=0, keepdims=True),
            dk.sum(axis=0, keepdims=True),
            dv.sum(axis=0, keepdims=True),
        ],
        axis=1,
    )
    dq_in = dq @ in_w[:d]
    dkv_in = dk @ in_w[d : 2 * d] + dv @ in_w[2 * d :]
    grads = [d_in_w, d_in_b, d_out_w, d_out_b]
    return dq_in, dkv_in, grads if defer else resolve_grads(grads)


def layer_inputs(inputs, attrs):
    """A layer task's input, memory and remaining inputs. Experiment 78's parallel
    layers: with attrs["parts"] = k, the k values after the input (and the
    memory) are added to it, so the layer after a parallel group reads
    x + r_1 + ... directly from the group's tasks."""
    decoder = attrs.get("decoder", False)
    start = 1 + decoder
    x = inputs[0]
    for part in inputs[start : start + attrs.get("parts", 0)]:
        x = x + part
    mem = inputs[1] if decoder else None
    return x, mem, inputs[start + attrs.get("parts", 0) :]


def layer_forward(x, mem, p, attrs):
    """With attrs["residual"] (experiment 78's parallel layers after the first of a
    group), the layer returns only its residual update, y - x."""
    nb, T, heads, eps = (
        attrs["batch"],
        attrs["steps"],
        attrs["heads"],
        attrs.get("epsilon", 1e-5),
    )
    decoder, final = (
        attrs.get("decoder", False),
        attrs.get("final_norm", False),
    )
    if (
        len(p)
        != (len(DECODER_PARAMS) if decoder else len(ENCODER_PARAMS))
        + 2 * final
    ):
        raise ValueError("Wrong number of layer parameters")
    if final:
        # The stack's final norm, applied by its last layer (its owner holds
        # the weights).
        p, (fw, fb) = p[:-2], p[-2:]
    n1w, n1b, sa_in_w, sa_in_b, sa_out_w, sa_out_b, n2w, n2b = p[:8]
    n1, c_n1 = layer_norm(x, n1w, n1b, eps)
    a, c_sa = attention(
        n1,
        n1,
        sa_in_w,
        sa_in_b,
        sa_out_w,
        sa_out_b,
        nb,
        T,
        T,
        heads,
        masks(attrs["key_mask"], T, T, attrs.get("causal", False)),
    )
    x1 = x + a
    cache = dict(c_n1=c_n1, c_sa=c_sa, x1=x1)
    if decoder:
        ca_in_w, ca_in_b, ca_out_w, ca_out_b, nfw, nfb = p[8:14]
        l1w, l1b, l2w, l2b = p[14:18]
        S = attrs["mem_steps"]
        n2, c_n2 = layer_norm(x1, n2w, n2b, eps)
        c, c_ca = attention(
            n2,
            mem,
            ca_in_w,
            ca_in_b,
            ca_out_w,
            ca_out_b,
            nb,
            T,
            S,
            heads,
            masks(attrs["mem_mask"], T, S, False),
        )
        x2 = x1 + c
        cache.update(c_n2=c_n2, c_ca=c_ca, x2=x2)
    else:
        nfw, nfb = n2w, n2b
        l1w, l1b, l2w, l2b = p[8:12]
        x2 = x1
    nf, c_nf = layer_norm(x2, nfw, nfb, eps)
    h_pre = nf @ l1w.T + l1b
    h = np.maximum(h_pre, 0.0)
    y = x2 + h @ l2w.T + l2b
    cache.update(c_nf=c_nf, nf=nf, h_pre=h_pre, h=h)
    if final:
        y, cache["c_final"] = layer_norm(y, fw, fb, eps)
    elif attrs.get("residual"):
        y = y - x
    return y, cache


def layer_backward(x, mem, dy, p, attrs, defer=False):
    """Gradients of a layer's input, memory and parameters, recomputing the
    forward pass. With `defer` (DO-9's split backward), the weight matrices'
    gradients come back as their pairs of factors, for resolve_grads to
    multiply after dx and dmem have gone on: the same products, so the same
    numbers."""
    nb, T, heads, _eps = (
        attrs["batch"],
        attrs["steps"],
        attrs["heads"],
        attrs.get("epsilon", 1e-5),
    )
    decoder, final = (
        attrs.get("decoder", False),
        attrs.get("final_norm", False),
    )
    _, c = layer_forward(x, mem, p, attrs)
    if final:
        p, (fw, _fb) = p[:-2], p[-2:]
        dy, d_fw, d_fb = layer_norm_backward(dy, fw, c["c_final"])
    n1w, n1b, sa_in_w, sa_in_b, sa_out_w, sa_out_b, n2w, n2b = p[:8]
    if decoder:
        ca_in_w, ca_in_b, ca_out_w, ca_out_b, nfw, nfb = p[8:14]
        l1w, l1b, l2w, l2b = p[14:18]
    else:
        nfw, _nfb = n2w, n2b
        l1w, l1b, l2w, l2b = p[8:12]
    # Feed-forward block and its norm.
    d_l2w, d_l2b = ((dy, c["h"]),), dy.sum(axis=0, keepdims=True)
    dh_pre = (dy @ l2w) * (c["h_pre"] > 0)
    d_l1w, d_l1b = ((dh_pre, c["nf"]),), dh_pre.sum(axis=0, keepdims=True)
    dxf, d_nfw, d_nfb = layer_norm_backward(dh_pre @ l1w, nfw, c["c_nf"])
    dx2 = dy + dxf
    if decoder:
        dn2, dmem, ca_grads = attention_backward(
            dx2,
            ca_in_w,
            ca_out_w,
            c["c_ca"],
            nb,
            T,
            attrs["mem_steps"],
            heads,
            defer=True,
        )
        dx1n, d_n2w, d_n2b = layer_norm_backward(dn2, n2w, c["c_n2"])
        dx1 = dx2 + dx1n
    else:
        dmem, dx1 = None, dx2
    dq_in, dkv_in, sa_grads = attention_backward(
        dx1, sa_in_w, sa_out_w, c["c_sa"], nb, T, T, heads, defer=True
    )
    dxn, d_n1w, d_n1b = layer_norm_backward(dq_in + dkv_in, n1w, c["c_n1"])
    dx = dx1 + dxn
    if decoder:
        grads = (
            [d_n1w, d_n1b]
            + sa_grads
            + [d_n2w, d_n2b]
            + ca_grads
            + [d_nfw, d_nfb, d_l1w, d_l1b, d_l2w, d_l2b]
        )
    else:
        grads = (
            [d_n1w, d_n1b]
            + sa_grads
            + [d_nfw, d_nfb, d_l1w, d_l1b, d_l2w, d_l2b]
        )
    if final:
        grads += [d_fw, d_fb]
    if attrs.get("residual") and not final:
        dx = dx - dy  # the residual update's gradient: the layer's, less dy
    return dx, dmem, grads if defer else resolve_grads(grads)


def execute(op, inputs, attrs):
    decoder = attrs.get("decoder", False)
    if op == "vocab_loss":
        return np.array([[vocab_row_loss(inputs, attrs).sum()]])
    if op == "tf_layer":
        x, mem, params = layer_inputs(inputs, attrs)
        return layer_forward(x, mem, params, attrs)[0]
    if op == "tf_layer_grad":
        x, mem, rest = layer_inputs(inputs, attrs)
        g, params = rest[0], rest[1:]
        dx, dmem, grads = layer_backward(x, mem, g, params, attrs)
        d = x.shape[1]
        layout, _ = packed_layout([q.shape for q in params], d)
        parts = [dx] + ([dmem] if decoder else []) + [pack(grads, layout, d)]
        return np.concatenate(parts, axis=0)
    return None


# Owned layers (experiment 31)
# ----------------------------------------------------------------
# A worker that owns a layer keeps its parameters and Adam moments between
# updates. Its band
# tasks take no parameters and its backward tasks keep the parameter gradients;
# one step task
# per layer sums them over the bands, in band order, and applies Adam with the
# arithmetic of the
# unowned plan (add_many, then adam_step), so both plans give the same numbers.
# Owned vocabulary (experiment 32): the tied embedding and output layer split
# by vocabulary rows.
# Owner s holds rows [v0, v1) of the table and of the output bias, with their
# Adam moments. It
# serves the embedding rows a batch uses (tf_vocab_gather); per band of decoder
# rows it returns
# logit statistics (tf_vocab_stats), then, from every shard's statistics, its
# part of the
# gradient of the decoder output (tf_vocab_grad). The logits, their gradient
# and the weight
# gradient never leave it; its step adds the embedding gradient rows and
# applies Adam.
VOCAB_OPS = (
    "tf_vocab_load",
    "tf_vocab_gather",
    "tf_vocab_stats",
    "tf_vocab_grad",
    "tf_vocab_embed_grad",
    "tf_vocab_step",
    "tf_vocab_publish",
)
OWNED_OPS = (
    "tf_owned_load",
    "tf_owned_layer",
    "tf_owned_grad",
    "tf_owned_step",
    "tf_owned_publish",
) + VOCAB_OPS


def vocab_stats(z, targets, v0):
    """Per row of a shard's logits: [max, sum of exp(z - max), the target's
    logit (0 if another
    shard holds the target), sum of the logits]."""
    top = z.max(axis=1)
    t = np.asarray(targets, dtype=np.int64) - v0
    inside = (t >= 0) & (t < z.shape[1])
    target = np.where(
        inside, z[np.arange(len(z)), np.clip(t, 0, z.shape[1] - 1)], 0.0
    )
    return np.stack(
        [top, np.exp(z - top[:, None]).sum(axis=1), target, z.sum(axis=1)],
        axis=1,
    )


def combine_stats(stats):
    """Every shard's statistics for a band, in shard order: per row the
    log-sum-exp over the whole
    vocabulary, the target's logit and the sum of the logits."""
    top = np.max([s[:, 0] for s in stats], axis=0)
    total = np.zeros_like(top)
    for s in stats:
        total += s[:, 1] * np.exp(s[:, 0] - top)
    target, logits = np.zeros_like(top), np.zeros_like(top)
    for s in stats:
        target += s[:, 2]
        logits += s[:, 3]
    return top + np.log(total), target, logits


def vocab_row_loss(stats, attrs):
    """A band's rows of the label-smoothed mean loss, as cross_entropy computes
    it."""
    lse, target, logits = combine_stats(stats)
    valid = np.asarray(attrs["targets"]) != attrs.get("ignore_index")
    smoothing = attrs.get("label_smoothing", 0.0)
    rows = (1 - smoothing) * (lse - target) + smoothing * (
        lse - logits / attrs["vocab"]
    )
    return np.where(valid, rows, 0.0) / attrs["count"]


NEWTON_SCHULZ = (3.4445, -4.7750, 2.0315)  # ygaraty/reference/muon.py


def orthogonalize(matrix, steps=5):
    """Muon's quintic Newton-Schulz iteration (reference/muon.py), in NumPy:
    the nearest semi-orthogonal matrix, approximately, on the smaller side."""
    a, b, c = NEWTON_SCHULZ
    x = matrix / (np.linalg.norm(matrix) + 1e-7)
    tall = x.shape[0] > x.shape[1]
    if tall:
        x = x.T
    for _ in range(steps):
        square = x @ x.T
        x = a * x + (b * square + c * square @ square) @ x
    return x.T if tall else x


def unpack(packed, layout, shapes):
    out = []
    for (offset, rows, mode), (a, b) in zip(layout, shapes):
        part = packed[offset : offset + rows]
        if mode == "rows":
            out.append(part.copy())
        elif mode == "transposed":
            out.append(part.T.copy())
        else:
            out.append(part.ravel()[:b].reshape(1, b).copy())
    return out


class OwnedLayers:
    """The layers this worker owns: parameters, moments and the current
    update's band gradients."""

    def __init__(self, split_backward=None):
        self.layers = {}
        # DO-9: tf_owned_grad returns dx and dmem and leaves the weight
        # products for flush(), which the worker calls once that result is on
        # its way, and the step calls in any case.
        self.split_backward = (
            os.environ.get("YGARATY_SPLIT_BACKWARD", "0") == "1"
            if split_backward is None
            else split_backward
        )

    def pending(self):
        return any(s.get("pending") for s in self.layers.values())

    def flush(self):
        """Compute the weight gradients tf_owned_grad deferred. Returns the
        number of bands resolved."""
        done = 0
        for layer in self.layers.values():
            for band, (grads, rows, d) in sorted(
                layer.get("pending", {}).items()
            ):
                layer["grads"][band] = pack(resolve_grads(grads), rows, d)
                done += 1
            layer["pending"] = {}
        return done

    def nbytes(self):
        return sum(
            a.nbytes
            for s in self.layers.values()
            for k in ("w", "m", "v")
            for a in s[k]
        ) + sum(
            g.nbytes for s in self.layers.values() for g in s["grads"].values()
        )

    def state(self, attrs):
        layer = self.layers.get(attrs["layer"])
        if layer is None:
            raise ValueError(
                f"Not holding owned layer {attrs['layer']}: its load "
                f"task ran elsewhere"
            )
        return layer

    def execute(self, op, inputs, attrs):
        if op in VOCAB_OPS:
            return self.vocab(op, inputs, attrs)
        decoder = attrs.get("decoder", False)
        if op == "tf_owned_load":
            n = len(inputs) // 3
            self.layers[attrs["layer"]] = dict(
                w=[np.array(a, dtype=np.float64) for a in inputs[:n]],
                m=[np.array(a, dtype=np.float64) for a in inputs[n : 2 * n]],
                v=[np.array(a, dtype=np.float64) for a in inputs[2 * n :]],
                grads={},
            )
            return np.zeros((1, 1))
        layer = self.state(attrs)
        if op in ("tf_owned_layer", "tf_owned_grad"):
            x, mem, rest = layer_inputs(inputs, attrs)
        if op == "tf_owned_layer":
            return layer_forward(x, mem, layer["w"], attrs)[0]
        if op == "tf_owned_grad":
            g = rest[0]
            dx, dmem, grads = layer_backward(
                x, mem, g, layer["w"], attrs, defer=self.split_backward
            )
            d = x.shape[1]
            packed_rows, _ = packed_layout([w.shape for w in layer["w"]], d)
            # A retry overwrites its band.
            if self.split_backward:
                layer["grads"].pop(attrs["band"], None)
                layer.setdefault("pending", {})[attrs["band"]] = (
                    grads,
                    packed_rows,
                    d,
                )
            else:
                layer["grads"][attrs["band"]] = pack(grads, packed_rows, d)
            return np.concatenate(
                [dx] + ([dmem] if decoder else []) + [np.zeros((1, d))], axis=0
            )
        if op == "tf_owned_step":
            self.flush()
            bands = attrs["bands"]
            if sorted(layer["grads"]) != list(range(bands)):
                raise ValueError(
                    f"Layer {attrs['layer']} has gradients for bands "
                    f"{sorted(layer['grads'])}, expected {bands}"
                )
            first = layer["grads"][0]
            total = np.zeros_like(first)
            for k in range(bands):  # add_many's order: zeros, then each band
                total += layer["grads"][k]
            shapes = [w.shape for w in layer["w"]]
            grads = unpack(
                total, packed_layout(shapes, first.shape[1])[0], shapes
            )
            b1, b2, step, lr, eps = (
                attrs["beta1"],
                attrs["beta2"],
                attrs["step"],
                attrs["learning_rate"],
                attrs["epsilon"],
            )
            muon = attrs.get("optimizer") == "muon"
            for i, g in enumerate(grads):  # adam_step's arithmetic
                if muon and min(g.shape) > 1:
                    # M-2: Muon on the layer's weight matrices, as
                    # ygaraty/reference/muon.py; its buffer lives in m.
                    momentum = attrs.get("momentum", 0.95)
                    buffer = momentum * layer["m"][i] + g
                    scale = max(1.0, g.shape[0] / g.shape[1]) ** 0.5
                    layer["w"][i] = layer["w"][i] - attrs[
                        "muon_learning_rate"
                    ] * scale * orthogonalize(
                        g + momentum * buffer, attrs.get("ns_steps", 5)
                    )
                    layer["m"][i] = buffer
                    continue
                m = b1 * layer["m"][i] + (1 - b1) * g
                v = b2 * layer["v"][i] + (1 - b2) * g**2
                layer["w"][i] = layer["w"][i] - lr * (m / (1 - b1**step)) / (
                    np.sqrt(v / (1 - b2**step)) + eps
                )
                layer["m"][i], layer["v"][i] = m, v
            layer["grads"] = {}
            return np.zeros((1, 1))
        if op == "tf_owned_publish":
            return layer[attrs["kind"]][attrs["index"]].copy()
        return None

    def vocab(self, op, inputs, attrs):
        """A vocabulary shard's operations; its state is w = [table rows, bias
        columns] and their moments."""
        if op == "tf_vocab_load":
            copies = [np.array(a, dtype=np.float64) for a in inputs]
            self.layers[attrs["layer"]] = dict(
                w=copies[0:2],
                m=copies[2:4],
                v=copies[4:6],
                grads={},
                embed=None,
            )
            return np.zeros((1, 1))
        shard = self.state(attrs)
        table, bias = shard["w"]
        if op == "tf_vocab_gather":
            return table[attrs["ids"]]
        if op == "tf_vocab_stats":
            return vocab_stats(
                inputs[0] @ table.T + bias, attrs["targets"], attrs["rows"][0]
            )
        if op == "tf_vocab_grad":
            h, stats, seed = (
                inputs[0],
                inputs[1 : 1 + attrs["shards"]],
                inputs[1 + attrs["shards"]],
            )
            lse, _, _ = combine_stats(stats)
            target = np.asarray(attrs["targets"], dtype=np.int64)
            valid = target != attrs.get("ignore_index")
            local = target - attrs["rows"][0]
            inside = np.flatnonzero((local >= 0) & (local < len(table)))
            # ce_backward's arithmetic on this shard's columns of the logits.
            grad = np.exp(h @ table.T + bias - lse[:, None])
            grad -= attrs.get("label_smoothing", 0.0) / attrs["vocab"]
            grad[inside, local[inside]] -= 1 - attrs.get(
                "label_smoothing", 0.0
            )
            grad *= valid[:, None] * seed[0, 0] / attrs["count"]
            shard["grads"][attrs["band"]] = (
                grad.T @ h,
                grad.sum(axis=0, keepdims=True),
            )  # a retry overwrites its band
            return np.concatenate(
                [grad @ table, np.zeros((1, h.shape[1]))], axis=0
            )
        if op == "tf_vocab_embed_grad":
            if "scatter" in attrs:
                # The gathered rows' gradient from the embeddings' gradient
                # bands: each side's rows
                # scattered in order (embedding_backward's arithmetic), then
                # the sides summed.
                total, start = None, 0
                for count, factor in zip(attrs["sides"], attrs["factors"]):
                    side = np.zeros((len(attrs["ids"]), inputs[0].shape[1]))
                    for k in range(start, start + count):
                        local = np.asarray(attrs["scatter"][k], dtype=np.int64)
                        keep = local >= 0
                        np.add.at(
                            side,
                            local[keep],
                            inputs[k][keep] * factor
                            if factor != 1.0
                            else inputs[k][keep],
                        )
                    total = side if total is None else total + side
                    start += count
                shard["embed"] = (
                    np.asarray(attrs["ids"], dtype=np.int64),
                    total,
                )
            else:
                shard["embed"] = (
                    np.asarray(attrs["ids"], dtype=np.int64),
                    np.array(inputs[0]),
                )
            return np.zeros((1, 1))
        if op == "tf_vocab_step":
            bands = attrs["bands"]
            if sorted(shard["grads"]) != list(range(bands)):
                raise ValueError(
                    f"Shard {attrs['layer']} has gradients for bands "
                    f"{sorted(shard['grads'])}, expected {bands}"
                )
            if attrs.get("embed") and shard["embed"] is None:
                raise ValueError(
                    f"Shard {attrs['layer']} has no embedding gradient"
                )
            dtable, dbias = np.zeros_like(table), np.zeros_like(bias)
            for k in range(bands):
                dtable += shard["grads"][k][0]
                dbias += shard["grads"][k][1]
            if attrs.get("embed"):
                ids, rows = shard["embed"]
                np.add.at(dtable, ids, rows)
            b1, b2, step, lr, eps = (
                attrs["beta1"],
                attrs["beta2"],
                attrs["step"],
                attrs["learning_rate"],
                attrs["epsilon"],
            )
            for i, g in enumerate((dtable, dbias)):  # adam_step's arithmetic
                m = b1 * shard["m"][i] + (1 - b1) * g
                v = b2 * shard["v"][i] + (1 - b2) * g**2
                shard["w"][i] = shard["w"][i] - lr * (m / (1 - b1**step)) / (
                    np.sqrt(v / (1 - b2**step)) + eps
                )
                shard["m"][i], shard["v"][i] = m, v
            shard["grads"], shard["embed"] = {}, None
            return np.zeros((1, 1))
        if op == "tf_vocab_publish":
            return shard[attrs["kind"]][attrs["index"]].copy()
        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported vocabulary operation: {op}")

# File: packages/inference-support/src/ygaraty/inference_support/artifacts.py
"""Safe local artifact lookup shared by inference backends."""
from pathlib import Path


def local_file(root, name):
    root = Path(root).resolve()
    if not isinstance(name, str) or Path(name).is_absolute() or '..' in Path(name).parts:
        raise ValueError('Model artifact paths must be relative to their bundle')
    path = (root / name).resolve()
    if not path.is_relative_to(root) or not path.is_file():
        raise ValueError('Model bundle file is missing or outside the model directory')
    return path


# File: packages/inference-support/src/ygaraty/inference_support/chat.py
"""Backend-independent prompt formatting and conversation identifiers."""
import re

QWEN_CHAT = dict(first="", generation="<|im_start|>assistant\n<think>\n\n</think>\n\n")


CHAT_SEPARATORS = ("\n", "<|im_sep|>")  # after the role: ChatML's newline, Phi-4's <|im_sep|>
CHAT_ENDS = ("\n", "")  # after <|im_end|>: ChatML's newline, nothing (Phi-4)


def encode_ids(tokenizer, text):
    """Token IDs of `text`, with the GIL released while the Rust tokenizer runs.

    `Tokenizer.encode` holds the GIL: one ~1.2 M-character prompt froze every other coordinator thread (all
    sessions' streams and contributor polls) for about 0.4 s, and four arriving together for 1.6 s. `encode_batch`
    returns the same IDs and releases it (4 such prompts: 0.58 s, other threads paused at most 24 ms).
    """
    return tokenizer.encode_batch([text])[0].ids


def chat_format(value=None):
    """A bundle's ChatML pieces (`ygaraty model inspect` derives them from the checkpoint's own template),
    checked; Qwen3.5's (thinking off) when absent. A message is `<|im_start|>{role}{sep}{content}<|im_end|>{end}`
    (`sep`, `end`: ChatML's newlines when absent)."""
    if value is None:
        return dict(QWEN_CHAT, sep="\n", end="\n")
    if (not isinstance(value, dict) or not isinstance(value.get("first"), str) or not isinstance(value.get("generation"), str)
            or len(value["first"]) > 4096 or not value["generation"].startswith("<|im_start|>assistant") or len(value["generation"]) > 256
            or value.get("sep", "\n") not in CHAT_SEPARATORS or value.get("end", "\n") not in CHAT_ENDS):
        raise ValueError("Invalid chat format")
    return dict(first=value["first"], generation=value["generation"], sep=value.get("sep", "\n"), end=value.get("end", "\n"))


def chat_message(role, content, fmt):
    return f"<|im_start|>{role}{fmt['sep']}{content}<|im_end|>{fmt['end']}"


def chat_prompt(messages, fmt=None):
    """The prompt for a whole message list ([(role, content)]) in the chat format, ending with the assistant's
    opening; the conversation prefix only when there is no system message (the template's own default)."""
    fmt = chat_format(fmt)
    text = "" if messages and messages[0][0] == "system" else fmt["first"]
    return text + "".join(chat_message(role, content, fmt) for role, content in messages) + fmt["generation"]


def validate_conversation_id(value):
    """An opaque session-local context selector, not an authorization credential."""
    if not isinstance(value, str) or not re.fullmatch(r'[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,64}', value):
        raise ValueError('conversation_id must be 1–64 letters, digits, underscores or hyphens')
    return value


# File: packages/inference-support/src/ygaraty/inference_support/http.py
"""HTTP replies and streaming shared by inference hosts."""
import json


def reply(h, code, body=b"", mime="application/json", extra=()):
    """One complete response on the request handler `h` (this module's or the general coordinator's)."""
    if isinstance(body, (dict, list)):
        body = json.dumps(body).encode()
    elif isinstance(body, str):
        body = body.encode()
    h.send_response(code)
    h.send_header("Content-Type", mime)
    h.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(body)))
    h.send_header("Cache-Control", "no-store")
    for k, v in extra:
        h.send_header(k, v)
    h.end_headers()
    h.wfile.write(body)

def stream(h, session, events):
    """Newline-delimited JSON events, chunked as they come."""
    if callable(getattr(h, "stream", None)):
        def guarded():
            try:
                yield from events
            except (BrokenPipeError, ConnectionResetError):
                return
            except Exception as error:
                session.say(f"error in chat stream: {error!r}")
                yield dict(error=repr(error))
            finally:
                if hasattr(events, 'close'):
                    events.close()
        return h.stream(guarded())
    h.send_response(200)
    h.send_header("Content-Type", "application/x-ndjson")
    h.send_header("Cache-Control", "no-store")
    h.send_header("Transfer-Encoding", "chunked")
    h.end_headers()

    def chunk(event):
        line = (json.dumps(event) + "\n").encode()
        h.wfile.write(f"{len(line):X}\r\n".encode() + line + b"\r\n")
        h.wfile.flush()
    try:
        for event in events:
            chunk(event)
    except (BrokenPipeError, ConnectionResetError):
        return
    except Exception as error:  # end the stream with the error, keep serving
        session.say(f"error in chat stream: {error!r}")
        chunk(dict(error=repr(error)))
    h.wfile.write(b"0\r\n\r\n")
    h.wfile.flush()


# File: packages/inference-support/src/ygaraty/inference_support/roster.py
"""Bounded contributor reservations and cumulative, SQLite-backed history."""
from contextlib import closing
import json
from pathlib import Path
import sqlite3
import threading
import time


class ContributorRoster:
    def __init__(self, assignments, timeout=90, clock=time.monotonic, database=None, preparing_timeout=300):
        self.assignments = assignments
        self.timeout, self.clock, self.preparing_timeout = timeout, clock, preparing_timeout
        self.records = {}
        self.lock = threading.RLock()
        self.database = Path(database).resolve() if database is not None else None
        if self.database:
            self.database.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            with closing(sqlite3.connect(self.database)) as db, db:
                db.execute("""CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS contributor_history (
                    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, slot INTEGER, state TEXT NOT NULL,
                    reason TEXT NOT NULL, assignment TEXT NOT NULL)""")
                # A restarted coordinator cannot resume the previous browser/GPU session.
                db.execute("UPDATE contributor_history SET state='former', reason='coordinator restarted' "
                           "WHERE state IN ('active', 'preparing')")
                for ident, slot, state, reason, assignment in db.execute(
                        "SELECT id, slot, state, reason, assignment FROM contributor_history ORDER BY rowid"):
                    self.records[ident] = dict(id=ident, slot=slot, state=state,
                                              reason=reason, assignment=json.loads(assignment), last_seen=None)

    def _save(self, record):
        if self.database:
            with closing(sqlite3.connect(self.database)) as db, db:
                db.execute("""INSERT INTO contributor_history VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
                    ON CONFLICT(id) DO UPDATE SET state=excluded.state, reason=excluded.reason""",
                    (record['id'], record['slot'], record['state'], record['reason'],
                     json.dumps(record['assignment'])))

    def reserve(self, ident, prefer=None, *, strict=False):
        """A free slot for `ident`: `prefer` if it is free (a browser coming back to the stage whose weights
        it has cached), else the first free one."""
        with self.lock:
            if ident in self.records:
                return dict(self.records[ident])
            occupied = {r['slot'] for r in self.records.values() if r['state'] != 'former'}
            free = [i for i in range(len(self.assignments)) if i not in occupied]
            slot = prefer if prefer in free else (None if strict else next(iter(free), None))
            if slot is None:
                return dict(id=ident, slot=None, state='rejected', reason='capacity reached', assignment=None)
            record = dict(id=ident, slot=slot, state='preparing' if slot is not None else 'former',
                          reason='' if slot is not None else 'capacity reached', last_seen=self.clock(),
                          assignment=self.assignments[slot] if slot is not None else None)
            self._save(record)
            self.records[ident] = record
            return dict(record)

    def touch(self, ident):
        with self.lock:
            record = self.records[ident]
            if record['state'] == 'former':
                raise ValueError('Contributor reservation has ended')
            record['last_seen'] = self.clock()

    def activate(self, ident):
        with self.lock:
            self.touch(ident)
            record = dict(self.records[ident], state='active')
            self._save(record)
            self.records[ident] = record

    def retire(self, ident, reason):
        with self.lock:
            record = self.records.get(ident)
            if record and record['state'] != 'former':
                retired = dict(record, state='former', reason=reason)
                self._save(retired)
                self.records[ident] = retired

    def expire(self):
        with self.lock:
            # A preparing browser downloads its stage, maybe for minutes on a slow link, often in a background
            # tab whose timers the browser slows: it gets longer before it counts as gone.
            expired = [dict(r) for r in self.records.values()
                       if r['state'] != 'former' and self.clock()-r['last_seen'] >
                       (self.preparing_timeout if r['state'] == 'preparing' else self.timeout)]
            for record in expired:
                self.retire(record['id'], 'heartbeat expired')
            return expired

    def revive(self, ident, state='preparing'):
        """A contributor retired for missing heartbeats that turns out to be alive gets its slot back, if no
        one has taken it. Returns whether it did."""
        with self.lock:
            record = self.records.get(ident)
            if not record or record['state'] != 'former' or record.get('reason') != 'heartbeat expired':
                return False
            if any(r['slot'] == record['slot'] and r['state'] != 'former' for r in self.records.values()):
                return False
            revived = dict(record, state=state, reason='', last_seen=self.clock())
            self._save(revived)
            self.records[ident] = revived
            return True

    def snapshot(self):
        with self.lock:
            records = [dict(r) for r in self.records.values()]
            return dict(required=len(self.assignments), active=sum(r['state']=='active' for r in records),
                        preparing=sum(r['state']=='preparing' for r in records),
                        former=sum(r['state']=='former' for r in records), contributors=records)


# File: packages/inference-support/src/ygaraty/inference_support/throughput.py
"""Bounded, aggregate session throughput; never records prompts or token IDs."""
import math
import threading
import time


class Throughput:
    window = 10

    def __init__(self, clock=time.monotonic):
        self.clock = clock
        self.lock = threading.RLock()
        self.tokens = 0
        self.seconds = 0.0
        self.peak = 0.0
        self.active = set()
        self.since = None
        self.buckets = {}
        self.closed = False

    def restore(self, saved):
        with self.lock:
            self.tokens = int(saved.get('generated_tokens', 0))
            self.seconds = float(saved.get('active_seconds', 0))
            self.peak = float(saved.get('peak_tokens_per_s', 0))

    def start(self, slot):
        with self.lock:
            if self.closed:
                return
            if not self.active:
                self.since = self.clock()
            self.active.add(slot)

    def _rate(self, now):
        cutoff = math.floor(now) - self.window + 1
        self.buckets = {s: n for s, n in self.buckets.items() if s >= cutoff}
        return sum(self.buckets.values()) / self.window

    def token(self, slot):
        with self.lock:
            if self.closed or slot not in self.active:
                return
            now = self.clock()
            second = math.floor(now)
            self.tokens += 1
            self.buckets[second] = self.buckets.get(second, 0) + 1
            self.peak = max(self.peak, self._rate(now))

    def finish(self, slot):
        with self.lock:
            self.active.discard(slot)
            if not self.active and self.since is not None:
                self.seconds += max(0, self.clock() - self.since)
                self.since = None

    def close(self):
        with self.lock:
            for slot in list(self.active):
                self.finish(slot)
            self.closed = True

    def snapshot(self):
        with self.lock:
            now = self.clock()
            seconds = self.seconds + (max(0, now-self.since) if self.since is not None else 0)
            return dict(version=1, generated_tokens=self.tokens,
                        active_seconds=round(seconds, 6),
                        average_tokens_per_s=round(self.tokens/seconds, 3) if seconds else None,
                        recent_tokens_per_s=round(self._rate(now), 3),
                        peak_tokens_per_s=round(self.peak, 3),
                        active_chats=len(self.active), window_seconds=self.window)


# File: packages/inference-support/tests/test_support.py
"""Backend-neutral behavior, with no model, GPU, network or host framework."""
import io
import sys
from types import SimpleNamespace

import pytest

from ygaraty.inference_support.artifacts import local_file
from ygaraty.inference_support.chat import chat_prompt, encode_ids, validate_conversation_id
from ygaraty.inference_support.http import reply, stream
from ygaraty.inference_support.roster import ContributorRoster
from ygaraty.inference_support.throughput import Throughput


def test_prompt_and_tokenizer_adapter():
    tokenizer = SimpleNamespace(encode_batch=lambda texts: [SimpleNamespace(ids=[len(texts[0])])])
    assert encode_ids(tokenizer, 'hello') == [5]
    assert '<|im_start|>user\nhello<|im_end|>' in chat_prompt([('user', 'hello')])
    assert validate_conversation_id('c_1-A') == 'c_1-A'
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        validate_conversation_id('../escape')


def test_artifacts_reject_parent_and_symlink_escape(tmp_path):
    root = tmp_path / 'model'; root.mkdir()
    (root / 'config').write_text('{}')
    (tmp_path / 'secret').write_text('private')
    (root / 'link').symlink_to(tmp_path / 'secret')
    assert local_file(root, 'config') == root / 'config'
    for name in ('../secret', 'link', str(tmp_path / 'secret'), 'missing'):
        with pytest.raises(ValueError):
            local_file(root, name)


def test_throughput_counts_concurrent_wall_time_once():
    now = [0.0]
    tracker = Throughput(clock=lambda: now[0])
    tracker.start('a'); tracker.start('b')
    now[0] = 2.0
    tracker.token('a'); tracker.token('b')
    tracker.finish('a'); tracker.finish('b')
    result = tracker.snapshot()
    assert result['generated_tokens'] == 2
    assert result['active_seconds'] == 2
    assert result['average_tokens_per_s'] == 1


def test_roster_restart_retires_live_reservations(tmp_path):
    path = tmp_path / 'roster.sqlite'
    roster = ContributorRoster([{'slot': 0}], database=path)
    roster.reserve('worker', 0)
    roster.activate('worker')
    restored = ContributorRoster([{'slot': 0}], database=path)
    assert restored.records['worker']['state'] == 'former'
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        restored.touch('worker')


def test_http_reply_and_stream_close_iterator():
    headers = {}
    handler = SimpleNamespace(wfile=io.BytesIO(), send_response=lambda code: headers.update(status=code),
                              send_header=lambda k,v: headers.update({k:v}), end_headers=lambda: None)
    reply(handler, 200, {'ok': True})
    assert headers['Content-Length'] == str(len(handler.wfile.getvalue()))
    closed = []
    def events():
        try:
            yield {'text': 'hello'}
        finally:
            closed.append(True)
    output = []
    stream(SimpleNamespace(stream=lambda it: output.extend(it)), SimpleNamespace(say=lambda text: None), events())
    assert output == [{'text': 'hello'}]
    assert closed == [True]

'''

# File: packages/webgpu/src/ygaraty/webgpu/_legacy/chat_slots.py
"""Bounded independent chat histories sharing one model's GPU cache slots."""

import threading

from .qwen_protocol import SessionProtocol, validate_conversation_id


class SlotMailbox:
    def __init__(self, channel, stage):
        self.channel, self.stage = channel, stage

    def put(self, packet):
        kind, meta, body = packet
        c = self.channel
        c.parent.inbox[self.stage].put(
            (
                kind,
                dict(
                    meta,
                    conv=c.slot,
                    epoch=c.parent.epoch,
                    chat_epoch=c.generation,
                    chat_turn=c.turn_id,
                ),
                body,
            )
        )


class ChatChannel(SessionProtocol):
    """Reuse turn streaming while keeping every mutable chat field private."""

    concurrent_chats = None
    chat_direct = None  # The legacy direct-browser grant has only one owner.

    def __init__(self, parent, identity):
        self.parent = parent
        self.slot = None
        self.generation = 0
        self.chat_lock = threading.Lock()
        self.active_conversation = identity
        self.chat_histories = {}
        self.turn, self.turns, self.turn_ids = None, [], []
        self.position, self.last_token = 0, None
        self.history, self.replay, self.diverged = [], False, False
        self.turn_id, self.finished_turn, self.fed = 0, None, {}
        self.inbox = [SlotMailbox(self, k) for k in range(len(parent.inbox))]
        self.busy = False

    def __getattr__(self, name):
        return getattr(self.parent, name)

    def say(self, line):
        self.parent.say(line)

    def deliver(self, stage, packet):
        self.inbox[stage].put(packet)

    def drop_stage_state(self):
        with self.parent.lock:
            pool = self.parent.concurrent_chats
            pool.generations[self.slot] += 1
            self.generation = pool.generations[self.slot]
            self.worker_epoch = self.parent.epoch
            self.turn = None
            self.position, self.replay = 0, bool(self.history)
            self.fed = {}
            for box in self.inbox:
                box.put(("reset", {}, b""))

    def reset_conversation(self):
        self.history, self.last_token = [], None
        self.drop_stage_state()


class ChatSlots:
    def __init__(self, parent, count):
        self.parent = parent
        self.channels = {}
        self.slots = [None] * count
        self.generations = [0] * count

    def channel(self, slot):
        if type(slot) is not int or not 0 <= slot < len(self.slots):
            return None
        return self.slots[slot]

    def accepts(self, meta):
        channel = self.channel(meta.get("conv", 0))
        return (
            channel is not None
            and meta.get("chat_epoch") == channel.generation
            and meta.get("chat_turn") == channel.turn_id
        )

    def chat(self, message, reset=False, cancel_event=None, **kwargs):
        identity = validate_conversation_id(
            kwargs.get("conversation_id")
            or (
                "api"
                if kwargs.get("prompt_tokens") is not None
                else "legacy-browser"
            )
        )
        with self.parent.lock:
            channel, error = self.acquire(identity, cancel_event)
        if error:
            yield error
        if channel is None:
            return
        try:
            yield from channel._chat_serial(
                message,
                reset,
                cancel_event,
                **dict(kwargs, conversation_id=identity),
            )
        finally:
            with self.parent.lock:
                channel.busy = False

    def acquire(self, identity, cancel_event):
        if cancel_event is not None and cancel_event.is_set():
            return None, None
        channel = self.channels.get(identity)
        if channel is None:
            if len(self.channels) >= 16:
                return None, {
                    "error": "This session reached its 16-conversation limit."
                }
            channel = ChatChannel(self.parent, identity)
        if channel.busy:
            return None, {
                "error": "This conversation already has an answer in progress."
            }
        if channel.slot is None:
            slot = next(
                (i for i, c in enumerate(self.slots) if c is None), None
            )
            if slot is None:
                slot = next(
                    (i for i, c in enumerate(self.slots) if not c.busy), None
                )
            if slot is None:
                return None, {
                    "error": "All chat slots are busy. Retry after completion."
                }
            previous = self.slots[slot]
            if previous is not None:
                previous.slot = None
                previous.position, previous.replay = 0, bool(previous.history)
            self.channels[identity] = channel
            self.slots[slot] = channel
            channel.slot = slot
            channel.drop_stage_state()
        elif channel.worker_epoch != self.parent.epoch:
            channel.drop_stage_state()
        channel.busy = True
        return channel, None

    def interrupt(self, reason):
        """Global worker replacement resets all slots; wake every stream."""
        for channel in self.channels.values():
            if channel.turn is not None:
                channel.turn["events"].put({"error": reason})
            channel.position, channel.replay = 0, bool(channel.history)
            channel.fed = {}

    def active(self):
        return any(c.busy for c in self.channels.values())

# File: packages/webgpu/src/ygaraty/webgpu/_legacy/conversations.py
"""Configurable isolated, interleaved chat slots over shared model weights.

A slot generation fences late packets without resetting other conversations.
Each turn replays its own text history into an empty slot; no state is shared.
"""

import queue
import threading
import time
import numpy as np
from ..buffers import TokenMailbox


class Conversations:
    def __init__(self, engine, slots=8):
        self.engine, self.slots = engine, slots
        self.lock = threading.RLock()
        self.active, self.histories = {}, {}
        self.generation = 0

    def current(self, meta):
        t = self.active.get(meta.get("conv", 0))
        return t if t and meta.get("generation") == t["generation"] else None

    def abort(self, reason):
        with self.lock:
            for t in self.active.values():
                if getattr(self.engine, "throughput", None):
                    self.engine.throughput.finish(t["slot"])
                t["events"].put({"error": reason})

    def compare(self, t):
        n = min(len(t["fed"]), len(t["tokens"]))
        if t["fed"][:n] != t["tokens"][:n]:
            t["events"].put(
                {
                    "error": "Contributors disagreed about the answer; retry this conversation."
                }
            )

    def fed(self, meta):
        with self.lock:
            t = self.current(meta)
            if not t:
                return
            items = meta.get("items")
            if (
                not isinstance(items, list)
                or any(type(x) is not int for x in items)
                or len(t["fed"]) + len(items) > t["limit"]
            ):
                raise ValueError("Invalid token receipt report")
            t["fed"].extend(items)
            self.compare(t)

    def token(self, meta, token, forward):
        e = self.engine
        with self.lock:
            t = self.current(meta)
            if not t or len(t["tokens"]) >= t["limit"] or t["complete"]:
                return
            if getattr(e, "throughput", None):
                e.throughput.token(t["slot"])
            t["tokens"].append(token)
            t["times"].append(time.time())
            self.compare(t)
            t["events"].put({"token": token})
            if token in e.stop_ids or len(t["tokens"]) >= t["limit"]:
                t["complete"] = True
                if getattr(e, "throughput", None):
                    e.throughput.finish(t["slot"])
                t["events"].put(None)
            elif forward:
                e.deliver(
                    0,
                    (
                        "token",
                        dict(
                            token=token,
                            conv=t["slot"],
                            generation=t["generation"],
                            epoch=e.epoch,
                        ),
                        b"",
                    ),
                )

    def chat(
        self,
        message,
        reset=False,
        cancel_event=None,
        *,
        prompt_tokens=None,
        max_tokens=None,
        direct_lease=None,
        conversation_id=None,
    ):
        from .qwen_protocol import validate_conversation_id, chat_prompt
        from .chat_contract import encode_ids

        e = self.engine
        selected = validate_conversation_id(
            conversation_id or "legacy-browser"
        )
        if not message and prompt_tokens is None:
            with self.lock:
                busy = any(
                    x["conversation"] == selected for x in self.active.values()
                )
                if reset and not busy:
                    self.histories.pop(selected, None)
            yield (
                {
                    "error": "This conversation already has a response in progress."
                }
                if busy
                else {"done": True, "position": 0}
            )
            return
        t = None
        direct = getattr(e, "chat_direct", None)
        invitation = None
        with self.lock:
            if e.state != "running":
                failure = "The contributors are not ready."
            elif any(
                x["conversation"] == selected for x in self.active.values()
            ):
                failure = (
                    "This conversation already has a response in progress."
                )
            elif len(self.active) >= self.slots:
                failure = "All conversation slots are busy. Retry when a response finishes."
            else:
                failure = None
                history = (
                    []
                    if reset or prompt_tokens is not None
                    else list(self.histories.get(selected, []))
                )
                messages = history + [("user", message)]
                tokens = (
                    list(prompt_tokens)
                    if prompt_tokens is not None
                    else encode_ids(
                        e.tokenizer, chat_prompt(messages, e.chat_style)
                    )
                )
                remaining = e.capacity - len(tokens)
                limit = (
                    remaining
                    if max_tokens is None
                    else min(max_tokens, remaining)
                )
                if limit <= 0:
                    failure = "The conversation fills the context window. Start a new conversation or shorten the messages."
                else:
                    slot = next(
                        i for i in range(self.slots) if i not in self.active
                    )
                    self.generation += 1
                    t = dict(
                        slot=slot,
                        conversation=selected,
                        generation=self.generation,
                        limit=limit,
                        tokens=[],
                        times=[],
                        fed=[],
                        events=TokenMailbox(),
                        complete=False,
                    )
                    self.active[slot] = t
                    if getattr(e, "throughput", None):
                        e.throughput.start(slot)
                    packet_meta = dict(
                        conv=slot,
                        generation=self.generation,
                        epoch=e.epoch,
                        turn=self.generation,
                        limit=limit,
                    )
                    for box in e.inbox:
                        box.put(("reset", packet_meta, b""))
        if failure:
            code = (
                "session_busy"
                if "eight" in failure
                else "conversation_busy"
                if "in progress" in failure
                else "inference_error"
            )
            yield {
                "error": failure,
                "code": code,
                "status": 429
                if code == "session_busy"
                else 409
                if code == "conversation_busy"
                else 400,
            }
            return
        try:
            if cancel_event is not None and cancel_event.is_set():
                return
            start, clock = time.time(), time.monotonic()
            packet = (
                "prompt",
                packet_meta,
                np.asarray(tokens, dtype=np.uint32).tobytes(),
            )
            if direct:
                invitation = direct.prepare(
                    direct_lease,
                    tokens,
                    t["generation"],
                    e.epoch,
                    limit,
                    conversation_id=selected,
                    conv=t["slot"],
                    generation=t["generation"],
                )
            if invitation:
                yield invitation
            else:
                e.inbox[0].put(packet)
            shown, last = "", time.monotonic()
            while True:
                if cancel_event is not None and cancel_event.is_set():
                    return
                if invitation and direct.relay_due():
                    e.inbox[0].put(packet)
                try:
                    event = t["events"].get(timeout=0.25)
                except queue.Empty:
                    if time.monotonic() - last >= e.args.turn_timeout:
                        yield {
                            "error": f"No token for {e.args.turn_timeout} s"
                        }
                        return
                    continue
                last = time.monotonic()
                if event is None:
                    break
                if "error" in event:
                    yield event
                    return
                text = e.tokenizer.decode(
                    [x for x in t["tokens"] if x not in e.stop_ids]
                )
                if not text.endswith("\ufffd") and text != shown:
                    yield {"text": text[len(shown) :]}
                    shown = text
            times = t["times"]
            record = dict(
                prompt_tokens=len(tokens),
                start_position=0,
                generated=len(t["tokens"]),
                total_s=time.monotonic() - clock,
                first_token_s=times[0] - start if times else None,
                tokens_per_s=(len(times) - 1) / (times[-1] - times[0])
                if len(times) > 1 and times[-1] > times[0]
                else None,
                ms_per_token=float(np.median(np.diff(times)) * 1000)
                if len(times) > 1
                else None,
                stopped="end" if t["tokens"][-1] in e.stop_ids else "length",
                prompt_transport=(direct.prompt_transport() or "unknown")
                if invitation
                else "coordinator",
            )
            with self.lock:
                if prompt_tokens is None:
                    self.histories.pop(selected, None)
                    self.histories[selected] = messages + [
                        ("assistant", shown)
                    ]
                    while len(self.histories) > 16:
                        self.histories.pop(next(iter(self.histories)))
                e.turns.append(record)
            yield dict(
                record,
                done=True,
                position=len(tokens) + len(t["tokens"]),
                full_text=shown,
            )
        finally:
            if invitation:
                direct.finish()
            with self.lock:
                # Fence outstanding direct packets before a slot is reused.
                self.generation += 1
                for box in e.inbox:
                    box.put(
                        (
                            "reset",
                            dict(
                                conv=t["slot"],
                                generation=self.generation,
                                epoch=e.epoch,
                            ),
                            b"",
                        )
                    )
                self.active.pop(t["slot"], None)
                if getattr(e, "throughput", None):
                    e.throughput.finish(t["slot"])


# File: packages/webgpu/src/ygaraty/webgpu/_legacy/model_artifacts.py
"""Safe local artifact lookup shared by inference backends."""

from ygaraty.inference_support.artifacts import local_file

from pathlib import Path




def bundle_capacity(data):
    if not isinstance(data, dict) or data.get("format") != "ygaraty-webgpu-v1":
        raise ValueError("Unsupported bundle format")
    options = data.get("options")
    if not isinstance(options, dict) or not isinstance(
        data.get("stages"), list
    ):
        raise ValueError("Invalid bundle metadata")
    capacity = options.get("capacity")
    if type(capacity) is not int or not 1 <= capacity <= 2**31 - 1:
        raise ValueError("Invalid bundle context capacity")
    for stage in data.get("stages", []):
        if not isinstance(stage, dict) or not isinstance(
            stage.get("fixture"), dict
        ):
            raise ValueError("Invalid prepared stage")
        limit = stage["fixture"].get("max_positions", capacity)
        if type(limit) is not int or limit < capacity:
            raise ValueError(
                "Bundle context exceeds a prepared stage capacity"
            )
    return capacity


# File: packages/webgpu/src/ygaraty/webgpu/_legacy/prepared.py
"""Load precomputed WebGPU manifests. Never load/execute GGUF tensors on the server."""

import copy
import queue
import json
import os
import re
import secrets
from pathlib import Path
from types import SimpleNamespace
from urllib.parse import quote
import numpy as np
from .qwen_protocol import (
    SessionProtocol,
    SELFTEST_CASES,
    MATVEC_DEFAULT,
    STAGE_NAMES,
    kernel_defaults,
    SignalRejected,
)


from .model_artifacts import local_file


def validate_distribution_plan(stored, compiled):
    """Accept only the current plan or its exact pre-contract v2 projection."""
    from ..operations import legacy_stage

    if stored == compiled:
        return
    legacy = dict(
        compiled,
        version=2,
        stages=[legacy_stage(stage) for stage in compiled["stages"]],
    )
    if stored != legacy:
        raise ValueError("Stored distribution plan differs from stages")


class PreparedSession(SessionProtocol):
    def __init__(
        self,
        bundle_path,
        out,
        assignments,
        memory_gb,
        *,
        storage=None,
        route="relay",
        turn=None,
        context_window=None,
        conversation_slots=8,
        metadata=None,
    ):
        from tokenizers import Tokenizer

        bundle_path = Path(bundle_path)
        if bundle_path.stat().st_size > 16 * 1024 * 1024:
            raise ValueError("Bundle manifest exceeds 16 MiB")
        data = (
            copy.deepcopy(metadata)
            if metadata is not None
            else json.loads(bundle_path.read_text())
        )
        if data.get("format") != "ygaraty-webgpu-v1":
            raise ValueError("Unsupported WebGPU bundle format")
        self.stages = copy.deepcopy(data["stages"])
        if not 1 <= len(self.stages) <= len(STAGE_NAMES) or [
            s["layers"] for s in self.stages
        ] != [a["layers"] for a in assignments]:
            raise ValueError(
                "Prepared stages do not match the selected distribution plan"
            )
        for i, stage in enumerate(self.stages):
            if (
                stage["stage"] != i
                or stage["fixture"].get("route") != "relay"
                or stage["fixture"].get("conversations") != 1
            ):
                raise ValueError(
                    "User chat bundles require ordered stages, one conversation and relay routing"
                )
            if stage["fixture"].get(
                "ice"
            ):  # a stored artifact never carries TURN servers or credentials
                raise ValueError(
                    "Bundles must not contain ICE servers; the coordinator issues them per contributor"
                )
            stage["fixture"]["memory_budget_bytes"] = memory_gb * 1024**3
        from ..distribution import compile_distribution

        self.distribution_plan = compile_distribution(self.stages)
        if "distribution_plan" in data:
            validate_distribution_plan(
                data["distribution_plan"], self.distribution_plan
            )
        from ..bundle_validation import remote_base, remote_piece_path

        # A public HTTPS base: contributors download pieces directly.
        self.storage = remote_base(data)
        self.remote_paths = {}
        self.files, self.hashes = {}, {}
        for name, file in data["files"].items():
            path = (
                None
                if self.storage
                else local_file(bundle_path.parent, file["path"])
            )
            offset, length = file["offset"], file["length"]
            if (
                not re.fullmatch(r"[A-Za-z0-9_.-]+", name)
                or type(offset) is not int
                or type(length) is not int
                or offset < 0
                or length <= 0
                or (path is not None and offset + length > path.stat().st_size)
                or not re.fullmatch(r"[a-f0-9]{64}", file["sha256"])
            ):
                raise ValueError("Invalid prepared weight range")
            if self.storage:
                if offset != 0:
                    raise ValueError(
                        "Remote weights must be complete piece objects"
                    )
                self.remote_paths[name] = remote_piece_path(file["path"])
            self.files[name] = (path, offset, length)
            self.hashes[name] = file["sha256"]
        for stage in self.stages:
            if (
                sum(self.files[n][2] for n in stage["names"])
                > memory_gb * 1024**3
            ):
                raise ValueError(
                    "Prepared stage weights exceed the approved contribution budget"
                )
        self.test_files = {}
        for name, path in data["test_files"].items():
            source = local_file(bundle_path.parent, path)
            if source.stat().st_size > 16 * 1024 * 1024:
                raise ValueError("Self-test artifact exceeds 16 MiB")
            self.test_files[name] = source.read_bytes()
        self.selftest = data["selftest"]
        self.expected = []
        for name in data["expected"]:
            path = local_file(bundle_path.parent, name)
            if path.stat().st_size > 16 * 1024 * 1024:
                raise ValueError("Self-test reference exceeds 16 MiB")
            self.expected.append(np.load(path, allow_pickle=False))
        if not self.expected or len(self.expected) != len(
            self.selftest["cases"]
        ):
            raise ValueError("Self-test references are incomplete")
        self.f16_x = np.frombuffer(
            self.test_files[self.selftest["f16"]], dtype=np.float32
        )
        with np.errstate(over="ignore"):
            self.f16_want = self.f16_x.astype(np.float16)
        options = dict(data["options"])
        from .model_artifacts import bundle_capacity

        validated_capacity = bundle_capacity(data)
        if context_window is not None:
            if (
                type(context_window) is not int
                or not 1 <= context_window <= validated_capacity
            ):
                raise ValueError(
                    "Requested context exceeds the validated bundle capacity"
                )
            options["capacity"] = context_window
            options["chunk"] = min(options["chunk"], context_window)
        for stage in self.stages:
            fixture = stage["fixture"]
            fixture["max_positions"] = options["capacity"]
            fixture["max_tokens"] = min(
                fixture.get("max_tokens", options["chunk"]),
                options["capacity"],
            )

        for key in ("capacity", "generate", "chunk"):
            if type(options[key]) is not int or options[key] <= 0:
                raise ValueError("Invalid prepared chat settings")
        self.out = Path(out)
        self.out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        # The network path is the session's (chosen by its owner at creation), never the bundle's. A direct
        # session needs the deployment's TURN server: it fails here rather than quietly using the relay.
        if route not in ("relay", "webrtc", "private"):
            raise ValueError("Unknown session route")
        if route in ("webrtc", "private"):
            # Direct links on one local network use host candidates; TURN-only
            # links cannot work without the server.
            if turn is None and route == "private":
                raise ValueError(
                    "TURN-only sessions need a TURN server; ask an administrator"
                )
            # One stage (DO-51): no links between stages, only the chat browser's link to that contributor.
        self.turn_config = turn
        self.args = SimpleNamespace(
            out=self.out,
            chat=True,
            kv=options["kv"],
            capacity=options["capacity"],
            generate=options["capacity"],
            chunk=options["chunk"],
            turn_timeout=120,
            route="relay" if route == "relay" else "webrtc",
            ice="",
            ice_policy="relay" if route == "private" else "all",
            relay_delay=0,
            batch=False,
            contributor_db=None,
            production_chat=True,
        )
        self.key = secrets.token_urlsafe(32)
        self.tokenizer = Tokenizer.from_file(
            str(local_file(bundle_path.parent, data["tokenizer"]))
        )
        self.prompts, self.prompt, self.prompt_source = (
            [[]],
            [],
            {"chat": True},
        )
        self.eos = data["eos"]
        chat_slots = conversation_slots
        if not 1 <= chat_slots <= 16:
            raise ValueError("YGARATY_CHAT_SLOTS must be between 1 and 16")
        self.conversations, self.capacity = chat_slots, options["capacity"]
        self.args.batch = chat_slots > 1
        from .qwen_protocol import chat_format

        self.chat_style = (
            chat_format(options["chat"])
            if options.get("chat") is not None
            else None
        )  # checked; None: Qwen's
        self.splits = [self.stages[0]["layers"][0]] + [
            s["layers"][1] for s in self.stages
        ]
        self.ice = []  # contributors fetch their own short-lived credentials from /api/ice/<id>
        self.initialize_protocol(self.args)
        from .conversations import Conversations
        from .throughput import Throughput

        self.throughput = Throughput()
        self.concurrent_chat = Conversations(self, chat_slots)
        # The kernel is the deployment's choice, not the bundle's; admission checks the one the stages will use.
        # Measured on Qwen3.5 only (its manifests name no family): dense (Phi-4) and kolibri1 bundles keep one
        # row per thread.
        qwen = all(
            s["manifest"].get("config", {}).get("family", "qwen35") == "qwen35"
            for s in self.stages
        )
        matvec = (
            None
            if not qwen or os.environ.get("YGARATY_MATVEC") == "off"
            else MATVEC_DEFAULT
        )
        if matvec:
            self.selftest = dict(self.selftest, matvec=matvec)
        # Experiments 121/125/127 kernels for Qwen3.5 and Kolibri-1 (both reference-checked); dense keeps the old ones.
        families = {
            s["manifest"].get("config", {}).get("family", "qwen35")
            for s in self.stages
        }
        # DO-80: Qwen3-MoE stages run the same kernels as Kolibri-1 (checked whole-model on an A100 with them).
        kernels = (
            kernel_defaults("qwen35" if families == {"qwen35"} else "kolibri1")
            if families <= {"qwen35", "kolibri1", "qwen3moe"}
            else {}
        )
        if kernels:
            self.selftest = dict(self.selftest, **kernels)
        for stage in self.stages:
            fixture = stage["fixture"]
            fixture.pop("ice", None)
            fixture["matvec"] = matvec
            fixture.update(kernels)
            # DO-61: a one-stage plan keeps its token loop on the contributor (the first stage's stop rule needs
            # `stop_ids` and `generate`); YGARATY_LOCAL_LOOP=off sends every token through the coordinator again.
            # DO-87: batched slots, plus a resident burst (experiment 129) while one chat is the only one decoding;
            # YGARATY_RESIDENT=off keeps every step in the batched loop.
            fixture.update(
                conversations=chat_slots,
                resident=os.environ.get("YGARATY_RESIDENT") != "off",
                batch=self.args.batch,
                concurrent_chat=False,
            )
            fixture.update(
                local_loop=os.environ.get("YGARATY_LOCAL_LOOP") != "off",
                generate=options["capacity"],
                stop_ids=sorted(self.stop_ids),
            )
            if (
                route != "relay"
            ):  # the first stage stops a turn by the coordinator's rule (chat_token)
                fixture.update(
                    chat_direct=True,
                    route="webrtc",
                    ice_policy=self.args.ice_policy,
                    stages=len(self.stages),
                    generate=options["capacity"],
                    stop_ids=sorted(self.stop_ids),
                )

    def ice_servers(self, session, slot):
        """Short-lived TURN credentials for one slot of a direct session (user_webrtc.md, section 3)."""
        if self.args.route != "webrtc":
            raise PermissionError("This session does not use direct links")
        if self.turn_config is None:
            return {"ice": []}  # local network: host candidates only
        return self.turn_config.credentials(session, slot)

    def ice_urls(self):
        return self.turn_config.urls() if self.turn_config else []

    def download(self, name):
        """Public URL descriptor for one piece (spaces_download.mjs)."""
        if not self.storage or name not in self.files:
            raise ValueError("No Spaces piece with that name")
        return {
            "url": self.storage + quote(self.remote_paths[name], safe="/"),
            "bytes": self.files[name][2],
            "sha256": self.hashes[name],
        }

    def close(self, *, restarting=False):
        self.state = "cancelled"
        self.wake_signal_waiters()
        self.throughput.close()
        self.concurrent_chat.abort("Session stopped")
        if self.turn is not None:
            self.turn["events"].put({"error": "Session stopped"})
        for box in self.inbox:
            for _ in range(box.qsize()):
                try:
                    box.get_nowait()
                except queue.Empty:
                    break
            try:
                box.put(
                    ("stop", {"restarting": True} if restarting else {}, b"")
                )
            except SignalRejected:
                pass  # Shutdown must still release logs and retire reservations.
        for record in self.contributor_roster().snapshot()["contributors"]:
            self.contributor_roster().retire(
                record["id"], "coordinator stopped"
            )
        with self.log_lock:
            self.log.close()


# File: packages/webgpu/src/ygaraty/webgpu/_legacy/qwen_protocol.py
"""Shared Qwen/WebGPU HTTP protocol, token routing and chat state.

No model execution, model preparation, compiler, experiment launcher or sockets.
Prepared web deployments and source-distribution jobs share these methods.
"""

from ygaraty.inference_support.http import reply, stream

import json
import queue
import secrets
import threading
import time
from contextlib import nullcontext
from http.server import BaseHTTPRequestHandler
from pathlib import Path
from urllib.parse import parse_qs, unquote, urlparse
import numpy as np

STATIC = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent / "browser"
PAGE = "qwen35_contributor.mjs"
MODULES = {
    "chat_slots.mjs",
    "contributor_network.mjs",
    "contributor_loading.mjs",
    "spaces_download.mjs",
    PAGE,
    "qwen35_gpu.mjs",
    "dense_gpu.mjs",
    "kolibri_gpu.mjs",
    "inference_plan.mjs",
    "inference_schema.mjs",
    "inference_contracts.mjs",
    "inference_layout.mjs",
    "speculative.mjs",
    "qwen35_peer.mjs",
    "webgpu_kernels.mjs",
}
STAGE_NAMES = (
    "ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ"  # Kolibri-1 plans use up to about 10 stages
)
# The matrix-vector kernel's rows per thread: 2 for one token, 4 for up to 8 tokens (one group). Same outputs
# bit for bit as one row per thread (experiment 104); 1.98x batched throughput on the 680M (108), 1.86x on two
# RTX 3060s (110), single tokens no slower. None: one row per thread (`--matvec off`, YGARATY_MATVEC=off).
MATVEC_DEFAULT = dict(single=2, rows=4, tokens=8)
