                    ("X-Meta", json.dumps(meta)),
                    ("X-Seq", str(seq)),
                ),
            )
        box = session.inbox[k]
        try:
            first = box.get(timeout=20)
        except queue.Empty:
            return respond(h, 204)
        if "all" not in query:
            kind, meta, data = first
            return respond(
                h,
                200,
                data,
                "application/octet-stream",
                (("X-Kind", kind), ("X-Meta", json.dumps(meta))),
            )
        items = [first]
        batch_bytes = BoundedMailbox.item_size(first)
        # Snapshot the item count and cap bytes: a concurrent sender must not
        # keep this drain alive forever or grow one response without a bound.
        for _ in range(min(box.qsize(), 255)):
            if batch_bytes >= 32 * 1024 * 1024:
                break
            try:
                item = box.get_nowait()
            except queue.Empty:
                break
            items.append(item)
            batch_bytes += BoundedMailbox.item_size(item)
        seq = (handed[0] if handed else 0) + 1
        if ack is not None:
            session.handed[k] = (seq, items)
        meta = [[kind, m, len(d)] for kind, m, d in items]
        return respond(
            h,
            200,
            b"".join(d for _, _, d in items),
            "application/octet-stream",
            (
                ("X-Kind", "bundle"),
                ("X-Meta", json.dumps(meta)),
                ("X-Seq", str(seq)),
            ),
        )
    if path.startswith("/api/signal/"):
        cid = path.rsplit("/", 1)[1]
        if (
            cid not in session.contributors
            or session.contributors[cid].get("stage") is None
        ):
            return respond(h, 404, {"error": "no stage"})
        return respond(h, 200, session.signals_for(cid))
    if path == "/api/status":
        return respond(h, 200, session.status())
    if path.startswith("/api/download/"):
        # Public piece URL; the browser checks size and SHA-256 itself.
        name = unquote(path.removeprefix("/api/download/"))
        try:
            return respond(h, 200, session.download(name))
        except (AttributeError, ValueError):
            return respond(h, 404, {"error": "no remote piece"})
    return respond(h, 404, {"error": path})


def session_post(h, session, path, query, data):
    """A POST on one session (`path` relative to it, `data` the raw body)."""
    if not authorized(h, session, query):
        return respond(h, 403, {"error": "missing or wrong session key"})
    parts = path.split("/")
    try:
        if (
            len(parts) > 2
            and parts[2] in {"join", "ready", "host", "heartbeat", "error"}
            and len(data) > 32768
        ):
            raise SignalRejected(413, "Contributor metadata exceeds 32 KiB")
        # `done` comes once per contributor with every call (and, with --logit-digests, every token's digest): a
        # 2048-token run or a long chat is far above 32 KiB, and was refused, losing the report (WS-27).
        if len(parts) > 2 and parts[2] == "done" and len(data) > DONE_BYTES:
            raise SignalRejected(
                413, f"Contributor report exceeds {DONE_BYTES >> 20} MiB"
            )
        if path == "/api/join":
            return respond(
                h, 200, session.join(json.loads(data), h.client_address[0])
            )
        if path == "/api/chat":
            request = json.loads(data or b"{}")
            return stream(
                h,
                session,
                session.chat(
                    str(request.get("message", "")).strip(),
                    bool(request.get("reset")),
                    cancel_event=getattr(h, "cancelled", None),
                    direct_lease=request.get("direct_lease"),
                    conversation_id=validate_conversation_id(
                        request.get("conversation_id", "legacy-browser")
                    ),
                ),
            )
        if path.startswith("/api/host/"):
            session.hosts[parts[3]] = json.loads(data)
            return respond(h, 200, {})
        if len(parts) < 3 or parts[1] != "api":
            return respond(h, 404, {"error": path})
        cid = parts[3] if len(parts) > 3 else ""
        if parts[2] == "error":
            session.fail(cid, data.decode(errors="replace"))
            return respond(h, 200, {})
        if cid not in session.contributors:
            return respond(h, 404, {"error": "unknown contributor"})
        if parts[2] == "heartbeat":
            session.expire_contributors()
            if not session.heartbeat(cid):
                return respond(
                    h,
                    410,
                    {
                        "error": "reservation ended: this browser's slot was given away; reload to rejoin"
                    },
                )
            session.note_progress(cid, data)
            return respond(
                h, 200, {"roster": session.contributor_roster().snapshot()}
            )
        if parts[2] == "leave":
            session.leave(cid)
            return respond(h, 200, {})
        if parts[2] == "blob":
            session.store_blob(cid, parts[4], data)
            return respond(h, 200, {})
        if parts[2] == "selftest":
            return respond(h, 200, session.check(cid))
        if parts[2] == "ready":
            session.ready(cid, json.loads(data))
            return respond(h, 200, {})
        if parts[2] == "send":
            # DO-85 (local harness only): `--send-delay MS` emulates the contributor's round trip on its sends,
            # half before the coordinator sees the message, half before the reply.
            delay = (getattr(session.args, "send_delay", 0) or 0) / 2e3
            if delay:
                time.sleep(delay)
            session.send(
                cid,
                query["kind"][0],
                json.loads(query.get("meta", ["{}"])[0]),
                data,
                int(query["n"][0]) if "n" in query else None,
            )
            if delay:
                time.sleep(delay)
            return respond(h, 200, {})
        if parts[2] == "done":
            session.done(cid, json.loads(data))
            return respond(h, 200, {})
        if parts[2] == "signal":
            if len(data) > SDP_BYTES + 1024:
                return respond(h, 413, {"error": "signal too large"})
            return respond(h, 200, session.signal(cid, json.loads(data)) or {})
        if (
            parts[2] == "route"
        ):  # DO-40: how each WebRTC link connected (candidate types, round trip)
            if len(data) > ROUTE_BYTES:
                return respond(h, 413, {"error": "route stats too large"})
            session.note_route(cid, json.loads(data))
            return respond(h, 200, {})
    except SignalRejected as error:
        return respond(h, error.status, {"error": str(error)})
    except Exception as error:  # report to the page and the log, keep serving
        session.say(f"error handling {path}: {error!r}")
        return respond(h, 500, {"error": repr(error)})
    return respond(h, 404, {"error": path})


def handler(session):
    """A server for one session at / (tests and lightweight fixtures; `serve` runs the general coordinator)."""

    class Handler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
        protocol_version = "HTTP/1.1"

        def log_message(self, *_):
            pass

        def reply(self, code, body=b"", mime="application/json", extra=()):
            return reply(self, code, body, mime, extra)

        def do_GET(self):
            url = urlparse(self.path)
            return session_get(self, session, url.path, parse_qs(url.query))

        def do_POST(self):
            url = urlparse(self.path)
            n = int(self.headers.get("Content-Length") or 0)
            return session_post(
                self,
                session,
                url.path,
                parse_qs(url.query),
                self.rfile.read(n) if n else b"",
            )

    return Handler


JOIN_PAGE = (STATIC / "session_contributor.html").read_text()
CHAT_PAGE = (STATIC / "session_chat.html").read_text()

# File: packages/webgpu/src/ygaraty/webgpu/_legacy/transport.py
"""ASGI transport for existing synchronous workload protocol services.

No sockets or HTTP server are created here. Responses flow through a bounded
queue; ASGI owns HTTP framing, disconnects, and transfer encoding.
"""

import asyncio
from email.message import Message
import io
import json
import queue
import threading
import time
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor

from fastapi import HTTPException
from starlette.responses import StreamingResponse


class Transport:
    # Concurrent synchronous requests over all sessions; above it, 503. A vLLM contributor alone sends one output
    # event per running chat (32) plus its job poll (DO-88). YGARATY_COORDINATOR_REQUESTS overrides.
    def __init__(self, workers=None):
        import os

        workers = workers or int(
            os.environ.get("YGARATY_COORDINATOR_REQUESTS", "128")
        )
        self.executor = ThreadPoolExecutor(
            max_workers=workers, thread_name_prefix="coordinator"
        )
        self.slots = threading.BoundedSemaphore(workers)
        # A 32-chat burst acknowledges 32 jobs at once beside polls and heartbeats; 16 slots overflowed (DO-91).
        control_workers = int(
            os.environ.get("YGARATY_WEBGPU_CONTROL_REQUESTS", "64")
        )
        self.control_executor = ThreadPoolExecutor(
            max_workers=control_workers, thread_name_prefix="webgpu-control"
        )
        self.control_slots = threading.BoundedSemaphore(control_workers)
        self.closed = threading.Event()
        self.requests = set()
        self.lock = threading.Lock()

    def close(self):
        self.closed.set()
        with self.lock:
            for stop in self.requests:
                stop.set()
        self.executor.shutdown(wait=True, cancel_futures=True)
        self.control_executor.shutdown(wait=True, cancel_futures=True)

    async def dispatch(
        self, request, protocol, path=None, transform=None, control=False
    ):
        slots = self.control_slots if control else self.slots
        executor = self.control_executor if control else self.executor
        if self.closed.is_set() or not slots.acquire(blocking=False):
            raise HTTPException(503, "Coordinator request capacity reached")
        stop = threading.Event()
        with self.lock:
            self.requests.add(stop)
        submitted = False
        try:
            # Bound both length-delimited and chunked bodies before protocol decoding.
            lengths = request.headers.getlist("content-length")
            if len(lengths) > 1 or (lengths and not lengths[0].isdigit()):
                raise HTTPException(400, "Invalid Content-Length")
            if lengths and int(lengths[0]) > 16 * 1024 * 1024:
                raise HTTPException(413, "Request exceeds 16 MiB")
            body_started = time.monotonic()
            body = bytearray()
            async with asyncio.timeout(30):
                async for part in request.stream():
                    body.extend(part)
                    if len(body) > 16 * 1024 * 1024:
                        raise HTTPException(413, "Request exceeds 16 MiB")
            if lengths and int(lengths[0]) != len(body):
                raise HTTPException(400, "Truncated request body")
            headers = Message()
            for name, value in request.headers.items():
                if name.lower() != "content-length":
                    headers[name] = value
            headers["Content-Length"] = str(len(body))
            loop = asyncio.get_running_loop()
            ready = loop.create_future()
            chunks = queue.Queue(maxsize=8)

            def put(value):
                while not stop.is_set() and not self.closed.is_set():
                    try:
                        chunks.put(value, timeout=0.1)
                        return
                    except queue.Full:
                        pass
                raise BrokenPipeError("ASGI client disconnected")

            def resolve(value):
                if not ready.done():
                    ready.set_result(value)

            class Writer:
                def write(self, value):
                    view = memoryview(value).cast("B")
                    for start in range(0, len(view), 65536):
                        put(bytes(view[start : start + 65536]))
                    return len(view)

                def flush(self):
                    pass

            class Context(protocol):
                def send_response(self, code, message=None):
                    self.code, self.output_headers = code, {}

                def send_header(self, name, value):
                    if name.lower() not in {"transfer-encoding", "connection"}:
                        self.output_headers[name.lower()] = str(value)

                def end_headers(self):
                    self.started = True
                    loop.call_soon_threadsafe(
                        resolve, (self.code, self.output_headers)
                    )

                def stream(self, events):
                    self.send_response(200)
                    self.send_header("Content-Type", "application/x-ndjson")
                    self.send_header("Cache-Control", "no-store")
                    self.end_headers()
                    try:
                        for event in events:
                            self.wfile.write(
                                (json.dumps(event) + "\n").encode()
                            )
                    finally:
                        if hasattr(events, "close"):
                            events.close()

                send_stream = stream

                def reply(
                    self, code, body=b"", mime="application/json", extra=()
                ):
                    if code >= 500:
                        body = {"error": "Contributor protocol failure"}
                    if transform and isinstance(body, (str, bytes, dict, list)):
                        body = transform(body, mime)
                    if isinstance(body, (dict, list)):
                        body = json.dumps(body).encode()
                    elif isinstance(body, str):
                        body = body.encode()
                    self.send_response(code)
                    self.send_header("Content-Type", mime)
                    self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(body)))
                    self.send_header("Cache-Control", "no-store")
                    for name, value in extra:
                        self.send_header(name, value)
                    self.end_headers()
                    self.wfile.write(body)

            context = object.__new__(Context)
            context.path = path or request.url.path
            if request.url.query:
                context.path += "?" + request.url.query
            context.headers, context.rfile = headers, io.BytesIO(body)
            context.wfile, context.started = Writer(), False
            context.cancelled = stop
            context.received_body_seconds = time.monotonic() - body_started
            context.client_address = (
                request.client.host if request.client else "unknown",
                0,
            )
            # Upload protocol compatibility: the ASGI body deadline already ran.
            context.connection = type(
                "ReadDeadline",
                (),
                {"gettimeout": lambda s: 30, "settimeout": lambda s, t: None},
            )()

            def run():
                try:
                    (
                        context.do_POST
                        if request.method == "POST"
                        else context.do_GET
                    )()
                except BrokenPipeError:
                    pass
                except Exception:
                    if not context.started:
                        loop.call_soon_threadsafe(
                            resolve,
                            (500, {"content-type": "application/json"}),
                        )
                        put(b'{"error":"Coordinator protocol failure"}')
                finally:
                    if not context.started:
                        loop.call_soon_threadsafe(resolve, (500, {}))
                    try:
                        put(None)
                    except BrokenPipeError:
                        pass
                    context.rfile.close()
                    slots.release()
                    with self.lock:
                        self.requests.discard(stop)

            executor.submit(run)
            submitted = True
            code, output_headers = await ready

            async def content():
                try:
                    while True:
                        try:
                            part = await asyncio.to_thread(
                                chunks.get, True, 0.1
                            )
                        except queue.Empty:
                            if stop.is_set() or self.closed.is_set():
                                break
                            continue
                        if part is None:
                            break
                        yield part
                finally:
                    stop.set()

            return StreamingResponse(
                content(), status_code=code, headers=output_headers
            )
        except BaseException as error:
            stop.set()
            if not submitted:
                slots.release()
                with self.lock:
                    self.requests.discard(stop)
            if isinstance(error, TimeoutError):
                raise HTTPException(
                    408, "Request body deadline exceeded"
                ) from error
            raise


# File: packages/webgpu/src/ygaraty/webgpu/bootstrap.py
"""Prepare and launch a local example through the existing public workflow."""

import json
import shlex
from dataclasses import dataclass, replace
from pathlib import Path
from types import SimpleNamespace


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class ModelProfile:
    model_id: str
    family: str
    layers: int
    splits: tuple[int, ...]
    memory_gb: int
    evidence: str
    validation: str


PROFILES = {
    "s5-nano": ModelProfile(
        "s5-nano",
        "qwen35",
        32,
        (0, 16, 32),
        3,
        "research/103-fast-sessions-rollout-rerun.md",
        "Two-stage execution measured; 3 GiB device acceptance pending",
    ),
    "qwen3.5-0.8b": ModelProfile(
        "qwen3.5-0.8b",
        "qwen35",
        24,
        (0, 12, 24),
        1,
        "Greedy tokens match the transformers reference (2026-11-15)",
        "1/2/4-worker WebGPU runs with headless contributors; "
        "browser and device variety pending",
    ),
    "qwen3.5-9b": ModelProfile(
        "qwen3.5-9b",
        "qwen35",
        32,
        (0, 8, 16, 24, 32),
        4,
        "research/092-qwen35-webgpu-four-contributors.md",
        "Measured four-contributor WebGPU run; extracted host still pending",
    ),
    "qwen3-coder-30b": ModelProfile(
        "qwen3-coder-30b",
        "qwen3moe",
        48,
        (0, 48),
        32,
        "research/DO-80.md",
        "Partial: whole-model hidden-state gate; "
        "multi-worker/tool gates pending",
    ),
}


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Bootstrap:
    profile: ModelProfile
    checkpoint: Path
    workspace: Path
    gguf_name: str = "model.gguf"
    context: int = 4096
    generate: int = 128
    host: str = "127.0.0.1"
    port: int = 8765

    def __post_init__(self):
        if Path(self.gguf_name).name != self.gguf_name:
            raise ValueError("--gguf must be a filename inside --checkpoint")
        if not 1 <= self.generate < self.context:
            raise ValueError("Require 0 < --generate < --context")
        if not 1 <= self.port <= 65535:
            raise ValueError("Port must be between 1 and 65535")

    def recipe(self):
        return {
            "version": 1,
            "model": self.profile.model_id,
            "checkpoint": str(self.checkpoint.resolve()),
            "gguf": self.gguf_name,
            "context": self.context,
            "generate": self.generate,
            "splits": list(self.profile.splits),
            "memory_gb": self.profile.memory_gb,
        }

    def serve_arguments(self):
        return SimpleNamespace(
            bundle=self.workspace / "model/bundle.json",
            model_id=self.profile.model_id,
            keys=self.workspace / "keys",
            state_dir=self.workspace / "state",
            context=self.context,
            memory_gb=self.profile.memory_gb,
            slots=1,
            host=self.host,
            port=self.port,
        )

    def describe(self):
        args = self.serve_arguments()
        command = ["ygaraty", "serve"]
        for name in (
            "bundle",
            "model_id",
            "keys",
            "state_dir",
            "context",
            "memory_gb",
            "slots",
            "host",
            "port",
        ):
            command.extend(
                ["--" + name.replace("_", "-"), str(getattr(args, name))]
            )
        return {
            **self.recipe(),
            "workspace": str(self.workspace.resolve()),
            "contributors": len(self.profile.splits) - 1,
            "evidence": self.profile.evidence,
            "validation": self.profile.validation,
            "resume_command": shlex.join(command),
            "actions": [
                "inspect local checkpoint",
                "prepare pinned bundle",
                "create owner/contributor keys",
                "validate bundle",
                "serve chat and contributor pages",
            ],
        }

    def prepare(self):
        """Reuse a completed matching workspace, never overwrite other data."""
        from .bundles import (
            BundleBuilder,
            Checkpoint,
            inspect_checkpoint,
            validate_bundle,
        )
        from .cli import initialize

        args = self.serve_arguments()
        receipt = self.workspace / "bootstrap.json"
        if self.workspace.exists():
            if not receipt.is_file() or receipt.stat().st_size > 16384:
                raise ValueError(
                    "Workspace is incomplete or unrelated; "
                    "choose a new --workspace"
                )
            saved = json.loads(receipt.read_text())
            if (
                not isinstance(saved, dict)
                or saved.get("recipe") != self.recipe()
            ):
                raise ValueError(
                    "Workspace belongs to a different bootstrap recipe"
                )
            for name in ("owner.key", "contributor.key"):
                key = args.keys / name
                if not key.is_file() or not key.read_text().strip():
                    raise ValueError(
                        "Workspace credentials missing; "
                        "refusing to replace keys"
                    )
            report = validate_bundle(args.bundle)
            if report.sha256 != saved.get("bundle_sha256"):
                raise ValueError(
                    "Workspace bundle differs from bootstrap receipt"
                )
            return args
        gguf = self.checkpoint / self.gguf_name
        config = self.checkpoint / "config.json"
        report = inspect_checkpoint(gguf, config)
        if (
            not report["compatible"]
            or report["family"] != self.profile.family
            or report["geometry"]["layers"] != self.profile.layers
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                "Checkpoint family/layer count does not match "
                "selected model profile"
            )
        chat = self.checkpoint / "chat.json"
        checkpoint = Checkpoint(
            gguf,
            config,
            self.checkpoint / "tokenizer.json",
            chat if chat.is_file() else None,
        )
        checkpoint.validate()
        self.workspace.mkdir(parents=True)
        # Existing builders own numerical references and atomic bundle export.
        # If preparation fails, keep the workspace for inspection; never serve.
        BundleBuilder(
            checkpoint,
            splits=self.profile.splits,
            context_tokens=self.context,
            max_output_tokens=self.generate,
        ).prepare(self.workspace / "model")
        initialize(args.keys)
        report = validate_bundle(args.bundle)
        pending = receipt.with_suffix(".tmp")
        pending.write_text(
            json.dumps(
                {"recipe": self.recipe(), "bundle_sha256": report.sha256},
                indent=2,
            )
            + "\n"
        )
        pending.replace(receipt)
        return args


def add_parser(commands):
    parser = commands.add_parser(
        "bootstrap", help="Prepare and launch the local WebGPU chat example"
    )
    parser.add_argument("--model", choices=PROFILES, default="qwen3.5-9b")
    parser.add_argument(
        "--num-workers",
        "--workers",
        "--contributors",
        dest="contributors",
        type=int,
        required=True,
        help="Required number of GPU workers",
    )
    source = parser.add_mutually_exclusive_group()
    source.add_argument("--checkpoint", type=Path)
    source.add_argument(
        "--bundle-url", help="Public prepared bundle; preset used by default"
    )
    parser.add_argument("--workspace", type=Path)
    parser.add_argument(
        "--gguf",
        default="model.gguf",
        help="GGUF filename (first shard for split files)",
    )
    parser.add_argument("--context", type=int)
    parser.add_argument("--generate", type=int)
    parser.add_argument("--host", default="127.0.0.1")
    parser.add_argument("--port", type=int, default=8765)
    parser.add_argument(
        "--dry-run",
        action="store_true",
        help="Print recipe; no model reads, writes, inference or server",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--prepare-only",
        action="store_true",
        help="Prepare numerical references without launching the host",
    )


def run(args):
    if args.workspace is None:
        args.workspace = (
            Path("runs") / args.model / f"{args.contributors}-workers"
        )
    if not 1 <= args.contributors <= 26:
        raise ValueError("--num-workers must be between 1 and 26")
    if args.checkpoint is None:
        from .downloads import PUBLIC_BUNDLES, public_url
        from .launch import run as launch

        url = args.bundle_url or PUBLIC_BUNDLES.get(
            (args.model, args.contributors)
        )
        if not url:
            raise ValueError(
                "No public bundle URL for this model; supply --bundle-url"
            )
        public_url(url)
        if args.dry_run:
            print(
                json.dumps(
                    {
                        "model": args.model,
                        "bundle_url": url,
                        "contributors": args.contributors,
                        "memory": "calculated from bundle at launch",
                        "workspace": str(args.workspace.resolve()),
                        "actions": [
                            "download and verify prepared bundle",
                            "initialize keys",
                            "serve",
                        ],
                    },
                    indent=2,
                )
            )
            return 0
        remote = SimpleNamespace(
            **vars(args),
            bundle=None,
            splits=None,
            memory_gb=None,
            slots=1,
            public_url=None,
            open_chat=False,
            local=True,
        )
        return launch(remote)
    profile = PROFILES[args.model]
    if args.contributors > profile.layers:
        raise ValueError("Worker count cannot exceed model layer count")
    if len(profile.splits) - 1 != args.contributors:
        profile = replace(
            profile,
            splits=tuple(
                i * profile.layers // args.contributors
                for i in range(args.contributors + 1)
            ),
        )
    setup = Bootstrap(
        replace(profile, memory_gb=64)
        if profile.family == "qwen35"
        else profile,
        args.checkpoint,
        args.workspace,
        args.gguf,
        args.context or 4096,
        args.generate if args.generate is not None else 128,
        args.host,
        args.port,
    )
    print(json.dumps(setup.describe(), indent=2))
    if args.dry_run:
        return 0
    from .cli import serve
    from .launch import configure_prepared_memory

    try:
        launch = setup.prepare()
    except ImportError as error:
        raise ValueError(
            "Install ygaraty[server,prepare] and its public packages"
        ) from error
    if profile.family == "qwen35":
        configure_prepared_memory(launch, args.contributors, setup.context, 1)
    if not args.prepare_only:
        print(f"Contributor page: http://{args.host}:{args.port}/")
        print(f"Chat page: http://{args.host}:{args.port}/chat")
        print(f"Keys: {launch.keys} (key values are never printed)")
        serve(launch)
    return 0


# File: packages/webgpu/src/ygaraty/webgpu/buffers.py
"""Bounded token notifications without blocking contributor control traffic."""

import queue
import threading


class TokenMailbox:
    def __init__(self, capacity: int = 512):
        self._queue = queue.Queue(maxsize=capacity)
        self._lock = threading.Lock()
        self._overflow = False

    def put(self, value) -> None:
        with self._lock:
            if self._overflow:
                return
            try:
                self._queue.put_nowait(value)
            except queue.Full:
                self._overflow = True
                # Wake the reader with a failure, never silently omit tokens.
                while True:
                    try:
                        self._queue.get_nowait()
                    except queue.Empty:
                        break
                self._queue.put_nowait({"error": "Response consumer too slow"})

    def get(self, timeout=None):
        return self._queue.get(timeout=timeout)


# File: packages/webgpu/src/ygaraty/webgpu/bundle_export.py
"""Serialize detached local bundle pieces and self-test artifacts."""

import json
import shutil
from pathlib import Path

from ._legacy.chat_contract import chat_format
from .bundle_io import file_digest, range_digest


def export_bundle(session, output, tokenizer):
    """Serialize prepared data; byte copies and NumPy serialization only."""
    import numpy as np

    output = Path(output)
    (output / "pieces").mkdir()
    (output / "selftest").mkdir()
    files = {}
    for name, (source, offset, length) in session.files.items():
        digest = session.hashes[name]
        if len(digest) != 64 or any(
            c not in "0123456789abcdef" for c in digest
        ):
            raise ValueError("Invalid prepared weight hash")
        relative = f"pieces/{digest}.bin"
        target = output / relative
        if target.exists():
            actual = range_digest(source, offset, length)
            if target.stat().st_size != length:
                raise ValueError("Conflicting content-addressed piece length")
        else:
            with target.open("xb") as destination:
                actual = range_digest(
                    source, offset, length, output=destination
                )
        if actual != digest:
            raise ValueError("Weight source changed during preparation")
        files[name] = dict(
            path=relative, offset=0, length=length, sha256=digest
        )
    pins, test_files, expected = {}, {}, []
    for index, (name, content) in enumerate(session.test_files.items()):
        relative = f"selftest/input-{index}.bin"
        (output / relative).write_bytes(content)
        test_files[name] = relative
        pins[relative] = file_digest(output / relative)
    for index, reference in enumerate(session.expected):
        relative = f"selftest/expected-{index}.npy"
        np.save(output / relative, reference, allow_pickle=False)
        expected.append(relative)
        pins[relative] = file_digest(output / relative)
    shutil.copyfile(tokenizer, output / "tokenizer.json")
    pins["tokenizer.json"] = file_digest(output / "tokenizer.json")
    stages = json.loads(json.dumps(session.stages))
    for stage in stages:
        if stage["fixture"].get("ice"):
            raise ValueError("Stored bundles must not carry ICE credentials")
        stage["fixture"].pop("ice", None)
        stage["fixture"]["route"] = "relay"
    data = dict(
        format="ygaraty-webgpu-v1",
        stages=stages,
        files=files,
        test_files=test_files,
        selftest=session.selftest,
        expected=expected,
        tokenizer="tokenizer.json",
        eos=session.eos,
        options=dict(
            capacity=session.args.capacity,
            generate=session.args.generate,
            chunk=session.args.chunk,
            kv=session.args.kv,
            chat=chat_format(session.chat_style),
        ),
        distribution_plan=session.distribution_plan,
        artifact_digests=pins,
    )
    manifest = output / ".bundle.pending.json"
    payload = json.dumps(data, indent=2)
    if len(payload.encode()) > 16 << 20:
        raise ValueError("Bundle manifest exceeds 16 MiB")
    with manifest.open("x") as handle:
        handle.write(payload + "\n")


# File: packages/webgpu/src/ygaraty/webgpu/bundle_io.py
"""Bounded local artifact I/O shared by preparation and validation."""

import hashlib
from pathlib import Path

from .configuration import json_object

BLOCK_BYTES = 1 << 20


def local_input(value, *, limit=None):
    if "://" in str(value):
        raise ValueError("Use a local file, not a URL")
    try:
        path = Path(value).resolve(strict=True)
    except FileNotFoundError as error:
        requested = Path(value).absolute()
        raise ValueError(
            f"Local file not found: {requested}. "
            "Provide your existing model files; /path/to/... in examples "
            "is a placeholder, not a bundled checkpoint."
        ) from error
    if not path.is_file():
        raise ValueError(f"Expected a local file: {path.name}")
    if limit is not None and path.stat().st_size > limit:
        raise ValueError(f"{path.name} exceeds the {limit}-byte limit")
    return path


def read_json(path, *, limit=16 << 20):
    return json_object(local_input(path, limit=limit).read_bytes())


def range_digest(path, offset, length, *, output=None):
    """Hash or copy exactly one range using a bounded buffer."""
    path = local_input(path)
    if (
        type(offset) is not int
        or type(length) is not int
        or offset < 0
        or length <= 0
        or offset + length > path.stat().st_size
    ):
        raise ValueError("Invalid artifact byte range")
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    with path.open("rb") as source:
        source.seek(offset)
        remaining = length
        while remaining:
            block = source.read(min(remaining, BLOCK_BYTES))
            if not block:
                raise ValueError("Truncated artifact byte range")
            digest.update(block)
            if output is not None:
                output.write(block)
            remaining -= len(block)
    return digest.hexdigest()


def file_digest(path):
    path = local_input(path)
    return range_digest(path, 0, path.stat().st_size)


# File: packages/webgpu/src/ygaraty/webgpu/bundle_validation.py
"""Verify local bundle artifacts and freshly compile their inference plans."""

import hashlib
import re
from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path
from urllib.parse import urlsplit

from ygaraty.backend_contracts.models import ModelIdentity, SessionSpec

from ._legacy.model_artifacts import local_file
from .bundle_io import file_digest, local_input, range_digest, read_json
from .configuration import WebGPUConfig
from .preparation import prepare_session


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class BundleReport:
    path: Path
    sha256: str
    stages: int
    context_tokens: int
    weight_bytes: int
    artifacts_pinned: bool


def remote_base(data):
    """Return the public HTTPS weight base URL, or None for local weights.

    A ``url`` backend means contributors fetch each hash-pinned piece from
    ``base_url + path`` themselves; the server holds no weight bytes.
    """
    storage = data.get("storage", {})
    backend = storage.get("backend", "local")
    if backend == "local":
        return None
    if backend != "url":
        raise ValueError("Unsupported bundle storage backend")
    base = storage.get("base_url")
    parsed = urlsplit(base) if isinstance(base, str) else None
    if (
        parsed is None
        or parsed.scheme != "https"
        or not parsed.hostname
        or parsed.username
        or parsed.password
        or parsed.query
        or parsed.fragment
        or not base.endswith("/")
    ):
        raise ValueError("Remote weights need a public HTTPS base URL")
    return base


def remote_piece_path(value):
    if (
        not isinstance(value, str)
        or not re.fullmatch(r"[A-Za-z0-9_./-]+", value)
        or value.startswith("/")
        or any(part in {"", ".", ".."} for part in value.split("/"))
    ):
        raise ValueError("Unsafe remote weight path")
    return value


def validate_bundle(path) -> BundleReport:
    """Check files and hashes without constructing an engine or executing it.

    Legacy bundles without artifact_digests remain readable. Weight ranges are
    verified, but tokenizer/self-test content is explicitly unpinned. Remote
    (``url`` storage) weights are checked for shape only; contributors verify
    each piece's SHA-256 when they download it.
    """
    path = local_input(path, limit=16 << 20)
    data = read_json(path)
    remote = remote_base(data) is not None
    if not data.get("files"):
        raise ValueError("Bundle has no weight ranges")
    total = 0
    for name, value in data["files"].items():
        if not re.fullmatch(r"[A-Za-z0-9_.-]+", name):
            raise ValueError("Invalid weight name")
        if remote:
            remote_piece_path(value["path"])
            if (
                value["offset"] != 0
                or type(value["length"]) is not int
                or value["length"] <= 0
                or not re.fullmatch(r"[a-f0-9]{64}", str(value["sha256"]))
            ):
                raise ValueError(
                    "Remote weights must be complete, hash-pinned pieces"
                )
        else:
            source = local_file(path.parent, value["path"])
            actual = range_digest(source, value["offset"], value["length"])
            if actual != value["sha256"]:
                raise ValueError(f"Weight hash mismatch: {name}")
        total += value["length"]
    tokenizer = local_file(path.parent, data["tokenizer"])
    read_json(tokenizer, limit=64 << 20)
    referenced = {data["tokenizer"]}
    for relative in data["test_files"].values():
        local_input(local_file(path.parent, relative), limit=16 << 20)
        referenced.add(relative)
    cases = data["selftest"]["cases"]
    if not cases or len(cases) != len(data["expected"]):
        raise ValueError("Incomplete self-test references")
    for case, relative in zip(cases, data["expected"]):
        import numpy as np

        source = local_file(path.parent, relative)
        local_input(source, limit=16 << 20)
        expected = np.load(source, allow_pickle=False)
        if not isinstance(expected, np.ndarray):
            expected.close()
            raise ValueError("Expected a NumPy array, not an archive")
        if expected.dtype.kind != "f" or expected.shape != (
            case["tokens"],
            case["shape"][0],
        ):
            raise ValueError("Invalid self-test reference shape or dtype")
        referenced.add(relative)
        for key in ("file", "x"):
            if case[key] not in data["test_files"]:
                raise ValueError("Missing self-test input")
    if data["selftest"]["f16"] not in data["test_files"]:
        raise ValueError("Missing float16 self-test input")
    pins = data.get("artifact_digests")
    if pins is not None:
        if not isinstance(pins, dict) or pins.keys() != referenced:
            raise ValueError("Incomplete artifact digest inventory")
        for relative, digest in pins.items():
            if file_digest(local_file(path.parent, relative)) != digest:
                raise ValueError(f"Artifact hash mismatch: {relative}")
    digest = hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
    config = WebGPUConfig(
        digest,
        tuple(tuple(stage["layers"]) for stage in data["stages"]),
        data["options"]["capacity"],
        max(total, 1),
    )
    identity = ModelIdentity("validation", digest, "local", "local", "bundle")
    spec = SessionSpec(
        "validation", "validation", identity, config.to_contract()
    )
    prepare_session(spec, bundle=path.read_bytes())
    return BundleReport(
        path,
        digest,
        len(data["stages"]),
        config.context_tokens,
        total,
        pins is not None,
    )


# File: packages/webgpu/src/ygaraty/webgpu/bundles.py
"""Standalone local checkpoint inspection and immutable bundle preparation."""

import os
import shutil
from dataclasses import dataclass, replace
from pathlib import Path
from types import SimpleNamespace

from ._legacy.chat_contract import chat_format
from .bundle_export import export_bundle
from .bundle_io import local_input, read_json
from .bundle_validation import BundleReport as BundleReport
from .bundle_validation import validate_bundle as validate_bundle
from .configuration import integer


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Checkpoint:
    gguf: Path
    config: Path
    tokenizer: Path
    chat_format: Path | None = None

    def validate(self):
        local_input(self.gguf)
        read_json(self.config)
        tokenizer = local_input(self.tokenizer, limit=64 << 20)
        from tokenizers import Tokenizer

        try:
            Tokenizer.from_file(str(tokenizer))
        except Exception as error:
            raise ValueError("Invalid tokenizer file") from error
        if self.chat_format is not None:
            chat_format(read_json(self.chat_format, limit=1 << 20))


def inspect_checkpoint(gguf, config) -> dict:
    """Inspect local headers/configuration without dequantizing any weights."""
    from .checkpoints import families, gguf_remote

    path = local_input(gguf)
    config = read_json(config)
    locations = [local_input(p) for p in gguf_remote.split_names(str(path))]
    header = gguf_remote.read_split_headers(locations)
    family = families.detect(config, header)
    findings = family.check(config, header)
    # Single-file parsing also needs the range check applied to split files.
    if len(locations) == 1:
        spans = []
        for tensor in header.tensors.values():
            if (
                tensor.offset < header.data_start
                or tensor.nbytes <= 0
                or tensor.offset + tensor.nbytes > header.size
            ):
                raise ValueError(f"Invalid tensor range: {tensor.name}")
            spans.append((tensor.offset, tensor.offset + tensor.nbytes))
        spans.sort()
        if any(a[1] > b[0] for a, b in zip(spans, spans[1:])):
            raise ValueError("Overlapping GGUF tensor ranges")
    return dict(
        family=family.name,
        architecture=header.architecture(),
        geometry=family.geometry(config, header),
        files=[str(p) for p in locations],
        findings=findings,
        compatible=not any(f["level"] == "error" for f in findings),
        chat_template=header.metadata.get("tokenizer.chat_template"),
    )


class BundleBuilder:
    """Prepare existing local files; no downloads, services or cloud adapters.

    Reference self-tests perform numerical computation and therefore require an
    authorized preparation host. Offline tests mock this computation.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        checkpoint: Checkpoint,
        *,
        splits: tuple[int, ...] | None = None,
        context_tokens: int = 4096,
        max_output_tokens: int = 256,
    ):
        self.checkpoint = checkpoint
        self.splits = splits
        integer(context_tokens, "context_tokens")
        self.context_tokens = context_tokens
        integer(max_output_tokens, "max_output_tokens")
        self.max_output_tokens = max_output_tokens
        if max_output_tokens > context_tokens:
            raise ValueError("Output token limit exceeds context")

    def _chat(self, report):
        if self.checkpoint.chat_format is not None:
            return chat_format(
                read_json(self.checkpoint.chat_format, limit=1 << 20)
            )
        template = report.get("chat_template")
        if not isinstance(template, str):
            source = Path(self.checkpoint.tokenizer).with_name(
                "tokenizer_config.json"
            )
            if source.is_file():
                template = read_json(source).get("chat_template")
        if not isinstance(template, str) or len(template) > 1 << 20:
            raise ValueError("Supply --chat-format or a local ChatML template")
        from .checkpoints.chat_template import chat_format as convert

        return chat_format(convert(template))

    def prepare(self, output) -> BundleReport:
        """Copy pieces and publish bundle.json last; never overwrite."""
        from .checkpoints.preparation import prepare_session

        output = Path(output).absolute()
        if output.exists():
            raise FileExistsError("Use a new bundle output directory")
        self.checkpoint.validate()
        report = inspect_checkpoint(
            self.checkpoint.gguf, self.checkpoint.config
        )
        if not report["compatible"]:
            raise ValueError(f"Unsupported checkpoint: {report['findings']}")
        layers = report["geometry"]["layers"]
        bounds = self.splits if self.splits is not None else (0, layers)
        if (
            not isinstance(bounds, tuple)
            or len(bounds) < 2
            or any(type(n) is not int for n in bounds)
            or bounds[0] != 0
            or bounds[-1] != layers
            or any(a >= b for a, b in zip(bounds, bounds[1:]))
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                "Splits must cover all layers in increasing order"
            )
        style = self._chat(report)
        # Keep the audited preparation path and its optimized manifest layout.
        args = SimpleNamespace(
            gguf=local_input(self.checkpoint.gguf),
            config=local_input(self.checkpoint.config),
            splits=",".join(map(str, bounds)),
            capacity=self.context_tokens,
            generate=self.max_output_tokens,
            chat_format=None,
        )
        session = prepare_session(args)
        session.chat_style = style
        output.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=False)
        try:
            export_bundle(session, output, self.checkpoint.tokenizer)
            pending = output / ".bundle.pending.json"
            report = validate_bundle(pending)
            final = output / "bundle.json"
            os.link(
                pending, final
            )  # Atomic publication, refusing replacement.
            pending.unlink()
            return replace(report, path=final.resolve())
        except BaseException:
            shutil.rmtree(output)
            raise


# File: packages/webgpu/src/ygaraty/webgpu/checkpoints/__init__.py
"""Local checkpoint loaders used by public bundle preparation."""


# File: packages/webgpu/src/ygaraty/webgpu/checkpoints/chat_template.py
"""Convert a locally supplied ChatML template to protocol pieces."""

import json


def chat_format(template, thinking=False):
    """The ChatML pieces of a checkpoint's own chat template (Jinja, rendered as transformers renders it):
    `first`, what a conversation starts with before the first user message (an empty system block for some
    models), `generation`, the assistant's opening after the last one (with thinking off unless asked), and
    `sep` and `end`, what follows a message's role and its <|im_end|> (ChatML's newlines; Phi-4's <|im_sep|>
    and nothing). Raises when the template is not ChatML-shaped: such a model needs its own chat format in the
    protocol."""
    from ygaraty.webgpu._legacy.qwen_protocol import CHAT_ENDS, CHAT_SEPARATORS

    import jinja2.sandbox

    env = jinja2.sandbox.ImmutableSandboxedEnvironment(
        trim_blocks=True,
        lstrip_blocks=True,
        extensions=["jinja2.ext.loopcontrols"],
    )
    env.filters["tojson"] = lambda value, **kw: json.dumps(
        value, ensure_ascii=kw.get("ensure_ascii", False)
    )
    env.globals["raise_exception"] = lambda message: (_ for _ in ()).throw(
        ValueError(message)
    )
    compiled = env.from_string(template)
    render = lambda messages, generation: compiled.render(  # noqa: E731
        messages=messages,
        add_generation_prompt=generation,
        enable_thinking=thinking,
        bos_token="",
        eos_token="",
    )
    marker = "\u2063USER\u2063"
    plain = render([dict(role="user", content=marker)], False)
    prompt = render([dict(role="user", content=marker)], True)
    shapes = [
        (sep, end)
        for sep in CHAT_SEPARATORS
        for end in CHAT_ENDS
        if plain.endswith(f"<|im_start|>user{sep}{marker}<|im_end|>{end}")
    ]
    if not shapes or not prompt.startswith(plain):
        raise ValueError(
            "The chat template is not ChatML-shaped (<|im_start|>role ... <|im_end|>): add a chat format for it"
        )
    sep, end = shapes[0]
    block = f"<|im_start|>user{sep}{marker}<|im_end|>{end}"
    first, generation = plain[: -len(block)], prompt[len(plain) :]
    if not generation.startswith("<|im_start|>assistant"):
        raise ValueError(f"Unexpected generation prompt {generation!r}")
    out = dict(
        first=first.lstrip("\n"), generation=generation, thinking=thinking
    )
    if (sep, end) != ("\n", "\n"):
        out.update(sep=sep, end=end)
    return out


# File: packages/webgpu/src/ygaraty/webgpu/checkpoints/dense_gguf.py
"""Dense decoder-only transformers (Llama shape) from their GGUF file: the `dense` model family.

Covers llama.cpp's `llama`, `granite`, `mistral` and `phi3` (Phi-4) architectures: RMSNorm, grouped-query
attention with RoPE, SwiGLU FFN, no biases. The browser stage (`dense_gpu.mjs`) reads the GGUF bytes unchanged; the NumPy reference
here computes in the GGUF's own layout, without Torch:

- llama.cpp's converter permutes the rows of `attn_q` and `attn_k` per head so that RoPE rotates adjacent
  pairs (2i, 2i+1) with frequency theta^(-2i/d) ("normal" RoPE); transformers rotates halves (i, i+d/2). The
  reference and the stage use the adjacent pairs; `hf_tensor` undoes the permutation for a transformers
  cross-check (`transformers_layer`).
- `phi3` is converted without that permutation, so its RoPE rotates halves (`rope_pairs="halves"`). It stores
  transformers' fused `qkv_proj` as `attn_qkv` ([q; k; v] rows) and `gate_up_proj` as `ffn_up` ([gate; up]
  rows); `Model` serves their row ranges as `attn_q`/`attn_k`/`attn_v` and `ffn_gate`/`ffn_up` (K-quant rows
  are whole, so a slice is a byte range of the stored tensor: bundles hard-link the GGUF as for any tensor).
- RMSNorm weights are stored as they are (no +1 offset, unlike Qwen3.5's GGUF).
- Granite's attention multiplier replaces 1/sqrt(head_dim); its embedding, residual and logit multipliers must
  be 1 (`families.Dense.check`).
"""

import json
from dataclasses import asdict, dataclass
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np

from ygaraty.webgpu.checkpoints import families, gguf_remote
from ygaraty.webgpu.checkpoints.qwen35_gguf import Tensor


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class DenseConfig:
    layers: int
    hidden: int
    intermediate: int
    heads: int
    kv_heads: int
    head_dim: int
    vocab: int
    rope_theta: float
    eps: float
    attention_scale: float
    tied_embeddings: bool
    rope_pairs: str = "adjacent"  # "adjacent" (2i, 2i+1): llama.cpp's permuted Q/K rows; "halves" (i, i + d/2)

    def manifest(self):
        return dict(asdict(self), family="dense")


def layer_type(index):
    return "dense"


def row_slice(t, name, first, rows):
    """Rows first..first+rows-1 of a stored tensor, as a tensor of its own named `name`."""
    width = t.row_bytes
    return Tensor(
        name,
        t.kind,
        (rows,) + t.shape[1:],
        t.data[first * width : (first + rows) * width],
        base=t.name,
        first_row=first,
    )


class Model:
    """A dense GGUF checkpoint: its tensors (memory-mapped, packed) and checked configuration."""

    family = "dense"

    def layer_type(self, index):
        return layer_type(index)

    def __init__(self, path, config_path, *, tensor_only=False):
        import gguf

        self.path = Path(path)
        document = json.loads(Path(config_path).read_text())
        header = gguf_remote.read_header(self.path)
        findings = families.FAMILIES["dense"].check(document, header)
        errors = [f["message"] for f in findings if f["level"] == "error"]
        if errors:
            raise ValueError(
                "Unsupported dense checkpoint: " + "; ".join(errors)
            )
        g = families.FAMILIES["dense"].geometry(document, header)
        self.config = DenseConfig(
            g["layers"],
            g["hidden"],
            g["intermediate"],
            g["heads"],
            g["kv_heads"],
            g["head_dim"],
            header.tensors["token_embd.weight"].shape[0],
            g["rope_theta"],
            float(g["eps"]),
            float(g["attention_scale"]),
            "output.weight" not in header.tensors,
            g["rope_pairs"],
        )
        if tensor_only:
            from ygaraty.webgpu.checkpoints.gguf_metadata import tensor_reader

            self.reader = tensor_reader(self.path)
        else:
            self.reader = gguf.GGUFReader(self.path)
        self.tensors = {}
        for t in self.reader.tensors:
            shape = tuple(int(n) for n in reversed(t.shape.tolist()))
            self.tensors[t.name] = Tensor(
                t.name,
                t.tensor_type.name,
                shape,
                t.data.view(np.uint8).reshape(-1),
            )
        c = self.config
        q, kv, ffn = (
            c.heads * c.head_dim,
            c.kv_heads * c.head_dim,
            c.intermediate,
        )
        for i in range(c.layers):
            p = f"blk.{i}."
            if p + "attn_qkv.weight" in self.tensors:
                fused = self.tensors.pop(p + "attn_qkv.weight")
                for name, first, rows in (
                    ("attn_q", 0, q),
                    ("attn_k", q, kv),
                    ("attn_v", q + kv, kv),
                ):
                    self.tensors[p + name + ".weight"] = row_slice(
                        fused, p + name + ".weight", first, rows
                    )
            if (
                p + "ffn_gate.weight" not in self.tensors
            ):  # fused [gate; up], checked by the family
                fused = self.tensors.pop(p + "ffn_up.weight")
                for name, first in (("ffn_gate", 0), ("ffn_up", ffn)):
                    self.tensors[p + name + ".weight"] = row_slice(
                        fused, p + name + ".weight", first, ffn
                    )

    def __getitem__(self, name):
        if (
            name == "output.weight"
            and name not in self.tensors
            and self.config.tied_embeddings
        ):
            name = "token_embd.weight"
        return self.tensors[name]

    def validate_webgpu(self):
        kinds = {}
        for t in self.tensors.values():
            kinds[t.kind] = kinds.get(t.kind, 0) + 1
        return kinds

    def layer_tensors(self, index):
        prefix = f"blk.{index}."
        return {
            name[len(prefix) :]: t
            for name, t in self.tensors.items()
            if name.startswith(prefix)
        }


# --- NumPy reference, in the GGUF's layout ------------------------------------


def rms_norm(x, w, eps):
    x = x.astype(np.float32)
    return x / np.sqrt(np.mean(x * x, axis=-1, keepdims=True) + eps) * w


def rope(x, positions, theta, pairs="adjacent"):
    """RoPE over the last axis (head_dim) of x[tokens, heads, head_dim], in place: pair i is (2i, 2i+1)
    ("adjacent") or (i, i + d/2) ("halves"), rotated by position * theta^(-2i/d)."""
    d = x.shape[-1]
    inv = theta ** (-np.arange(0, d, 2, dtype=np.float64) / d)
    angle = np.asarray(positions, dtype=np.float64)[:, None] * inv[None, :]
    c, s = (
        np.cos(angle).astype(np.float32)[:, None, :],
        np.sin(angle).astype(np.float32)[:, None, :],
    )
    first, second = (
        (np.s_[..., 0::2], np.s_[..., 1::2])
        if pairs == "adjacent"
        else (np.s_[..., : d // 2], np.s_[..., d // 2 :])
    )
    a, b = x[first].copy(), x[second].copy()
    x[first] = a * c - b * s
    x[second] = b * c + a * s
    return x


class LayerWeights:
    def __init__(self, model, index):
        t = model.layer_tensors(index)
        get = lambda name: t[name].dequantize()  # noqa: E731
        self.attn_norm, self.ffn_norm = (
            get("attn_norm.weight"),
            get("ffn_norm.weight"),
        )
        self.q, self.k, self.v, self.o = (
            get("attn_q.weight"),
            get("attn_k.weight"),
            get("attn_v.weight"),
            get("attn_output.weight"),
        )
        self.gate, self.up, self.down = (
            get("ffn_gate.weight"),
            get("ffn_up.weight"),
            get("ffn_down.weight"),
        )


def layer_forward(c, w, x, cache, position, kv="f32"):
    """One layer over x[tokens, hidden] at `position`, appending to cache = {"k": [], "v": []}."""
    T = x.shape[0]
    h = rms_norm(x, w.attn_norm, c.eps)
    q = (h @ w.q.T).reshape(T, c.heads, c.head_dim)
    k = (h @ w.k.T).reshape(T, c.kv_heads, c.head_dim)
    v = (h @ w.v.T).reshape(T, c.kv_heads, c.head_dim)
    positions = np.arange(position, position + T)
    rope(q, positions, c.rope_theta, c.rope_pairs)
    rope(k, positions, c.rope_theta, c.rope_pairs)
    if (
        kv == "f16"
    ):  # as the cache stores them (the current tokens' too: attention reads them back)
        k, v = (
            k.astype(np.float16).astype(np.float32),
            v.astype(np.float16).astype(np.float32),
        )
    cache["k"].append(k)
    cache["v"].append(v)
    K, V = (
        np.concatenate(cache["k"]),
        np.concatenate(cache["v"]),
    )  # [S, kv_heads, d]
    group = c.heads // c.kv_heads
    out = np.empty((T, c.heads, c.head_dim), dtype=np.float32)
    S = K.shape[0]
    mask = (
        np.arange(S)[None, :] > (positions[:, None])
    )  # causal: key s visible to token t if s <= position+t
    for hh in range(c.heads):
        g = hh // group
        scores = (
            q[:, hh, :].astype(np.float64) @ K[:, g, :].T.astype(np.float64)
        ) * c.attention_scale
        scores[mask] = -np.inf
        scores -= scores.max(axis=1, keepdims=True)
        p = np.exp(scores)
        p /= p.sum(axis=1, keepdims=True)
        out[:, hh, :] = (p @ V[:, g, :].astype(np.float64)).astype(np.float32)
    x = x + out.reshape(T, -1) @ w.o.T
    h = rms_norm(x, w.ffn_norm, c.eps)
    a, b = h @ w.gate.T, h @ w.up.T
    return x + ((a / (1.0 + np.exp(-a))) * b) @ w.down.T


class StageReference:
    """Layers first..last-1 with their KV cache, for prefill then cached steps (weights kept dequantized:
    meant for a few layers)."""

    def __init__(self, model, first, last, kv="f32"):
        self.c, self.kv, self.position = model.config, kv, 0
        self.layers = [
            (i, LayerWeights(model, i), {"k": [], "v": []})
            for i in range(first, last)
        ]

    def __call__(self, hidden):
        x = np.ascontiguousarray(hidden, dtype=np.float32)
        for _, w, cache in self.layers:
            x = layer_forward(self.c, w, x, cache, self.position, self.kv)
        self.position += x.shape[0]
        return x


def embed(model, tokens):
    return model["token_embd.weight"].dequantize(rows=tokens)


def run_layers(model, x, first, last, kv="f32"):
    """Layers first..last-1 over the whole sequence in one causal pass, one layer's weights at a time."""
    for i in range(first, last):
        x = layer_forward(
            model.config, LayerWeights(model, i), x, {"k": [], "v": []}, 0, kv
        )
    return x


def logits(model, hidden):
    """Final norm and output projection of hidden[positions, hidden] (float64)."""
    h = rms_norm(
        hidden, model["output_norm.weight"].dequantize(), model.config.eps
    ).astype(np.float64)
    W = model["output.weight"]
    out = np.empty((h.shape[0], W.shape[0]))
    for r in range(0, W.shape[0], 16384):
        rows = np.arange(r, min(r + 16384, W.shape[0]))
        out[:, rows] = h @ W.dequantize(rows=rows).astype(np.float64).T
    return out


# --- transformers cross-check (optional Torch) --------------------------------


def unpermute(w, heads):
    """Undo llama.cpp's per-head Q/K row permutation (adjacent pairs back to rotate-half halves)."""
    rows = w.shape[0]
    return (
        w.reshape(heads, rows // heads // 2, 2, *w.shape[1:])
        .swapaxes(1, 2)
        .reshape(w.shape)
    )


def transformers_layer(model, config_path, index, x, kv="f32"):
    """The same layer through transformers' own decoder layer (Granite, Llama or Phi-3) on un-permuted
    (re-fused, for Phi-3) weights: an independent check of the reference's RoPE convention, multipliers,
    tensor splits and attention."""
    import torch
    from transformers import AutoConfig
    from transformers.models.auto.modeling_auto import (
        MODEL_FOR_CAUSAL_LM_MAPPING_NAMES,
    )  # noqa: F401
    import importlib

    config = AutoConfig.from_pretrained(
        str(Path(config_path).parent), local_files_only=True
    )
    config._attn_implementation = "eager"
    kind = config.model_type
    module = importlib.import_module(
        f"transformers.models.{kind}.modeling_{kind}"
    )
    Layer = getattr(module, f"{kind.capitalize()}DecoderLayer")
    Rotary = getattr(module, f"{kind.capitalize()}RotaryEmbedding")
    c, w = model.config, LayerWeights(model, index)
    layer = Layer(config, index).to(torch.float32).eval()
    state = {
        "input_layernorm.weight": w.attn_norm,
        "post_attention_layernorm.weight": w.ffn_norm,
        "self_attn.o_proj.weight": w.o,
        "mlp.down_proj.weight": w.down,
    }
    if kind == "phi3":
        state.update(
            {
                "self_attn.qkv_proj.weight": np.concatenate([w.q, w.k, w.v]),
                "mlp.gate_up_proj.weight": np.concatenate([w.gate, w.up]),
            }
        )
    else:
        state.update(
            {
                "self_attn.q_proj.weight": unpermute(w.q, c.heads),
                "self_attn.k_proj.weight": unpermute(w.k, c.kv_heads),
                "self_attn.v_proj.weight": w.v,
                "mlp.gate_proj.weight": w.gate,
                "mlp.up_proj.weight": w.up,
            }
        )
    layer.load_state_dict(
        {
            k: torch.from_numpy(np.ascontiguousarray(v))
            for k, v in state.items()
        },
        strict=True,
    )
    T = x.shape[0]
    with torch.no_grad():
        h = torch.from_numpy(np.ascontiguousarray(x, dtype=np.float32))[None]
        positions = torch.arange(T)[None]
        embeddings = Rotary(config=config)(h, positions)
        mask = torch.triu(torch.full((T, T), float("-inf")), diagonal=1)[
            None, None
        ]
        out = layer(
            h,
            attention_mask=mask,
            position_ids=positions,
            position_embeddings=embeddings,
        )
        out = out[0] if isinstance(out, tuple) else out
    return out[0].numpy()


# File: packages/webgpu/src/ygaraty/webgpu/checkpoints/families.py
"""Model families: which execution path runs a checkpoint, and what it checks first.

A family owns one WebGPU stage implementation, its GGUF loader and reference, its device self-test cases and
its chat format. Onboarding a checkpoint means finding its family (`detect`) and passing that family's checks
(`check`); a new architecture is a new family, not a copy of an existing one.

- `qwen35`: Qwen3.5 text models (3 DeltaNet layers per full-attention layer): Qwen3.5-9B, S5-nano.
  Stage `qwen35_gpu.mjs`, loader `qwen35_gguf.py`.
- `dense`: dense decoder-only transformers of the Llama shape (RMSNorm, grouped-query attention with RoPE,
  SwiGLU FFN, no biases), as llama.cpp stores them (`llama`, `granite`, `mistral`, `phi3` GGUF architectures).
  Stage `dense_gpu.mjs`, loader `dense_gguf.py`. Granite's attention multiplier is supported; its
  embedding, residual and logit multipliers must be 1 (true of Granite 4). `phi3` (Phi-4) stores Q/K/V as one
  `attn_qkv` and gate/up as one `ffn_up` (the loader serves their row ranges as separate tensors) and its Q/K
  rows unpermuted, so RoPE rotates halves (`rope_pairs`).
- `kolibri1`: Aleph Alpha's Kolibri-1, a mixture of experts in every layer (top-k of many experts by a biased
  sigmoid router, plus a shared expert), per-head Q/K norms, norms after attention and after the MLP, and
  sliding-window attention with RoPE alternating with global attention without it. Our own GGUF
  (`kolibri_convert.py`; llama.cpp has no such architecture). Stage `kolibri_gpu.mjs`, loader `kolibri_gguf.py`.
- `qwen3moe`: Qwen3 mixture-of-experts text models (Qwen3-Coder-30B-A3B, DO-80): full causal grouped-query
  attention with per-head Q/K norms and RoPE over halves, and in every layer the top k of many experts by a
  softmax router, renormalized (`norm_topk_prob`), without a shared expert or post-branch norms; hidden and
  query widths differ. llama.cpp's GGUF (`qwen3moe`). Loader `qwen3moe_gguf.py`; stage `kolibri_gpu.mjs` (its qwen3moe profile).
- `qwen3next`: Qwen3-Next hybrid MoE text models (Qwen3-Coder-Next): Gated DeltaNet layers with one gated
  full-attention layer every `full_attention_interval`, and in every layer softmax-routed experts plus a shared
  expert scaled by a sigmoid gate. llama.cpp's legacy layout (see `Qwen3Next`). Loader and NumPy reference
  `qwen3next_gguf.py`; **no stage yet**: `check` (= `admit` + the stage's finding) always reports that, so
  `inspect` never calls it compatible and `load_model` refuses it.
"""

import json
from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path

PACKED = {
    "F32",
    "F16",
    "Q4_K",
    "Q5_K",
    "Q6_K",
    "Q8_0",
}  # what the WebGPU matrix kernels decode


def finding(level, message):
    return dict(level=level, message=message)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Family:
    name: str
    stage_module: str

    def check(self, config, header):  # -> list of findings
        raise NotImplementedError

    def geometry(self, config, header):
        raise NotImplementedError

    def layer_bytes(self, header):
        layers = int(header.get("block_count"))
        sizes = [0] * layers
        for name, t in header.tensors.items():
            if name.startswith("blk."):
                index = int(name.split(".")[1])
                if index < layers:
                    sizes[index] += t.nbytes
        return sizes

    def endpoint_bytes(self, header):
        t = header.tensors
        embedding = t["token_embd.weight"].nbytes
        output = (
            t["output.weight"].nbytes if "output.weight" in t else embedding
        )  # tied: stored again on the last stage
        return dict(
            embedding=embedding,
            output=output,
            output_norm=t["output_norm.weight"].nbytes,
        )


class Qwen35(Family):
    def check(self, config, header):
        import tempfile
        from ygaraty.webgpu.checkpoints.qwen35_gguf import WebGPUConfig

        with tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile("w", suffix=".json") as f:
            json.dump(config, f)
            f.flush()
            try:
                WebGPUConfig.from_file(f.name)
            except ValueError as error:
                return [finding("error", str(error))]
        return []

    def geometry(self, config, header):
        text = config.get("text_config", config)
        layers = text["num_hidden_layers"]
        conv = (
            2 * text["linear_num_key_heads"] * text["linear_key_head_dim"]
            + text["linear_num_value_heads"] * text["linear_value_head_dim"]
        )
        return dict(
            layers=layers,
            hidden=text["hidden_size"],
            intermediate=text["intermediate_size"],
            heads=text["num_attention_heads"],
            kv_heads=text["num_key_value_heads"],
            head_dim=text["head_dim"],
            vocab=header.tensors["token_embd.weight"].shape[0],
            context=header.get("context_length"),
            tied_embeddings="output.weight" not in header.tensors,
            # only the full-attention layers keep a KV cache; the DeltaNet layers a fixed state
            kv_layers=[i for i in range(layers) if i % 4 == 3],
            state_bytes=4
            * (
                text["linear_num_value_heads"]
                * text["linear_key_head_dim"]
                * text["linear_value_head_dim"]
                + 3 * conv
            ),
            scratch_width=2 * conv,
        )


DENSE_ARCHITECTURES = {
    "llama": "llama",
    "granite": "granite",
    "mistral": "mistral",
    "phi3": "phi3",
}  # GGUF arch -> config model_type
FUSED_ARCHITECTURES = {
    "phi3"
}  # attn_qkv = [q; k; v] and ffn_up = [gate; up] rows (transformers' qkv_proj, gate_up_proj)
HALVES_ARCHITECTURES = {
    "phi3"
}  # Q/K rows stored as transformers has them: RoPE rotates (i, i + d/2), not (2i, 2i+1)


class Dense(Family):
    def geometry(self, config, header):
        hidden, heads = config["hidden_size"], config["num_attention_heads"]
        return dict(
            layers=config["num_hidden_layers"],
            hidden=hidden,
            intermediate=config["intermediate_size"],
            heads=heads,
            kv_heads=config.get("num_key_value_heads", heads),
            head_dim=config.get("head_dim") or hidden // heads,
            vocab=config["vocab_size"],
            context=config.get("max_position_embeddings"),
            tied_embeddings=bool(config.get("tie_word_embeddings", False)),
            rope_theta=_rope_theta(config),
            eps=config.get("rms_norm_eps", 1e-5),
            attention_scale=header.get("attention.scale")
            or (config.get("head_dim") or hidden // heads) ** -0.5,
            rope_pairs="halves"
            if header.architecture() in HALVES_ARCHITECTURES
            else "adjacent",
        )

    def check(self, config, header):
        out = []
        arch = header.architecture()
        if config.get("model_type") != DENSE_ARCHITECTURES.get(arch):
            out.append(
                finding(
                    "error",
                    f"config model_type {config.get('model_type')!r} does not match GGUF architecture {arch!r}",
                )
            )
        g = self.geometry(config, header)
        if g["head_dim"] not in (64, 128):
            out.append(
                finding(
                    "error",
                    f"head_dim {g['head_dim']}: the dense stage supports 64 or 128",
                )
            )
        if g["heads"] % g["kv_heads"] or g["heads"] // g["kv_heads"] > 8:
            out.append(
                finding(
                    "error",
                    f"{g['heads']} query heads over {g['kv_heads']} KV heads: need a whole group of at most 8",
                )
            )
        if g["hidden"] != g["heads"] * g["head_dim"]:
            out.append(
                finding(
                    "error",
                    "attention width (heads x head_dim) differs from the hidden width",
                )
            )
        for key in ("hidden", "intermediate"):
            if g[key] % 256:
                out.append(
                    finding(
                        "error",
                        f"{key} {g[key]} is not a multiple of 256 (K-quant blocks)",
                    )
                )
        if config.get("attention_bias") or config.get("mlp_bias"):
            out.append(
                finding("error", "attention/MLP biases are not supported")
            )
        if config.get("hidden_act", "silu") != "silu":
            out.append(
                finding(
                    "error",
                    f"activation {config.get('hidden_act')}: only SiLU (SwiGLU) is supported",
                )
            )
        rope = (
            config.get("rope_parameters") or config.get("rope_scaling") or {}
        )
        if (
            rope.get("rope_type") or rope.get("type") or "default"
        ) != "default":
            out.append(
                finding(
                    "error",
                    f"RoPE scaling {rope}: only default RoPE is supported",
                )
            )
        if config.get("sliding_window"):
            out.append(
                finding("error", "sliding-window attention is not supported")
            )
        for key in ("embedding_scale", "residual_scale", "logit_scale"):
            value = header.get(key)
            if value is not None and abs(value - 1.0) > 1e-6:
                out.append(
                    finding(
                        "error",
                        f"GGUF {arch}.{key} = {value}: the dense stage requires 1",
                    )
                )
        if header.get("rope.dimension_count", g["head_dim"]) != g["head_dim"]:
            out.append(finding("error", "partial RoPE is not supported"))
        if int(header.get("block_count")) != g["layers"]:
            out.append(
                finding(
                    "error",
                    "GGUF block count differs from config num_hidden_layers",
                )
            )
        out += _tensor_findings(header, g, fused=arch in FUSED_ARCHITECTURES)
        if g["context"] and g["context"] > 32768:
            out.append(
                finding(
                    "note",
                    f"context {g['context']}: serving capacity is set per bundle (prompt + generated), "
                    "and KV memory grows with it",
                )
            )
        return out


def _rope_theta(config):
    rope = config.get("rope_parameters") or {}
    return float(rope.get("rope_theta", config.get("rope_theta", 10000.0)))


def _tensor_findings(header, g, fused=False):
    out, t = [], header.tensors
    hidden, ffn, kv = (
        g["hidden"],
        g["intermediate"],
        g["kv_heads"] * g["head_dim"],
    )
    matrices = (
        (
            ("attn_qkv.weight", (hidden + 2 * kv, hidden)),
            ("ffn_up.weight", (2 * ffn, hidden)),
        )
        if fused
        else (
            ("attn_q.weight", (hidden, hidden)),
            ("attn_k.weight", (kv, hidden)),
            ("attn_v.weight", (kv, hidden)),
            ("ffn_gate.weight", (ffn, hidden)),
            ("ffn_up.weight", (ffn, hidden)),
        )
    )
    matrices += (
        ("attn_output.weight", (hidden, hidden)),
        ("ffn_down.weight", (hidden, ffn)),
    )

    def need(name, shape, floats=False):
        if name not in t:
            out.append(finding("error", f"missing tensor {name}"))
            return
        if t[name].shape != shape:
            out.append(
                finding(
                    "error", f"{name}: shape {t[name].shape}, expected {shape}"
                )
            )
        allowed = {"F32"} if floats else PACKED
        if t[name].kind not in allowed:
            out.append(
                finding(
                    "error",
                    f"{name}: type {t[name].kind} is not decoded by the WebGPU kernels ({sorted(allowed)})",
                )
            )

    vocab = (
        t["token_embd.weight"].shape[0]
        if "token_embd.weight" in t
        else g["vocab"]
    )
    need("token_embd.weight", (vocab, hidden))
    if "output.weight" in t:
        need("output.weight", (vocab, hidden))
    elif not g["tied_embeddings"]:
        out.append(
            finding("error", "missing output.weight in an untied model")
        )
    need("output_norm.weight", (hidden,), floats=True)
    for i in range(g["layers"]):
        p = f"blk.{i}."
        for name, shape in matrices:
            need(p + name, shape)
        for name in ("attn_norm.weight", "ffn_norm.weight"):
            need(p + name, (hidden,), floats=True)
    extra = sorted(
        {n.split(".", 2)[-1] for n in t if n.startswith("blk.")}
        - {name for name, _ in matrices}
        - {"attn_norm.weight", "ffn_norm.weight"}
    )
    if extra:
        out.append(
            finding(
                "error", f"layer tensors the dense stage would ignore: {extra}"
            )
        )
    return out


FAST = {
    "Q4_K",
    "Q5_K",
    "Q6_K",
}  # what the expert kernel decodes (the matrix-vector kernel's word-wise decoders)
KOLIBRI_LAYER_TYPES = {"sliding_attention", "full_attention"}


class Kolibri(Family):
    """Kolibri-1 (`kolibri1`): see `kolibri_gguf` for the layer and the GGUF layout our converter writes."""

    def geometry(self, config, header):
        hidden, heads = config["hidden_size"], config["num_attention_heads"]
        head_dim = config.get("head_dim") or hidden // heads
        kv, experts, top_k = (
            config["num_key_value_heads"],
            config["num_experts"],
            config["num_experts_per_tok"],
        )
        expert_ffn, shared_ffn = (
            config["moe_intermediate_size"],
            config["shared_expert_intermediate_size"],
        )
        t = header.tensors if header is not None else {}
        vocab = (
            t["token_embd.weight"].shape[0]
            if "token_embd.weight" in t
            else config["vocab_size"]
        )
        return dict(
            layers=config["num_hidden_layers"],
            hidden=hidden,
            heads=heads,
            kv_heads=kv,
            head_dim=head_dim,
            vocab=vocab,
            experts=experts,
            top_k=top_k,
            expert_ffn=expert_ffn,
            shared_ffn=shared_ffn,
            window=config.get("sliding_window"),
            rope_theta=_rope_theta(config),
            eps=config.get("rms_norm_eps", 1e-6),
            attention_scale=head_dim**-0.5,
            sliding=[
                kind == "sliding_attention"
                for kind in config.get("layer_types") or []
            ],
            context=config.get("max_position_embeddings"),
            tied_embeddings=False,
            # per-token MLP scratch: the routed experts' gate/up rows (stage_memory's `intermediate`), and
