            # their outputs, the router logits, the shared expert and the normalized Q/K
            intermediate=top_k * 2 * expert_ffn,
            scratch_width=top_k * hidden
            + experts
            + 2 * shared_ffn
            + heads * head_dim
            + 3 * kv * head_dim,
        )

    def check(self, config, header):
        out = []
        arch = header.architecture()
        if arch != "kolibri1" or config.get("model_type") != "kolibri1":
            out.append(
                finding(
                    "error",
                    f"GGUF architecture {arch!r} / config model_type {config.get('model_type')!r}: "
                    "need kolibri1 (our converter's GGUF)",
                )
            )
            return out
        g = self.geometry(config, header)
        if g["head_dim"] not in (64, 128):
            out.append(
                finding(
                    "error",
                    f"head_dim {g['head_dim']}: the stage supports 64 or 128",
                )
            )
        if g["heads"] % g["kv_heads"] or g["heads"] // g["kv_heads"] > 16:
            out.append(
                finding(
                    "error",
                    f"{g['heads']} query heads over {g['kv_heads']} KV heads: need a whole group of at most 16",
                )
            )
        for key in ("hidden", "expert_ffn", "shared_ffn"):
            if g[key] % 256:
                out.append(
                    finding(
                        "error",
                        f"{key} {g[key]} is not a multiple of 256 (K-quant blocks)",
                    )
                )
        if (g["heads"] * g["head_dim"]) % 256:
            out.append(
                finding(
                    "error",
                    "attention width (heads x head_dim) is not a multiple of 256",
                )
            )
        if not 1 <= g["top_k"] <= 16 or g["experts"] < g["top_k"]:
            out.append(
                finding(
                    "error",
                    f"top {g['top_k']} of {g['experts']} experts: the router supports 1-16 of at least as many",
                )
            )
        if config.get("norm_topk_prob"):
            out.append(
                finding(
                    "error",
                    "norm_topk_prob: renormalized routing weights are not supported",
                )
            )
        if config.get("attention_bias") or config.get("mlp_bias"):
            out.append(
                finding("error", "attention/MLP biases are not supported")
            )
        if config.get("hidden_act", "silu") != "silu":
            out.append(
                finding(
                    "error",
                    f"activation {config.get('hidden_act')}: only SiLU (SwiGLU) is supported",
                )
            )
        if config.get("tie_word_embeddings"):
            out.append(finding("error", "tied embeddings are not supported"))
        rope = (
            config.get("rope_parameters") or config.get("rope_scaling") or {}
        )
        if (
            rope.get("rope_type") or rope.get("type") or "default"
        ) != "default" or rope.get("partial_rotary_factor", 1.0) != 1.0:
            out.append(
                finding(
                    "error",
                    f"RoPE {rope}: only default RoPE over the whole head is supported",
                )
            )
        types = config.get("layer_types") or []
        if len(types) != g["layers"] or set(types) - KOLIBRI_LAYER_TYPES:
            out.append(
                finding(
                    "error",
                    f"layer_types must give each of the {g['layers']} layers one of {sorted(KOLIBRI_LAYER_TYPES)}",
                )
            )
        if "sliding_attention" in types and not (
            config.get("use_sliding_window", True)
            and isinstance(g["window"], int)
            and g["window"] > 0
        ):
            out.append(
                finding(
                    "error",
                    "sliding layers need a positive integer sliding_window",
                )
            )
        if (
            header.get("block_count") is not None
            and int(header.get("block_count")) != g["layers"]
        ):
            out.append(
                finding(
                    "error",
                    "GGUF block count differs from config num_hidden_layers",
                )
            )
        pattern = header.get("attention.sliding_window_pattern")
        if pattern is not None and [bool(v) for v in pattern] != g["sliding"]:
            out.append(
                finding(
                    "error",
                    "the GGUF's sliding-window layers differ from config layer_types",
                )
            )
        out += _kolibri_tensor_findings(header, g)
        if g["context"] and g["context"] > 32768:
            out.append(
                finding(
                    "note",
                    f"context {g['context']}: serving capacity is set per bundle (prompt + generated), "
                    "and KV memory grows with it (every layer keeps its keys and values)",
                )
            )
        return out


def _kolibri_tensor_findings(header, g):
    out, t = [], header.tensors
    H, D, E = g["hidden"], g["head_dim"], g["experts"]
    q, kv, F, S = (
        g["heads"] * D,
        g["kv_heads"] * D,
        g["expert_ffn"],
        g["shared_ffn"],
    )

    def need(name, shape, allowed=PACKED):
        if name not in t:
            out.append(finding("error", f"missing tensor {name}"))
            return
        if t[name].shape != shape:
            out.append(
                finding(
                    "error", f"{name}: shape {t[name].shape}, expected {shape}"
                )
            )
        if t[name].kind not in allowed:
            out.append(
                finding(
                    "error",
                    f"{name}: type {t[name].kind} is not one of {sorted(allowed)}",
                )
            )

    vocab = g["vocab"]
    need("token_embd.weight", (vocab, H))
    need("output.weight", (vocab, H))
    need("output_norm.weight", (H,), {"F32"})
    layer = {
        "attn_q.weight": ((q, H), PACKED),
        "attn_k.weight": ((kv, H), PACKED),
        "attn_v.weight": ((kv, H), PACKED),
        "attn_output.weight": ((H, q), PACKED),
        "ffn_gate_shexp.weight": ((S, H), PACKED),
        "ffn_up_shexp.weight": ((S, H), PACKED),
        "ffn_down_shexp.weight": ((H, S), PACKED),
        "ffn_gate_up_exps.weight": ((E, 2 * F, H), FAST),
        "ffn_down_exps.weight": ((E, H, F), FAST),
        "ffn_gate_inp.weight": ((E, H), {"F32"}),
        "exp_probs_b.bias": ((E,), {"F32"}),
        "attn_q_norm.weight": ((D,), {"F32"}),
        "attn_k_norm.weight": ((D,), {"F32"}),
        **{
            f"{n}.weight": ((H,), {"F32"})
            for n in (
                "attn_norm",
                "attn_post_norm",
                "ffn_norm",
                "ffn_post_norm",
            )
        },
    }
    for i in range(g["layers"]):
        for name, (shape, allowed) in layer.items():
            need(f"blk.{i}.{name}", shape, allowed)
    extra = sorted(
        {n.split(".", 2)[-1] for n in t if n.startswith("blk.")} - set(layer)
    )
    if extra:
        out.append(
            finding("error", f"layer tensors the stage would ignore: {extra}")
        )
    return out


QWEN3MOE_ARCHITECTURES = {"qwen3moe": "qwen3_moe"}


class Qwen3Moe(Family):
    """Qwen3-MoE (`qwen3moe`): see `qwen3moe_gguf` for the layer and llama.cpp's GGUF layout."""

    def geometry(self, config, header):
        hidden, heads = config["hidden_size"], config["num_attention_heads"]
        head_dim = config.get("head_dim") or hidden // heads
        kv, experts, top_k = (
            config["num_key_value_heads"],
            config["num_experts"],
            config["num_experts_per_tok"],
        )
        expert_ffn = config["moe_intermediate_size"]
        t = header.tensors if header is not None else {}
        vocab = (
            t["token_embd.weight"].shape[0]
            if "token_embd.weight" in t
            else config["vocab_size"]
        )
        return dict(
            layers=config["num_hidden_layers"],
            hidden=hidden,
            heads=heads,
            kv_heads=kv,
            head_dim=head_dim,
            vocab=vocab,
            experts=experts,
            top_k=top_k,
            expert_ffn=expert_ffn,
            norm_topk=bool(config.get("norm_topk_prob", False)),
            rope_theta=_rope_theta(config),
            eps=config.get("rms_norm_eps", 1e-6),
            attention_scale=head_dim**-0.5,
            context=config.get("max_position_embeddings"),
            tied_embeddings=False,
            # per-token MLP scratch: the routed experts' gate and up rows, their outputs, the router logits
            # and the normalized Q/K
            intermediate=top_k * 2 * expert_ffn,
            scratch_width=top_k * hidden
            + experts
            + heads * head_dim
            + 3 * kv * head_dim,
        )

    def check(self, config, header):
        out = []
        arch = header.architecture()
        if (
            arch not in QWEN3MOE_ARCHITECTURES
            or config.get("model_type") != QWEN3MOE_ARCHITECTURES[arch]
        ):
            out.append(
                finding(
                    "error",
                    f"GGUF architecture {arch!r} / config model_type {config.get('model_type')!r}: "
                    "need qwen3moe / qwen3_moe",
                )
            )
            return out
        g = self.geometry(config, header)
        if g["head_dim"] not in (64, 128):
            out.append(
                finding(
                    "error",
                    f"head_dim {g['head_dim']}: the stage supports 64 or 128",
                )
            )
        if g["heads"] % g["kv_heads"] or g["heads"] // g["kv_heads"] > 16:
            out.append(
                finding(
                    "error",
                    f"{g['heads']} query heads over {g['kv_heads']} KV heads: need a whole group of at most 16",
                )
            )
        for key in ("hidden", "expert_ffn"):
            if g[key] % 256:
                out.append(
                    finding(
                        "error",
                        f"{key} {g[key]} is not a multiple of 256 (K-quant blocks)",
                    )
                )
        if (g["heads"] * g["head_dim"]) % 256:
            out.append(
                finding(
                    "error",
                    "attention width (heads x head_dim) is not a multiple of 256",
                )
            )
        if not 1 <= g["top_k"] <= 16 or g["experts"] < g["top_k"]:
            out.append(
                finding(
                    "error",
                    f"top {g['top_k']} of {g['experts']} experts: the router supports 1-16 of at least as many",
                )
            )
        if config.get("decoder_sparse_step", 1) != 1 or config.get(
            "mlp_only_layers"
        ):
            out.append(
                finding(
                    "error",
                    "dense MLP layers (decoder_sparse_step > 1 or mlp_only_layers) are not supported",
                )
            )
        if config.get("shared_expert_intermediate_size"):
            out.append(
                finding(
                    "error",
                    "a shared expert is not part of qwen3moe (that is qwen2moe)",
                )
            )
        if config.get("attention_bias") or config.get("mlp_bias"):
            out.append(
                finding("error", "attention/MLP biases are not supported")
            )
        if config.get("hidden_act", "silu") != "silu":
            out.append(
                finding(
                    "error",
                    f"activation {config.get('hidden_act')}: only SiLU (SwiGLU) is supported",
                )
            )
        if config.get("tie_word_embeddings"):
            out.append(finding("error", "tied embeddings are not supported"))
        if config.get("use_sliding_window"):
            out.append(
                finding(
                    "error",
                    "sliding-window attention is not supported for qwen3moe",
                )
            )
        rope = (
            config.get("rope_parameters") or config.get("rope_scaling") or {}
        )
        if (
            rope.get("rope_type") or rope.get("type") or "default"
        ) != "default" or rope.get("partial_rotary_factor", 1.0) != 1.0:
            out.append(
                finding(
                    "error",
                    f"RoPE {rope}: only default RoPE over the whole head is supported",
                )
            )
        if (
            header.get("block_count") is not None
            and int(header.get("block_count")) != g["layers"]
        ):
            out.append(
                finding(
                    "error",
                    "GGUF block count differs from config num_hidden_layers",
                )
            )
        for key, want in (
            ("expert_count", g["experts"]),
            ("expert_used_count", g["top_k"]),
            ("expert_feed_forward_length", g["expert_ffn"]),
        ):
            if header.get(key) is not None and int(header.get(key)) != want:
                out.append(
                    finding(
                        "error",
                        f"GGUF {key} {header.get(key)} differs from the config's {want}",
                    )
                )
        out += _qwen3moe_tensor_findings(header, g)
        if g["context"] and g["context"] > 32768:
            out.append(
                finding(
                    "note",
                    f"context {g['context']}: serving capacity is set per bundle (prompt + generated), "
                    "and KV memory grows with it (every layer keeps its keys and values)",
                )
            )
        return out


def _qwen3moe_tensor_findings(header, g):
    out, t = [], header.tensors
    H, D, E = g["hidden"], g["head_dim"], g["experts"]
    q, kv, F = g["heads"] * D, g["kv_heads"] * D, g["expert_ffn"]

    def need(name, shape, allowed=PACKED):
        if name not in t:
            out.append(finding("error", f"missing tensor {name}"))
            return
        if t[name].shape != shape:
            out.append(
                finding(
                    "error", f"{name}: shape {t[name].shape}, expected {shape}"
                )
            )
        if t[name].kind not in allowed:
            out.append(
                finding(
                    "error",
                    f"{name}: type {t[name].kind} is not one of {sorted(allowed)}",
                )
            )

    need("token_embd.weight", (g["vocab"], H))
    need("output.weight", (g["vocab"], H))
    need("output_norm.weight", (H,), {"F32"})
    layer = {
        "attn_q.weight": ((q, H), PACKED),
        "attn_k.weight": ((kv, H), PACKED),
        "attn_v.weight": ((kv, H), PACKED),
        "attn_output.weight": ((H, q), PACKED),
        "ffn_gate_exps.weight": ((E, F, H), FAST),
        "ffn_up_exps.weight": ((E, F, H), FAST),
        "ffn_down_exps.weight": ((E, H, F), FAST),
        "ffn_gate_inp.weight": ((E, H), {"F32"}),
        "attn_q_norm.weight": ((D,), {"F32"}),
        "attn_k_norm.weight": ((D,), {"F32"}),
        "attn_norm.weight": ((H,), {"F32"}),
        "ffn_norm.weight": ((H,), {"F32"}),
    }
    for i in range(g["layers"]):
        for name, (shape, allowed) in layer.items():
            need(f"blk.{i}.{name}", shape, allowed)
    extra = sorted(
        {n.split(".", 2)[-1] for n in t if n.startswith("blk.")} - set(layer)
    )
    if extra:
        out.append(
            finding("error", f"layer tensors the stage would ignore: {extra}")
        )
    return out


QWEN3NEXT_STAGE = None  # no WebGPU stage runs qwen3next yet: admission reports it as an error


class Qwen3Next(Family):
    """Qwen3-Next (`qwen3next` GGUF, `qwen3_next` config), as in Qwen/Qwen3-Coder-Next-GGUF (Q4_K_M, 4 shards).

    That GGUF has llama.cpp's *legacy* DeltaNet layout, which differs from our `qwen35` GGUFs (and from newer
    llama.cpp converters, which write split `attn_qkv` / `attn_gate`):

    - `ssm_in` [2*Kh*Dk + 2*Vh*Dv, hidden] is transformers' `in_proj_qkvz` unchanged: rows grouped by key head,
      each group [q (Dk), k (Dk), v (Vh/Kh*Dv), z (Vh/Kh*Dv)]; `ssm_ba` [2*Vh, hidden] likewise [b, a] per key head.
    - Value heads stay in grouped order (value head j reads key head j div Vh/Kh), also in `ssm_conv1d`'s V
      channels (its channels are [q all heads, k all heads, v]), `ssm_a`, `ssm_dt.bias` and `ssm_out`'s columns.
      `ssm_out` is K-quantized over blocks of 256 columns and a value head is 128 wide, so the qwen35 kernels'
      tiled order cannot be produced by permuting packed bytes: a stage must index grouped order itself.
    - Full attention: `attn_q` [2*heads*D, hidden] interleaves per head [query (D), output gate (D)];
      attention output is multiplied by sigmoid(gate). Per-head Q/K RMSNorm; RoPE over halves of the first
      `rope.dimension_count` (partial_rotary_factor * D) dims.
    - The norm before the MLP is `post_attention_norm` (not `ffn_norm`). RMSNorm weights are stored plus one
      (the converter adds transformers' zero-centred offset), except `ssm_norm`; `ssm_a` = -exp(A_log).
    - MoE in every layer: router `ffn_gate_inp` (F32), softmax, top k renormalized (`norm_topk_prob`), experts
      split gate/up/down (`_exps`), plus a shared expert (`_shexp`) times sigmoid(`ffn_gate_inp_shexp` . x),
      whose gate vector is [hidden] (F16 in the Q4_K_M artifact).
    """

    def geometry(self, config, header):
        c = config.get("text_config", config)
        heads, head_dim = c["num_attention_heads"], c["head_dim"]
        layers, interval = (
            c["num_hidden_layers"],
            c.get("full_attention_interval", 4),
        )
        Kh, Vh = c["linear_num_key_heads"], c["linear_num_value_heads"]
        Dk, Dv, conv_kernel = (
            c["linear_key_head_dim"],
            c["linear_value_head_dim"],
            c["linear_conv_kernel_dim"],
        )
        experts, top_k = c["num_experts"], c["num_experts_per_tok"]
        expert_ffn, shared_ffn = (
            c["moe_intermediate_size"],
            c["shared_expert_intermediate_size"],
        )
        conv = 2 * Kh * Dk + Vh * Dv
        types = c.get("layer_types") or [
            "full_attention" if (i + 1) % interval == 0 else "linear_attention"
            for i in range(layers)
        ]
        t = header.tensors if header is not None else {}
        vocab = (
            t["token_embd.weight"].shape[0]
            if "token_embd.weight" in t
            else c["vocab_size"]
        )
        rope_parameters = c.get("rope_parameters") or {}
        rope_dims = int(
            head_dim
            * rope_parameters.get(
                "partial_rotary_factor", c.get("partial_rotary_factor", 1.0)
            )
        )
        return dict(
            layers=layers,
            hidden=c["hidden_size"],
            heads=heads,
            kv_heads=c["num_key_value_heads"],
            head_dim=head_dim,
            vocab=vocab,
            context=c.get("max_position_embeddings"),
            tied_embeddings=False,
            rope_theta=_rope_theta(c),
            rope_dims=rope_dims,
            eps=c.get("rms_norm_eps", 1e-6),
            attention_scale=head_dim**-0.5,
            layer_types=types,
            kv_layers=[
                i for i, kind in enumerate(types) if kind == "full_attention"
            ],
            linear_key_heads=Kh,
            linear_value_heads=Vh,
            linear_key_dim=Dk,
            linear_value_dim=Dv,
            conv_kernel=conv_kernel,
            conv_channels=conv,
            experts=experts,
            top_k=top_k,
            expert_ffn=expert_ffn,
            shared_ffn=shared_ffn,
            norm_topk=bool(c.get("norm_topk_prob", False)),
            value_order="grouped",
            layout="legacy_qkvz",
            # per DeltaNet layer and sequence: the recurrent state (float32) and the convolution's history
            state_bytes=4 * (Vh * Dk * Dv + (conv_kernel - 1) * conv),
            # per-token MLP scratch: the routed experts' gate/up rows (stage_memory's `intermediate`)
            intermediate=top_k * 2 * expert_ffn,
            # the widest per-token buffers besides it: the fused DeltaNet projection, the gated query, the
            # routed outputs, router logits and the shared expert's gate/up rows
            scratch_width=max(2 * Kh * Dk + 2 * Vh * Dv, 2 * heads * head_dim)
            + top_k * c["hidden_size"]
            + experts
            + 2 * shared_ffn,
        )

    def check(self, config, header):
        out = self.admit(config, header)
        if QWEN3NEXT_STAGE is None:
            out.append(
                finding(
                    "error",
                    "no WebGPU stage runs qwen3next yet (admission only: the artifact was checked, "
                    "not executed)",
                )
            )
        return out

    def admit(self, config, header):
        """The artifact's findings without the stage's: what the loader and the NumPy reference require."""
        out = []
        arch, c = header.architecture(), config.get("text_config", config)
        if arch != "qwen3next" or c.get("model_type") != "qwen3_next":
            out.append(
                finding(
                    "error",
                    f"GGUF architecture {arch!r} / config model_type {c.get('model_type')!r}: "
                    "need qwen3next / qwen3_next",
                )
            )
            return out
        g = self.geometry(config, header)
        if len(g["layer_types"]) != g["layers"] or set(g["layer_types"]) - {
            "linear_attention",
            "full_attention",
        }:
            out.append(
                finding(
                    "error",
                    f"layer types must give each of the {g['layers']} layers linear_attention "
                    "or full_attention",
                )
            )
        if not g["kv_layers"]:
            out.append(finding("error", "no full-attention layer"))
        if g["heads"] % g["kv_heads"] or g["heads"] // g["kv_heads"] > 16:
            out.append(
                finding(
                    "error",
                    f"{g['heads']} query heads over {g['kv_heads']} KV heads: need a whole group of at most 16",
                )
            )
        if g["linear_value_heads"] % g["linear_key_heads"]:
            out.append(
                finding(
                    "error",
                    f"{g['linear_value_heads']} DeltaNet value heads over {g['linear_key_heads']} key "
                    "heads: need a whole number per key head",
                )
            )
        for key in ("hidden", "expert_ffn", "shared_ffn"):
            if g[key] % 256:
                out.append(
                    finding(
                        "error",
                        f"{key} {g[key]} is not a multiple of 256 (K-quant blocks)",
                    )
                )
        if (g["heads"] * g["head_dim"]) % 256 or (
            g["linear_value_heads"] * g["linear_value_dim"]
        ) % 256:
            out.append(
                finding(
                    "error",
                    "attention or DeltaNet value width is not a multiple of 256 (K-quant blocks)",
                )
            )
        if not 1 <= g["top_k"] <= 16 or g["experts"] < g["top_k"]:
            out.append(
                finding(
                    "error",
                    f"top {g['top_k']} of {g['experts']} experts: the router supports 1-16 of at least as many",
                )
            )
        if not g["norm_topk"]:
            out.append(
                finding(
                    "error",
                    "norm_topk_prob false: only renormalized top-k routing is admitted",
                )
            )
        if c.get("decoder_sparse_step", 1) != 1 or c.get("mlp_only_layers"):
            out.append(
                finding(
                    "error",
                    "dense MLP layers (decoder_sparse_step > 1 or mlp_only_layers) are not supported",
                )
            )
        if not g["shared_ffn"]:
            out.append(
                finding("error", "no shared expert: qwen3next layers have one")
            )
        if c.get("attention_bias") or c.get("mlp_bias"):
            out.append(
                finding("error", "attention/MLP biases are not supported")
            )
        if c.get("hidden_act", "silu") != "silu":
            out.append(
                finding(
                    "error",
                    f"activation {c.get('hidden_act')}: only SiLU (SwiGLU) is supported",
                )
            )
        if c.get("tie_word_embeddings", config.get("tie_word_embeddings")):
            out.append(finding("error", "tied embeddings are not supported"))
        if c.get("use_sliding_window"):
            out.append(
                finding("error", "sliding-window attention is not supported")
            )
        rope = c.get("rope_parameters") or c.get("rope_scaling") or {}
        if (
            rope.get("rope_type") or rope.get("type") or "default"
        ) != "default":
            out.append(
                finding(
                    "error",
                    f"RoPE {rope}: only default (partial) RoPE is supported",
                )
            )
        if not 0 < g["rope_dims"] <= g["head_dim"] or g["rope_dims"] % 2:
            out.append(
                finding(
                    "error",
                    f"RoPE over {g['rope_dims']} of {g['head_dim']} dims: need an even count",
                )
            )
        for key, want in (
            ("block_count", g["layers"]),
            ("expert_count", g["experts"]),
            ("expert_used_count", g["top_k"]),
            ("expert_feed_forward_length", g["expert_ffn"]),
            ("expert_shared_feed_forward_length", g["shared_ffn"]),
            ("attention.head_count", g["heads"]),
            ("attention.head_count_kv", g["kv_heads"]),
            ("attention.key_length", g["head_dim"]),
            ("attention.value_length", g["head_dim"]),
            ("rope.dimension_count", g["rope_dims"]),
            ("ssm.conv_kernel", g["conv_kernel"]),
            ("ssm.state_size", g["linear_key_dim"]),
            ("ssm.group_count", g["linear_key_heads"]),
            ("ssm.time_step_rank", g["linear_value_heads"]),
            (
                "ssm.inner_size",
                g["linear_value_heads"] * g["linear_value_dim"],
            ),
        ):
            if header.get(key) is not None and int(header.get(key)) != want:
                out.append(
                    finding(
                        "error",
                        f"GGUF {key} {header.get(key)} differs from the config's {want}",
                    )
                )
        out += _qwen3next_tensor_findings(header, g)
        if g["context"] and g["context"] > 32768:
            out.append(
                finding(
                    "note",
                    f"context {g['context']}: serving capacity is set per bundle; only the "
                    f"{len(g['kv_layers'])} full-attention layers keep a KV cache",
                )
            )
        return out


def _qwen3next_tensor_findings(header, g):
    out, t = [], header.tensors
    H, D, E = g["hidden"], g["head_dim"], g["experts"]
    Kh, Vh, Dk, Dv = (
        g["linear_key_heads"],
        g["linear_value_heads"],
        g["linear_key_dim"],
        g["linear_value_dim"],
    )
    F, S = g["expert_ffn"], g["shared_ffn"]

    def need(name, shape, allowed=PACKED):
        if name not in t:
            out.append(finding("error", f"missing tensor {name}"))
            return
        if t[name].shape != shape:
            out.append(
                finding(
                    "error", f"{name}: shape {t[name].shape}, expected {shape}"
                )
            )
        if t[name].kind not in allowed:
            out.append(
                finding(
                    "error",
                    f"{name}: type {t[name].kind} is not one of {sorted(allowed)}",
                )
            )

    need("token_embd.weight", (g["vocab"], H))
    need("output.weight", (g["vocab"], H))
    need("output_norm.weight", (H,), {"F32"})
    common = {
        "attn_norm.weight": ((H,), {"F32"}),
        "post_attention_norm.weight": ((H,), {"F32"}),
        "ffn_gate_inp.weight": ((E, H), {"F32"}),
        "ffn_gate_exps.weight": ((E, F, H), FAST),
        "ffn_up_exps.weight": ((E, F, H), FAST),
        "ffn_down_exps.weight": ((E, H, F), FAST),
        "ffn_gate_shexp.weight": ((S, H), PACKED),
        "ffn_up_shexp.weight": ((S, H), PACKED),
        "ffn_down_shexp.weight": ((H, S), PACKED),
        "ffn_gate_inp_shexp.weight": ((H,), {"F32", "F16"}),
    }
    linear = {
        "ssm_in.weight": ((2 * Kh * Dk + 2 * Vh * Dv, H), PACKED),
        "ssm_ba.weight": ((2 * Vh, H), PACKED),
        "ssm_conv1d.weight": ((g["conv_channels"], g["conv_kernel"]), {"F32"}),
        "ssm_a": ((Vh,), {"F32"}),
        "ssm_dt.bias": ((Vh,), {"F32"}),
        "ssm_norm.weight": ((Dv,), {"F32"}),
        "ssm_out.weight": ((H, Vh * Dv), PACKED),
    }
    full = {
        "attn_q.weight": ((2 * g["heads"] * D, H), PACKED),
        "attn_k.weight": ((g["kv_heads"] * D, H), PACKED),
        "attn_v.weight": ((g["kv_heads"] * D, H), PACKED),
        "attn_output.weight": ((H, g["heads"] * D), PACKED),
        "attn_q_norm.weight": ((D,), {"F32"}),
        "attn_k_norm.weight": ((D,), {"F32"}),
    }
    split = sorted(
        {n.split(".", 2)[-1] for n in t if n.startswith("blk.")}
        & {"attn_qkv.weight", "attn_gate.weight"}
    )
    if split:
        out.append(
            finding(
                "error",
                f"split DeltaNet tensors {split} (newer llama.cpp layout): only the legacy fused "
                "ssm_in / ssm_ba layout is admitted",
            )
        )
    for i, kind in enumerate(g["layer_types"]):
        layer = dict(common, **(full if kind == "full_attention" else linear))
        for name, (shape, allowed) in layer.items():
            need(f"blk.{i}.{name}", shape, allowed)
        extra = sorted(
            n.split(".", 2)[-1]
            for n in t
            if n.startswith(f"blk.{i}.") and n.split(".", 2)[-1] not in layer
        )
        if extra:
            out.append(
                finding(
                    "error",
                    f"layer {i} ({kind}) tensors the stage would ignore: {extra}",
                )
            )
    return out


FAMILIES = {
    "qwen35": Qwen35("qwen35", "qwen35_gpu.mjs"),
    "dense": Dense("dense", "dense_gpu.mjs"),
    "kolibri1": Kolibri("kolibri1", "kolibri_gpu.mjs"),
    "qwen3moe": Qwen3Moe("qwen3moe", "kolibri_gpu.mjs"),
    "qwen3next": Qwen3Next("qwen3next", None),
}


def detect(config, header=None):
    """The family that runs this checkpoint (by its config, and its GGUF architecture when given)."""
    arch = header.architecture() if header is not None else None
    text = config.get("text_config", config)
    if (
        arch == "qwen3next" or text.get("model_type") == "qwen3_next"
    ):  # before qwen35: both have DeltaNet keys
        return FAMILIES["qwen3next"]
    if (
        arch == "qwen35"
        or text.get("model_type", "").startswith("qwen3_5")
        or "linear_num_value_heads" in text
    ):
        return FAMILIES["qwen35"]
    if arch == "kolibri1" or config.get("model_type") == "kolibri1":
        return FAMILIES["kolibri1"]
    if (
        arch in QWEN3MOE_ARCHITECTURES
        or config.get("model_type") in QWEN3MOE_ARCHITECTURES.values()
    ):
        return FAMILIES["qwen3moe"]
    if (
        arch in DENSE_ARCHITECTURES
        or config.get("model_type") in DENSE_ARCHITECTURES.values()
    ):
        return FAMILIES["dense"]
    raise ValueError(
        f"No model family runs architecture {arch or config.get('model_type')!r} yet: "
        "add one (see docs/internal/inference-model-onboarding.md)"
    )


def load_model(gguf, config_path, *, tensor_only=False):
    """The checkpoint's loader for its family: `qwen35_gguf.Model`, `dense_gguf.Model` or `kolibri_gguf.Model`
    (all expose `family`, `config.layers`, `config.manifest()`, `layer_type(i)`, `layer_tensors(i)`,
    `validate_webgpu()`)."""
    config = json.loads(Path(config_path).read_text())
    name = detect(config).name
    if name == "qwen3next":
        if QWEN3NEXT_STAGE is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "qwen3next has no WebGPU stage yet (admission only): its loader and NumPy reference are "
                "qwen3next_gguf.Model; see docs/internal/inference-model-onboarding.md"
            )
        from ygaraty.webgpu.checkpoints import qwen3next_gguf

        return qwen3next_gguf.Model(gguf, config_path, tensor_only=tensor_only)
    if name == "kolibri1":
        from ygaraty.webgpu.checkpoints import kolibri_gguf

        return kolibri_gguf.Model(gguf, config_path, tensor_only=tensor_only)
    if name == "qwen3moe":
        from ygaraty.webgpu.checkpoints import qwen3moe_gguf

        return qwen3moe_gguf.Model(gguf, config_path, tensor_only=tensor_only)
    if name == "dense":
        from ygaraty.webgpu.checkpoints import dense_gguf

        return dense_gguf.Model(gguf, config_path, tensor_only=tensor_only)
    from ygaraty.webgpu.checkpoints import qwen35_gguf

    return qwen35_gguf.Model(gguf, config_path, tensor_only=tensor_only)


# File: packages/webgpu/src/ygaraty/webgpu/checkpoints/gguf_metadata.py
"""Skip unused GGUF tokenizer metadata without allocating an object per token.

Only bundle preparation opts in. Tensor parsing/dequantization remains gguf's;
all non-tokenizer metadata (including alignment) retains the normal reader path.
"""

import struct

SCALAR_BYTES = {
    0: 1,
    1: 1,
    2: 2,
    3: 2,
    4: 4,
    5: 4,
    6: 4,
    7: 1,
    10: 8,
    11: 8,
    12: 8,
}


def skip_value(data, offset, kind, endian="<", depth=0):
    """Return the next byte offset of a GGUF value, checking bounds at every step."""
    if depth > 32:
        raise ValueError("GGUF metadata nesting exceeds 32 levels")

    def advance(position, size):
        if position < 0 or size < 0 or position + size > len(data):
            raise ValueError("Truncated GGUF metadata")
        return position + size

    if kind in SCALAR_BYTES:
        return advance(offset, SCALAR_BYTES[kind])
    if kind == 8:  # STRING: uint64 length, then UTF-8 bytes
        start = advance(offset, 8)
        length = struct.unpack_from(endian + "Q", data, offset)[0]
        return advance(start, length)
    if (
        kind == 9
    ):  # ARRAY: element type, count, then values (without repeated type tags)
        start = advance(offset, 12)
        element, count = struct.unpack_from(endian + "IQ", data, offset)
        if element in SCALAR_BYTES:
            return advance(start, count * SCALAR_BYTES[element])
        if element not in (8, 9):
            raise ValueError("Unknown GGUF array element type")
        minimum = 8 if element == 8 else 12
        advance(start, count * minimum)
        for _ in range(count):
            start = skip_value(data, start, element, endian, depth + 1)
        return start
    raise ValueError("Unknown GGUF metadata type")


def tensor_reader(path):
    import gguf

    class TensorReader(gguf.GGUFReader):
        def _build_fields(self, offset, count):
            endian = "<" if self.endianess == gguf.GGUFEndian.LITTLE else ">"
            for _ in range(count):
                end = skip_value(self.data, offset, 8, endian)
                name = bytes(self.data[offset + 8 : end]).decode("utf-8")
                type_end = skip_value(self.data, end, 4, endian)
                kind = struct.unpack_from(endian + "I", self.data, end)[0]
                if name.startswith("tokenizer."):
                    offset = skip_value(self.data, type_end, kind, endian)
                else:
                    offset = super()._build_fields(offset, 1)
            return offset

    return TensorReader(path)


# File: packages/webgpu/src/ygaraty/webgpu/checkpoints/gguf_remote.py
"""Local GGUF header and tensor metadata.

The historical module name is retained for loader compatibility.
This extraction has no HTTP or repository-download helpers.
"""

import json
import re
import struct
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from pathlib import Path

# GGUF value types: 0 u8, 1 i8, 2 u16, 3 i16, 4 u32, 5 i32, 6 f32, 7 bool, 8 string, 9 array, 10 u64, 11 i64, 12 f64
SCALARS = {
    0: "B",
    1: "b",
    2: "H",
    3: "h",
    4: "I",
    5: "i",
    6: "f",
    7: "?",
    10: "Q",
    11: "q",
    12: "d",
}
# GGML tensor types: (name, elements per block, bytes per block)
GGML_TYPES = {
    0: ("F32", 1, 4),
    1: ("F16", 1, 2),
    2: ("Q4_0", 32, 18),
    3: ("Q4_1", 32, 20),
    6: ("Q5_0", 32, 22),
    7: ("Q5_1", 32, 24),
    8: ("Q8_0", 32, 34),
    9: ("Q8_1", 32, 36),
    10: ("Q2_K", 256, 84),
    11: ("Q3_K", 256, 110),
    12: ("Q4_K", 256, 144),
    13: ("Q5_K", 256, 176),
    14: ("Q6_K", 256, 210),
    15: ("Q8_K", 256, 292),
    16: ("IQ2_XXS", 256, 66),
    17: ("IQ2_XS", 256, 74),
    18: ("IQ3_XXS", 256, 98),
    19: ("IQ1_S", 256, 50),
    20: ("IQ4_NL", 32, 18),
    21: ("IQ3_S", 256, 110),
    22: ("IQ2_S", 256, 82),
    23: ("IQ4_XS", 256, 136),
    24: ("I8", 1, 1),
    25: ("I16", 1, 2),
    26: ("I32", 1, 4),
    27: ("I64", 1, 8),
    28: ("F64", 1, 8),
    29: ("IQ1_M", 256, 56),
    30: ("BF16", 1, 2),
}


@dataclass
class TensorInfo:
    name: str
    kind: str
    shape: tuple  # NumPy order: (rows, columns); rows are output features
    offset: (
        int  # from the start of the file (its shard's file in a split GGUF)
    )
    nbytes: int
    shard: int = 0  # which file of a split GGUF holds it

    @property
    def row_bytes(self):
        _, block, size = next(
            v for v in GGML_TYPES.values() if v[0] == self.kind
        )
        return self.shape[-1] // block * size


@dataclass
class Header:
    version: int
    metadata: dict
    tensors: dict = field(default_factory=dict)
    data_start: int = 0
    size: int | None = (
        None  # bytes of the file (of all shards for a split GGUF)
    )
    shards: list = field(
        default_factory=list
    )  # split GGUF: [{"size", "data_start", "tensors"}] per shard

    def architecture(self):
        return self.metadata.get("general.architecture")

    def get(self, key, default=None):
        """A metadata value under the architecture's prefix (`llama.block_count`) or as given."""
        arch = self.architecture()
        return self.metadata.get(
            f"{arch}.{key}", self.metadata.get(key, default)
        )


class _Source:
    """Read bounded local GGUF header bytes; never resolve a URL."""

    def __init__(self, where, first=8 << 20):
        if "://" in str(where):
            raise ValueError("Checkpoint preparation accepts local files only")
        self.where = Path(where).resolve(strict=True)
        self.size = self.where.stat().st_size
        self.data = b""
        self.ensure(first)

    def ensure(self, end):
        if end <= len(self.data):
            return
        if end > 64 << 20:
            raise ValueError("GGUF header exceeds 64 MiB")
        if len(self.data) >= self.size:
            raise ValueError("Truncated GGUF header")
        with self.where.open("rb") as handle:
            self.data = handle.read(
                min(64 << 20, max(end, 2 * len(self.data)))
            )


def read_header(where, first=8 << 20) -> Header:
    """The GGUF header (metadata and tensor table) of a local file."""
    src = _Source(where, first)
    pos = 0

    def take(fmt):
        nonlocal pos
        size = struct.calcsize("<" + fmt)
        src.ensure(pos + size)
        values = struct.unpack_from("<" + fmt, src.data, pos)
        pos += size
        return values if len(values) > 1 else values[0]

    def string():
        nonlocal pos
        n = take("Q")
        src.ensure(pos + n)
        s = src.data[pos : pos + n].decode("utf-8", "replace")
        pos += n
        return s

    def value(kind, summarize=False):
        nonlocal pos
        if kind in SCALARS:
            return take(SCALARS[kind])
        if kind == 8:
            return string()
        if kind == 9:
            element, count = take("I"), take("Q")
            if summarize:  # tokenizer arrays: skipped, only their length kept
                for _ in range(count):
                    value(element, True)
                return {"array_of": element, "length": count}
            return [value(element) for _ in range(count)]
        raise ValueError(f"Unknown GGUF value type {kind}")

    if take("4s") != b"GGUF":
        raise ValueError("Not a GGUF file")
    version = take("I")
    if version not in (2, 3):
        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported GGUF version {version}")
    n_tensors, n_kv = take("Q"), take("Q")
    metadata = {}
    for _ in range(n_kv):
        key = string()
        kind = take("I")
        metadata[key] = value(
            kind, summarize=key.startswith("tokenizer.ggml.") and kind == 9
        )
    tensors = {}
    for _ in range(n_tensors):
        name = string()
        dims = [take("Q") for _ in range(take("I"))]
        kind, offset = take("I"), take("Q")
        if kind not in GGML_TYPES:
            raise ValueError(f"Unknown GGML type {kind} for {name}")
        type_name, block, size = GGML_TYPES[kind]
        elements = 1
        for d in dims:
            elements *= d
        tensors[name] = TensorInfo(
            name,
            type_name,
            tuple(reversed(dims)),
            offset,
            elements // block * size,
        )
    alignment = int(metadata.get("general.alignment", 32))
    data_start = (pos + alignment - 1) // alignment * alignment
    for t in tensors.values():
        t.offset += data_start
    return Header(version, metadata, tensors, data_start, src.size)


SPLIT_NAME = re.compile(
    r"^(?P<stem>.*)-(?P<no>\d{5})-of-(?P<count>\d{5})\.gguf$"
)


def split_names(first):
    """All shard names of a split GGUF given its first (`X-00001-of-00004.gguf` -> 4 names), or [first] for a
    single file. Raises for a later shard: the first one holds the model's metadata."""
    m = SPLIT_NAME.match(first)
    if not m:
        return [first]
    count = int(m["count"])
    if int(m["no"]) != 1 or count < 1:
        raise ValueError(
            f"{first}: name the first shard (-00001-of-{m['count']}.gguf)"
        )
    return [
        f"{m['stem']}-{i:05d}-of-{count:05d}.gguf" for i in range(1, count + 1)
    ]


def read_split_headers(locations, first=8 << 20) -> Header:
    """One logical header for a split GGUF, from its shards in order (local paths; one location is an unsplit
    file). Metadata comes from the first shard (later shards carry only `split.*`). Checked: every shard's
    `split.no` and `split.count`, one `split.tensors.count` equal to the tensors found, no tensor in two shards,
    each tensor's bytes inside its shard, and no two tensors of a shard overlapping."""
    headers = [read_header(where, first) for where in locations]
    if len(headers) == 1 and "split.count" not in headers[0].metadata:
        return headers[0]
    count = len(headers)
    expected_tensors = headers[0].metadata.get("split.tensors.count")
    errors = []
    for i, h in enumerate(headers):
        if (
            h.metadata.get("split.no") != i
            or h.metadata.get("split.count") != count
        ):
            errors.append(
                f"shard {i + 1}: split.no {h.metadata.get('split.no')} / split.count "
                f"{h.metadata.get('split.count')}, expected {i} / {count}"
            )
        if h.metadata.get("split.tensors.count") != expected_tensors:
            errors.append(
                f"shard {i + 1}: split.tensors.count {h.metadata.get('split.tensors.count')} differs "
                f"from the first shard's {expected_tensors}"
            )
    if headers[0].architecture() is None:
        errors.append("the first shard has no general.architecture")
    tensors, shards = {}, []
    for i, h in enumerate(headers):
        spans = []
        for name, t in h.tensors.items():
            if name in tensors:
                errors.append(
                    f"tensor {name} is in shards {tensors[name].shard + 1} and {i + 1}"
                )
                continue
            if h.size is not None and t.offset + t.nbytes > h.size:
                errors.append(
                    f"tensor {name} ends at byte {t.offset + t.nbytes}, past shard {i + 1}'s {h.size}"
                )
            t.shard = i
            tensors[name] = t
            spans.append((t.offset, t.offset + t.nbytes, name))
        spans.sort()
        for (_, end, a), (start, _, b) in zip(spans, spans[1:]):
            if start < end:
                errors.append(f"tensors {a} and {b} overlap in shard {i + 1}")
        shards.append(
            dict(size=h.size, data_start=h.data_start, tensors=len(h.tensors))
        )
    if expected_tensors is None or len(tensors) != expected_tensors:
        errors.append(
            f"{len(tensors)} tensors across {count} shards, split.tensors.count says {expected_tensors}"
        )
    if errors:
        raise ValueError("Invalid split GGUF: " + "; ".join(errors))
    sizes = [s["size"] for s in shards]
    return Header(
        headers[0].version,
        dict(headers[0].metadata),
        tensors,
        headers[0].data_start,
        sum(sizes) if None not in sizes else None,
        shards,
    )


# File: packages/webgpu/src/ygaraty/webgpu/checkpoints/kolibri_gguf.py
"""Kolibri-1 (Aleph Alpha, `kolibri1`) from our own GGUF: the `kolibri1` model family.

Upstream llama.cpp has no `kolibri1` architecture, so `kolibri_convert.py` writes the GGUF from the official FP8
checkpoint itself (tensor names and layout below). The browser stage (`kolibri_gpu.mjs`) reads its bytes
unchanged; the NumPy reference here computes in the same layout, without Torch, following transformers'
`modeling_kolibri1.py` (5.22):

- 50 decoder layers, hidden 2,560; attention: 48 query heads and 4 KV heads of 128, Q/K/V/O without bias,
  per-head RMSNorm on Q and K (before RoPE). `layer_types`: 4 of every 5 layers are sliding-window attention
  (key k visible to query q when q - window < k <= q, window 513: 512 earlier tokens and the current one) with
  RoPE over halves (theta 10,000, the whole head); every fifth layer is global causal attention without
  position encoding ("NoPE").
- Every layer's MLP is a mixture of experts: a router (float32 logits from `ffn_gate_inp`), the top 6 of 384
  experts by logit + `exp_probs_b` (the selection bias, not in the weights), weighted by sigmoid(logit) without
  renormalization; each expert a SwiGLU of width 512 (`ffn_gate_up_exps`: gate rows then up rows), plus one
  shared SwiGLU expert of width 512 added unweighted.
- Four norms per layer (all RMSNorm, eps 1e-6, weights as they are): `attn_norm` before attention,
  `attn_post_norm` on the attention output before the residual add, `ffn_norm` before the MLP, `ffn_post_norm`
  on the MLP output before the residual add. Then `output_norm` and an untied `output` head.

GGUF tensors (NumPy shapes, rows are output features; `blk.N.` per layer):

    token_embd.weight [vocab, hidden]   output.weight [vocab, hidden]   output_norm.weight [hidden] F32
    attn_norm, attn_post_norm, ffn_norm, ffn_post_norm .weight [hidden] F32
    attn_q.weight [heads*128, hidden]   attn_k/attn_v.weight [kv*128, hidden]   attn_output.weight [hidden, heads*128]
    attn_q_norm, attn_k_norm .weight [128] F32
    ffn_gate_inp.weight [experts, hidden] F32 (the router)   exp_probs_b.bias [experts] F32
    ffn_gate_up_exps.weight [experts, 2*512, hidden]   ffn_down_exps.weight [experts, hidden, 512]
    ffn_gate_shexp, ffn_up_shexp .weight [512, hidden]   ffn_down_shexp.weight [hidden, 512]

`Model` serves each expert tensor as chunks of whole experts (`ffn_gate_up_exps.K.weight`, 2-D: experts x rows
by columns), each at most `EXPERT_CHUNK_BYTES` (WebGPU's default storage binding size); a chunk is a byte range
of the stored tensor, so bundles hard-link the GGUF as for any tensor.
"""

import os
import json
from collections import OrderedDict
from dataclasses import asdict, dataclass
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np

from ygaraty.webgpu.checkpoints import families, gguf_remote, moe_gguf
from ygaraty.webgpu.checkpoints.moe_gguf import rms_norm, rope_halves, swiglu  # noqa: F401 (shared; re-exported)

ARCHITECTURE = "kolibri1"
# YGARATY_KOLIBRI_EXPERT_CHUNK_BYTES: smaller chunks for tests on tiny models (several chunks per layer)
EXPERT_CHUNK_BYTES = int(
    os.environ.get("YGARATY_KOLIBRI_EXPERT_CHUNK_BYTES", 128 * 2**20)
)
EXPERT_TENSORS = ("ffn_gate_up_exps", "ffn_down_exps")
LAYER_MATRICES = (
    "attn_q.weight",
    "attn_k.weight",
    "attn_v.weight",
    "attn_output.weight",
    "ffn_gate_shexp.weight",
    "ffn_up_shexp.weight",
    "ffn_down_shexp.weight",
)
LAYER_FLOATS = (
    "attn_norm.weight",
    "attn_post_norm.weight",
    "ffn_norm.weight",
    "ffn_post_norm.weight",
    "attn_q_norm.weight",
    "attn_k_norm.weight",
    "ffn_gate_inp.weight",
    "exp_probs_b.bias",
)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class KolibriConfig:
    layers: int
    hidden: int
    heads: int
    kv_heads: int
    head_dim: int
    vocab: int
    experts: int
    top_k: int
    expert_ffn: int  # each routed expert's SwiGLU width
    shared_ffn: int  # the shared expert's
    window: int  # keys a sliding layer sees, the current one included
    rope_theta: float
    eps: float
    attention_scale: float
    sliding: tuple  # per layer: True for sliding-window attention with RoPE, False for global attention without
    tied_embeddings: bool = False

    @property
    def intermediate(
        self,
    ):  # the per-token MLP scratch width the plan reserves (routed experts' gate/up rows)
        return self.top_k * 2 * self.expert_ffn

    def manifest(self):
        out = asdict(self)
        out["sliding"] = list(self.sliding)
        return dict(out, family=ARCHITECTURE, intermediate=self.intermediate)


def layer_type(config, index):
    return "sliding" if config.sliding[index] else "full"


def expert_chunks(experts, expert_bytes, limit=EXPERT_CHUNK_BYTES):
    """[(first expert, count)]: as few chunks as fit `limit` bytes each, as even as possible."""
    return moe_gguf.expert_chunks(experts, expert_bytes, limit)


class Model:
    """A Kolibri-1 GGUF: its tensors (memory-mapped, packed) and checked configuration."""

    family = ARCHITECTURE

    def layer_type(self, index):
        return layer_type(self.config, index)

    def __init__(self, path, config_path, *, tensor_only=False):
        import gguf

        self.path = Path(path)
        document = json.loads(Path(config_path).read_text())
        header = gguf_remote.read_header(self.path)
        family = families.FAMILIES[ARCHITECTURE]
        errors = [
            f["message"]
            for f in family.check(document, header)
            if f["level"] == "error"
        ]
        if errors:
            raise ValueError(
                "Unsupported Kolibri checkpoint: " + "; ".join(errors)
            )
        g = family.geometry(document, header)
        self.config = KolibriConfig(
            g["layers"],
            g["hidden"],
            g["heads"],
            g["kv_heads"],
            g["head_dim"],
            g["vocab"],
            g["experts"],
            g["top_k"],
            g["expert_ffn"],
            g["shared_ffn"],
            g["window"],
            g["rope_theta"],
            float(g["eps"]),
            float(g["attention_scale"]),
            tuple(g["sliding"]),
        )
        if tensor_only:
            from ygaraty.webgpu.checkpoints.gguf_metadata import tensor_reader

            self.reader = tensor_reader(self.path)
        else:
            self.reader = gguf.GGUFReader(self.path)
        self.tensors, self.experts = moe_gguf.read_tensors(
            self.reader, EXPERT_CHUNK_BYTES
        )

    def __getitem__(self, name):
        return self.tensors[name]

    def expert_rows(self, index, kind, expert):
        """Float32 weights of one expert: gate/up [2*expert_ffn, hidden] or down [hidden, expert_ffn]."""
        flat = self.experts[(index, kind)]
        rows = (
            2 * self.config.expert_ffn
            if kind == "ffn_gate_up_exps"
            else self.config.hidden
        )
        return flat.dequantize(
            rows=np.arange(expert * rows, (expert + 1) * rows)
        )

    def validate_webgpu(self):
        kinds = {}
        for t in self.tensors.values():
            kinds[t.kind] = kinds.get(t.kind, 0) + 1
        return kinds

    def layer_tensors(self, index):
        prefix = f"blk.{index}."
        return {
            name[len(prefix) :]: t
            for name, t in self.tensors.items()
            if name.startswith(prefix)
        }

    def expert_chunk_names(self, index, kind):
        return moe_gguf.chunk_names(self.layer_tensors(index), kind)


# --- NumPy reference, in the GGUF's layout ------------------------------------


def route(c, router, bias, h):
    """Top-k experts of each row of h: (indices [T, k] by descending biased score, ties to the lower index;
    weights [T, k] = sigmoid of the unbiased logits; margins [T]: the k-th minus the (k+1)-th biased score,
    how near the selection is to changing)."""
    logits = h.astype(np.float64) @ router.astype(np.float64).T
    scores = logits + bias.astype(np.float64)
    order = np.argsort(-scores, axis=1, kind="stable")
    chosen = order[:, : c.top_k]
    kth = np.take_along_axis(scores, chosen[:, -1:], axis=1)[:, 0]
    nxt = (
        np.take_along_axis(scores, order[:, c.top_k : c.top_k + 1], axis=1)[
            :, 0
        ]
        if c.experts > c.top_k
        else np.inf
    )
    weights = 1.0 / (1.0 + np.exp(-np.take_along_axis(logits, chosen, axis=1)))
    return chosen, weights, kth - nxt


class LayerWeights:
    """One layer's dense weights (dequantized), and its experts dequantized on demand (a few kept)."""

    def __init__(self, model, index, keep=64):
        self.model, self.index, self.keep = model, index, keep
        t = model.layer_tensors(index)
        get = lambda name: t[name].dequantize()  # noqa: E731
        self.attn_norm, self.attn_post_norm = (
            get("attn_norm.weight"),
            get("attn_post_norm.weight"),
        )
        self.ffn_norm, self.ffn_post_norm = (
            get("ffn_norm.weight"),
            get("ffn_post_norm.weight"),
        )
        self.q_norm, self.k_norm = (
            get("attn_q_norm.weight"),
            get("attn_k_norm.weight"),
        )
        self.q, self.k, self.v, self.o = (
            get("attn_q.weight"),
            get("attn_k.weight"),
            get("attn_v.weight"),
            get("attn_output.weight"),
        )
        self.router, self.bias = (
            get("ffn_gate_inp.weight"),
            get("exp_probs_b.bias"),
        )
        self.gate_s, self.up_s, self.down_s = (
            get("ffn_gate_shexp.weight"),
            get("ffn_up_shexp.weight"),
            get("ffn_down_shexp.weight"),
        )
        self.cache = OrderedDict()

    def expert(self, e):
        if e in self.cache:
            self.cache.move_to_end(e)
            return self.cache[e]
        value = (
            self.model.expert_rows(self.index, "ffn_gate_up_exps", e),
            self.model.expert_rows(self.index, "ffn_down_exps", e),
        )
        self.cache[e] = value
        if len(self.cache) > self.keep:
            self.cache.popitem(last=False)
        return value


def moe(c, w, h, routes=None):
    """The MLP of h[T, hidden]: routed experts (weighted) plus the shared expert. `routes`: appended with
    (indices, weights, margins)."""
    chosen, weights, margins = route(c, w.router, w.bias, h)
    if routes is not None:
        routes.append((chosen, weights, margins))
    out = swiglu(h @ w.gate_s.T, h @ w.up_s.T) @ w.down_s.T
    for e in np.unique(chosen):
        rows, slots = np.nonzero(chosen == e)
        gate_up, down = w.expert(int(e))
        g = h[rows] @ gate_up.T
        y = swiglu(g[:, : c.expert_ffn], g[:, c.expert_ffn :]) @ down.T
        out[rows] += (weights[rows, slots][:, None] * y).astype(np.float32)
    return out


def attention(c, sliding, q, K, V, positions):
    """Grouped-query attention of q[T, heads, d] over the cache K, V [S, kv_heads, d] (float64 softmax)."""
    return moe_gguf.grouped_attention(
        c.heads,
        c.kv_heads,
        c.attention_scale,
        q,
        K,
        V,
        positions,
        c.window if sliding else None,
    )


def layer_forward(c, w, x, cache, position, kv="f32", routes=None):
    """Layer `w.index` over x[tokens, hidden] at `position`, appending to cache = {"k": [], "v": []}."""
    T = x.shape[0]
    sliding = c.sliding[w.index]
    h = rms_norm(x, w.attn_norm, c.eps)
    q = rms_norm((h @ w.q.T).reshape(T, c.heads, c.head_dim), w.q_norm, c.eps)
    k = rms_norm(
        (h @ w.k.T).reshape(T, c.kv_heads, c.head_dim), w.k_norm, c.eps
    )
    v = (h @ w.v.T).reshape(T, c.kv_heads, c.head_dim)
    positions = np.arange(position, position + T)
    if sliding:
        rope_halves(q, positions, c.rope_theta)
        rope_halves(k, positions, c.rope_theta)
    if (
        kv == "f16"
    ):  # as the cache stores them (the current tokens' too: attention reads them back)
        k, v = (
            k.astype(np.float16).astype(np.float32),
            v.astype(np.float16).astype(np.float32),
        )
    cache["k"].append(k)
    cache["v"].append(v)
    out = attention(
        c,
        sliding,
        q,
        np.concatenate(cache["k"]),
        np.concatenate(cache["v"]),
        positions,
    )
    x = x + rms_norm(out.reshape(T, -1) @ w.o.T, w.attn_post_norm, c.eps)
    h = rms_norm(x, w.ffn_norm, c.eps)
    return x + rms_norm(moe(c, w, h, routes), w.ffn_post_norm, c.eps)


class StageReference:
    """Layers first..last-1 with their KV cache, for prefill then cached steps. `routes[layer]`: each call's
    (indices, weights, margins)."""

    def __init__(self, model, first, last, kv="f32"):
        self.c, self.kv, self.position = model.config, kv, 0
        self.layers = [
            (i, LayerWeights(model, i), {"k": [], "v": []})
            for i in range(first, last)
        ]
        self.routes = {i: [] for i in range(first, last)}

    def __call__(self, hidden):
        x = np.ascontiguousarray(hidden, dtype=np.float32)
        for i, w, cache in self.layers:
            x = layer_forward(
                self.c, w, x, cache, self.position, self.kv, self.routes[i]
            )
        self.position += x.shape[0]
        return x


def embed(model, tokens):
    return model["token_embd.weight"].dequantize(rows=tokens)


def run_layers(model, x, first, last, kv="f32", routes=None):
    """Layers first..last-1 over the whole sequence in one causal pass, one layer's weights at a time.
    `routes`: a dict filled with each layer's (indices, weights, margins)."""
    for i in range(first, last):
        log = [] if routes is not None else None
        x = layer_forward(
            model.config,
            LayerWeights(model, i),
            x,
            {"k": [], "v": []},
            0,
            kv,
            log,
        )
        if routes is not None:
            routes[i] = log[0]
    return x


def logits(model, hidden):
    """Final norm and output projection of hidden[positions, hidden] (float64)."""
    h = rms_norm(
        hidden, model["output_norm.weight"].dequantize(), model.config.eps
    ).astype(np.float64)
    W = model["output.weight"]
    out = np.empty((h.shape[0], W.shape[0]))
    for r in range(0, W.shape[0], 16384):
        rows = np.arange(r, min(r + 16384, W.shape[0]))
        out[:, rows] = h @ W.dequantize(rows=rows).astype(np.float64).T
    return out


# --- transformers cross-check (optional Torch) --------------------------------


def hf_layer_state(model, index):
    """Layer `index` as transformers' Kolibri1DecoderLayer state dict (float32 NumPy), from the GGUF tensors."""
    c, w = model.config, LayerWeights(model, index, keep=0)
    experts = [
        model.expert_rows(index, "ffn_gate_up_exps", e)
        for e in range(c.experts)
    ]
    downs = [
        model.expert_rows(index, "ffn_down_exps", e) for e in range(c.experts)
    ]
    return {
        "input_layernorm.weight": w.attn_norm,
        "post_self_attn_layernorm.weight": w.attn_post_norm,
        "post_attention_layernorm.weight": w.ffn_norm,
        "post_mlp_layernorm.weight": w.ffn_post_norm,
        "self_attn.q_proj.weight": w.q,
        "self_attn.k_proj.weight": w.k,
        "self_attn.v_proj.weight": w.v,
        "self_attn.o_proj.weight": w.o,
        "self_attn.q_norm.weight": w.q_norm,
        "self_attn.k_norm.weight": w.k_norm,
        "mlp.gate.weight": w.router,
        "mlp.gate.e_score_correction_bias": w.bias,
        "mlp.experts.gate_up_proj": np.stack(experts),
        "mlp.experts.down_proj": np.stack(downs),
        "mlp.shared_experts.gate_proj.weight": w.gate_s,
        "mlp.shared_experts.up_proj.weight": w.up_s,
        "mlp.shared_experts.down_proj.weight": w.down_s,
    }


def transformers_layer(model, config_path, index, x):
    """The same layer, from position 0 over x[T, hidden], through transformers' own Kolibri1DecoderLayer
    (eager attention and experts, float32): an independent check of the reference's norms, routing, RoPE,
    window and NoPE layers."""
    import torch
    from transformers import AutoConfig
    from transformers.masking_utils import (
        create_causal_mask,
        create_sliding_window_causal_mask,
    )
    from transformers.models.kolibri1 import modeling_kolibri1 as m

    config = AutoConfig.from_pretrained(
        str(Path(config_path).parent), local_files_only=True
    )
    config._attn_implementation = "eager"
    config._experts_implementation = "eager"
    config.quantization_config = None
    layer = m.Kolibri1DecoderLayer(config, index).to(torch.float32).eval()
    layer.load_state_dict(
        {
            k: torch.from_numpy(np.ascontiguousarray(v))
            for k, v in hf_layer_state(model, index).items()
        },
        strict=True,
    )
    T = x.shape[0]
    with torch.no_grad():
        h = torch.from_numpy(np.ascontiguousarray(x, dtype=np.float32))[None]
        positions = torch.arange(T)[None]
        embeddings = m.Kolibri1RotaryEmbedding(config=config)(h, positions)
        kwargs = dict(
            config=config,
            inputs_embeds=h,
            attention_mask=None,
            past_key_values=None,
            position_ids=positions,
        )
        try:
            mask = (
                create_sliding_window_causal_mask
                if config.layer_types[index] == "sliding_attention"
                else create_causal_mask
            )(**kwargs)
        except TypeError:  # older signature: cache_position
            kwargs["cache_position"] = torch.arange(T)
            mask = (
                create_sliding_window_causal_mask
                if config.layer_types[index] == "sliding_attention"
                else create_causal_mask
            )(**kwargs)
        out = layer(
            h,
            attention_mask=mask,
            position_ids=positions,
            position_embeddings=embeddings,
        )
        out = out[0] if isinstance(out, tuple) else out
    return out[0].numpy()


# File: packages/webgpu/src/ygaraty/webgpu/checkpoints/kquant.py
"""Q4_K and Q6_K quantization in NumPy (the `gguf` package only decodes K-quants).

A port of llama.cpp's reference quantizers (ggml-quants.c, `quantize_row_q4_K_ref` with `make_qkx2_quants`, and
`quantize_row_q6_K_ref` with `make_qx_quants`), without importance weights, vectorized over blocks of 256
values. The block layouts are GGML's, so `gguf.quants.dequantize` and the WebGPU kernels decode the output
unchanged. Bit-exactness with llama.cpp is not a goal (its float order differs); the format and the search are.

    quantize(x, "Q4_K") -> uint8 [rows, row_bytes]     x: float32 [rows, columns], columns a multiple of 256
"""

import numpy as np

QK = 256
BLOCK_BYTES = {"Q4_K": 144, "Q6_K": 210}


def row_bytes(kind, columns):
    if columns % QK:
        raise ValueError(
            f"{kind} rows need a multiple of {QK} columns, not {columns}"
        )
    return columns // QK * BLOCK_BYTES[kind]


def _rint(x):
    return np.rint(x).astype(
        np.int32
    )  # round half to even, as llama.cpp's nearest_int


def _q4k_subblocks(x, steps=20):
    """make_qkx2_quants(32, 15, x, weights, rmin=-1, rdelta=0.1, nstep) over x[..., 32]: (scale, min, L)."""
    nmax = 15
    av = np.sqrt(np.mean(x * x, axis=-1, keepdims=True))
    w = av + np.abs(x)
    lo = np.minimum(x.min(axis=-1, keepdims=True), 0.0)
    hi = x.max(axis=-1, keepdims=True)
    span = hi - lo
    flat = span[..., 0] == 0
    span = np.where(flat[..., None], 1.0, span)
    sum_w = w.sum(axis=-1, keepdims=True)
    sum_x = (w * x).sum(axis=-1, keepdims=True)

    iscale = nmax / span
    scale = 1.0 / iscale
    L = np.clip(_rint(iscale * (x - lo)), 0, nmax)
    best = (w * (scale * L + lo - x) ** 2).sum(axis=-1, keepdims=True)
    best_min = lo.copy()
    for step in range(steps + 1):
        iscale = (-1.0 + 0.1 * step + nmax) / span
        La = np.clip(_rint(iscale * (x - lo)), 0, nmax).astype(np.float32)
        sum_l = (w * La).sum(axis=-1, keepdims=True)
        sum_l2 = (w * La * La).sum(axis=-1, keepdims=True)
        sum_xl = (w * La * x).sum(axis=-1, keepdims=True)
        D = sum_w * sum_l2 - sum_l * sum_l
        ok = D > 0
        Ds = np.where(ok, D, 1.0)
        this_scale = (sum_w * sum_xl - sum_x * sum_l) / Ds
        this_min = (sum_l2 * sum_x - sum_l * sum_xl) / Ds
        positive = this_min > 0
        this_min = np.where(positive, 0.0, this_min)
        this_scale = np.where(
            positive, sum_xl / np.where(sum_l2 > 0, sum_l2, 1.0), this_scale
        )
        error = (w * (this_scale * La + this_min - x) ** 2).sum(
            axis=-1, keepdims=True
        )
        better = ok & (error < best)
        L = np.where(better, La.astype(np.int32), L)
        best = np.where(better, error, best)
        scale = np.where(better, this_scale, scale)
        best_min = np.where(better, this_min, best_min)
    scale = np.where(flat[..., None], 0.0, scale)
    return scale[..., 0].astype(np.float32), (-best_min[..., 0]).astype(
        np.float32
    )


def quantize_q4_k(x, steps=20):
    """x float32 [blocks, 256] -> uint8 [blocks, 144]: d, dmin (float16), 12 bytes of 6-bit scales and mins,
    128 bytes of 4-bit quants (units j and j+1 of each 64 share bytes: low and high nibbles)."""
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float32).reshape(-1, 8, 32)
    n = x.shape[0]
    scales, mins = _q4k_subblocks(x, steps)  # [n, 8]
    max_scale, max_min = scales.max(axis=1), mins.max(axis=1)
    inv_scale = np.where(
        max_scale > 0, 63.0 / np.where(max_scale > 0, max_scale, 1.0), 0.0
    )
    inv_min = np.where(
        max_min > 0, 63.0 / np.where(max_min > 0, max_min, 1.0), 0.0
    )
    ls = np.minimum(63, _rint(inv_scale[:, None] * scales)).astype(np.uint8)
    lm = np.minimum(63, _rint(inv_min[:, None] * mins)).astype(np.uint8)
    packed = np.zeros((n, 12), dtype=np.uint8)
    packed[:, 0:4] = ls[:, 0:4]
    packed[:, 4:8] = lm[:, 0:4]
    packed[:, 8:12] = (ls[:, 4:8] & 0xF) | ((lm[:, 4:8] & 0xF) << 4)
    packed[:, 0:4] |= (ls[:, 4:8] >> 4) << 6
    packed[:, 4:8] |= (lm[:, 4:8] >> 4) << 6
    d = (max_scale / 63.0).astype(np.float16)
    dmin = (max_min / 63.0).astype(np.float16)
    sd = d.astype(np.float32)[:, None] * ls.astype(np.float32)
    sm = dmin.astype(np.float32)[:, None] * lm.astype(np.float32)
    safe = np.where(sd != 0, sd, 1.0)[..., None]
    L = np.clip(_rint((x + sm[..., None]) / safe), 0, 15)
    L = np.where((sd != 0)[..., None], L, 0).astype(
        np.uint8
    )  # llama.cpp leaves a zero-scale unit's quants 0
    L = L.reshape(
        n, 4, 2, 32
    )  # 64-value groups: low nibble from the first 32, high from the second
    quants = (L[:, :, 0, :] | (L[:, :, 1, :] << 4)).reshape(n, 128)
    out = np.empty((n, 144), dtype=np.uint8)
    out[:, 0:2] = d.view(np.uint8).reshape(n, 2)
    out[:, 2:4] = dmin.view(np.uint8).reshape(n, 2)
    out[:, 4:16] = packed
    out[:, 16:] = quants
    return out


def _q6k_units(x):
    """make_qx_quants(16, 32, x, rmse_type=1) over x[..., 16]: the scale and quants L in 0..63."""
    nmax = 32
    a = np.abs(x)
    index = a.argmax(axis=-1)
    mx = np.take_along_axis(
        x, index[..., None], axis=-1
    )  # the signed value of largest magnitude
    amax = np.take_along_axis(a, index[..., None], axis=-1)
    tiny = amax < 1e-15
    mx_safe = np.where(tiny, 1.0, mx)
    w = x * x

    def fit(iscale):
        q = np.clip(_rint(iscale * x), -nmax, nmax - 1)
        qf = q.astype(np.float32)
        return (
            q,
            (w * x * qf).sum(axis=-1, keepdims=True),
            (w * qf * qf).sum(axis=-1, keepdims=True),
        )

    L, sumlx, suml2 = fit(-nmax / mx_safe)
    scale = np.where(suml2 > 0, sumlx / np.where(suml2 > 0, suml2, 1.0), 0.0)
    best = scale * sumlx
    for step in range(-9, 10):
        if step == 0:
            continue
        q, sumlx, suml2 = fit(-(nmax + 0.1 * step) / mx_safe)
        better = (suml2 > 0) & (sumlx * sumlx > best * suml2)
        L = np.where(better, q, L)
        new = sumlx / np.where(suml2 > 0, suml2, 1.0)
        scale = np.where(better, new, scale)
        best = np.where(better, new * sumlx, best)
    scale = np.where(tiny, 0.0, scale)
    return scale[..., 0].astype(np.float32)


def quantize_q6_k(x):
    """x float32 [blocks, 256] -> uint8 [blocks, 210]: 128 bytes of low 4 bits, 64 of high 2 bits, 16 int8
    scales, d (float16)."""
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float32).reshape(-1, 16, 16)
    n = x.shape[0]
    scales = _q6k_units(x)  # [n, 16]
    index = np.abs(scales).argmax(axis=1)
    max_scale = scales[np.arange(n), index]
    zero = np.abs(max_scale) < 1e-15
    iscale = np.where(zero, 0.0, -128.0 / np.where(zero, 1.0, max_scale))
    d = np.where(zero, 0.0, 1.0 / np.where(zero, 1.0, iscale)).astype(
        np.float16
    )
    q_scales = np.minimum(127, _rint(iscale[:, None] * scales)).astype(np.int8)
    unit = d.astype(np.float32)[:, None] * q_scales.astype(np.float32)
    safe = np.where(unit != 0, unit, 1.0)[..., None]
    L = np.clip(_rint(x / safe), -32, 31) + 32
    L = (
        np.where((unit != 0)[..., None], L, 32)
        .reshape(n, 2, 4, 32)
        .astype(np.uint8)
    )  # [n, half, quarter, 32]
    ql = np.empty((n, 2, 64), dtype=np.uint8)
    ql[:, :, :32] = (L[:, :, 0] & 0xF) | ((L[:, :, 2] & 0xF) << 4)
    ql[:, :, 32:] = (L[:, :, 1] & 0xF) | ((L[:, :, 3] & 0xF) << 4)
    qh = (
        (L[:, :, 0] >> 4)
        | ((L[:, :, 1] >> 4) << 2)
        | ((L[:, :, 2] >> 4) << 4)
        | ((L[:, :, 3] >> 4) << 6)
    )
    out = np.empty((n, 210), dtype=np.uint8)
    out[:, 0:128] = ql.reshape(n, 128)
    out[:, 128:192] = qh.reshape(n, 64)
    out[:, 192:208] = q_scales.view(np.uint8)
    out[:, 208:210] = d.view(np.uint8).reshape(n, 2)
    out[zero] = 0
    return out


def quantize(x, kind, *, steps=20, rows_per_call=4096):
    """Rows of x (float32 [rows, columns]) as GGML `kind` rows: uint8 [rows, row_bytes(kind, columns)]."""
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float32)
    rows, columns = x.reshape(-1, x.shape[-1]).shape
    width = row_bytes(kind, columns)
    out = np.empty((rows, width), dtype=np.uint8)
    flat = x.reshape(rows, columns)
    for first in range(0, rows, rows_per_call):
        part = flat[first : first + rows_per_call].reshape(-1, QK)
        packed = (
            quantize_q4_k(part, steps)
            if kind == "Q4_K"
            else quantize_q6_k(part)
            if kind == "Q6_K"
            else None
        )
        if packed is None:
            raise ValueError(f"No quantizer for {kind}")
        out[first : first + rows_per_call] = packed.reshape(-1, width)
    return out
